### [1] [HyperWorld: Hypergraph-Structured State Serialization Improves Learned Textual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00002)
*Yun-Jian Zhang,Chen-Wei Liang,Tian-Yi Zhang,Jian Ding,Yi-Lun Wu,Ao-Bo Li,Wei-Cong Su,Saifullah,Hong-Yu An,Mu-Jiang-Shan Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出HyperWorld研究文本环境中状态序列化对世界模型的影响，发现实体中心超边序列化在中小模型和分布外场景下表现最优，能提升效果预测和规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本环境中世界模型需从序列化状态描述学习符号动作效果，但序列化结构的作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 对比原始观测和三种符号序列化（独立句子、成对三元组、实体中心超边单元），所有变体使用相同训练目标：给定状态和动作，预测符号效果或判断动作不可行。

Result: 超边序列化在0.5B-1.5B模型和分布偏移下增益最明显；大模型缩小差距；成对三元组在分布内精确匹配可持平或略超超边，但超边在分布外事实F1和中小规模权衡上最强；下游贪婪规划中超边成功率最高。

Conclusion: 高阶状态组织是学习符号世界模型的简单有效归纳偏置，尤其在模型容量受限或测试环境与训练不同时。

Abstract: World models enable language-model agents to predict environment dynamics and plan before acting. In text environments, the model must learn symbolic action effects from serialized state descriptions, but the role of serialization structure remains underexplored. We present HyperWorld, a controlled study of state serialization for learned textual world models. We compare raw observations with three symbolic serializations of the same ground-truth state: independent sentences, pairwise triples, and entity-centered hyperedge units that group multiple related facts around entities and relations. All variants use the same training objective: given a state and an action, predict symbolic effects or judge the action infeasible. Across model scales, data budgets, and in-distribution and out-of-distribution test worlds, hyperedge serialization gives the clearest gains for 0.5B--1.5B models and under distribution shift. Larger models reduce the gap, and pairwise triples can match or slightly exceed hyperedges on in-distribution exact match, but hyperedges achieve the strongest out-of-distribution fact F1 and the best small-to-medium scale trade-off between feasibility detection and effect prediction. In downstream greedy planning, the hyperedge world model also attains the highest success rate among the tested representations. These results show that higher-order state organization is a simple but effective inductive bias for learned symbolic world models, especially when model capacity is limited or test environments differ from training.

</details>


### [2] [Discrete-Time MDP Modeling for Multi-Item Capacitated Lot Sizing with Stochastic Demand Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00004)
*Léa Bayati,Mohamed Dahmoune,Melek Rodoplu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文研究有限期内多物品有容量约束的批量生产问题，其中需求数量确定但到达期随机，提出需求层决策的DTMDP模型，并设计遗传算法进行求解，实验显示平均最优间隙约3.44%，速度提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际生产中需求到货时间往往不确定，但传统批量生产模型多假设需求到达期确定，难以刻画需求层面的容量竞争、特定需求延迟及分配相关的库存动态，因此需要一种能处理随机到达期且保持计算可行的方法。

Method: 将问题构建为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程，定义状态空间、动作集、转移核及单期成本；为隔离随机性影响，先与各分布最可能到达期的确定性对照比较；随后提出遗传算法搜索可行的状态反馈策略，并通过DTMDP精确评估策略适应度。

Result: 在330个基准实例上，遗传算法与精确解平均最优间隙约3.44%；90个困难案例中均低于5%阈值，平均优化速度提升6.89±1.41（95%置信水平）；对无法精确求解的实例，采用经验回归估算所需时间并外推加速效果。

Conclusion: 随机需求到达期会显著增加问题规模和求解难度，但所提出的遗传算法能在保持良好解质量的同时大幅降低计算耗时，为实际随机时序批量问题提供有效求解途径。

Abstract: This paper studies a finite-horizon multi-item capacitated lot-sizing problem in which demand quantities are deterministic, while demand-arrival periods are stochastic. Each demand occurs once within a known time window and must be satisfied no later than its deadline. The proposed model makes production and allocation decisions at the demand level, allowing it to represent capacity competition, demand-specific backlog, and allocation-dependent inventory dynamics. The stochastic problem is formulated as a discrete-time Markov decision process (DTMDP), including the state space, feasible actions, transition kernel, and one-period cost function. To isolate the computational effect of stochastic timing, each stochastic instance is first compared with a deterministic counterpart in which each arrival distribution is replaced by its most likely arrival period. This comparison shows that stochastic timing substantially increases the number of states, the number of transitions, solution time, and memory pressure. A genetic algorithm (GA) is then proposed for the stochastic-timing problem. The GA searches over feasible state-feedback policies and evaluates each policy exactly under the DTMDP transition model. Computational experiments on 330 benchmark instances show that the GA remains close to the exact stochastic solution whenever the latter is available, with an average optimality gap of about $3.44\%$. On the difficult benchmark instances, comprising 90 test cases, the GA remains below the $5\%$ optimality-gap threshold and achieves an average optimization speedup of $6.89 \pm 1.41$ at the $95\%$ confidence level. For instances that cannot be solved exactly on the available hardware, an empirical Bellman-time regression is used to estimate the missing exact resolution time and extrapolate the expected GA speedup.

</details>


### [3] [DISTAL: Distillation and Self-Supervised Pretraining for Structure-Agnostic Materials Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00059)
*Weiran Wang,Xintong Huo,Yueying Wang,Yusi Fan,Wenyan Wang,Xin Feng,Ruihao Xin,Lan Huang,Kewei Li,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出DISTAL双先验框架，结合自监督成分预训练与结构知识蒸馏，实现无需结构输入的材料性能预测，在39个基准任务中37项优于参考基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料性能预测在小数据场景下困难，且依赖晶体结构的模型不适用于早期筛选阶段；需要开发无需结构输入的高精度预测方法。

Method: 利用145个成分描述符在大规模虚拟成分空间进行自监督预训练，再通过知识蒸馏将预训练ALIGNN教师的结构知识转移给成分条件学生模型，最后在统一框架中集成显式成分描述符、预训练潜特征与蒸馏结构特征进行多模态预测。

Result: 在39个基准任务上，最佳多模态配置结合全部三种信号，其中37项任务超过参考基准，整体性能为所有特征组合中最强。

Conclusion: 成分预训练与结构蒸馏提供互补先验，为小数据材料信息学中稳健的成分唯预测提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Materials property prediction remains difficult in low-data settings, where many target properties are supported by only a limited number of labeled samples. Models with the strongest predictive accuracy often depend on crystal structures, which restricts their use in early-stage screening when structural information is limited or unavailable. To address this challenge, we propose DISTAL, a dual-prior framework for structure-agnostic materials property prediction that combines self-supervised compositional pretraining with structure-aware knowledge distillation. DISTAL first learns transferable compositional representations from a large virtual composition space using 145 composition-derived descriptors. It then distills structural knowledge from a pretrained ALIGNN teacher into a composition-conditioned student. This setting allows structural priors to be used during training without requiring structural inputs at inference. By integrating explicit compositional descriptors, pretrained latent features, and distilled structural features within a unified prediction pipeline, DISTAL captures complementary signals that are difficult to recover from any single representation alone. Across 39 benchmark tasks, the best-performing multimodal configuration combines all three signals, and improves over the reference benchmark on 37 tasks. DISTAL achieves the strongest overall performance among all evaluated feature combinations. These results indicate that compositional pretraining and structural distillation provide complementary priors and offer a practical route to robust composition-only prediction in small-data materials informatics. The source code and the pre-trained models are anonymously available at: https://osf.io/eq96d/overview?view_only=451617f42f7849e08750bd1852b48980 and will be released at the official link after acceptance.

</details>


### [4] [ReNFT: Repairing Mode Collapse in Reward Post-Training via Internal Probability-Mass Recalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00061)
*Yuchen Bao,Chao Wen,Haowei Wang,Ruoxin Chen,Donghao Luo,Jiahui Zhan,Wenjian Huang,Shen Chen,Yiting Wang,Taiping Yao,Chengjie Wang,Shouhong Ding,Jianguo Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ReNFT，通过内部概率质量重校准修复已坍缩的扩散模型适配器，在保留98.9%-99.0%奖励的同时将多样性提升55%-58.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励后训练导致扩散生成器陷入模式坍缩，现有缓解方法依赖外部信号或接口，无法修复已坍缩且保留已获奖励的适配器。作者发现坍缩本质是概率质量抑制而非内容删除，可从生成器内部逆转。

Method: 利用无条件探针优先选择'反枢纽'提示，构造配对反事实提议（冻结基座方向与后训练无条件倾向），结合奖励排序与自适应翻转守卫分配推拉角色，执行联合成对NFT更新以重校准内部概率质量。

Result: 在PickScore和GenEval上，ReNFT分别保留NFT奖励的98.9%和99.0%，同时将DreamSim-Div多样性提升58.8%和55.0%。

Conclusion: ReNFT提供了一种无需外部干预的互补方案，可在不牺牲奖励的前提下有效恢复生成多样性，验证了坍缩可逆性及内部修复的可行性。

Abstract: Reward post-training of diffusion generators inevitably concentrates probability mass on a few reward-favored modes, a mode collapse that erases within-prompt diversity. Existing methods for mitigating collapse rely on external signals or interfaces, augmenting the reward with perceptual objectives, adjusting reference regularization, or modifying the text encoder, but none repairs an adapter that has already collapsed while preserving the acquired reward. We observe that online post-training primarily reallocates probability mass over capabilities inherited from pretraining rather than learning new visual content. Collapse is therefore suppression, not deletion, and can be reversed from within the generator. We propose ReNFT, which repairs a high-reward, low-diversity adapter through internal probability-mass recalibration. Unconditional probes first prioritize "anti-hub" prompts where the prompt-independent bias is easiest to expose. Two policy-dominated mixed routes then generate matched counterfactual proposals from the same prompt and initial noise, one probing the frozen base direction for suppressed alternatives and the other exposing the post-trained unconditional tendency. Reward ranking with an adaptive flipping guard assigns pull and push roles, and a joint-and-paired NFT update realizes the repair. On PickScore and GenEval, ReNFT retains 98.9% and 99.0% of NFT's reward while improving DreamSim-Div by 58.8% and 55.0%, respectively, offering a complementary alternative to external interventions.

</details>


### [5] [Long-Horizon State Tracking in LLMs: Executing MD5 through a Deep Sequence of Dependent Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00012)
*Dheeraj Mohandas Pai,Lu Xian*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文通过让LLM逐步计算MD5哈希（196次依赖工具调用）来纯净测试长程状态跟踪能力，发现gpt-oss-120b在多数运行中能完整携带状态并输出正确摘要，且成功取决于保留推理上下文和对思考型worker投票去错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程任务评估混淆了状态跟踪与指令理解，缺少隔离控制组，且易被幻觉最终答案等捷径干扰，无法解释长程失败原因；LLM能否跨多次工具调用精确携带中间状态本身尚未证实。

Method: 设计MD5逐步计算任务：64轮共196次依赖工具调用，模型在上下文中携带四个32位字(a,b,c,d)，每步与RFC 1321 ground-truth对齐并逐位校验摘要；最强设置下用第二个LLM替换所有原始工具（驱动者+工作者无精确算术预言机），并测试保留推理上下文和投票机制的影响。

Result: gpt-oss-120b（约5.5B激活参数，温度0，短固定提示）在多数完成运行中跨196次调用携带完整状态并返回正确摘要；成功由两个非权重因素决定：每轮保留模型自身推理上下文，以及对启用思考的工作者投票以消除模算术错误；残差失败按来源（状态携带/算术/服务）被定位。

Conclusion: LLM在纯簿记任务中能够跨长程依赖调用精确携带状态，且无需权重改变即可通过上下文保留和投票提升成功率；这为长程智能体评估提供了隔离状态跟踪难度的可控方法，并揭示了失败的具体来源。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks remain uncommon in large language model (LLM) evaluation, and for a reason: when each step depends on the last, per-step accuracy that looks excellent in isolation decays catastrophically, as errors cascade and the end-to-end failure probability grows sharply with length. Existing agentic benchmarks report end-to-end success but confound this state-tracking difficulty with instruction interpretation, give no control group that isolates it, and are vulnerable to shortcuts such as a hallucinated final answer, so they cannot say why a long run fails. Whether an LLM can carry exact intermediate state across many tool calls at all is itself not well established. We test this cleanly by having the model compute a cryptographic hash, MD5, step by step: a sequence of $196$ dependent tool calls over $64$ rounds while it carries four $32$-bit words $(a,b,c,d)$ in its own context from one call to the next. Interpretation is trivial and, because we implement MD5 from scratch (RFC~1321), we align every call to the ground-truth trace and check the digest to the bit, so any failure is pure bookkeeping. gpt-oss-120b, a mixture-of-experts model with only $\sim$5.5B active parameters per token, at temperature $0$ with a short fixed prompt, carries the full state across all $196$ calls and returns the correct digest on a majority of completed runs. In the strongest setting we replace every primitive tool with a second LLM, so a driver and a worker compute the whole hash from scratch with no exact-arithmetic oracle in the loop. Two ingredients decide success and neither changes the weights: keeping the model's own reasoning in its context each turn, and voting over a thinking-enabled worker to remove its modular-arithmetic slips. We localize the residual failures by origin, separating state-carrying from arithmetic and from serving.

</details>


### [6] [Attention Sensitivity Is Not Enough: Dissociating Attention-Level and Behavioural In-Context Learning under Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00064)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Peng He,He Hu,Yin Yuan,ShengShuo Jiao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文证明仅优化注意力层面的上下文学习（ICL）代理指标会导致Goodhart效应：ICL敏感性可被推至几何上限，但行为层ICL能力几乎不变且MMLU准确率下降，说明该代理不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL保护诊断常依赖注意力变化作为上下文敏感性的代理，但该代理被直接优化时是否仍反映真实行为尚未验证。

Method: 提出ICL敏感性（ICS）与行为准确率差距（ICL-GAP）双指标，在Llama-2-7B上设计四臂消融实验，对比ICS最大化正则化器、行为门控等目标，并结合端点统计与随机标签协议分析机制。

Result: ICS最大化正则化器使ICS达1.413（达几何上限99.5%），但ICL-GAP几乎为零，MMLU从0.371降至0.279；注意力集中于格式与演示主体标记而非标签，随机标签协议下行为探针仍有动态范围。

Conclusion: 注意力级ICL代理仅经行为差距验证后才能作为训练目标；锚定预训练计算的目标能兼顾高MMLU与中等ICS，而行为门控仅部分缓解，核心启示是诊断工具需行为校准。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) lets large language models adapt to new tasks from demonstrations, and fine-tuning can erode this behaviour. Many preservation diagnostics inspect attention: if attention changes when demonstrations change, the model is treated as context-sensitive. This paper asks how far that proxy can be trusted once it is optimised. We formalise \emph{In-Context Sensitivity} (ICS), the average row distance between last-token attention on matched and mismatched demonstration prefixes, and pair it with \emph{ICL-GAP}, the behavioural accuracy gap between the same prefixes. In a controlled four-arm ablation on Llama-2-7B, an ICS-maximising regulariser ($\armKL$) drives ICS to $1.413$, within $0.5\%$ of its geometric ceiling. The behavioural readout tells a different story: ICL-GAP stays near zero and MMLU accuracy moves from $0.371$ to $0.279$, a Goodhart dissociation of the bounded attention proxy. Endpoint statistics locate the mechanism: attention grows sharp and near-disjoint across prefixes yet routes to formatting and demonstration-body tokens rather than labels. A random-label protocol confirms that the behavioural probe family retains dynamic range at the same checkpoints. In a constructive sweep, behaviour gating partially mitigates the effect, while objectives anchored to pretrained computation hold the high-MMLU, moderate-ICS region that divergence maximisers leave. The main lesson is diagnostic: attention-level ICL proxies earn their place as training targets only after validation against behavioural gaps.

</details>


### [7] [SCAFFOLD: A Large-Scale Structured Dataset of Computer Science Research Figures with Diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00018)
*Ranjit Raut,Aarav Subedi,Sagun Rai,Sudan Jha*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SCAFFOLD数据集，包含计算机科学论文图表与问答及思维链推理，并基于Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct进行基线实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学论文严重依赖图表，但缺乏同时包含图、标题、上下文、问题、答案和逐步推理的公开数据集，限制了视觉语言模型的理解能力。

Method: 通过版面检测和PDF解析从arXiv论文中提取元组，结合AI辅助问题生成，构建三种规模的数据集（SCAFFOLD-157K/37K/12K）。

Result: 构建大规模数据集（157K对，覆盖3058篇论文），并对SCAFFOLD-12K进行基线实验。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD填补了该领域数据空白，但需进一步验证模型在更大数据集上的性能。

Abstract: Computer science papers rely heavily on diagrams: architecture drawings, system flowcharts, and pipeline schematics that often carry more information than the text around them. There is currently no public dataset that pairs this specific kind of figure with captions, context, questions, answers, and step-by-step reasoning, which is exactly what is needed to train a vision-language model to understand them. We present \textbf{SCAFFOLD}\footnote{https://github.com/theranjitraut/scaffold}, a large-scale structured dataset of computer science research figures with diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought reasoning traces. This dataset consists of (image, caption, context, question-answer, chain-of-thought) tuples from arXiv computer science papers prepared using layout detection and PDF parsing, with an AI-assisted question-generation step. The resulting large-sized SCAFFOLD-157K dataset spans 3,058 papers with 29,887 figures (157,387 pairs), a medium-sized SCAFFOLD-37K dataset (36,797 pairs), and a small-sized SCAFFOLD-12K dataset (12,000 pairs). We used SCAFFOLD-12K for baseline experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct.

</details>


### [8] [Assessing Alignment and Stability of Feature Importance Explanations via Weight of Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00090)
*Eddie Conti,Claudio Daka,Álvaro Parafita,Antonio L. Alfeo,Axel Brando,Mario G. C. A. Cimino*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出将特征重要性方法嵌入基于证据权重（WoE）的假设检验框架，以量化证据对特征重要性假设的支持程度，为FIMs提供对比性、基于证据的评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FIMs仅提供归因分数，难以揭示模型推理过程，缺乏对特征重要性假设进行系统化验证与评估的框架。

Method: 将FIMs置于WoE假设检验框架中，参考假设可来自领域知识、真实标签或FIM自身，并通过理论推导建立WoE与归因方差之间的联系。

Result: 在LIME和SHAP上，针对不同参考假设的实验验证了框架的适用性与灵活性。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估特征重要性方法提供了一种补充性的、基于证据的对比视角，有助于更深入理解特征归因的可靠性。

Abstract: Feature importance Methods (FIMs) are widely used in Explainable AI to interpret model predictions, yet attribution scores alone often provide limited insight into the underlying reasoning process. In this work, we introduce a novel perspective by embedding FIMs within a hypothesis-testing framework based on Weight of Evidence (WoE). We quantify how strongly the observed evidence supports any given hypothesis on feature importance. The reference hypothesis can stem from domain knowledge, ground truth, or be derived from the FIM itself. This formulation enables a principled evaluation of FIMs, capturing both their alignment with prior knowledge and their variability. We further provide theoretical results linking WoE to attribution variance. Empirical results shows the applicability and flexibility of our strategy analyzing LIME and SHAP explanations in settings with different reference hypotheses. Overall, our framework offers a complementary tool for assessing FIMs through a contrastive, evidence-based lens.

</details>


### [9] [When Prediction Error Is Not Enough: Evaluating Nuisance-Function Prediction for Causal Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00071)
*Cong Cao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本研究通过蒙特卡洛模拟，在部分线性模型中比较了多种估计方法，发现预测误差与因果估计性能（如偏差、RMSE、覆盖率）之间的关系并不一致，联合误差度量与因果偏差关联较弱，预测误差不应被直接视为因果估计质量的衡量标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断中常用预测误差评估 nuisance 函数估计器，但其与因果估计器性能（如偏差、区间覆盖）的关系可能因性能指标而异，现有研究对此缺乏系统验证。

Method: 在部分线性模型设定下，采用蒙特卡洛模拟，比较 OLS、GAMs、XGBoost 和 DML-XGBoost，评估 nuisance 函数预测误差、偏差、RMSE 及95%置信区间覆盖率，并引入基于暴露和结局 nuisance 函数估计误差绝对交叉积的联合误差度量。

Result: 非 oracle 方法中 XGBoost 的 RMSE 最低，DML-XGBoost 的置信区间覆盖率通常更好；预测误差在不同方法和设定下未能一致地反映因果偏差，点估计最优的方法未必有最佳覆盖率；联合误差度量与因果偏差仅呈弱相关，不适合作为独立性能指标。

Conclusion: 预测误差对评估 nuisance 函数估计有用，但不应用作因果估计质量的直接度量；研究强调了性能评估需区分点估计与推断覆盖，并建议谨慎依赖预测误差选择因果估计方法。

Abstract: Prediction error is widely used to evaluate nuisance-function estimators in causal inference, but its relationship with causal estimator performance may differ across performance measures. We studied this question in a partially linear model using Monte Carlo simulations. We compared ordinary least squares (OLS), generalized additive models (GAMs), XGBoost, and Double Machine Learning with XGBoost (DML-XGBoost), evaluating nuisance-function prediction error, bias, RMSE, and 95\% confidence interval coverage. We also examined a simple joint-error measure based on the absolute cross-product of estimation errors from the exposure and outcome nuisance functions. Across the simulated settings, XGBoost had the lowest RMSE among the non-oracle methods, while DML-XGBoost generally provided better confidence interval coverage. Prediction error did not consistently track causal bias across methods and settings, and the method with the best point-estimation performance did not necessarily have the best confidence interval coverage. The joint-error measure was only weakly associated with causal bias and did not provide a useful standalone measure of causal performance. These results suggest that prediction error is useful for assessing nuisance-function estimation, but it should not be treated as a direct measure of the quality of the resulting causal estimator.

</details>


### [10] [Faster Than Flash: Exploiting Attention Sparsity for Efficient Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00097)
*Zhigeng Liu,Zhiyuan Ning,Ruixiao Li,Xiaoran Liu,Yuerong Song,Min Zhang,Ziwei He,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Faster Flash Decoding（FFD），一种硬件-算法协同设计的解码框架，通过全融合内核、低比特量化和top-delta动态稀疏策略，在不损失精度的情况下实现最高11.6倍内核加速和2.37倍端到端吞吐提升，支持256K上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM解码受限于内存带宽瓶颈和注意力机制的二次复杂度，现有基于元数据的指标存在内存开销，而自适应选择策略计算效率低，需同时解决两者间的固有矛盾。

Method: FFD将选择器与计算器融合为单一内核，用低比特量化的内容感知扫描替代外部元数据索引；提出top-delta策略，无需全局同步即可动态过滤块，实现分布自适应稀疏；支持训练免费、即插即用，并复用扫描结果用于计算。

Result: 在RULER和LongBench上验证，FFD实现高达11.6倍内核加速、2.37倍端到端吞吐提升，可扩展至256K上下文，同时保持模型精度和高比例稀疏性。

Conclusion: FFD通过软硬件协同设计有效突破长上下文解码的内存墙，提供一种无需训练的高效解决方案，兼顾性能与精度，代码已开源。

Abstract: The development of long-context Large Language Models (LLMs) is constrained by the memory bandwidth bottleneck and quadratic complexity of the attention mechanism during decoding. To overcome the inherent trade-offs between the memory overhead of metadata-based metrics and the computational inefficiency of adaptive selection strategies, we present Faster Flash Decoding (FFD), a novel hardware-algorithm co-design framework designed to break the memory wall in long-context decoding. FFD integrates the selector and computer into a fully fused kernel, replacing external metadata indices with content-aware scanning via low-bit quantization. Furthermore, we introduce the top-delta strategy, which dynamically filters blocks to achieve distribution-adaptive sparsity without global synchronization. Offering a training-free and plug-and-play solution, FFD also enables the reuse of scanning results for computation, achieving up to 11.6x kernel-level speedup and scaling to 256K context length, with 2.37x end-to-end throughput improvement. Empirical validation on RULER and LongBench confirms that FFD maintains model accuracy while delivering high-ratio sparsity, with code available at https://github.com/qluoluo/faster-flash-decoding

</details>


### [11] [Deploying and Evaluating a Smart-Agriculture Agentic Engine for Full-Season Soybean Farm Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00106)
*Ao Qu,Panagiotis Michelakis,Linyuan Han,Yiannis Hadjiyianni,Kun Ouyang,Konstantinos Siskos,Feng Li,Ran Meng,Jingchi Jiang,Dimitrios Stamoulis,Jie Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FAIRY，一个部署于实际大豆研究农场的全栈式智慧农业智能体系统，采用'一切皆事件'的执行范式，并评估了九种先进控制器在百个全季场景中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智慧农业系统缺乏能执行和评估从整地到仓储的全季时空农艺流程的智能体系统，需要整合多种数据源并在真实农场环境中验证。

Method: FAIRY基于'事件驱动世界模型'，将土壤/冠层传感器、无人机、卫星、气象站和农机操作等统一为状态变更事件，并在其上构建原子农艺技能库、多智能体控制器、边缘模型执行和全路径日志追踪。

Result: 在64垄田间开展了100个全季大豆场景的实验，结合智能体成功率、全路径时空正确性、token成本和边缘设备运行时对9种先进智能体控制器进行评估。

Conclusion: FAIRY提供了一个可部署的全栈解决方案，并展示了在真实农场环境中系统化评估不同智能体控制器的可行性，为智慧农艺智能体研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper presents FAIRY, a full-stack smart-agriculture agent system developed for and deployed to an operating soybean research farm at Harbin Institute of Technology's smart-agriculture site. We develop FAIRY to execute and evaluate agentic agronomic operations on full-season spatiotemporal workflows that span ridge preparation, planting, irrigation, fertilization, pest and disease treatment, harvest, grain handling, drying, and storage. FAIRY integrates APIs and infrastructure across production-grade machinery, fixed soil and canopy sensors, multispectral and thermal drones, satellite vegetation products, a weather station, calibrated crop-process models, agronomic records, and multi-season yield histories. The system is built around the novel "everything is an event" execution paradigm, which represents spatiotemporal world evolution, remote sensing and UAV observations, sensor readings, crop-growth transitions, machinery actions, and management interventions as state-changing events in a shared farm process engine. On top of this event-driven world model, FAIRY implements a complete agentic stack: a knowledge library of atomic agronomic skills; multi-agent controller and orchestration backends; frontier- and edge-model execution; full-path trace logging; and deployment profiling on local nodes. We use FAIRY to evaluate nine state-of-the-art agent controllers across one hundred full-season soybean scenarios that preserve the operational coupling between spatial observations in a 64-ridge field, temporal decision sequences, agronomic constraints, delayed effects, and final yield. We develop an evaluation suite that combines agentic success, full-path spatiotemporal correctness, token cost, and edge-device runtime.

</details>


### [12] [Good Memory Has ECC: Evaluating the Memory of Vision-Language Models Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00103)
*Shmuel Berman,Jia Deng*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ECCBench基准，从效率、压缩、校准三个维度评估LLM/VLM的记忆能力，发现现有模型在压缩和校准上存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准仅关注准确率，无法反映实际长周期任务中效率、压缩和不确定性处理等关键属性。

Method: 定义ECC三轴评估协议：效率（FLOPs）、压缩（可压缩输入的记忆增益）、校准（基于不确定性的弃权能力），并系统性对比多种记忆架构。

Result: 预训练VLM在文本记忆上有压缩能力但视频上缺失，两类输入校准均差；非Transformer架构在压缩-校准权衡上优于RoPE Transformer。

Conclusion: 非Transformer架构可能更适合长周期智能体任务，ECCBench为记忆评估提供了超越准确率的新标准。

Abstract: Memory is widely viewed as an important unsolved problem for LLMs and VLMs, and current benchmarks typically evaluate it by testing accuracy over long text or video. However, accuracy alone misses properties that matter for real long-horizon tasks. We introduce ECCBench, a benchmark and evaluation protocol that measures memory beyond a system's capacity--its raw accuracy at a specific budget--via three axes we call ECC: efficiency--the computation, in FLOPs, needed to answer from memory; compression--whether compressible inputs are remembered more accurately or efficiently; and calibration--whether the system abstains in response to its own uncertainty and the cost of an error. We find that pretrained VLMs compress their memory over text but not video and are poorly calibrated on both. Among a broader set of memory backbones, several non-Transformer architectures achieve better compression-calibration tradeoffs than RoPE Transformers, suggesting they may be useful components for agents operating over long horizons.

</details>


### [13] [Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00129)
*J. M. Diederik Kruijssen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出'天生缺陷，进化完美'机制，通过AI模型系综的故意变异作为统计对冲，在非平稳环境中可持续提升性能，并以四定理保证遗憾减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML模型在环境漂移下性能退化，而传统优化导致多样性丧失，缺乏应对非平稳性的通用设计原则。

Method: 对模型系数施加偏离最优的随机变异，形成种群级统计对冲；通过四定理证明遗憾减少；提出自适应控制器调节变异漂移率以匹配环境漂移率。

Result: 在合成线性回归任务中，变异系综在约80%的环境变化后提供最佳模型，且变异率匹配环境漂移率时效果最优。

Conclusion: 该机制可作为AI/ML系统抗非平稳性的通用设计原则，其与生物进化的类比暗示可能是有机智能形式的关键缺失要素。

Abstract: The performance of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) models degrades when the problem they were trained on drifts. This is a near-universal feature of real-world problems, which often change unpredictably. Biological evolution has achieved intelligence by overcoming this obstacle through natural selection acting on heritable variation. AI/ML techniques have long incorporated forms of natural selection, but it has been challenging to maintain model diversity as optimization naturally drives convergence. Here we show that a swarm of AI/ML models subjected to deliberate mutations of their model coefficients away from optimality can reliably and sustainably improve performance in changing environments by acting as a statistical hedge against non-stationarity. We call this mechanism 'Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution', reflecting that the collective performance gain goes at the expense of individual performance. We prove via four theorems that the resulting regret reduction is guaranteed under general conditions, establishing the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism as a generalizable design principle for AI/ML systems. We validate these results on synthetic linear regression tasks, demonstrating that the mutated swarm delivers the best model in $\sim80\%$ of environment changes and that inference synthesis successfully translates this individual advantage into a collective one. The mechanism proves to be most effective when the mutation drift rate matches the drift rate of the environment. We outline a simple, adaptive controller that enables practical applications by tuning the mutation drift rate to match the unknown drift rate of the environment. The close analogy of the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism to biological evolution suggests it may have been a critical missing ingredient for the organic discovery of AI forms that more closely mimic biological intelligence.

</details>


### [14] [IMPACT: Attention Is the Interaction Map for Scalable Interaction-Aware World Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00161)
*Rongze Tang,Jianjie Fang,Zhaolu Wang,Ziyou Wang,Xvyuan Liu,Haisheng Su,Xin Zhang,Wei Wu,Chen Gao,Yong Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出IMPACT框架，通过内部注意力先验和局部误差校准重新加权去噪监督，解决世界模型在动作条件下物理交互生成中静态内容主导训练导致动态区域监督不足的问题，无需外部表示或推理时修改，显著提升交互保真度与物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在动作条件未来预测中难以建模物理上合理的交互，常依赖外部稠密表示（如运动、几何、语义）来约束生成，但这些表示需要辅助估计器或人工标注，限制训练可扩展性。作者发现全局平均MSE去噪目标存在监督分配不匹配：静态内容占据主导梯度信号，而关键的稀疏动态对象区域监督不足，导致交互生成质量差。

Method: IMPACT框架包括三步：1）利用与操作对象令牌关联的交叉注意力作为内部时空先验，指示动作条件变化区域；2）从先验采样候选区，并用分离梯度的局部预测误差校准，构建交互图；3）用该交互图重加权去噪监督损失，实现自适应训练。无需外部表示或推理改动，兼容不同DiT骨干和控制模态。

Result: 在机械臂和人类手部操作任务上（涵盖多种控制模态和DiT骨干），IMPACT一致优于对应MSE训练的基线，在交互保真度、物理合理性和视觉质量三项指标上均有提升，验证了内部先验+重加权机制的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过重新审视训练目标并纠正监督分配失衡，IMPACT证明了仅利用模型内部注意力先验和动态损失加权即可大幅提升世界模型的物理交互生成能力，为无需外部稠密标注的可扩展交互感知训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: World models have made remarkable progress in action-conditioned future prediction for embodied agents, yet still struggle to model physically plausible interactions. Existing approaches address this limitation by constraining the generation process with external representations encoding motion, geometry, or semantics. Obtaining these spatiotemporally dense representations typically requires auxiliary estimators or manual annotations, limiting training scalability. We instead revisit the training objective and identify a supervision-allocation mismatch under the globally averaged mean squared error (MSE) denoising objective: prevalent static content dominates the optimization signal, leaving sparse dynamic-object regions critical to interaction generation disproportionately under-supervised. Motivated by this observation, we introduce IMPACT, a scalable Interaction-aware Model training framework with Prior-guided Attention Calibration and Targeting. IMPACT uses cross-attention associated with manipulated-object tokens as an internal spatiotemporal prior for action-conditioned changes. It samples candidate regions from this prior, calibrates them with detached local prediction errors to construct an interaction map, and uses the map to reweight denoising supervision, requiring neither external representations nor inference-time modifications. Extensive experiments on robot-arm and human-hand manipulation, spanning diverse control modalities and DiT backbones, show that IMPACT consistently outperforms the corresponding MSE-trained baselines, improving interaction fidelity, physical plausibility, and visual quality.

</details>


### [15] [WHALE: A Simple Recipe for Joint Harness-Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00196)
*Haechan Kim,Yoonho Lee,Gisang Lee,Chelsea Finn,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出WHALE，一种交替更新模型权重与执行代码（harness）的联合优化方法，在三个领域中显著优于仅优化单一组件或阶段式优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体性能受模型参数与执行代码共同影响，单独优化任一环节会因另一环节固定而受限。现有方法仅联合优化权重与文本提示，未考虑更广泛的执行代码。

Method: WHALE交替执行两个阶段：在当前harness下用在线拒绝采样微调更新模型，然后在更新后的模型下用Meta-Harness搜索更好的harness。切换时机通过固定周期或基于训练信号的适应性耐心规则决定，以避免过拟合或误判。

Result: 基于Qwen3.5-2B/4B智能体，在搜索问答、数学推理和象棋谜题三个任务中，WHALE在最佳mean@8准确率上比仅权重、仅harness及Fast-Slow训练高出4.15到24.38个百分点。

Conclusion: 权重与harness均可成为瓶颈，交替小步更新优于阶段式先权重后harness优化，在准确率和降低回滚成本方面均更优。

Abstract: Agent performance depends jointly on the model parameters and the executable harness code that manages context and control flow. Optimizing either component in isolation can leave the system bottlenecked by its frozen counterpart: weight updates can change which harness is effective, while harness updates can change which model capabilities are exposed. Existing joint-adaptation methods optimize weights and textual prompts but leave the broader harness fixed. We propose Weight-Harness Alternating LEarning (WHALE), a simple recipe that alternates two phases: updating the model under the current harness, then searching for a better harness under the updated model. We instantiate these two phases with online rejection-sampling fine-tuning and Meta-Harness, respectively. When to switch is a key design choice: to separate real improvements from noise without over-optimizing against a changing counterpart, WHALE uses either fixed phase durations or an adaptive patience rule over training signals. Using Qwen3.5-2B/4B agents across three domains (search question answering, mathematical reasoning, and chess puzzles), WHALE outperforms weight-only, harness-only, and Fast-Slow Training by 4.15-24.38 percentage points in best mean@8 accuracy. Either component can be the bottleneck: harness search matches peak weight-only accuracy with far fewer rollouts in SearchQA, but improves math accuracy only after a weight update. Small interleaved updates also outperform stagewise weight-then-harness optimization in accuracy and rollout cost. The code is available at https://github.com/krafton-ai/WHALE.

</details>


### [16] [QTEA: Ternary LLMs with Sparse Residual Salient Weight and By-Column Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00224)
*Yipin Guo,Arun M George,Jie Fu,Tareq Mahmoud,Sixue Xing,Siddharth Joshi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: QTEA是一个面向大语言模型的亚2比特仅权重后训练量化框架，通过三元量化、基于1:4半结构化稀疏性的显著权重残差补偿、列级重缩放与交替更新以及误差衰减技术，在有效1.7比特/权重的压缩下显著提升精度与困惑度，并实现7.2倍生成加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仅权重PTQ方法在低于2比特时精度严重下降且跨模型泛化性差；利用非结构化稀疏虽缓解精度损失，但破坏硬件友好性与执行规律性。需一种同时兼顾极低比特精度与GPU高效执行的方案。

Method: 将权重量化为三元值，并用显著权重作为残差补偿器；残差被分配到具有半结构化1:4稀疏性的显著列中。在GPTQ逐列量化基础上引入列级重缩放细化，交替更新每列缩放因子与三元分配。识别GPTQ中顺序依赖的误差传播，引入误差衰减机制抑制后期误差累积。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B上，有效1.7比特/权重，平均精度较最强三元PTQ基线提升16.7%，WikiText和C4困惑度分别降低1.40倍与2.61倍；在Llama3-8B上精度提升6.6%，困惑度分别降低1.34倍与1.95倍。所提基于查找表的核实现较FP16基线在逐词生成上快7.2倍。

Conclusion: QTEA证明结合半结构化稀疏残差补偿、列级交替优化与误差衰减，可在亚2比特下实现高精度与硬件高效的大模型压缩，为极低比特PTQ提供实用方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ) can alleviate the computational burden of serving large language models (LLMs) at scale. However, existing PTQ methods often fail to generalize across models and suffer severe accuracy loss below 2 bits. Many leverage unstructured sparsity to mitigate this loss, but at the cost of regularity and GPU-friendly execution. We present QTEA, a sub-2-bit PTQ framework that quantizes weights into ternary values and uses salient weights as residual error compensators. To maintain hardware efficiency, residuals are assigned to selected columns with semi-structured \(1{:}4\) sparsity within the salient columns. We further add column-wise rescale refinement to GPTQ-style column-by-column quantization, alternately updating per-column scales and ternary assignments to reduce reconstruction error. We also identify order-dependent error propagation in GPTQ and introduce error decay to attenuate late-stage error accumulation. On Qwen3-14B, QTEA compresses all weights to an effective 1.7 bits per weight while improving average accuracy over the strongest ternary PTQ baseline by 16.7\%. It also achieves 1.40\(\times\) and 2.61\(\times\) lower perplexity on WikiText and C4 respectively. This trend holds on Llama3-8B, where QTEA obtains a 6.6\% accuracy gain and 1.34\(\times\)/1.95\(\times\) lower perplexity on the same datasets. Finally, we develop a lookup-table based kernel that achieves 7.2\(\times\) faster per-token generation over an FP16 baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Intelligent-Microsystems-Lab/QTEA.

</details>


### [17] [Do LLMs Know Your Neighborhood? Auditing LLM Priors for Neighborhood-Level Mobility Prediction and Structural Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00345)
*Saad Mohammad Abrar,Eesha Kurella,Arnav Dadarya,Naman Awasthi,Kazi Tasnim Zinat,Vanessa Frias-Martinez*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该研究评估了零样本大语言模型在 Census Block Group 级别预测人类流动性的能力，发现其准确率（0.435）显著低于监督模型（0.580），且预测依赖粗粒度先验，与实证趋势存在方向性偏差，尤其是对受保护群体预测因子的处理不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精细轨迹数据常受隐私和使用限制，LLM 可能作为替代数据源生成或预测流动性，但现有研究缺乏对 LLM 在聚合邻里层面（如 Census Block Group）预测能力的系统性检验。

Method: 使用四个美国大都市区的匿名 Cuebiq 轨迹数据，构建点级、轨迹级和时间维度流动性结果，结合社会人口和建成环境预测因子；比较零样本 LLM 与监督基线模型，并引入方向一致性分析验证 LLM 隐含的预测因子效应是否与 OLS 和 Jonckheere-Terpstra 趋势一致。

Conclusion: LLM 能部分从城市上下文恢复聚合流动性模式，但其预测缺乏结构基础，不应直接视为可靠，需审计经验对齐性和潜在偏见后才可使用。

Abstract: Human mobility is central to urban planning, transportation, public health, and emergency response, yet fine-grained trajectory data are often proprietary, restricted, and privacy-sensitive. Large language models (LLMs) offer a potential alternative by generating plausible mobility traces and predicting individual movement, but their ability to infer aggregate neighborhood-level mobility remains unclear. We compare LLM predictions with supervised baselines and introduce a directional alignment analysis to test whether LLM-implied predictor effects agree with empirical OLS and Jonckheere-Terpstra trends. *Tarik Can Ozden,Sachidanand VS,Furkan Horoz,Ozgur Kara,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Junho Kim,James Matthew Rehg*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ConvDeck，一个通过阶段特定对话循环让用户参与学术论文转幻灯片迭代生成的多智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有论文转幻灯片方法中，用户对迭代细化过程的参与有限，反馈往往在完整幻灯片生成后才引入，无法在叙事流、内容分配和重点呈现等关键决策阶段进行干预。

Method: ConvDeck采用多智能体流水线，将交互分散在各阶段（如大纲生成和幻灯片生成），每个阶段设有对话循环；循环内智能体具备思考、发言和行动能力，可直接编辑或通过对话澄清用户反馈与讨论修订方案。

Conclusion: 在决策发生的阶段引入用户对话式反馈，可在不损害幻灯片质量的前提下实现更符合用户意图的自动生成。

Abstract: Automatic academic paper-to-slide generation is inherently iterative, because creating an effective presentation requires repeated cycles of generation, critique, and revision. Recent multi-agent systems partially acknowledge this through internal critique-and-revise loops, while conversational approaches allow users to refine generated slide decks through dialog. However, these refinement processes either remain largely closed to the user or introduce feedback only after a complete deck has been produced, limiting the user's ability to participate in the iterative refinement of narrative flow, content allocation, and presentation emphasis. To address this gap, we introduce ConvDeck, a multi-agent pipeline for conversational paper-to-slide generation that distributes interaction across the pipeline through stage-specific loops, allowing users to iteratively refine both the presentation outline and the final slide deck at the stages where each kind of decision is made. These loops are driven by a refinement mechanism in which agents can think, speak, and act, enabling them to either directly apply edits or respond conversationally to clarify user feedback and discuss revision options. Our evaluation shows that stage-specific conversational feedback improves user-goal satisfaction while preserving narrative coherence, content quality, and visual presentation.

</details>


### [19] [Deterministic LLM Inference Across GPU Kernels: Power-of-Two INT8 Quantization Scales and the Limits of Tolerance-Based Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00363)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 量化GEMM内核的一致性测试套件对容差敏感，部分故障仅在单bfloat16间隔内移动输出导致无法检测；将权重缩放改为2的幂可使不同内核位级一致并保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符合性套件声称能验证实现间一致性，但实际能检测的故障范围未知，需量化其检测能力并探索可部署的改进。

Method: 向INT8流水线注入9种故障，在Qwen3-1.7B的8232个单元上评估；进一步将权重缩放最近2的幂，测试CUTLASS与Triton的一致性及生成质量。

Result: 5种尾声故障仅移动单bfloat16间隔，容差1间隔无法检测4种；强制2的幂缩放使线性层位级一致（196/196和252/252），困惑度变化小，但以前报告的+157%是伪影。

Conclusion: 此类套件仅能确认前提条件、操作数共享和差异范围，而非完全互换性；2的幂缩放是可行的改进，且需分离权重-缩放失配与约束本身的效应。

Abstract: Conformance suites for quantized GEMM kernels ask whether two implementations agree within a tolerance. We measure what such a suite can detect. Injecting nine faults into a reference INT8 pipeline over 8,232 layer--fault--regime cells of Qwen3-1.7B, we find that every one of five epilogue faults -- scale precision, double rounding, multiplication order, output truncation, fused ordering -- moves the output by at most a single bfloat16 spacing, and by exactly one whenever it moves it at all, across 5,880 cells. A tolerance of one spacing is therefore blind to the entire class by construction: four of the five faults are detected by no check in the suite, and the fifth only under power-of-two scales. Faults that violate the accumulator's exactness preconditions, or that break operand sharing, are detected without exception, and a null fault never fires. What a tolerance-based suite of this shape establishes is therefore narrower than interchangeability: that the preconditions hold, that operands are shared, and that differences stay within one spacing. The power-of-two constraint that exposes the one detected fault is also deployable. Requantizing every weight scale to its nearest power of two makes CUTLASS and Triton agree bitwise at every linear layer (196/196 and 252/252, against 8/196 and 10/252 under the checkpoints' own scales) and yields byte-identical generated token sequences at 1.7B, 8B and 14B (8/8 prompts, against 0/8 at all three). Observed perplexity point estimates are +0.32%, -0.28% and +0.48%; the 90% intervals cover zero at the two smaller sizes but not at 14B, reaching +0.71% and +0.76%. A previously reported +157% perplexity for this intervention was an artifact of a probe that rewrote scales without requantizing the weights; separating the effects attributes 99.8% of it to the resulting weight--scale mismatch rather than to the power-of-two constraint itself.

</details>


### [20] [Learning What to Retain: Gated-Memory Routing for Efficient Collaboration in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00237)
*Rakibul Hasan Rajib,Mengxing Zheng,Qian Lou*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出门控记忆路由框架，通过可学习的写入门和检索门维护紧凑执行记忆，动态选择角色和骨干模型并自适应停止，在推理和代码生成任务上提升准确率并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统难以根据协作状态动态调整编排，仅依赖查询的路由缺少中间进度信息，而基于完整执行历史的路由又会累积冗余上下文导致高成本，因此需要一种紧凑且信息丰富的状态表示来指导决策。

Method: 设计学习型记忆写入门筛选非冗余推理步骤，学习型检索门为每个智能体提供紧凑的相关记忆子集，每个决策步骤基于查询和记忆选择下一角色与骨干模型，由自适应停止控制器在证据充分时终止执行。

Conclusion: 门控记忆路由通过压缩执行历史为关键记忆，在保持高准确性的同时显著降低计算开销，为多智能体系统的高效自适应编排提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems tackle complex reasoning by orchestrating how multiple agents are configured and how they collaborate. A central challenge is to adapt orchestration to the evolving collaboration state. Routing from the query alone cannot adapt to intermediate progress or errors, which hurts accuracy. Routing from the complete execution history supplies this missing context, but forces later decisions to process every prior step, including redundant or low-utility ones. This creates an execution-history overload that inflates cost. Effective orchestration instead requires a compact state that captures useful progress without accumulating redundant context. We propose Gated-Memory Routing, which conditions each decision on the query and a learned execution memory. A learned Memory Write Gate commits only non-redundant reasoning steps, and a learned Retrieval Gate supplies each agent a compact, relevant subset, so every decision conditions on a clean, informative state. At each step, the system selects the next role and backbone from this memory, while an Adaptive Halting Controller stops execution once the memory contains sufficient evidence for answering. Across five reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, our framework is both effective and efficient: it attains the best average accuracy, exceeding the strongest baseline by 2.44 points, while reducing HumanEval inference cost by 31.9% relative to that baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/rajibrhasan/gated-memory-routing

</details>

*Michael Wu,Arquimedes Canedo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出失效契约协议层，通过版本戳+缓存提示标记API错误修复建议，在不牺牲节省的前提下处理服务端数据漂移。行级失效在高合规模型上提升0-66.7个百分点，恢复29-33%基线token成本，表级失效则导致全部失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理缓存API错误修复可减少token与模型调用，但服务端数据漂移使缓存修复静默失败；完整重推导消除节省，需要一种精确失效机制。

Method: 设计失效契约协议层：每个恢复建议附带版本戳与可缓存性提示；客户端按行级区间失效删除过时条目，保留有效缓存；将节省分解为有效性（协议决定）与合规性（规划模型决定）两个独立因子。

Result: 7模型×3服务路径×2领域×9400集评估：行级失效提升合规0~66.7百分点（3个模型达55.6~66.7），4/7模型恢复29-33%基线token成本；表级失效使5/7模型首次尝试成功率为0；行级精度1.00；协议额外开销15%且零契约失败。

Conclusion: 行级失效契约是处理数据漂移的实用方法，区分协议决定的有效性与模型决定的合规性；模型输入schema保守性（Sonnet 5拒绝新增字段）是合规瓶颈，而契约本身跨模型一致性高且确定性可靠。

Abstract: LLM agents that cache recovery suggestions from API errors can skip re-derivation in later episodes, spending fewer tokens and fewer model calls on constraints they have already learned. Server-side data drift turns those cached fixes into silent failures, and the usual remedy, re-deriving on every episode, gives the savings back. We introduce invalidation contracts, a protocol layer that attaches version stamps and cacheability hints to every recovery suggestion so the client can evict stale entries without trial and error, and keep the rest. The contract decomposes realized savings into two independent factors: validity, the fraction of cached suggestions that remain correct after a drift event, and compliance, the fraction the planner applies on the first attempt. Validity depends only on the protocol and is vendor-independent. Compliance depends on the planner model: identical wire bytes yield 100% first-try compliance on Claude Haiku 4.5 and 11% or below on Claude Sonnet 5, which exhibits input-schema conservatism, refusing fixes that add fields the original request did not contain. We evaluate across seven models, three serving paths, two domains, and approximately 9,400 episodes. Row-level invalidation raises compliance by 0 to 66.7 percentage points across the seven models, 55.6 to 66.7 on three, and recovers 29-33% of baseline token cost on four of seven models, while table-level invalidation destroys co-located entries and drops post-drift first-try rates to 0% on five of seven. Eviction precision is 1.00 at row granularity on every model under the row-level oracle of Section 4.1. The contract adds 15% to response payload. Version-stamp validity is deterministic by construction and produced identical results across every model and serving path, with zero contract failures in the entire evaluation.

</details>

Abstract: A lack of suitable datasets has limited the research into the privacy risks of novel smart city sensors, such as thermal cameras, depth cameras, and lidar. Given the number of unsubstantiated privacy claims and their potential widespread deployment into many people's everyday life, understanding the privacy risks of these sensors -- in isolation and in like-for-like comparisons -- is crucial. With MultiGait, we collected the first multi-sensor, multi-perspective, multi-session gait-focused dataset, for the corresponding, and additional more far-reaching investigations. The dataset, validated with multiple state-of-the-art recognition systems, comprises various walking modes and annotated personal attributes for 199 individuals, to ensure the benefit for advanced studies including cross-sensor recognition and anonymization at the edge. MultiGait represents a foundation for rigorous privacy investigations, demonstrated through an extensive identity inference benchmark across eight sensors, four perspectives, and three recording sessions. Our benchmark incidentally reveals that sensors often assumed to be privacy-friendly do still entail considerable identity inference risks, while the poor cross-session generalization of existing methods underscores an important research gap.

</details>


### [23] [A Multi-Branch Feature Fusion Approach for Health Misinformation Detection and Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00403)
*Mkululi Sikosana,Sean Maudsley-Barton,Oluwaseun Ajao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多分支融合框架，结合ELM与TPB理论，融合语义、修辞、立场及心理特征，用于检测健康 misinformation 并引入可解释的传播风险评分CPS。在三个数据集上表现优异，AUC最高0.9999，排序相关性接近完美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 misinformation 检测多依赖纯语义，缺乏理论指导与传播风险量化，且在无完整互动数据时难以评估扩散风险，需要更透明、可解释且可扩展的检测方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a multi-branch fusion framework for detecting and characterising the propagation of health misinformation in online social networks (OSNs). In addition to binary classification, we introduce the Cognitive Propagation Score (CPS), an interpretable post-hoc auxiliary score computed from psychologically motivated, text-derived cues capturing argument complexity, emotional intensity, and content-derived virality potential, to support diffusion-risk reasoning when engagement ground truth is incomplete or unavailable. Compared with representative literature baselines, the fusion model improves detection on Constraint and COVID--19\_FNIR, while Monkeypox remains more challenging, reflecting domain- and signal-specific differences. Ablation analysis further indicates that psychological and rhetorical branches provide complementary gains beyond semantic embeddings. Overall, the framework bridges cognitive theory and neural modelling to improve transparency and to support scalable misinformation monitoring, with future work required to validate CPS against human-centred diffusion judgements.

</details>


### [24] [How Temporal Correlations Shape Memory in Linear Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00420)
*Arnol Manuel Fokam,Fasseu Sieyondji Akpevwoghene,Edem Fiifi Dawson*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该文精确求解了线性递归神经网络（LRNN）在相关输入下的学习动力学，发现输入相关性会增加记忆保持的成本，并改变学习轨迹：记忆先构建后超调再部分删除，最终保留较少过去信息；记忆在由相邻输入相似度决定的阈值处关闭，且最优网络会自发构建前馈通路以实现零误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅在输入不相关时研究了LRNN的训练动力学，但真实序列通常具有相关性。理解相关性如何影响网络记忆的习得与保留，是揭示递归网络在真实数据上行为的关键，也是解释为何相关数据使网络变为变化检测器的基础。

Method: 作者对具有相关输入的LRNN学习过程进行精确解析求解。将输入相关性纳入记忆保持的成本项，推导出动力学方程，并分析其固定点、学习轨迹以及最优网络结构（含是否出现前馈通路）。通过将相关性简化为一阶相邻相似度参数，分离出阈值条件与记忆开关行为。

Result: （1）输入相关性重塑学习过程：记忆先增长、超调、再被部分清除，最终稳态记忆低于不相关情形；（2）记忆在由相邻输入相关系数决定的阈值处完全关闭，序列长度和长程相关不改变此阈值；（3）达到零误差需网络具有前馈通路，当存在一个额外隐含维度时训练会自动构建该通路，且网络最终表现为变化检测器。

Conclusion: 输入相关性将记忆保持转化为一项代价，该代价决定了网络是否及如何学习记忆。工作将输入单一统计属性转化为对记忆习得行为的定量预测，解释了递归网络在相关数据下倾向成为变化检测器的原因，并为理解训练诱导的架构自组织提供解析依据。

Abstract: The linear recurrent neural network (LRNN) is a simple model for studying how much memory a network builds up as it trains. For uncorrelated inputs, earlier work found that training itself settles the network between keeping the past and reacting only to the present. Real sequences are correlated, and we solve the learning dynamics exactly for correlated inputs. In the solution, keeping the past carries a cost. The whole effect of correlation lands on that cost. This cost reduces to the earlier one when inputs are uncorrelated and grows once they are positively correlated. Three findings follow. (1) Correlation reshapes the course of learning, not only its end. Memory builds, overshoots, and is partly removed, and the settled network keeps less of the past. (2) Memory switches off at a threshold set by one number, how much each input resembles the one just before it. Neither sequence length nor longer-range correlation moves this threshold. Memory is worth keeping only when the task needs the previous input more than the current input already supplies it through correlation with the past. (3) The best network changes too. Zero error demands a feedthrough, a path that passes the current input straight to the network's output and remembers nothing, and training builds it unprompted when given one spare hidden dimension. Our work turns one property of the input into a prediction of whether a network learns memory and explains why correlated data turns recurrent networks into change detectors.

</details>


### [25] [Group Adaptive Clipping Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00444)
*Sheng Jia,Xiao Wang,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan,Rein Houthooft*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出GAPO，一种自适应裁剪边界的GRPO改进方法，通过反向KL信任区域视角为高信号样本分配更大更新空间，在数学和代码推理任务上提升Pass@1和Pass@k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定IS比率裁剪边界忽略不同rollout的学习信号差异，低组成功率样本携带更强梯度信号却受抑制，导致探索不足。

Method: 基于反向KL信任区域，将裁剪边界动态关联到rollout优势，信号越强边界越宽，仅修改GRPO裁剪阈值，无需奖励塑形，保持原代理目标。

Abstract: Group relative policy optimization for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically uses a fixed importance-sampling (IS) ratio clipping boundary across all rollouts. We identify a key limitation: rare correct rollouts on harder problems and abundant correct rollouts on easier problems are clipped at comparable rates, despite contributing very different learning signals. Rollouts with low group success exhibit larger IS ratios and carry stronger gradient signal for exploration and solving new problems, yet are disproportionately suppressed by fixed clipping.

GAPO is motivated by a reverse-KL trust-region perspective, which suggests that rollouts with larger learning signal should receive proportionally greater update headroom. GAPO requires no reward shaping and preserves the standard PPO/GSPO surrogate while adapting only the clipping threshold. Across Qwen and Llama models, GAPO consistently improves both Pass@1 and Pass@k over fixed clipping and advantage-shaping baselines on math reasoning and coding benchmarks where the pass rates by the base model are relatively low.

</details>


### [26] [CRAD: Class-wise Reliability-Aware Distillation for Decentralized Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00446)
*Baraa Bilbeisi,Mengchen Fan,Baocheng Geng,Qing Tian*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化知识蒸馏联邦学习框架CRAD，通过按类别的可靠性加权聚合教师预测，解决异质架构和非独立同分布数据下的联邦学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习依赖参数平均，要求客户端架构相同且数据独立同分布，实际部署难以满足。现有去中心化蒸馏方法对所有教师预测简单平均，忽略不同教师在不同类别上的可靠性差异。

Method: 构建无中心服务器的去中心化知识蒸馏框架，每个客户端用本地数据评估其他客户端模型快照并蒸馏软预测。提出CRAD方法：对每个类别，先剔除与同行共识不一致的教师，再按类别可靠性（精确率或逆方差）加权平均剩余教师预测，支持度通过样本量n自动体现。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和PathMNIST三个图像分类基准上，跨异质架构且严重非独立同分布偏移场景下，CRAD的全局准确率一致优于对比方法。

Conclusion: CRAD通过按类别感知可靠性聚合教师知识，有效应对异质架构和非独立同分布数据，提升联邦学习性能。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning (FL) relies on parameter averaging, which forces clients to be doubly homogeneous: it demands an identical architecture and degrades under non-IID data. Real-world deployments usually break both assumptions. We sidestep both by building a decentralized knowledge distillation framework in which each client evaluates its peers' model snapshots on its own local data and distills from the resulting soft predictions. Because knowledge is transferred through the shared class posterior, clients are free to run different architectures; and because every teacher is evaluated on the student's own device, raw data never leaves the client, with no central server or public dataset required. Within this setting, we identify and address an under-examined problem: how to combine the peer teacher predictions. Existing methods, like uniform averaging, ignore how knowledge reliability varies across teachers and classes. We propose Class-wise Reliability-Aware Distillation (CRAD), which, per class, first discards teachers that disagree with the peer consensus and then takes a weighted average of the rest, weighting each teacher by its per-class reliability (precision, or inverse variance). Since the variance of an accuracy from $n$ samples scales as $1/n$, support enters automatically: among the teachers that survive filtering, a teacher is trusted for a class to the degree that it is both accurate and well-evidenced for it. On three image-classification benchmarks (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and PathMNIST colon pathology), across heterogeneous architectures under severe non-IID skew, CRAD consistently outperforms competing methods in global accuracy.

</details>


### [27] [HBQ: Hierarchical Scaling Block Quantization with Hardware-Efficiency-Aware Design for Accurate LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00450)
*Chun-Ting Chen,Dongmin Han,Hangyeol Mun,Jake Hyun,Arnab Raha,Amit Agarwal,Mark Anders,Mohamed Abdelfattah,Jae-sun Seo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出分层块量化（HBQ），通过大块量化与有效数字缩放平衡硬件效率与精度，在激活、权重和KV缓存上实现高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块量化（BQ）相比标量权重量化（WoQ）能同时量化权重和激活，提高硬件效率并支持统一数据路径推断，但其设计空间（位宽、块大小、缩放、数值格式）未被充分探索，且块大小增大会提高效率但降低精度，限制传统BQ方法。

Method: HBQ采用大块最大化效率，引入低开销的有效数字（SIG）缩放进行二级量化，针对激活和权重分布差异分配量化水平，补偿大块误差；并设计HBQ-A和HBQ-E变体，集成部分和BQ方案，在28nm ASIC上实现。

Result: HBQ-A以W4A5达到W4A16精度且面积小于NVFP4；HBQ-E硬件成本降低17%并保持更高精度。相比WoQ，面积/能量效率提升2.3倍/4.6倍；相比现有BQ，系统能量降低1.6-3.3倍，速度提升1.5-3.0倍且精度最优。

Conclusion: HBQ有效解决BQ中效率与精度权衡，通过在大型语言模型部署中提供最佳精度和显著硬件优势，优于现有权重量化和块量化方法。

Abstract: Block Quantization (BQ) is a promising approach for efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs), enabling low-precision computation with controlled accuracy degradation. Compared to scalar weight-only quantization (WoQ), BQ quantizes both weight and activation, offering higher hardware efficiency and end-to-end inference on a unified datapath, but its design space, spanning bit-width, block size, scaling, and numeric formats, remains underexplored.
  We provide hardware/benchmark results through design space exploration (DSE). We find that increasing block size improves hardware efficiency by amortizing dequantization and accumulation costs, but degrades accuracy. This trade-off limits conventional BQ methods.
  Motivated by this insight, we propose Hierarchical Block Quantization (HBQ). Unlike prior methods [1], [2], which use small blocks and conventional Power-of-Two (PoT) or integer-based scaling, HBQ uses large blocks to maximize efficiency and introduces low-overhead significand (SIG) scaling for second-level quantization. By allocating quantization levels effectively and accounting for distinct activation and weight distributions, SIG scaling compensates for large-block errors more effectively than prior PoT and INT schemes. HBQ-A (accurate) achieves W4A16-level accuracy using only W4A5 while requiring less silicon area than NVFP4. HBQ-E (efficient) further reduces hardware cost by 17% while maintaining higher accuracy than all existing BQ methods.
  We implemented a 28nm ASIC accelerator applying HBQ to weights, activations, and KV cache, and integrated a novel partial-sum BQ scheme to further reduce EMA energy.
  Compared to state-of-the-art WoQ, HBQ delivers $2.3\times$/$4.6\times$ higher area/energy efficiency at the same accuracy level; $1.6$--$3.3\times$ system energy reduction and $1.5$--$3.0\times$ speedup over prior BQ methods while providing best accuracy.

</details>


### [28] [Human-AI Co-Interpretation for Responsible AI: A Hermeneutic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00334)
*Behrooz Razeghi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM输出在解释性任务中缺乏明确解释框架、可替代解读和可溯源证据的“解释错位”问题，并从哲学诠释学出发提出人机协同解释的设计原则与数字诠释学素养框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律、教育、政策分析和公共道德论证等领域依赖LLM输出进行需证据和规范标准支撑的解释性工作，但模型生成的解读常被当作确定含义，缺乏解释框架、可替代方案和溯源能力，导致问责性丧失，风险不仅是事实错误更是责任不清。

Method: 基于哲学诠释学分析解释错位风险，区分LLM输出（候选解读）与人类解释者（负责任的诠释理解），将人机交互建模为AI中介的解释循环，对现有LLM技术进行重组，形成负责任使用的设计模式，并对相关学术文献进行结构化综合。

Result: 提出设计原则用于构建人机协同解释，识别出诠释学与AI领域研究的反复论证线索和设计空白，将现有技术转化为适用解释场景的设计模式，并讨论了对法律实践、教育评估、学术生产和公共道德论证的启示。

Conclusion: 倡导将数字诠释学视为一种素养，即通过检查框架、溯源和解读来阅读AI中介文本并质疑输出，以此恢复解释性任务中的问责性与可评估性。

Abstract: Across law, education, policy analysis, and public moral argumentation, LLM outputs are being used often for work that requires interpretations to be justified with textual evidence and explicit normative standards. Yet a recurrent failure mode -- what I call \textit{interpretive misplacement} -- is that model-generated readings get treated as settled meanings without an explicit interpretive frame (sources, scope constraints, normative commitments), without preserving defensible alternatives, and without provenance that lets readers find the supporting passages. In such settings, the risk is not only factual error but lost accountability: readers and institutions cannot reliably assess what an output commits them to, or on what basis. Drawing on philosophical hermeneutics, this paper discusses this risk and derives design principles for structuring human-AI co-interpretation. The paper also provides a structured synthesis of recent scholarship on hermeneutics and AI, organizing this emerging literature into a set of recurrent lines of argument and design-relevant gaps. LLM outputs are treated as candidate readings, whereas hermeneutic understanding is reserved for accountable human interpreters situated in disciplinary historical-linguistic traditions. Human-AI interaction is characterized as an AI-mediated interpretive loop. Hermeneutic understanding is distinguished from token-prediction--based text generation. On this basis, existing LLM techniques are reorganized into design patterns for hermeneutically responsible use in interpretive settings. Finally, the discussion turns to implications for legal practice, educational assessment and feedback, scholarly knowledge production, and public moral argumentation. It also treats digital hermeneutics as a literacy: the capacity to read AI-mediated texts by examining frames, provenance, and readings, and by contesting outputs.

</details>


### [29] [Can LLMs Use Relational Transformer Embeddings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00457)
*Francisco Galuppo Azevedo,Clarissa Lima Loures*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 将冻结的关系编码器嵌入以软令牌形式注入LLM并不奏效，混合模型性能常低于随机基线且不稳定，需更强的对齐目标和模式感知设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验将关系编码器嵌入作为软令牌注入LLM的融合策略，以避免有损文本序列化并利用LLM的语言与推理能力。

Method: 将冻结的Relational Transformer嵌入通过MLP投影注入Qwen3.5-4B，结合LoRA适配，先SFT后GSPO，在RelBench的10个二分类任务上按四种监督机制评估。

Conclusion: 软令牌融合需更强对齐目标和模式感知设计，否则无法成为可靠的关系预测路径。

Abstract: Injecting frozen relational-encoder embeddings as soft tokens into a large language model (LLM) is a conceptually appealing fusion strategy: the encoder handles multi-table structure, the LLM handles language and reasoning, and no lossy text serialization is required. We test this hypothesis concretely by injecting embeddings from a frozen Relational Transformer (RT) into Qwen3.5-4B via a learned MLP projection and LoRA adaptation, trained first with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on chain-of-thought reasoning traces and then with group-based reinforcement learning (GSPO). We evaluate across 10 binary classification tasks on 6 relational databases from RelBench, under four supervision regimes: single-task (ST), within-dataset (WD), cross-dataset (CD), and all-task (ALL). The hybrid model does not consistently outperform standalone RT: it is frequently below random, highly sensitive to serialization format and relational-token budget, and unstable under RL training. We report these negative results and analyze the failure modes, arguing that soft-token fusion requires stronger alignment objectives and schema-aware design before it can serve as a reliable route to relational prediction.

</details>


### [30] [SlideBank: A Persistent Hierarchical Evidence Bank for Consistent Whole-Slide Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00342)
*Beidi Zhao,Gexin Huang,Ciro Zhang,Anqi Li,Yusheng Tan,Chen Zhou,Gang Wang,Zu-hua Gao,Xiaoxiao Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SlideBank，一种无需训练的WSI视觉语言推理框架，将全切片图像转化为持久、概念索引、空间锚定的证据库，通过问题无关的粗细粒度探索和跨层级共识实现高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI中诊断相关的形态信息稀疏、异质且跨多尺度分布，现有模型难以在保留视觉证据关联的同时进行语义化访问和证据整合。

Method: 采用两阶段策略：前期进行问题无关的粗细粒度探索，提取多尺度形态观察并锚定到具体补丁和坐标；推理时通过问题路由到相关证据，并利用基于置信度的跨层级共识整合全局、区域和补丁信息。

Result: 在WSI-VQA和SlideBench-BCNB上分别达到52.77%（Patho-R1）和50.92%（Quilt-LLaVA）的准确率，结构化检索优于随机采样；重复查询时重述一致性超99%，且通过持久证据复用显著降低摊销推理成本。

Conclusion: SlideBank有效提升了WSI视觉语言推理的语义可访问性和证据利用效率，并通过证据持久化机制在保证鲁棒性的同时降低计算开销。

Abstract: Whole-slide images (WSIs) are challenging for vision-language reasoning because diagnostically relevant morphology is sparse, heterogeneous, and distributed across gigapixel-scale images and multiple spatial resolutions. Existing WSI models and pathology agents can aggregate slide features or actively acquire evidence, but the information retained after exploration is often difficult to access semantically while preserving its connection to the original visual evidence. We introduce SlideBank, a training-free framework that represents each WSI as a persistent, concept-indexed, and spatially grounded evidence bank. SlideBank performs question-independent coarse-to-fine exploration to identify informative regions and multi-scale views, converts them into explicit morphological observations, and grounds pathology signals to their supporting patches and WSI coordinates. At inference time, questions are routed to relevant signals and evidence scales, and the linked global, regional, and patch evidence is integrated through confidence-based cross-level consensus. Experiments on WSI-VQA and SlideBench-BCNB show that with Patho-R1, SlideBank reaches 52.77% on WSI-VQA and with Quilt-LLaVA, it reaches 50.92% average accuracy on SlideBench-BCNB, while structured signal-guided retrieval consistently outperforms random evidence sampling. Reusing the same bank across repeated queries further achieves over 99% rephrasing consistency and substantially reduces amortized inference cost through persistent evidence reuse.

</details>


### [31] [Context Window Failures in Relational Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00460)
*Denis Oliveira Correa,Francisco Galuppo Azevedo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 现有关系型深度学习基础模型在高基数关系数据上表现不佳，而简单的时态预聚合可显著提升性能，质疑其实际适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证关系型基础模型在处理高基数（如数万条相关记录）真实数据时的能力，指出其受限于邻居预算导致截断问题。

Method: 构造合成金融数据集Animus，其中收入预测需聚合数万交易；对比三个最新模型（RT、Griffin、RelGT）在原始表示和单一常规时态预聚合后的R²。

Result: 原始表示下三个模型R²≤0.18，预聚合后R²提升至0.65。

Conclusion: 当前关系型基础模型尚无法应对高基数真实世界数据，简单预聚合即可大幅超越，凸显其架构局限。

Abstract: Recent Relational Deep Learning architectures have been proposed as foundation models for multi-table relational data, yet they impose constrained neighborhood budgets that force row truncation when an entity has many related records. We introduce Animus, a synthetic financial dataset in which predicting customer income requires aggregating up to tens of thousands of transactions. On the raw representation, three recently proposed models (RT, Griffin, RelGT) achieve $R^2 \le 0.18$; a single, routine, temporal pre-aggregation step recovers $R^2$ up to $0.65$. This questions whether current relational foundation models are ready for high-cardinality real-world data.

</details>


### [32] [Vision Is Not Overhead: One-Pass Block Drafting for Lossless Speculative Decoding in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00355)
*Jungseob Lee,Seongtae Hong,Dongyub Jude Lee,Chanjun Park,Jaehyung Seo,Sugyeong Eo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 首个单遍块草稿器GLANCE，通过读取目标模型已融合的视觉-语言状态，在不修改目标模型情况下实现无损加速，解码速度最高提升2.93倍，接受块长度比现有方法长2.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码在视觉语言模型上陷入自败循环：草稿模型必须保持自回归而小规模，小模型无法每步处理图像，导致视觉信息被压缩或隐藏，从而在图像最能使文本可预测的地方反而最不可靠。

Method: 提出GLANCE，采用块扩散头直接读取目标模型的视觉-语言融合状态，无需额外视觉成本；一次前向生成整个块，用宽候选树在单次目标验证中并行校验，保证无损匹配贪心解码。

Result: 在统一测试条件下，解码速度比自回归快2.93倍；每轮只需一次草稿（EAGLE3-VL需八次）；接受块长度比同语料训练的EAGLE-3长2.7倍；接受长度由目标下一token熵决定，拟合斜率随任务接地性增强。

Conclusion: 接受长度与目标熵的关系在不同任务和模态间成立，指出自由运行文本仍适用于链式草稿，而接地任务则进入逐字复制模式，使块草稿显著受益。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates generation without changing its output, yet on vision-language models (VLMs) it has been caught in a self-defeating cycle. The drafter stays autoregressive, so it must stay small. A small drafter cannot afford the image at every step, so vision is compressed, pruned, or hidden. A drafter cut off from the image is then least reliable exactly where the image makes text predictable. We present GLANCE, the first one-pass block drafter that is lossless on an unmodified VLM target, and it breaks the cycle at both ends. A block-diffusion head reads the target's already-fused vision-language state, so vision costs the drafter nothing, and fills a whole block in one forward pass, so depth costs no sequential steps. A wide candidate tree is verified in one target pass, and every audited prompt reproduces greedy decoding exactly. Grounded workloads reward this most, entering a verbatim-copy regime whose long runs cost an autoregressive drafter a pass for every token and a block drafter one in total. Under one engine and one round budget, GLANCE decodes up to 2.93x faster than autoregression, from one draft pass a round where the production EAGLE3-VL head takes eight, and accepts 2.7x longer blocks than an EAGLE-3 head trained on the same corpus. One law organizes these results. Accepted length is set by the target's next-token entropy, with a fitted slope that steepens with grounding across all five tasks. The law transfers across targets and modalities and names its own boundary, since free-running text still favors a chain. Our code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/GLANCE.

</details>


### [33] [Dr. Claw: An AI Scientist Workspace for Vibe Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00365)
*Dingjie Song,Hanrong Zhang,Dawei Liu,Yixin Liu,Zongxia Li,Zhengqing Yuan,Siqi Zhang,Henry Peng Zou,Zhiling Yan,Yuxuan Zhang,Yanfang Ye,Philip S. Yu,Lichao Sun*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: Dr. Claw 是一个开源工作区，通过可审计的人机协同工作流包装现有编码代理执行器，实现研究过程的完整、可恢复和可追踪，而非引入新的自主代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有命令行编码代理虽能读写文件并支持长会话，但端到端研究仍分散于聊天、IDE、终端和写作工具中，且关键决策未被保留，导致过程缺乏可审计性。

Method: Dr. Claw 利用持久化状态对象、可复用技能库和多执行器协调，将计划、执行与写作整合为单一可追踪循环，并支持人工介入控制（人类在环），同时提供任务图、状态对象和技能库作为编排层。

Result: 在固定后端执行器条件下，Dr. Claw 相比裸命令行代理在研究完整性上得分更高，并保存了可审计、可恢复的过程轨迹。

Conclusion: Dr. Claw 通过编排层增强了现有编码代理的可用性和透明度，为复杂研究任务提供了可验证的工作流方案，并以开源形式发布。

Abstract: Command-line coding agents (e.g., Claude Code, Gemini CLI) can already read and write files and sustain long sessions, yet end-to-end research still fragments across chat tools, IDEs, terminals, and writing environments, and the decisions that make it auditable are rarely preserved. We present Dr. Claw, an open-source workspace that wraps existing coding-agent executors in a controllable and auditable human-in-the-loop workflow rather than introducing another autonomous agent. Persistent state objects, a reusable skill library, and multi-executor coordination link human decisions to AI execution, turning planning, execution, and writing into one traceable, recoverable loop. We demonstrate Dr. Claw through an interactive three-view scenario and a failure-recovery walkthrough, and evaluate it against a bare command-line agent sharing the same backend executor, so the comparison contrasts the whole orchestration layer (task graph, state objects, and skill library) with the agent it wraps. Holding the executor fixed, Dr. Claw scores higher on research completeness while persisting an auditable, recoverable process trail. Demo access: repository https://github.com/OpenLAIR/dr-claw, released under AGPL-3.0 with GPL-3.0 upstream components.

</details>


### [34] [RestoreBench: Can AI Agents Restore Power Flow Convergence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00384)
*Riccardo Mansutti,Andrea Pomarico,Robert Jakob,Qian Zhang,Alberto Berizzi,Kevin O'Sullivan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基准测试，用于评估LLM代理在电力系统潮流不收敛场景中的诊断和修复能力，涵盖三种架构和两个电网。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潮流不收敛的修复需要工程判断和试错，这是LLM代理的潜在应用，但目前缺乏系统评估基准。

Method: 构建仿真环境、观测/动作空间和评估指标，并比较chatbot、单代理和多代理三种架构在46个案例上的表现。

Result: 基准测试能区分不同LLM和架构的性能，并识别出多代理系统的潜在优势。

Conclusion: 该工作为开发电力系统规划与运行中的智能体AI提供了可复现的评估基础。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly automate multi-step engineering workflows through tool use, interpretation of intermediate results, and iterative planning. Diagnosing and resolving non-convergent power flow cases is a promising yet largely unexplored application, as it requires engineering judgment, experimentation, and decision-making within constrained action spaces. We introduce a benchmark that evaluates these capabilities across multiple LLMs and three architectures: \emph{chatbot}, \emph{single agent}, and \emph{multi-agent} systems. The evaluation covers two power grids and 46 cases per grid, each requiring one or more corrective actions to restore convergence. The benchmark defines the simulation environment, observation and action spaces, and evaluation metrics, providing a reproducible foundation for developing agentic AI systems for power system planning and operation. The code is available at https://github.com/Mansutti081/RestoreBench

</details>


### [35] [VATO: A Vortex-Force-Aware Transformer Operator for Unsteady Separated Aerofoil Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00507)
*Xingxin Yang,Zhan Zhang,Yichen Li,Juan Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出VATO方法，将涡力映射与几何感知神经算子结合，通过训练监督和交叉注意力机制提升非定常分离流场预测精度与气动力准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真CFD成本高，标准代理模型未区分对气动载荷贡献最大的流动区域，导致预测不准确。

Method: VATO-S引入VFM力贡献场的训练监督，不增加模型规模；VATO-A利用VFM贡献与灵敏度场优先选择力相关源位置进行残差交叉注意力。

Result: VATO-S将速度、压力、涡量误差分别降低10.4%、1.0%、15.6%；VATO-A分别降低15.8%、7.5%、31.2%，并在外推时保持优势。

Conclusion: 力感知的算子学习能同时改善非定常分离流的流场预测与气动功能精度。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of unsteady separated flows is challenging because the aerodynamic loads depend on nonlinear separation and vortex-shedding dynamics. Although high-fidelity CFD resolves these mechanisms, its cost limits repeated use in design and control. Standard field-level surrogate training, however, does not distinguish the flow regions that contribute most strongly to the aerodynamic loads. We introduce VATO (Vortex-Force-Aware Transformer Operator), which couples the Vortex Force Map (VFM) method to a geometry-aware neural operator through two complementary mechanisms. VATO-S adds training-only supervision of the local VFM force-contribution field, with no increase in model size or inference cost. VATO-A uses VFM contribution and sensitivity fields to prioritise force-relevant source locations for residual cross attention. The methods are evaluated on unsteady CFD data for double-edged-plate aerofoils over 54 trajectories from nine geometries. Over lead times of 1-20~ms, VATO-S reduces velocity, pressure, and vorticity errors by 10.4\%, 1.0\%, and 15.6\%, respectively, while VATO-A achieves reductions of 15.8\%, 7.5\%, and 31.2\%. VATO-S gives the lowest VFM-derived drag error, whereas VATO-A gives the lowest pressure-derived lift and drag errors. Over lead times extending 50\% beyond the training range, VATO-A retains a 26.9\% reduction in vorticity error and larger improvements in all four force readouts, despite reduced gains in velocity and pressure. These results show that force-aware operator learning can improve both flow-field prediction and aerodynamic functional accuracy in unsteady separated flows.

</details>


### [36] [Dependency-Aware Chain-of-Thought Compression for Financial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00413)
*Wenjun Wu,Lei Fu,Kejian Tong,Tao Ning,Sichen Zhao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出HSDN，一种层次化语义蒸馏网络，用于压缩思维链推理，在保持准确率的同时减少推理开销。在金融数据集AFAC2025上达到91.0%准确率和68.4%压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链虽提升复杂推理，但长中间步骤导致推理成本高，阻碍了金融等领域的实际部署。

Method: HSDN结合语义分割、依赖图构建、双编码器重要性评分、约束性段选择和局部边界重写。使用冻结的Qwen3 4B仅用于特征提取和最终答案生成，压缩过程结构化且可解释。

Result: 在AFAC2025基准上，HSDN达到91.0%准确率和68.4%压缩率，在总体得分和推理连贯性上优于强压缩基线。

Conclusion: 图引导的压缩方法对高风险的金融推理任务有效，展示了在保持逻辑连贯性的同时降低推理成本的可能性。

Abstract: Chain of thought prompting improves complex reasoning, but its long intermediate traces create substantial inference cost and hinder practical deployment in financial settings. We present a Hierarchical Semantic Distillation Network, HSDN, for compressing reasoning chains while preserving answer accuracy and logical coherence. The framework combines semantic segmentation, dependency graph construction, dual encoder importance scoring, constrained segment selection, and local boundary rewriting. A frozen Qwen3 4B model is used only for feature extraction and final answer generation, while the compression process remains structured and interpretable. On the AFAC2025 benchmark, HSDN achieves 91.0% accuracy with 68.4% compression, outperforming strong compression baselines in overall score and reasoning coherence. The results show that graph guided compression is effective for high stakes financial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [37] [SpecMind: Enabling Spectrum Intelligence via Multi-Agent Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00427)
*Songwei Dong,Bingyan Lu,Makayla Kienlen,J. Nicholas Laneman,Cong Shen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SpecMind，一种面向频谱智能的多智能体RAG系统，通过协调子智能体检索异构数据，并构建SpecBench评估集，在频谱任务上以超80%胜率优于传统RAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线设备激增导致频谱管理需精细化决策，但数据源异构分散、格式人工化，难以集成和自动化处理，缺乏领域专用RAG评估资源。

Method: 设计多智能体RAG架构SpecMind，包含协调主智能体与专职子智能体，分别处理政策文件、法规和许可数据库，实现分步检索与知识综合；同时构建基于真实执照和程序记录的问答数据集SpecBench。

Result: SpecMind在频谱相关任务上对强基线取得超80%胜率，检索准确率、上下文推理和多种查询类型的任务完成度均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 智能体协同机制能有效提升异构领域数据的检索与推理能力，为频谱管理自动化提供可行方案，并填补该领域评估基准空白。

Abstract: The exponential growth of wireless devices is driving unprecedented spectrum demand, pushing spectrum management toward more fine-grained decisions across space, time, and device constraints. As a result, spectrum policymakers and engineers must process large volumes of data that come from diverse sources and take many different forms, such as text and tables. These data sources are often disaggregated and require significant time and effort to integrate, search, and interpret. Furthermore, most of this information is formatted for human understanding and is not readily accessible to automated systems. To address this challenge, we propose SpecMind, a novel Multi-Agent Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system for spectrum intelligence that performs reasoning over heterogeneous data sources. This system enables autonomous agents to coordinate specialized sub-agents that retrieve and synthesize knowledge across policy proceedings, legal regulations, and license databases. We develop SpecBench, a question and answer (Q&A) dataset based on real-world license records and policy proceedings, addressing the lack of evaluation resources for RAG systems in the spectrum domain. Experimental results demonstrate that SpecMind outperforms traditional, general-purpose RAG systems across spectrum-related tasks, achieving over 80% win rate against strong baselines. The agent-based design enables more accurate retrieval, better contextual reasoning, and improved task completion across diverse query types.

</details>


### [38] [Conversation Coach: A Voice-enabled AI System that Helps Practice Difficult Workplace Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00441)
*Fanyou Wu,Suraj Maharjan,Ainur Yessenalina,Dennis Xu Chen,Rahul Srivastava,Srinivasan H. Sengamedu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出Conversation Coach，一个用于管理者练习困难对话的语音优先AI系统。通过对比端到端与级联架构，发现端到端延迟更低、成本更省，但级联推理更强。最终选择级联架构部署，已有4万多名管理者使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
