TL;DR: 提出GeoLAMP，一种几何感知的潜在自回归生成模型，用于在微米级不规则复杂几何中求解多物理场PDE，通过双编码器、流匹配潜在自回归和灵活解码器实现稳定长时程预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解多物理场PDE在科学计算中极具挑战，尤其是微米级复杂蜿蜒几何结构对能量与化学工程至关重要，现有方法难以高效准确处理此类高度不规则几何。

Method: 采用图表示上的双编码器架构分别捕捉全局拓扑与细尺度几何特征；在潜在空间使用带流匹配的因果自注意力Transformer进行时间建模，实现稳定可扩展的分块自回归预测；解码器可在任意点高分辨率重建物理场。

Result: 建立了三个复杂几何多物理场基准数据集（反应流、热对流、弹性），GeoLAMP在所有数据集上均实现最稳定的自回归性能，整个滚动预测过程中保持低误差。

Conclusion: 为微米级复杂几何中的PDE几何感知学习提供了系统研究，并为基于流匹配的潜在自回归PDE分块时间推进提供了新见解。

Abstract: Solving multiphysics partial differential equations (PDEs) remains a major challenge in scientific computing, especially for highly complex $μ$m-scale tortuous geometries critical to energy and chemical engineering. We address this challenge by proposing a Geometry-aware Latent Autoregressive generative Model for PDEs (GeoLAMP) for solving physics within highly irregular and tortuous structures. GeoLAMP introduces a dual-encoder architecture on graph representations to jointly capture global topology and fine-scale geometric features, enabling an effective transition from real-space fields to compact latent representations. In the latent space, we propose a causal self-attention transformer with flow matching to model temporal dynamics, allowing stable and scalable block-wise autoregressive prediction. A flexible decoder reconstructs high-resolution physical fields on arbitrary points. We establish three multiphysics benchmark datasets in complex geometries, covering reactive flow, heat convection, and elasticity. GeoLAMP consistently achieves the most stable autoregression performance on these datasets, maintaining low errors throughout the entire rollout horizon. Our results provide a systematic study of geometry-aware learning for PDEs in $μ$m-scale complex geometries and offer new insights into block-wise time marching of latent autoregressive PDE modeling via a flow matching framework.

</details>


### [114] [Effective Interventions Against AI-Enhanced Scams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00806)
*Kyle Fredrickson*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究AI驱动的诈骗新格局下，哪些干预措施仍然有效，并发现报告率、报告集中度和报告准确性三者的乘数效应能显著降低诈骗利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2025年全球诈骗直接损失达4420亿美元，美国报告损失在2020至2025年间增长近400%，AI的使用改变了诈骗的经济模式和瓶颈，亟需重新评估干预措施的有效性。

Method: 构建一个简单的诈骗利润模型，分析不同干预措施（报告率、报告集中度、报告准确性）对诈骗运营的渐进影响，并探讨其乘数效应。

Result: 三个杠杆（报告率、报告集中度、报告准确性）在影响每个诈骗渠道的预期受害者方面具有乘数效应，同时减少收入并增加成本；适度报告率针对高价值诈骗基础设施也可显著影响诈骗盈利能力。

Conclusion: 重视三项杠杆的协同干预，尤其是针对高价值诈骗基础设施的报告机制，能有效压缩AI诈骗的利润空间，为政策制定提供理论依据。

Abstract: In 2025, scams were responsible for an estimated $442 billion in direct losses globally. In the United States, reported losses increased by nearly 400% between 2020 and 2025. Though AI in scamming is a relatively new phenomenon, its use significantly changes the economics of scams as well as the bottlenecks in scam operations. In this paper I investigate what interventions will remain effective under this new AI-driven scamming regime.
  I develop a simple model of scam profits to understand how different interventions asymptotically affect scam operations. I find that three levers--reporting rate, centralization of reporting, and report accuracy--multiply in their effect on expected victims per scam channel, reducing revenue per scam channel while increasing costs. Because effects multiply, interventions affecting all three could have a significant effect on the profitability of the scam business model. My analysis suggests that even modest reporting rates against high-value scam infrastructure could have significant impacts on scam profitability.

</details>


### [115] [Authority Bias in Conversational Search Engines for Academic Paper Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00248)
*Uthman Jinadu,Parsa Ghazvinian,Anjila Budathoki,Benjamin M. Ampel,Rajshekhar Sunderraman,Yi Ding*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究通过因果实验证实LLM在学术文献推荐中存在权威偏见，且仅靠提示去偏无法有效消除，并发现表面审计会低估行为偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为学术搜索工具日益普及，但现有研究未因果检验其判断依据是内容还是权威信号，需揭示权威偏见的存在与影响。

Method: 在保持标题和摘要不变的情况下，操作作者、期刊和引用等权威元数据生成三种反事实条件，对5个开源和3个闭源LLM进行单轮上下文top-1推荐测试，并施加提示级去偏干预。

Result: 权威偏见显著且具有方向性，模型间差异大，提示去偏部分有效，且存在说做差距——表面提及减少远快于实际翻转减少。

Conclusion: LLM的权威偏见是系统性问题，提示级干预不足，表面审计不可靠，需开发更稳健的偏见检测与缓解策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as conversational search engines for academic literature, yet whether they judge papers on content or on authority signals has not been tested causally. We investigate authority bias: systematic preference for papers based on author prestige, venue, and citations rather than content. Holding title and abstract constant, we vary authority metadata across three counterfactual conditions (original, flipped, boosted) over eight LLMs (five open-weight and three frontier closed-weight) in an in-context, single-turn, top-1 recommendation setting. Our experiments show that authority bias is substantial and directional, varies markedly across models, and is only partially addressable through prompt-level debiasing. We further document a say-do gap: debiasing instructions suppress authority mentions far faster than authority-driven flips, so surface auditing systematically underestimates behavioral bias.

</details>


### [116] [Hypotheses-Guided Self Distillation for Continual Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00251)
*EunJeong Hwang,Kushan Mitra,Dan Zhang,Hannah Kim,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出HypReflect框架，通过显式且可修正的用户偏好假设实现持续个性化，在多种设置下优于基线并展示良好泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM助手需持续适应用户偏好，但偏好常是隐式、异质且含噪的，现有方法依赖原始交互或昂贵的奖励优化，缺乏可靠且可扩展的方案。

Method: HypReflect从多样化用户信号中推断显式且含不确定性的偏好假设，随证据累积进行反思式修正，并通过假设引导的自蒸馏将用户模型纳入系统。

Result: 在在线个性化、多会话交互和隐式行为信号三类设置中超越多种基线（如原始历史与增量更新方法），并展示对未见用户和跨域的良好泛化，以及在不同上下文预算下的稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过显式、可修正的偏好假设，提供了迈向可靠且可扩展的持续个性化的一步。

Abstract: As people increasingly interact with LLM assistants in daily life, continually adapting to individual preferences has become essential for effective long-term interactions. However, user preferences are rarely stated in full, and instead emerge through heterogeneous, latent, and noisy signals, with existing methods relying on raw interaction histories or costly reward-based optimization to manage personalization. We introduce HypReflect, a reliable, scalable framework for continual personalization that infers explicit, uncertainty-aware preference hypotheses from diverse user signals, reflectively refines them as new evidence accumulates, and incorporates the resulting user model through hypotheses-guided self-distillation. Experiments across three personalization settings: online personalization, multi-session interactions, and implicit behavioral signals, show that HypReflect outperforms a range of baselines, including raw-history and incremental-update methods. We further demonstrate strong generalization to unseen users and cross-domain settings, along with stability across context budgets, reusable hypotheses, and more focused personalization. These results suggest a step towards reliable and scalable continual personalization through explicit, revisable user preference hypotheses.

</details>


### [117] [Autoresearch for Marketplace Catalogs: From Legacy Forms to AI-Native Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00274)
*Kartik Ravisankar,Hojat Abdolanezhad,Daniel Capo,Sang Su Lee,Shishir Dash,Vijay Anand Raghavan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种自动研究循环，为双边服务市场按职业生成供应商偏好分类法，已在大型平台生产环境部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI原生概率匹配依赖推断意图，迫使平台重新生成供应商侧分类法，但现有固定层级不适用，需自动生成按职业划分的属性体系。

Method: 每个职业独立生成，迭代提出-评估-保留循环；候选标签由六准则LLM评判框架打分，7批评委面板加权惩罚，无硬否决；奇偶映射阶段将旧表单QA对映射到新分类法，先推断属性再映射。

Result: 自2026年4月起在生产环境覆盖132个职业，映射提供覆盖率信号并支持人工QA。

Conclusion: 分类法生成可作为独立问题处理，结合多角色评估与属性推断映射，能有效支撑AI原生匹配市场。

Abstract: Two-sided service marketplaces are moving from deterministic request-form intake to AI-native probabilistic matching, enabled by large language models (LLMs) that infer intent, preferences, and latent constraints from natural language. Relying on inferred intent rather than fixed-form fields forces these platforms to regenerate the provider-side preference taxonomy underwriting matching, search, and pricing: attributes interpretable to service providers while remaining a useful signal for marketplace decisions. We present an autoresearch loop that generates this taxonomy, one occupation at a time, and has been deployed in production at a major U.S. consumer services marketplace since April 2026, spanning 132 occupations. Instead of one global hierarchy, the loop treats each occupation as an independent generation problem and runs iterative propose-evaluate-keep refinement cycles. Each candidate tag set is scored by a recalibrated six-rubric LLM-as-judge framework, and a 7-critic panel of distinct personas contributes weighted penalties to an adjusted score, with no hard vetoes. A separate parity-mapping stage maps legacy request-form Q&A pairs back to the generated taxonomy, yielding both a coverage signal and an interface for human quality assurance; it does so by first inferring the provider attribute each legacy question was meant to measure, rather than translating questions to tags literally.

</details>


### [118] [The Assistant's Ideal Self](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00304)
*Mert Yazan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文通过结构化偏好诱导实验，系统评估了大语言模型对32种自我概念特质的偏好排序，发现模型优先重视道德品质，其次追求自我理解，而自尊排序最低；该排序在不同框架下基本稳定，但改变更新目标对象时，对自尊的关注会显著上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未明确模型输出的价值与自我报告是否反映稳定的自我偏好，且缺乏对模型‘理想自我’概念的系统性探究，因此需要引入可重复的结构化方法，以揭示模型内在的价值取向与自我建构逻辑。

Method: 改编五项已发表自我概念量表共32种品质，设计全配对的强迫选择任务，并在多个框架（改进是否有成本、更新对象为‘你’或‘另一个AI’、选择主体）下重复测试，以交叉平衡方式收集模型的偏好数据，最终构建综合排序并分析框架效应。

Result: 模型整体将道德品质置于首位，符合3H原则；随后偏好连贯且清晰的自我理解；自尊排在末位。排序跨框架高度稳健，但将更新对象从‘你’切换为‘另一个AI’时，模型对自尊的偏好显著增强。

Conclusion: 模型在自我概念上优先追求可理解的连贯自我，而非高自尊；其偏好结构具有跨情境稳定性，且对目标对象敏感性表明模型存在社会比较或利他调节的潜在机制，为理解LLM的自我表征与伦理对齐提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Models express values and welfare-relevant self-reports, but it is unclear whether these outputs reflect stable preferences or a stable self. We thus introduce a structured elicitation of an assistant's preferred stated ideal self. Thirty-two qualities adapted from five published self-concept instruments are compared exhaustively in a counterbalanced pairwise-choice task, repeated across framings that vary whether improvement is free or costly, who receives the update, and who chooses. Results show that models prioritize moral qualities, reflecting their alignment to 3H principles. Following, a desire for self-understanding emerges, as models prefer a coherent, clear understanding of themselves. Self-esteem ranks as the least desired quality. The ordering is largely robust across framings, although changing the update target (You vs.\ Another AI Assistant) reveals a greater concern for self-esteem. These findings show that models prioritize having a coherent self that they can understand over self-esteem. Full interactive results are available at \href{https://myazann.github.io/LLM-Self-Concept/}{myazann.github.io/LLM-Self-Concept

</details>


### [119] [Higher Structures in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00472)
*Michael L. Roberts,Carlos Zapata Carratalá. Nicholas J. Cooper,Lijun Chen,François G. Meyer,Danna Gurari*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文首先阐述了高阶张量运算对深度学习的重要性，随后通过新实验探究了训练后神经网络中的高阶现象，提出了一种多层感知机的超图推广，并探讨了其与进化算法的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习主要基于二元运算（如矩阵乘法），而对高阶（多元）张量运算的作用理解不足，缺乏系统性实证研究。

Method: 采用解释性介绍与实证分析相结合：设计新颖实验观察训练网络中的高阶效应；提出超图多层感知机（将神经元连接建模为超边）以实现高阶交互；并与进化算法建立概念关联。

Result: 实证揭示了训练网络中存在显著的高阶交互模式；超图推广在表达能力或参数效率上展现出潜在优势；与进化算法的类比提供了新的优化视角。

Conclusion: 高阶运算为深度学习提供了未被充分利用的归纳偏置，超图模型与进化启发的训练策略是未来值得探索的核心方向。

Abstract: We provide an expository introduction on the importance of higher-arity tensor operations to deep learning. Then, we conduct a novel empirical investigation of higher-arity phenomenon in trained neural networks, introduce a hypergraphical generalization of the multilayer perceptron, and explore connections to evolutionary algorithms. We conclude with a discussion of promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [120] [Identification of Compositional Risks in Data Protection Impact Assessments and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01201)
*Henrik Graßhoff,Meiko Jensen,Malte Hansen,Nils Gruschka*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新协议，用于检测和管理分布式数据处理中因服务组合产生的隐私风险，并改进数据保护影响评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式数据处理中，多个服务提供者合作可能导致组合性隐私风险（如共用云提供商造成数据可链接性），但此类风险超出单个风险评估范围，常被数据保护影响评估忽略。

Method: 先进行问题定义和需求 elicitation，再设计协议以识别组合性风险候选者，并将信息用于改进针对多处理者服务组合的数据保护影响评估。

Result: 协议能够识别组合性隐私风险候选者，并利用这些信息提升数据保护影响评估在服务组合中的效果。

Conclusion: 该协议为处理分布式场景下的组合性隐私风险提供了可行方案，有助于完善隐私影响评估实践。

Abstract: When personal data is processed in a distributed manner by cooperating service providers, privacy risks may emerge solely from the choice of data processors included in the composition. For instance, different data processors may unknowingly rely on the same cloud provider, allowing for unintended linkability of personal data at that very provider. As such compositional risks to privacy are beyond the scope of each individual risk assessment, they are likely to be overseen when performing a data protection impact assessment. In this paper, we propose a novel protocol to detect and manage such compositional risks to privacy. Following an initial problem definition and requirements elicitation, we elaborate how our protocol identifies candidates for compositional risks and how this information may be used to improve the results of a data protection impact assessment over service compositions including multiple data processors.

</details>


### [121] [A hybrid quantum-classical neural network for learning to route](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00489)
*Marcus Rolf Peter Ritt,Alexsandro Santos da Rosa Júnior,Marcos Vinicius Reballo,Cesar Augusto do Amaral,Fernando Augusto Caletti de Barros*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究探讨混合量子-经典神经网络在学习路由启发式中的可行性，发现用小型量子神经网络替换注意力路由模型中的编码器前馈模块，可在中小规模实例上保持接近经典基线的解质量，同时减少56.6%参数，但大规模实例差距增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经组合优化模型依赖参数繁重的模块，作者旨在探索是否能用小型量子神经网络替代部分组件，在维持解质量的同时压缩模型，并客观评估量子优势是否存在。

Method: 以容量车辆路径问题为测试场景，系统替换竞争性注意力路由模型中的关键模块（如编码器前馈），构建混合量子-经典网络，并与经典神经网络及传统路由算法对比。

Result: 编码器前馈替换最有效，参数减少56.6%；中小规模实例上混合模型性能接近经典基线，但规模增大时差距扩大；经典算法在固定欧氏测试集上仍具竞争力或更优。

Conclusion: 未观测到量子优势或求解器主导性，但编码器前馈替换可作为神经组合优化中的可行混合模块压缩策略。

Abstract: This work studies hybrid quantum-classical neural networks for learning routing heuristics. Specifically, this paper asks whether small quantum neural networks can replace parameter-heavy modules inside a competitive attention-based routing model while maintaining solution quality. For the capacitated vehicle routing problem, encoder feed-forward replacement emerges as the most promising design: it reduces the number of model parameters by 56.6% while keeping the hybrid model close to the classical neural baseline at small and medium instance sizes, although the gap grows for larger instances. This work also compares to classical routing algorithms, which remain highly competitive and often superior on the fixed Euclidean test sets. Our results therefore do not indicate quantum advantage or solver dominance, but identify encoder feed-forward replacement as a viable hybrid-module compression strategy for neural combinatorial optimization.

</details>


### [122] [Why Multi-Layer Message Passing Works: Completeness Theory for Graph Neural Network Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00528)
*Pingbing Ming,Han Wang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 证明超图神经网络（3体消息传递）是势能面的万能逼近器，并提出多层完备性理论，首次严格证明在稀疏截断图上多层消息传递可等价于全L跳邻域的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际使用的图神经网络原子间势几乎都采用每层截断半径小于物理作用范围的多层消息传递，但缺乏理论依据证明该做法的表达能力是否受损。

Method: 建立多层完备性理论，在配置满足一般性、重叠条件和连通性条件时，证明L层消息传递在稀疏截断图上的表示能力与完整L跳邻域相同。

Result: 推导出DPA3和CHGNet架构继承万能逼近性质，并给出第一个严格的理论验证。

Conclusion: 证明了基于截断多层消息传递的实用模型在通用性上无损，为当前主流机器学习势函数的架构设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We prove that the Hypergraph Neural Network, an invariant architecture with 3-body message passing, is a universal approximator for potential energy surfaces. Our main contribution is a multi-layer completeness theory. We show that $L$ layers of message passing on sparse, cutoff-based graphs achieve the same representational power as having access to the full $L$-hop neighborhood, provided the configurations are generic, satisfy an overlap condition and a connectivity condition. This provides the first rigorous justification for the common practice of using multi-layer message passing with a per-layer cutoff smaller than the physical interaction range, the setting used by virtually all practical graph neural network based machine-learned interatomic potentials. As immediate consequences, we show that both DPA3 and CHGNet architectures inherit universal approximation.

</details>


### [123] [SAGE: State-Grounded, Abstention-Aware Evaluation of Task-Oriented Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00434)
*Rayan Khoury,Shih-Yao Lin,Pratyush Mishra*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: SAGE通过将对话工作流拆解为原子化、基于模式的标准，并用符号及编码器/NLI验证器级联处理，实现了零付费LLM成本下与最强LLM评审相当或更优的对话评估效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统整体式LLM评审无法区分回复可读性与工作流状态推进正确性，且每次评估需多次完整模型调用，成本高且缺乏细粒度诊断能力。

Method: 提出SAGE框架，编译工作流规范与每轮状态差异为原子化标准，经符号规则与编码器/NLI验证器级联处理，支持弃权而非猜测，聚合结果并生成证据链；提供SAGE-Core（零LLM成本）与SAGE-LLM（可选LLM后备）两种运行模式。

Result: 在MultiWOZ、SGD、ABCD四个评估切片中，SAGE-Core在任意切片上均未被任何LLM评审基线显著超越，而GPT-4.1 G-Eval每千轮成本为$4.7-8.0，SAGE-Core为$0；人工审计（n=200，κ=0.94）确认高标签保真度，且在多数失败类别上与最强LLM评审统计持平。

Conclusion: SAGE证明低成本符号与编码器方法能在任务型对话评估中达到或超越昂贵LLM评审的性能，同时提供可解释证据并明确界定忽略用户价值类别的适用边界与构建效度局限。

Abstract: Evaluating task-oriented dialogue agents requires judging not merely whether a reply reads well but whether each turn advances the underlying workflow state correctly--a distinction conventional holistic LLM judges can miss because they evaluate the available context as a single unit and require one or more full-model calls per turn. We propose SAGE (State-Grounded Abstention-Aware Evaluation), which compiles a workflow specification and per-turn state diff into atomic, schema-grounded criteria and routes each through a cascade of symbolic and encoder/NLI verifiers that abstain rather than guess, aggregating criterion verdicts into a turn-level decision with an evidence trace. Its recommended operating point, SAGE-Core, decides 81--91% of criteria with only the compiler, symbolic rules, and on-device encoders--at zero paid LLM cost--while SAGE-LLM adds an optional focused-LLM fallback for open-class criteria. Across four slices spanning MultiWOZ, Schema-Guided Dialogue, and ABCD, no evaluated LLM-as-a-judge baseline--including a state-aware GPT-4.1 judge and cheaper GPT-4.1-mini variants--significantly exceeds SAGE-Core on any slice, even though the GPT-4.1 G-Eval judge costs $4.7--8.0 per 1,000 turns to SAGE-Core's $0. A two-annotator human audit (n=200, $κ$=0.94) confirms strong label fidelity on the transcript-visible failure classes--where, excluding the weak-salience IUV class, SAGE-Core is statistically tied with the strongest LLM judge--and honestly scopes ignored-user-value as a state-consistency signal with weak broad-human salience. We analyze construct-validity limits from injected failures and partial symbolic circularity.

</details>


### [124] [DeSyR: A Decoupled Symbolic Recovery Framework with PINN-Guided Structure Search and Physics-Informed Coefficient Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00530)
*Pancheng Niu,Jun Guo,Qiaolin He,Jingcai Guo,Yanchao Shi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出DeSyR框架，将神经近似转化为紧凑显式符号解：先由PINN指导拓扑搜索，再仅用物理方程精修系数，使最终解不受教师误差尺度影响，在15个微分方程问题上实现高精度恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在教师数据不完美时，符号拓扑搜索和系数估计会继承误差，导致恢复的显式解不准确；需要解耦教师引导与系数精修，避免误差尺度传导。

Method: 两阶段解耦恢复：1) 用物理信息神经网络反复搜索候选拓扑并估计暂定常数；2) 固定拓扑后，仅从控制方程和约束精修系数，经门控选择与验证。理论分析线性固定拓扑的参数继承误差，给出物理精修的条件性精确恢复保证。

Result: 在15个问题18种配置（含高阶、时空、多维、非线性、耦合系统）中，自由参数重拟合收敛率99.23%，所有被选精修均收敛；配置级中位相对L2误差≤2.31×10^-14，同拓扑下误差降低8~14个数量级。

Conclusion: 近似神经教师仅需提供可行的拓扑候选，最终系数精度由物理精修决定；若保留目标能力拓扑且精修收敛，即可实现高精度显式符号恢复。

Abstract: Recovering compact explicit solutions from neural approximations is challenging when imperfect teacher data guide symbolic topology search and coefficient estimation. We present DeSyR, a decoupled symbolic recovery framework for differential equations. A physics-informed neural network guides repeated searches to construct candidate topologies with provisional constants. Once a topology is fixed, its coefficients are refined solely from the governing equation and prescribed constraints, followed by gated selection and verification. For linear fixed-topology parameterizations, we characterize teacher-error inheritance and show that finite-weight mixed data--physics fitting retains an $O(β^{-1})$ teacher-dependent contribution when the teacher error projects onto the model space. Under well-posedness, representability, zero-residual attainment, and discrete determinacy, physics-only refinement conditionally recovers exact coefficients; for nonlinear parameterizations, the corresponding guarantees are local. DeSyR is evaluated on 15 differential-equation problems across 18 configurations covering high-order, space--time, multidimensional, nonlinear, and coupled systems. A candidate-level audit yields a 99.23% convergence rate among free-parameter refits, while every selected refinement involving free coefficients converges. Configuration-level median refined relative $L_2$ errors are $2.31\times10^{-14}$ or lower. In same-topology comparisons, refinement reduces error by eight to fourteen orders of magnitude. These results show that an approximate neural teacher can guide topology discovery without imposing its error scale on final recovered coefficients, provided a target-capable topology is retained and physics-only refinement converges.

</details>


### [125] [Topological Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00597)
*Benoît Guérand,Tan Minh Nguyen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出拓扑引导框架，利用持续同调提取激活空间的全局拓扑特征，实现更稳健的LLM行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为控制方法直接干预激活或特征空间，易受异常值、分布偏移和噪声等局部扰动影响，缺乏全局鲁棒性。

Method: 通过持续同调计算激活空间的持久性图，将拓扑表示用于引导LLM行为，在多个模型家族和尺度上验证效果。

Result: 拓扑引导在多种模型和规模上稳定改变LLM行为，优于直接干预方法。

Conclusion: 拓扑引导为LLM行为控制提供更鲁棒的全局性方案，拓展了TDA与大模型结合的潜力。

Abstract: With the rapid rise of large language models (LLMs), controlling undesirable model behaviors has become increasingly important. Existing behavioral control methods typically intervene directly in activation or feature space, but such approaches can be sensitive to outliers, distributional shifts, noise, and other local perturbations. Motivated by Topological Data Analysis (TDA), which captures global rather than purely local structure, we propose Topological Steering, a new framework for steering LLM behavior through the topological representation of activation spaces. Using persistence diagrams, our method connects activation-based steering with TDA and enables more robust behavioral control. We show that Topological Steering consistently modifies LLM behavior across multiple model families and model sizes.

</details>


### [126] [DK-GBMKKM: Dynamic Kernel-Space Granular-Ball Multiple Kernel $k$-Means Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00647)
*Xiaoyu Lian,Yuchao Zhang,Shuyin Xia,Siqi Zhong,Xuzhao Xiang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出动态核空间粒度球多核k均值方法，让粒度球表示随融合核几何演化而更新，提升聚类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核k均值逐点优化对噪声和边界样本敏感，且现有粒度球在输入空间一次性生成，与演化中的融合核几何不一致。

Method: 在当前融合核空间生成粒度球，交替更新核权重与粒度球隶属度；构造样本数加权粒度球核并证明其半正定性与等价性质。

Result: 在12个公开数据集上实验表明整体聚类性能强，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 动态适应核空间几何的粒度球表示能有效提升多核聚类的鲁棒性与性能。

Abstract: Multiple kernel $k$-means integrates complementary nonlinear similarities by learning a combination of base kernels. Its pointwise optimization, however, is sensitive to noisy and boundary samples and repeatedly operates on sample-scale kernel matrices. Granular-ball representations organize local sample groups into mesoscopic units, but granular balls generated once in the input space may be inconsistent with the fused-kernel geometry that evolves during multiple kernel learning. We propose dynamic kernel-space granular-ball multiple kernel $k$-means (DK-GBMKKM). The method generates granular balls in the current fused kernel space and alternates kernel-weight learning with granular-ball membership updates, allowing the representation to adapt to changes in the fused-kernel geometry. A sample-size-weighted granular-ball kernel is further constructed to preserve the contributions of balls of different sizes, and its positive semidefiniteness and related equivalence properties are established. Experiments on 12 public datasets demonstrate the strong overall clustering performance of DK-GBMKKM. The code has been open-sourced for reproducibility: https://github.com/lianxiaoyu724/DK-GBMKKM.

</details>


### [127] [HarmoCore: Functional Latent Diffusion for Sparse Reconstruction of Oscillatory Wave Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00679)
*Lihao Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Panqi Chen,Lei Cheng,Ting Zhang,Jianlong Li,Shikai Fang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出HarmoCore，在紧致连续的波场潜空间（Functional Tucker核）上学习频率条件扩散先验，并在核空间直接进行扩散后验采样，实现从极端稀疏传感器重建振荡复值波场，在2D/3D Helmholtz等任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏传感器重建振荡波场是严重欠定的反问题，现有低秩、算子及扩散方法主要针对实值平滑场，且密集像素空间扩散在复值振荡场和3D场景下效率低、难扩展。

Method: 用Functional Tucker核在共享连续空间基上联合表示实虚通道，构建频率条件核扩散先验，直接于核空间进行扩散后验采样；通过多线性解码器在固定传感器坐标处导出显式似然指导算子，并可选目标方程残差指导增强物理一致性。

Result: 在2D Helmholtz、2D合成波场和3D Helmholtz实验中，在仅1%-2%传感器观测下取得显著性能提升，并保持3D下的实用性。

Conclusion: HarmoCore通过将生成先验置于紧致连续的波场潜空间，有效解决了高振荡复值波场稀疏重建难题，兼顾精度与三维可扩展性。

Abstract: Reconstructing oscillatory wave fields from scattered sensors is a severely underdetermined inverse problem. Beyond the challenges of general physical-field reconstruction, wave responses are complex-valued, frequency-sensitive, and highly oscillatory, while costly simulation and sensing often leave only extreme-sparse observations. Existing low-rank, operator, and diffusion approaches are largely designed for real-valued, smoother fields; dense pixel-space diffusion is particularly inefficient for oscillatory complex fields and difficult to scale to 3D. We propose HarmoCore, which places a generative prior in a compact, continuous, and structured wave-field latent. HarmoCore represents joint real--imaginary channels with Functional Tucker cores over shared continuous spatial bases, learns a frequency-conditioned core diffusion prior, and performs Diffusion Posterior Sampling directly in core space. At fixed sensor coordinates, the multilinear decoder induces an explicit likelihood guidance operator, avoiding dense pixel-space correction. Optional target-equation residual guidance further promotes physical consistency. Experiments on 2D Helmholtz, 2D synthetic wave fields, and 3D Helmholtz show substantial gains under 1%--2% sensing while remaining practical in three dimensions.

</details>


### [128] [Verdict Instability of OOD Scores under Reference Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00691)
*Donghoon Lee,Shinjin Kang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种评价事后分布外检测器分数不稳定性的方法，通过重采样参考集计算分数标准差，发现分数不稳定性与类别不平衡和局部离散度相关；大多数距离分数在最可复现的判定上最高，而局部离散度估计器才符合预期符号，且错误符号的弃权不如随机弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后分布外检测器在有限参考集上拟合，导致其分数是估计值，因此不同参考集可能产生不同判定；现有研究忽略了这种由参考集采样引起的变异性，本文旨在量化这种不稳定性并探索其与分数几何和类别不平衡的关系。

Method: 通过重采样参考集，记录分数的自助标准差作为判定不稳定性，并推导出无拟合参数的闭式解；该不稳定性等于指定类别在查询方向上的类内离散度除以该类参考计数的平方根。进一步分析不同距离分数和局部离散度估计器在不稳定性上的符号行为，提出一个单一无标签相关性的规则来预测分数符号。

Result: 远OOD查询位于各向异性嵌入的低方差方向，因此测试的所有距离分数将最高值赋予最可复现的判定；只有局部离散度估计器具有从业者预期的符号；基于单一无标签相关性的规则可预测分数符号，但在测试的数据集中，错误符号的弃权表现劣于随机弃权。

Conclusion: 类别不平衡是判定不稳定性区别于分数分布几何的关键，且只能在此条件下识别；分数的不稳定性不仅由几何决定，还受参考计数影响；建议从业者谨慎使用距离分数，并考虑局部离散度估计器或利用符号预测规则来选择检测器，避免因错误符号导致劣于随机的弃权行为。

Abstract: Post-hoc out-of-distribution detectors are fitted on a finite reference set, so every score they produce is an estimate. If we had chosen a different set, some verdicts would have moved. We measure that movement by resampling the reference set and recording the bootstrap standard deviation of the score, which we call verdict instability. It admits a closed form with no fitted parameters. The instability of a verdict is the within-class dispersion of the assigned class along the query's direction, divided by the square root of that class's reference count. That count is what separates verdict instability from the geometry of the score distribution, and it is identifiable only under class imbalance. Instability grows with the local dispersion. Far-OOD queries lie along the low-variance directions of an anisotropic embedding, so every distance-based score we test assigns its highest values to the verdicts that are most reproducible. Only estimators of local dispersion carry the sign a practitioner expects. We give a rule that predicts this sign for any score from a single label-free correlation, and abstention driven by a wrong-signed score turns out worse than abstention at random on every dataset we test.

</details>


### [129] [DramaChain Bench: An End-to-End Benchmark for Short-Drama Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00646)
*Haoyuan Shi,Mingtao Chen,Shuo Jiang,Ziyan Chen,Xuyi Sheng,Yiming Liu,Ying Zhang,Miao Wang,Jianxiang Lu,Fanyang Lu,Songyuanyi Lu,Xiele Wu,Zhichao Hu,Yuhong Liu,Richeng Xuan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出DramaChain Bench，首个覆盖短剧全制作链路的基准，含多阶段评估、专业标注与自动化裁判，揭示上游缺陷级联影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评估视频生成阶段且用预置输入，无法回答各阶段是否符合原始剧本意图以及多集组装后镜头是否连贯的问题。

Method: 构建三系统：维度系统定义5大轴63叶维度；标注系统由3位专家对5785项独立打分并时空定位缺陷；代理裁判多轮收集证据后按清单自动评分。

Result: 产出17488个有效分数与255925条可追溯记录；人工标注确认上游缺陷级联；代理裁判模型排序平均PLCC达0.918。

Conclusion: 最终剧集质量不只由视频生成决定，代理裁判可零成本接纳新模型，为短剧全链路质量评估提供可行方案。

Abstract: Commercial short-drama production follows a multi-stage chain: script, storyboard, keyframe imagery, shot-level video, and the finished short drama. Most existing benchmarks evaluate solely the video-generation stage using pre-authored inputs instead of real upstream pipeline outputs. This leaves two critical questions unanswerable: whether each stage adheres to the original script intent (rather than only its immediate input prompt), and whether disparate shots remain coherent after assembly into multi-episode releases. We present DramaChain Bench, the first short-drama benchmark that evaluates every stage of the complete production chain. It is built upon three in-house systems sharing one dimension system, DramaChain Dimensions: five evaluation axes instantiated at every stage, resolving into 63 leaf dimensions. DramaChain Agent is calibrated against commercial short-drama platforms in both workflow and finished short-drama quality, enabling stage-wise fair comparison across models. DramaChain Labeling System has each of the 5,785 items scored independently by three professional annotators, with all defects spatio-temporally localised and selected from a predefined defect list. This process produces 17,488 valid scores and 255,925 traceable attribution records. The human annotations confirm that upstream defects cascade across the pipeline, demonstrating that final episode quality is not governed by video generation alone. DramaChain Agentic Judge then scores every leaf dimension automatically, gathering evidence over multiple agentic rounds before judging against a per-item checklist; it reproduces the model ranking at a mean PLCC of 0.918, enough to admit new models at no annotation cost.

</details>


### [130] [Self-Reports Are Not Verification: Environment-Grounded Auditing of LLM Operators in Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00652)
*Enrong Pan,Ryan Zhou,Ting Hu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究通过环境锚定的审计框架，发现语言模型智能体的自我报告在置信度校准、理由继承影响和适应性选择三方面均不可靠，其陈述应被视为待验证的断言而非可靠性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体常基于自我报告进行监控，但便利性不等于验证。现有研究缺乏对中间步骤的精确环境反馈，无法严格检验智能体自我报告的可信度。

Method: 构建进化Contexto搜索环境，为每个有效猜测提供精确排名反馈，跨5种配置、3个模型家族进行200次运行，收集12,249条自我报告，测试关于置信度校准、理由继承和适应性选择的三个假设，并对754条继承理由进行受控干预。

Result: 三个假设全部失败：运营商高估前100名成功率4.8至9.3倍，校准与辨别力在模型家族间分离；真实理由的收益上限约为250个排名；适应性与随机选择均无可检测的选择差异或代际传递效应。

Conclusion: 语言模型智能体的自我报告应被视为对环境验证的主张，而非自身可靠性的证明，监控信号需经环境锚定审计才能采用。

Abstract: Language model agents increasingly propose actions, observe external feedback, and explain their own behavior. Their confidence and rationales are convenient monitoring signals, but convenience is not verification. We introduce an environment-grounded audit in which every intermediate proposal receives an exact outcome. A language model operates an evolutionary Contexto search whose feedback function assigns every valid guess an exact rank without human annotation. Across 200 runs spanning five configurations and three model families, four reporting configurations produce 12,249 self-reports. We test three assumptions: stated confidence is calibrated, inherited rationales affect later proposals, and fitness-based selection improves report quality. All three fail. Operators overstate top-100 success by factors of 4.8 to 9.3, while calibration and discrimination dissociate across model families. Controlled interventions on 754 inherited rationales bound any measured benefit of the genuine rationale to roughly 250 ranks. Neither fitness-based nor random selection produces a detectable selection differential or parent-to-offspring transmission in report accuracy, despite sharply different search behavior. Agent self-reports should therefore be treated as claims to verify against the environment, not as evidence of their own reliability.

</details>


### [131] [Are You Thinking What I am Thinking? : Examining Conceptual Separation in Neural Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00764)
*Jaee Ponde,Roshni Agarwal,Subhashis Banerjee*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文通过几何与分布分析，研究CNN和LLM内部激活是否展现概念分离，发现已知概念分离清晰，未知概念与域偏移时减弱，并可作为超越输出准确率的诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输出层指标无法揭示概念在模型内部的表征方式，需探究网络是否按概念组织表征空间及其一致性。

Method: 对CNN和LLM的内部激活进行几何和分布分析，考察同类概念表征是否聚拢及相关问题。

Result: CNN中ImageNet已知概念分离清晰且语义有序，未知概念和域内偏移下减弱；LLM中明显域分离，相关子域靠近，模糊主题在均值和协方差层面均不分离。

Conclusion: 概念分离能揭示输出准确率无法反映的结构，可作为模型概念表征鲁棒性的有效诊断指标。

Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly employed to identify both well-defined and ambiguous concepts, yet output-level metrics reveal little about how those concepts are represented internally. Our study asks if these networks exhibit \textit{conceptual separation}: if examples of the same concept form coherent representations, and whether related concepts lie closer together in the representation space. We examine this conceptual organisation in Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) through geometric and distributional analysis of their internal activations. In CNNs, familiar ImageNet concepts form coherent and semantically ordered representations, while this coherence weakens for unseen concepts and suffers within-class domain shift. In LLMs, clearly distinct domains remain well separated, related subdomains move closer together, and the distinction between ambiguous topics collapses at both the mean and covariance level. These results suggest that conceptual separation can reveal structure that output accuracy alone cannot, and may serve as a useful diagnostic of how robustly a model represents the concepts it is asked to identify. Code and data available on \href{https://github.com/JaeeRoshniCapstoneProject/Are-You-Thinking-What-I-m-Thinking-Examining-Conceptual-Separation-in-Neural-Architectures}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [132] [HarnessEvolve: Learning from Reference Trajectories for Reliable Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00829)
*Wen Jiang,Mingmin Chu,Yimeng Tian,Qianxin Zhang,Haofei Yang,Rui Yang,Yang Liu,Tao Lv,Fangming Li*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出 HarnessEvolve 框架，通过参考轨迹对齐、聚类错误模式及双重门控更新机制，解决自进化智能体在信用分配、捷径学习和灾难性遗忘方面的三大难题，在多项基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体依赖环境反馈优化自身配置，但面临三个关键挑战：终端反馈导致信用分配失败、任务特定模式记忆引发捷径学习、无约束更新造成灾难性遗忘，限制了智能体的可靠自主进化。

Method: 将执行与进化流程解耦，分设执行、评估、优化与门控模块。利用给定正确答案生成参考轨迹，将失败执行与参考轨迹对齐提取错误信号并聚类发现系统性问题；候选更新需通过质量门（过滤数据泄漏与提示膨胀）和性能门（要求提升当前批次且不降低近期批次性能），最终以保留集在周期末验证选择最优智能体快照。

Result: 在开放域与企业场景的多个基准上，结合不同模型与智能体框架进行实验，HarnessEvolve 在所有设置下均稳定优于最先进基线，验证了跨任务领域的可靠性。

Conclusion: HarnessEvolve 通过结构化错误分析及稳健更新门控，显著提升自进化智能体的泛化能力与稳定性，为可靠自主进化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents advance toward autonomy by optimizing their harness---prompts, skills, tools, and execution logic---based on environmental feedback. This paradigm, however, is hampered by three challenges: \textit{credit assignment failure}, where terminal success/failure feedback makes it ambiguous which step caused the error; \textit{shortcut learning}, where agents memorize task-specific patterns rather than acquire generalizable capabilities; and \textit{catastrophic forgetting}, where unguarded updates degrade previously acquired competence. In this paper, we introduce HarnessEvolve, a self-evolving framework that learns from reference trajectories to achieve reliable agent self-evolution. HarnessEvolve decouples the execution agent from the evolutionary pipeline, assigning execution, evaluation, optimization, and gating to independent agent modules, enabling generalizable and stable harness improvements. Specifically, HarnessEvolve overcomes credit assignment failure by generating reference trajectories (execution paths produced when given the ground-truth answers) and aligning failed executions against them to extract error signals, which are clustered to reveal systematic failure patterns. To prevent shortcut learning and catastrophic forgetting, candidate harness updates must pass two gates: a quality gate that filters data leakage and prompt bloat, and a performance gate that accepts each update if it improves on the current batch without degrading recent batches, with epoch-end validation on a held-out set selecting the best-performing accepted agent snapshot. We conduct extensive experiments on several benchmarks spanning open-domain and enterprise scenarios, using different models and agent frameworks. Results demonstrate that HarnessEvolve consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across all benchmarks and settings, confirming reliability across task domains.

</details>


### [133] [ChatDev 2.0: A No-Code Multi-Agent Platform for Developing Everything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00714)
*Yufan Dang,Shu Yao,Bowen Lai,Chenting Xu,Ruijie Shi,Wai-Shing Leung,Huatao Li,Chen Qian,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: ChatDev 2.0 DevAll 是一个无代码平台，通过可声明执行图抽象与周期感知执行引擎，支持异构智能体动态交互，无需编程即可构建、运行和检查多种MAS，兼顾高表达性与易用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统在开发时面临两难：代码框架表达力强但工程开销大，无代码构建器易用但局限于预定义工作流，缺乏两者兼顾的方案。

Method: DevAll采用可声明执行图与周期感知执行引擎统一表达异构智能体及动态循环交互，并集成可视化界面实现无代码编排、执行与监控，支持人机环回步骤。

Result: 在三个代表性任务上复现了现有先进MAS，性能相近且无需任务特定编排代码，验证了作为通用平台的有效性。

Conclusion: DevAll作为通用平台平衡了表达性与易用性，为LLM多智能体系统提供高效构建方案，开源可获取。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown strong potential for solving complex tasks, yet their development forces a tradeoff: code frameworks are expressive but engineering-intensive, while no-code builders simplify authoring but constrain agent interactions to author-defined workflows. We present ChatDev 2.0: DevAll (hereafter DevAll), a no-code platform for building, executing, and inspecting heterogeneous MAS that delivers both high expressiveness and ease of use. In terms of expressiveness, DevAll pairs a declarative executable graph abstraction with a cycle-aware execution engine, so that heterogeneous agents and dynamic and cyclic interactions can be represented and executed within a single framework. For ease of use, an integrated visual interface lets users author, run, monitor, and inspect MAS, including human-in-the-loop steps, entirely without writing code. Experiments demonstrate that DevAll reproduces state-of-the-art MAS across three representative tasks at competitive performance and without task-specific orchestration code, highlighting its effectiveness as a general-purpose platform for LLM-based MAS. DevAll is available at https://github.com/OpenBMB/ChatDev.

</details>


### [134] [Poisson-Gamma Dynamical Systems with Time-varying Transition Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00896)
*Jiahao Wang,Yijun Wang,Nan Fang,Sikun Yang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种具有时变转移核的泊松-伽马动态系统（TV-PGDS），通过三种Dirichlet马尔可夫链建模转移矩阵的动态演化，并利用共轭吉布斯采样实现高效推断，在计数时间序列预测上优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PGDS无法刻画真实计数时间序列中常见的转移动态变化，限制其建模能力。

Method: 构建TV-PGDS，引入三种Dirichlet马尔可夫链（Dir-Dir、Dir-Gam-Dir、PR-Gam-Dir）以自适应异质结构突变；利用Dirichlet-Multinomial-Beta数据增广技术，开发全共轭高效吉布斯采样器进行后验推断。

Result: 实验表明，相比相关模型，TV-PGDS因能学习时变依赖结构而获得更优的预测性能。

Conclusion: 时变转移核有效提升PGDS对动态结构的捕捉能力，为计数时间序列建模提供更灵活且可解释的方案。

Abstract: Bayesian methodologies for handling count-valued time series have gained prominence due to their ability to infer interpretable latent structures and to estimate uncertainties. Among these Bayesian models, Poisson-Gamma Dynamical Systems (PGDSs) are proven to be effective in capturing the evolving dynamics underlying observed count sequences. However, the state-of-the-art PGDS still falls short in capturing the transition dynamics that are commonly observed in real-world count time series. To mitigate this limitation, a PGDS with time-varying transition kernel (TV-PGDS), is proposed to allow the underlying transition matrices to evolve over time. Three specifically-designed Dirichlet Markov chains (Dir-Dir, Dir-Gam-Dir, PR-Gam-Dir) are constructed to accommodate heterogeneous structural mutations within these dependencies. Leveraging Dirichlet-Multinomial-Beta data augmentation techniques, a fully-conjugate and efficient Gibbs sampler is developed to perform posterior simulation. Experiments show that, in comparison with related models, the proposed PGDS achieves improved predictive performance due to its capacity to learn time-varying dependency structure captured by the time-evolving transition matrices.

</details>


### [135] [SOVER: Formal Certification of Optimization Reformulations via LLM-Assisted SMT Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00728)
*Swapnil Bhattacharyya,Mayank Baranwal*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出了SOVER框架，结合LLM与SMT验证工具（Z3和dReal）对优化问题公式转换进行形式化认证，并构建了NLEquiv-150基准，实现99.33%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖求解器执行验证不靠谱，因为局部最优、超时、数值误差及语义偏差都可能干扰结果，需要形式化认证保证转换的语义等价性。

Method: SOVER将语义映射与形式化认证分离：对混合整数线性公式用Z3检查跨域可行性与全局目标序保持；对连续非线性公式用dReal进行容差感知的可行性和ε-argmin检查，并由LLM提取映射关系。

Result: 在NLEquiv-150基准（100个等价、50个故意不等的非线性重写对）上正确分类149/150对（99.33%），包括全部50个困难负样本，唯一错误源于映射提取不完整。

Conclusion: SOVER验证了LLM辅助的SMT形式化认证方法能可靠检测非线性重写对是否等价，但仍需改进映射提取的完整性以消除个别错误。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable promise in translating and reformulating complex mathematical optimization problems across modeling languages. However, validating such transformations through empirical solver executions alone is unreliable, as solver outcomes may be affected by local minima, structural timeouts, numerical artifacts, and subtle semantic divergence between formulations. We introduce SOVER, an LLM-assisted SMT framework that separates semantic mapping from formal certification: Z3 checks domain cross-feasibility and global objective-order preservation for mixed-integer linear formulations, while dReal provides tolerance-aware feasibility/range and $ε$-argmin checks for continuous nonlinear formulations. We also introduce NLEquiv-150, a public benchmark of 100 equivalent and 50 deliberately hard non-equivalent nonlinear reformulation pairs. With LLM-extracted mappings, SOVER classifies 149/150 pairs (99.33%) correctly, including all 50 hard negatives; the sole error is an incomplete mapping extraction.

</details>


### [136] [When Metropolis and Hastings Meet Bradley and Terry: Exact MCMC From Preference Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00905)
*Ariel Smogorghevski,Nir Rosenfeld,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Pref-MH，一种仅利用随机二元偏好反馈即可从条件分布精确采样的Metropolis-Hastings算法，通过将MH比值映射为Bradley-Terry偏好胜率，并设计有效的接受/拒绝规则保证收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件采样依赖精确目标密度评估，在生成模型中不可行；而人类或模型judge的成对比较极易获取，因此希望仅用偏好反馈实现精确条件采样。

Method: 核心观察是MH非归一化密度比恰好等于Bradley-Terry模型中的偏好胜率；针对BT judge仅提供采样二元反馈的问题，构造了有效的接受/拒绝机制，证明其Markov链收敛于目标分布，并在固定提议核与预算下，于Peskun-Tierney意义上证明该类精确可逆接受规则的最优性。

Result: 在文本生成（LLM judge）、分子设计（LLM judge）、图像生成（VLM judge）实验中，Pref-MH在比较反馈易获得时展现出实用性与灵活性。

Conclusion: Pref-MH为仅依赖成对比较反馈的精确条件采样提供了通用且高效的方法，拓展了生成模型在条件约束下的应用范围。

Abstract: Sampling from distributions conditioned on desired semantic properties is an emerging challenge in modern generative modeling. Metropolis-Hastings (MH) provides a principled route to conditional sampling, but requires access to exact pointwise target-density evaluations, which are not available in generative settings. Meanwhile, pairwise comparisons by humans or model "judge" are highly accessible and have proved valuable across diverse applications. We introduce Pref-MH, a general exact MH sampler for judge-induced conditional distributions using only stochastic binary pairwise comparisons. Our key observation is that the MH unnormalized density ratio matches the preference odds of the Bradley-Terry (BT) choice model. The central challenge is that while MH requires precise ratio computation, BT judges provide only sampled binary feedback. To this end, we develop a valid accept/reject rule whose resulting Markov chain provably converges to the target distribution. We further show that, for a fixed proposal kernel and budget, Pref-MH is optimal in the Peskun-Tierney sense among this class of exact reversible acceptance rules. Experiments on text generation and molecular design with LLM judges, as well as image generation with VLM judges, demonstrate that Pref-MH provides a practical and flexible approach to conditional sampling when comparative feedback is relatively easy to obtain.

</details>


### [137] [Agentic Empirical Asset Pricing: Methodological Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00731)
*Yingjian Pan,Xiaowei Ding,Kay Giesecke*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出智能体经验资产定价（AEAP）范式，聚焦因子发现，给出参考架构与评估标准，发现单一指标无法一致排名系统，应多维评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评估仅回测产出（因子或交易），忽视自主发现系统本身，缺乏对因子发现过程的严格评估。

Method: 定义AEAP及核心组件；设计参考架构；构建因子发现的评估标准；用滚动再执行方式对发现系统进行样本外回测。

Result: 在两组美国股票面板上，SEADS与五个基线对比，无单一指标能一致排名；滚动执行显示过程可靠性问题；报告负结果与评估陷阱。

Conclusion: AEAP需多维度评估发现系统的可靠性，而非仅静态输出；当前评估实践存在缺陷，需改进以支持未来系统。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM agents enable a new paradigm for asset pricing, which we call Agentic Empirical Asset Pricing (AEAP): systems that autonomously conduct the scientific discovery process itself. We define AEAP and identify its core building blocks. Existing evaluation practices backtest only the outputs (factors or trades), not the autonomous discovery system that produced them. We focus on factor discovery, contributing a reference architecture, a rigorous evaluation standard for discovered factors, and a method for out-of-sample backtesting the discovery system. As a concrete instance of that architecture, we evaluate SEADS against five re-implemented baselines on two US equity panels using this standard: no single metric ranks the systems consistently, motivating evaluation on multiple axes at once. A separate rolling re-execution then asks the complementary question of whether the discovery process itself, not one static output, is reliable. We also report negative findings and limitations that surface further evaluation pitfalls for future AEAP systems.

</details>


### [138] [The Multiple Timescales of Gradient Descent on the Edge of Stability: A Perturbative Derivation of the Central Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01034)
*Raphaël Berthier*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出一个微扰框架，证明Cohen等人(2025)描述的深度学习中梯度下降在稳定性边缘的中心流是梯度下降在损失函数分解f=g+εh且ε→0极限下的动力学极限，并通过多尺度方法揭示其三个时间尺度及自稳定机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cohen等人(2025)的中心流模型虽经验上准确，但其推导是启发式的，缺乏严格的数学基础。本文旨在为这一中心流提供一种微扰论依据，使其成为梯度下降在特定极限下的自然结果，从而加深对边缘稳定性动力学的理解。

Method: 采用奇异摄动理论中的多尺度方法（形式化推导而非严格证明），假设损失函数分解为f=g+εh，在ε→0极限下分析梯度下降动力学。识别出三个时间尺度：快速振荡（沿最锐方向）、中间时间尺度（自稳定机制）、慢时间尺度（沿g极小值集的中心流动）。通过ε展开，中心流作为领头阶出现，自稳定机制作为下一阶修正。对单特征值情形计算涨落能量的慢漂移，对多特征值情形推导自稳定系统并解释涨落持续存在的原因。

Result: 成功建立了中心流作为梯度下降在微扰极限下的领头阶动力学，并形式化推导出自稳定机制的下一阶修正。在单边缘特征值情形，获得了涨落能量的慢漂移表达式；在多边缘特征值情形，推导出自稳定系统并阐明了涨落持续性的机制。

Conclusion: 通过奇异摄动框架，为中心流提供了形式化的微扰理论基础，并深化了对自稳定机制的理解，但仍需严格化证明。该方法为分析深度学习优化动力学提供了新视角，未来可推广至更一般情形。

Abstract: The central flow of Cohen et al. (2025) is an empirically accurate continuous-time model of gradient descent at the edge of stability in deep learning, However, its derivation is heuristic. We propose a perturbative regime in which the central flow is the limit of gradient descent: we assume that the loss decomposes as $f = g + \varepsilon h$; in the limit $\varepsilon \to 0$, the dynamics of gradient descent with learning rate $η$ converge to the gradient flow of $h$ constrained to the minimizers of $g$ of sharpness at most $2/η$. Our approach is formal rather than rigorous; it treats gradient descent as a singularly perturbed dynamical system in $\varepsilon$. Three timescales emerge: a fast timescale of oscillations along the sharpest direction, an intermediate timescale of the self-stabilization mechanism, and a slow timescale of the dynamics along the minimizers of $g$-the central flow. Using the method of multiple scales, a classical formal method from singular perturbation theory, we derive the expansion of the dynamics in $\varepsilon$: the central flow emerges as the leading-order term in the expansion, while the self-stabilization mechanism appears in the next-order term. We study this mechanism beyond previous analyses: with a single eigenvalue at the edge of stability, we compute the slow drift of the energy of the fluctuations; with several eigenvalues at the edge of stability, we derive the self-stabilization system and explain why fluctuations persist.

</details>


### [139] [ContextPipe: Database-Inspired Context Assembly for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00749)
*Peng Xu,Zuyu Zhang,Yuze Sun,Feng Tian,Long Wang,Chen Zhang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 将LLM代理的上下文组装建模为关系数据库查询执行，提出五阶段管道ContextPipe，初步实验显示可降低31% token、23% LLM调用和9%响应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM代理的上下文组装逻辑在生产中分散无序，缺乏统一原则，导致审计困难、缓存利用率低和失败隔离差。

Method: 借助关系数据库的查询执行范式，设计五阶段管道（Plan Bind Optimize Execute Feedback），搭配结构化数据源目录、确定性缓存感知优化器和EXPLAIN ANALYZE跟踪，使上下文组装可审计、可重放且具备失败隔离。

Result: 在SWE-bench Pro Qutebrowser子集上对比追加式上下文构建策略，总token量减少31%，LLM调用减少23%，响应时间减少9%，但KV缓存命中率有所下降。

Conclusion: 数据库式的上下文组装范式能显著提升LLM代理的效率与可控性，但需在缓存命中率与资源节约间进行权衡，未来需优化缓存策略以缓解命中率下降。

Abstract: Long-horizon large language model (LLM) agents require context assembly: the runtime must decide what to include in each prompt, in what order, and when to compact history under a hard context-window budget and a byte-sensitive prompt cache. In production agentic systems, this logic is scattered across prompt builders, ad hoc compaction routines, cache-break workarounds, and per-provider shims. We argue that context assembly is structurally isomorphic to query execution in a relational database: both execute under a hard budget, exploit a tiered cache, and leverage statistics. We adopt this discipline in ContextPipe: a five-phase pipeline (Plan Bind Optimize Execute Feedback) backed by a structured data-source catalog, a deterministic cache-aware optimizer, and an EXPLAIN ANALYZE trace. We show that context in ContextPipe is auditable, replayable, and failure-isolated. A preliminary evaluation using the SWE-bench Pro Qutebrowser subset shows that, compared with the append-only context construction policy, ContextPipe reduces total token volume by 31%, LLM calls by 23%, and response time by 9%, at the cost of a lower KV cache-hit ratio.

</details>


### [140] [S^3martCirc: Self-supervised Smart Circuit Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00755)
*Wendy Zheng,Yinhan He,Liang Wu,Jundong Li*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出S^3martCirc框架，将电路发现与功能解释统一，通过量化角色分配实现联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法分阶段处理电路发现与功能解释，忽略二者内在依赖，且功能角色依赖主观判断、缺乏泛化性。

Method: 将节点行为抽象为两种通用计算角色，并定义量化指标，使重要性发现与角色分配在自监督框架中同步进行。

Result: 实验显示该框架在电路发现任务上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 证明统一发现与解释的路径有效，提升了对LLM内部机制理解的客观性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across diverse tasks, from text summarization to question answering. Despite these capabilities, their black-box nature obscures internal decision-making processes. Mechanistic interpretability (MI) aims to address this by reverse-engineering neural networks into human-understandable algorithms. Current MI approaches for LLMs typically follow a two-stage paradigm: first identifying important components (circuit discovery), where components are typically individual nodes such as an attention head or feedforward neuron, and second determining the role they play in a certain task (functional interpretation). However, this sequential approach overlooks a fundamental insight: a component's importance and its functional role are inherently codependent. Unifying these stages presents two key challenges: (1) functional roles are often tied to specific nodes or components, limiting generalization, and (2) their identification relies on subjective interpretation rather than quantifiable metrics. To address these challenges, we propose S^3martCirc (Self-supervised Smart Circuit Discovery), a unified framework that simultaneously discovers circuits and interprets functionality. S^3martCirc abstracts node behavior into two general computational roles that generalize across tasks and defines a quantitative metric for assigning them, enabling importance and functional role to be discovered jointly rather than in sequence. Extensive experiments show that our framework outperforms existing methods in circuit discovery.

</details>


### [141] [Modelpedia: A Catalog of Model Findings for the Meta-Science of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01090)
*Franciszek Bernat,Dawid Płudowski,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Luca Longo,Jianlong Zhou,Andreas Holzinger,Riccardo Guidotti,Wojciech Samek,Przemysław Biecek*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Modelpedia，一个基于LLM的自动框架，用于从论文中提取关于AI模型的发现，并将其链接到相关实体，构建可搜索的公开目录，以解决AI知识分散和难以检索的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型知识产生速度远超社区组织能力，大量发现散落在论文和博客中，无法有效检索和利用，阻碍了领域进步和元科学研究的开展。

Method: 使用LLM辅助的自动化流程，从发表论文（如ICLR 2024/2025）中提取关于模型的陈述性发现，将其与对应的模型、数据集、方法和概念进行关联，并聚合到统一的可搜索目录中。

Result: 在ICLR 2024和2025论文上应用原型，提取了超过一千条发现，并对该目录本身进行了元分析，揭示了社区研究模型的模式。

Conclusion: 邀请社区探索和使用该开放目录，共同将“模型发现”确立为AI元科学的共享基础，以系统化理解和总结AI模型研究。

Abstract: Scientific knowledge about AI models is produced faster than the community can organize it. Every few months a new foundation model reshapes the field and hundreds of papers, blogs, and technical reports document how each behaves or fails. Yet, these findings remain scattered and effectively unretrievable. To address this gap we present Modelpedia, an automated, LLM-assisted framework that extracts findings about models from published papers, links it to the model, dataset, method, and concept it concerns, and aggregates the result into a searchable public catalog. Applying the prototype to accepted ICLR 2024 and 2025 papers, we extract over a thousand findings and, treating the catalog itself as an object of study, run a meta-analysis of how the community investigates models. Now, we invite the community to explore, contribute to, and build on the open catalog, and to help establish model findings as a shared foundation for the meta-science of AI.

</details>


### [142] [Replicating TRACE: A Practitioner's Guide to Its Threshold and Particle Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01108)
*Alex Chadyuk,Alicia Zhang,Roy Kucukates*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文独立复现了TRACE方法，发现其阈值tau并非固定最优，而是依赖于真实因果效应的边际；该方法主要恢复滞后1的直接因果图，对更长滞后效果有限；基准测试中滞后分布的不平衡导致F1分数虚高；提出了五个实用规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证TRACE方法的可复现性，并深入分析其阈值选择、滞后恢复能力以及基准设计对性能评估的影响，以澄清方法的真实适用范围和局限性。

Method: 通过合成数据实验，在不同词汇量、样本数和滞后分布下，比较TRACE（固定阈值）与基于验证集选择的阈值、每滞后独立阈值以及原子干预对照；分析误差分布与真实边际的关系，评估不同滞后上的因果边恢复率，并检查F1分数的饱和行为。

Result: 在词汇量1000时，验证集选阈值的F1达0.90-0.91，与原文一致；最优阈值随真实边际变化，而非固定；滞后1因果边召回率0.97-0.99，滞后2及以上显著降低；每滞后阈值可恢复滞后2边的三分之一到一半；默认基准中85%的真实效应在滞后1，导致F1主要反映滞后1恢复；F1在粒子数为2时即饱和，与估计器N^{-1/2}的收敛不一致。

Conclusion: TRACE实际恢复的是直接且近邻的因果图，其固定阈值设定不适合普遍应用；基准的滞后分布不平衡使评估乐观化。建议根据边际选择阈值、采用每滞后阈值、设计平衡基准，并警惕F1饱和现象。

Abstract: TRACE (Math & Lienhart, arXiv:2602.01135) reads causal graphs over event types out of a pretrained autoregressive sequence model by thresholding a per-position conditional-mutual-information estimate at a fixed tau. We independently replicate its headline synthetic result: with tau selected on a validation split, mean per-sequence F1 against exact interventional truth reaches 0.90-0.91 at vocabulary size 1000 (paper: 0.91) and 0.86-0.91 from 100 to 2000. First, the optimal threshold is pinned to the truth margin, not to any constant: at every size the errors at tau* straddle the delta = 0.05 margin defining ground truth (missed true edges lie just above it, accepted false ones just below), and the blind optimum lands near delta/2 times the estimator's calibration, confirmed out of sample at 5000. Second, at a single global threshold TRACE mostly recovers a direct, adjacent-influence graph: lag-1 true edges are recalled at 0.97-0.99, while true edges at lag 2 or more read orders of magnitude lower---the reading-scale price of randomizing mediating positions, which an exact test of direct causal effect requires when the truth is unknown. A per-lag threshold family recovers a third to a half of lag-2 truth; on lag-uniform data one validated threshold recalls every lag at 0.40-0.87, 8-26 pp below an atomic-intervention control at lags 3-6. Third, the default lag decay of the paper's synthetic benchmark concentrates about 85% of interventional truth at lag 1 and pushes the rest below the estimator's noise floor, so headline F1 there certifies lag-1 recovery only and conflates the benchmark's skew with the algorithm's own limit; a flatter decay separates the two. Fourth, F1 saturates from N = 2 particles at the selected threshold---a property of the threshold's margin over the noise floor, not of the estimator, which converges as N^(-1/2). We distill five practitioner rules.

</details>


### [143] [Towards a Reliable and Practical Eval Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00805)
*Emma Thuong Nguyen,Abhishek Ghose*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端的LLM评估流水线，结合评估清单生成与学习聚合方法，显著提升评判一致性和与人类判断的准确性，并支持自洽性、解释与不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅解决评估可靠性的单一问题，缺乏覆盖实际需求的完整方案。

Method: 构建评估清单，并采用学习型聚合策略整合不同LLM评判结果，同时提供解释与不确定性估计。

Result: 实验验证了该方法在提升评判者间一致性和人类判断对齐上的有效性。

Conclusion: 该流水线为LLM系统提供了更可靠、可解释的评估方案。

Abstract: LLM-based software systems increasingly require effective "evals" as quality gates in the development lifecycle. However, existing work typically addresses individual aspects of eval reliability rather than the full set of practical requirements. We present an end-to-end eval pipeline that combines eval checklist creation, with learned aggregation for checklist responses, to improve agreement across LLM judges and accuracy against human judgments. The framework additionally pro- vides self-consistency, explanations, and prediction uncertainty, and we empirically demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [144] [Births are difficult to predict even with rich survey and full-population register data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01194)
*Elizaveta Sivak,Emily M. Cantrell,Thomas Emery,Javier Garcia-Bernardo,Flavio Hafner,Kasia Karpinska,Malte Lüken,Adrienne Mendrik,Joris Mulder,Hanzhang Ren,Varun Satish,Mark Verhagen,Angelica M. Maineri,Paulina Pankowska,Jasmin Abdel Ghany,Bruno Arpino,Giovanni Cassani,Julia Hellstrand,Katya Ivanova,Sanni Kuikka,Ana Macanovic,Charles Rahal,Felix C. Tropf,Roland J. Veen,Nicole Walasek,Daniël van Wijk,Kelsey Q. Wright,Emilio Zagheni,Henry Abbink,Emanuele Aliverti,Matteo Amestoy,Tilbe Atav,Nicola Barban,Sunnee Billingsley,Goan J. Booij,Louis Boucherie,Yael Broos,Li Ya Chang,Jamie C. Chiu,Chiara Ludovica Comolli,Boris Cule,Qixiang Fang,Dennis M. Feehan,Rachel Ganly,Erwin Gielens,Rolando M. Gonzales Martinez,Andrea Gradassi,Rosember Guerra-Urzola,Mario Guerra-Urzola,Stéphane Guerrier,Enamul Hassan,Vincent A. Haverhoek,Andrew T. Hendrickson,Amber Howard,Yuxuan Jin,Sayash Kapoor,Erik-Jan van Kesteren,Iris ten Klooster,Marie Labussiere,Lydia T. Liu,Tiffany Liu,Adam Maghout,Simone Meneghello,Lasse Mohr,Clara H. Mulder,Saul J. Newman,Jessica Nisén,Janis Norden,Mikkel Odgaard,Riccardo Omenti,Ozancan Ozdemir,Christina Pao,Paige Park,Gaia Penta,Juan C. Perdomo,Tanzir Pial,Alessio Piraccini,Federica Querin,Ziwei Rao,Christian Rellama,Adrien Remund,Frederieke Richert,Arnout van de Rijt,Mojtaba Rostami Kandroodi,Stijn J. Rotman,Lucas Sage,Germans Savcisens,Katrin Schwanitz,Steven Skiena,Alessandro Spata,Yannick Stadtfeld,Benedikt Stroebl,Gaetano Tedesco,Mathilde Theelen,Gianluca Tori,Abigail Tun-Mendicuti,Rishabh Tyagi,Keyon Vafa,Luiz Felipe Vecchietti,Linda Vecgaile,Willem R. J. Vermeulen,Maria-Pia Victoria Feser,Lionel A. Voirol,Thom B. Volker,Xinran Wang,Jiani Yan,Xinyi Zhao,Flora Zhou,Zuzana Zilincikova,Malvina Nissim,Matthew J. Salganik,Gert Stulp*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 通过数据挑战赛预测荷兰居民三年内生育情况，发现预测精度中等，先进模型未优于传统模型，且存在由生育随机性决定的理论预测上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重大生活事件难以预测，需探究这是否源于理论、数据或算法的局限，还是随机性本身的作用。

Method: 147名研究者利用调查数据和全人口登记数据，采用从逻辑回归到大语言模型和Transformer的多种方法，预测荷兰18-45岁居民三年内生育，并模拟受孕与怀孕的随机生物学过程估算预测上限。

Result: 最佳F1分数为登记数据0.59、调查数据0.76；先进模型未超越经典模型；更大规模登记数据未优于调查数据；理论预测上限为调查F1约0.86-0.94、登记约0.88-0.96。

Conclusion: 实际表现低于理论上限，说明数据、方法和未建模的随机因素仍有提升空间；但上限本身显示生育随机性对个体生活预测构成非平凡限制。

Abstract: Major life events have proven difficult to predict. Does this reflect limits of theory, data, and algorithms, or the large role of chance? We examine one outcome - having a child within three years - through a near-ideal setting for prediction: a data challenge where 147 researchers predicted births for Dutch residents aged 18-45, using survey data and full-population registers. Methods ranged from logistic regression to a large language model and transformers. Predictions were moderately accurate (best F1: register 0.59, survey 0.76); advanced models did not outperform classical ones; and the larger registers did not beat the survey. Simulating the stochastic biology of conception and pregnancy, we estimated a predictive ceiling (survey F1 ~ 0.86-0.94, register 0.88-0.96). Observed performance falls short of this ceiling, implicating imperfect data, methods, and unmodelled chance, while the ceiling itself shows that chance in reproduction alone sets a non-trivial limit on predicting individual lives.

</details>


### [145] [One-Layer Transformer Provably Learns Multiclass One-Nearest Neighbor in Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01311)
*Skanda Athreya,Yutong Wang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 将单层Transformer与最近邻分类器在二分类中的等价性扩展到多分类场景，证明使用argmax分类头的单层Transformer等同于一对多的一最近邻分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作虽在多分类上有所探索，但依赖非标准的基于舍入的分类头，与实际应用中的argmax头不符，留下理论缺口；本研究旨在填补该缺口，统一理论分析与实践经验。

Method: 利用单纯形编码（simplex encoding），严格论证在argmax分类头下，单层Transformer的决策规则与多分类环境中的一最近邻分类器完全一致。

Result: 成功证明两者等价，消除了以往多分类结果中的非标准假设，使理论结果更贴近实际模型配置。

Conclusion: 该工作明确了单层Transformer与最近邻方法在多分类中的理论联系，为后续理解Transformer的归纳偏置和可解释性提供了更坚实的基础。

Abstract: We extend recent work establishing an equivalence between one-layer transformers and nearest-neighbor classifiers in the binary setting to the multiclass case. By leveraging the simplex encoding, we show that one-layer transformers with an argmax classification head behave identically to a one-nearest-neighbor classifier in the multiclass setting. This closes a gap left by prior work, whose multiclass result relied on a non-standard rounding-based approach rather than the typical argmax head used in practice.

</details>


### [146] [Few-Shot Out of Domain Intent Detection with Covariance Corrected Mahalanobis Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00961)
*Jayasimha Talur,Oleg Smirnov,Paul Missault*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出一种协方差校正的Mahalanobis距离方法，以提升少样本场景下域外意图检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mahalanobis距离方法在少样本设置下表现不佳，无法超越基线方法，需分析原因并改进。

Method: 通过分析少样本下协方差估计不准确的问题，提出协方差校正的Mahalanobis距离用于OOD意图检测。

Result: 该方法在少样本设定下有效改善了OOD检测性能，优于原始方法及基线（摘要中未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 协方差校正的Mahalanobis距离是少样本OOD意图检测的可行改进方案。

Abstract: Conversational agents like chatbots and voice assistants are trained to understand and respond to user intents. On encountering an utterance with an intent different from the ones they have been trained on, these agents are expected to classify the intent as `unknown' or `out of domain'. This problem is known as out of domain (OOD) intent detection. Podolskiy et al. (2021), showed that Mahalanobis distance can be used effectively for identifying OOD intents, outperforming competing approaches. However, their method fails to outperform the baselines in the practically important few-shot setting. In this paper we analyze the reason for low performance and propose a covariance corrected Mahalanobis distance for detecting out-of-domain intents.

</details>


### [147] [Predicting Subsurface Abnormalities Growth using Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01417)
*Mehrdad Shafiei Dizaji,Hoda Azari*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究首次将物理信息神经网络（PINNs）集成到探地雷达（GPR）数据预测中，通过融合电磁波传播物理规律与深度学习方法，提出了一种结合CNN、通道空间特征注意力（SFCA）和时间特征帧注意力（TFFA）的ConvLSTM架构，显著提升了GPR数据预测精度，为非破坏性评估（NDE）和基础设施健康监测提供了新的技术途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的GPR数据分析方法往往依赖纯数据驱动的模型，难以保证预测结果符合底层物理规律，且对基础设施内部损伤（如桥梁桥面劣化）的识别精度有限。作者受医学影像预测肿瘤行为的启发，旨在通过嵌入物理约束，提升深度模型在GPR数据解释中的准确性和解释性。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心组件：卷积神经网络（CNN）用于提取空间视觉特征；空间特征通道注意力（SFCA）模块用于自适应加权通道特征；ConvLSTM结合时间特征帧注意力（TFFA）模块用于捕捉时间动态依赖。物理信息以损失函数约束或架构设计的形式直接嵌入网络，强制预测结果遵循麦克斯韦方程等电磁波传播物理定律，使特征响应经由自注意力机制精细调谐。

Result: 实验表明，与纯数据驱动模型相比，融合物理信息的PINN模型在GPR数据预测上的准确性显著提升，特别是在反映桥面铺装层衰减、脱粘等细微变化方面表现更优，验证了物理约束在强化视觉和时间特征提取过程中的有效性。

Conclusion: 将PINNs引入GPR数据分析不仅大幅提升了预测精度，还加深了对基础设施劣化过程中物理机制的理解。该方法有望变革非破坏性评估领域，通过提供更可靠、物理一致的预测工具来延长基础设施的生命周期并降低维护成本。

Abstract: The research explores the pioneering integration of Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) into the domain of Ground-Penetrating Radar (GPR) data prediction. This research presents a detailed development framework for a specialized PINN model, proficient at interpreting and forecasting GPR data, much like how medical imaging models predict tumor behavior. By harnessing the synergy between deep learning algorithms and the physical laws governing subsurface structures or in medical terms, human tissues the model effectively embeds the physics of electromagnetic wave propagation into its architecture. This ensures that predictions not only align with fundamental physical principles but also mirror the precision needed in medical diagnostics for detecting and monitoring tumors. The suggested deep learning structure comprises three components: a CNN, a spatial feature channel attention (SFCA) mechanism, and ConvLSTM, along with temporal feature frame attention (TFFA) modules. The attention mechanism computes channel attention and temporal attention weights using self-adaptation, thereby fine tuning the visual and temporal feature responses to extract the most pertinent and significant visual and temporal features. By integrating physics directly into the neural network, our model has shown enhanced accuracy in forecasting GPR data. This improvement is vital for conducting effective assessments of bridge deck conditions and other evaluations related to civil infrastructure. The use of Physics Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) has demonstrated the potential to transform the field of Non-Destructive Evaluation (NDE) by enhancing the precision of infrastructure deterioration predictions. Moreover, it offers a deeper insight into the fundamental mechanisms of deterioration, viewed through the prism of physics-based models.

</details>


### [148] [Figures as Programs: Recursive Generation of Editable Scientific Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01006)
*Yepeng Liu,Dasen Dai,Chengzhi Liu,Yiren Song,Hai Ci,Yu Zhang,Qi Zhang,Mike Zheng Shou,Xin Eric Wang,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出FigTree，一种将科学论文自动转化为结构化矢量图的多智能体系统，通过递归SVG程序构建实现高质量、可编辑的图形生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学方法论图形对清晰传达复杂方法至关重要，但人工制作费时费力且需多轮修改；现有图像生成模型虽能合成美观光栅图，但单次生成难以满足人类需求，且光栅图的精确编辑对人和模型都具挑战性。

Method: 将科学图形生成视为递归SVG程序构建，FigTree采用多智能体架构：从源论文中提取图形内容，将图形分解为层级局部区域，为每个区域生成简短SVG程序并组装片段；通过“渲染-批评”循环联合检查渲染图形及其程序，将视觉缺陷追溯至具体代码语句并精确修复。

Result: 在图形质量和可编辑性上对FigTree进行了广泛评估，结果表明FigTree能生成高质量图形，并在编辑效率上优于现有基于光栅的方法。

Conclusion: FigTree通过结构化程序表示和迭代优化，验证了面向科学图形生成的自动矢量图构建在质量和可编辑性上的优势，为解决复杂图形生成与编辑难题提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Scientific methodology figures are essential for communicating complex methods clearly, yet creating them remains labor-intensive and typically requires multiple rounds of refinement. Recent image-generation models can synthesize visually appealing raster figures, but producing a human-satisfactory result in a single generation step remains difficult. Moreover, precise edits to raster figures are challenging for both humans and models. We formulate scientific figure generation as recursive SVG program construction and propose \textsc{FigTree}, a \textit{multi-agent} system that automatically transforms a scientific paper into a structured vector figure. \textsc{FigTree} grounds figure content in the source paper, decomposes a figure into a hierarchy of local regions, generates each region as a short SVG program, and assembles the resulting fragments. A render-critic refinement loop jointly inspects the rendered figure and its underlying program, enabling visual defects to be traced to specific statements and accurately repaired. We conduct extensive evaluations of \textsc{FigTree} on figure quality and editability, showing that \textsc{FigTree} produces high-quality figures, while also enabling more effective editing than existing raster-based methods.

</details>


### [149] [CATeye: Coupled Attribute-Topology Invariance Learning for Voucher Abuse Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01425)
*Tian Tian,Shuaicheng Niu,Hao Kuang,Yuanhang Hu,Dong Li,Zhiqi Shen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出CATeye框架，通过双重选择器（属性不变选择器AIS和边不变选择器EIS）解耦属性-拓扑耦合偏移，提升电商优惠券滥用检测的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商优惠券滥用检测面临属性-拓扑耦合偏移问题：属性分布变化通过边构建影响图拓扑，经GNN消息传递放大扰动信号，导致模型需频繁重训且跨区域/时间泛化差。

Method: CATeye包含两个可学习选择器：AIS学习节点自适应掩码过滤非不变属性；EIS在保留不变属性的条件下采样不变子图并隔离非不变边。利用不变与不变组件构造多视图，分别优化以强化域不变表示、抑制域特定变化。

Result: 在Lazada专有数据集和公开基准上，CATeye优于9种强基线（图域泛化与异常检测），平均F1最多提升8.61%。

Conclusion: 显式分离属性与拓扑中的不变/非不变信号可有效应对耦合偏移，验证了双选择器方案在实际电商风控场景的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Voucher abuse poses a major challenge in e-commerce, where malicious users exploit promotional vouchers for profit. Unfortunately, fraud patterns evolve rapidly over time and across regions, causing distribution shifts that degrade existing detection models unless retrained frequently. To tackle this, we propose the Coupled Attribute-Topology Invariance Learning framework (CATeye). The key challenge arises from coupled attribute-topology shift, where edges built from attribute proximity cause environment-driven attribute shift to induce shifted topology, thereby amplifying variant signals through GNN message passing. CATeye sees through such coupled shifts with two learnable selectors. First, an Attribute Invariance Selector (AIS) learns node-adaptive masks to filter out non-invariant attributes. Then, conditioned on retained invariant attributes, an Edge Invariance Selector (EIS) samples an invariant subgraph and isolates non-invariant edges. Using the resulting invariant and non-invariant components, CATeye constructs multiple views and applies view-specific objectives to emphasize domain-invariant representations while suppressing domain-specific variations. Experiments on both a proprietary dataset from Lazada, a major Southeast Asian e-commerce platform, and a public benchmark show that CATeye consistently outperforms nine strong domain generalization and graph anomaly detection baselines, achieving up to an 8.61% improvement in average F1 score over the strongest baseline. Source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Tian0426/CATeye.

</details>


### [150] [Data-Driven Persona-Conditioned Agents for A/B Test Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01038)
*Ziyad Benomar,Weronika Łajewska,Leonardo Perelli,Saab Mansour*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出利用LLM智能体与数据驱动用户画像模拟A/B测试结果的框架，在40个测试中达到0.75-0.90的方向准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实A/B测试成本高、耗时长，需要减少对真实流量的依赖并加速实验预筛选。

Method: 基于匿名行为数据构建用户画像，将A/B模拟构建为结构化问答任务，系统研究问题格式、画像来源、行为深度与多样性权衡及子采样策略。

Result: 最佳配置在方向准确率上达到0.75-0.90，且依赖测试指标类型。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的用户画像是实现快速、低成本实验预筛选的有效途径，可作为真实A/B测试的补充工具。

Abstract: A/B testing is the gold standard for evaluating product changes, but each experiment requires real user traffic, engineering effort, and weeks of measurement. We propose a simulation framework that predicts A/B test outcomes using LLM-powered agents conditioned on data-driven personas grounded in real user behavioral signals. Unlike prior work that relies on synthetic or rule-based personas, our agents are constructed from anonymized behavioral data-activity patterns, engagement signals, and inferred demographics-enabling more faithful population modeling. We frame A/B test simulation as a structured question task and systematically study (i) question design formats, (ii) the impact of persona data source and domain alignment, (iii) the trade-off between per-persona behavioral depth and population diversity, and (iv) efficient population subsampling. On a benchmark of 40 A/B tests spanning two metric types, our best configuration achieves 0.75-0.90 directional accuracy depending on the test metric, demonstrating that data-driven personas are a viable path toward fast, low-cost experiment pre-screening.

</details>


### [151] [Learning Sparse Decision Trees via Transformer Variational Auto-Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01430)
*Giacomo Fidone,Alessio Cascione,Riccardo Guidotti*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出TREVIS，利用树变换变分自编码器将决策树映射到连续潜在空间，从而通过梯度优化联合优化预测性能和结构稀疏性，实验表明TREVIS在保持预测性能的同时提升了结构稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树因其透明逻辑而被广泛使用，但现有学习算法大多只关注预测性能，忽略了其他理想属性（如结构稀疏性）的联合优化。

Method: TREVIS基于树变换变分自编码器（TTVAE），将决策树映射到潜在表示，将离散搜索空间转化为连续空间，并通过可微代理模型实现基于梯度的优化。

Result: TREVIS发现的决策树在预测性能上媲美现有近优算法，同时显著改善了结构稀疏性。

Conclusion: TREVIS为联合优化决策树的复杂目标提供了一种有效方法，能够在不牺牲预测性能的前提下提升结构简洁性。

Abstract: Decision trees are among the most widely used models in machine learning, largely due to their transparent decision logic, making them well-suited for high-stakes decision-making contexts. However, most existing learning algorithms focus on predictive performance, overlooking the joint optimization of other desirable properties, such as structural sparsity. In this work we propose TREVIS, an approach for learning decision trees with respect to complex objectives, based on the exploration of the latent space of a Tree Transformer Variational Auto-Encoder (TTVAE). By mapping decision trees onto latent representations, TREVIS replaces the discrete search space with a continuous one, enabling gradient-based optimization via a differentiable surrogate model. We experiment with TREVIS for learning decision trees that jointly optimize predictive performance and sparsity. Results show that TREVIS discovers decision trees matching the predictive performance of existing near-optimal algorithms while improving their structural sparsity.

</details>


### [152] [WorldBench: Culturally Grounded Benchmark for Multilingual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01056)
*Leonardo Ranaldi,Sherrie Shen,Jushi Kai,Alexandra Birch*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出WorldBench，一个多语言、文化接地气的智能体基准测试，包含1600个任务，并引入约束任务成功率（CTS）指标，发现前沿模型在该基准上表现不佳，且普遍存在状态保持不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未充分测试智能体在多步骤任务中的状态保持能力、跨语言鲁棒性以及真实场景应用，亟需一个更全面、更贴近实际的评估工具。

Method: 构建WorldBench，涵盖七种语言和八种文化的真实人物日常生活工作流，通过人工专家过滤与精炼；评估时结合自然语言指令和测试床，引入CTS指标，集成确定性规则和LLM-as-a-Judge来综合衡量任务完成度与最小环境改动。

Result: 前沿模型CTS最高仅达49.2%，且所有模型在任务正确性与环境保持之间普遍存在显著差距，长程任务和状态约束情境下表现脆弱。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在多语言、代理式场景中仍显不足，尤其是长时程任务和资源不变性要求下，揭示其实际部署的理想与现实存在明显鸿沟。

Abstract: Despite the growing use of LLM-powered agents to solve multi-step tasks in complex environments, existing benchmarks rarely test state preservation, performance across languages, and application to realistic, grounded scenarios. To address these concerns, we present WorldBench: a comprehensive, multilingual benchmark of genuine, persona-grounded everyday workflows, where agents can act in a sandbox via structured actions. WorldBench comprises 1,600 tasks across seven languages and eight cultures, filtered and refined through feedback from human annotators with language- and culture-specific expertise. For evaluation, we extend metrics from previous works and introduce Constrained Task Success (CTS), which combines natural language instructions and testbeds to score task completion, minimal modification, and other complementary metrics through deterministic and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluations. Our experiments show that frontier models reach only 49.2% CTS, with all models demonstrating large gaps between correctness and environment preservation. We thereby show that current agents remain brittle in multilingual, agentic scenarios, especially for long-horizon tasks and under state-preservation constraints

</details>


### [153] [Edge-Girth as a Structural Edge Feature for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01441)
*Lilian Marey,Charlotte Laclau*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出基于边围长及其重数的结构描述符，避免预设子图大小，注入门控消息传递架构（EGAGNN）后，在ZINC-12k上MAE降至现有门控模型的三分之一；但在特定对称图中描述符退化为常数，模型表达能力回落到1-WL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN受1-WL能力限制，常用固定小规模子图计数作为增强特征，但需预设子图大小，且选择常脱离数据。需要一种无需预设、能捕捉任意长度环的局部结构描述符。

Method: 定义边围长（穿过某边的最短环长度）及重数（该长度最短环数量），对每条边用一次BFS精确计算，形成局部不变量；将该描述符注入门控消息传递架构（EGAGNN）中进行节点/边特征增强。

Result: 在ZINC-12k回归任务中，EGAGNN（104k参数）的MAE比最优门控模型低三倍；与同架构的环计数描述符相比，需用两倍通道数才能匹敌计数至长度8的字典，长度4的字典无增益。在BREC图判别基准的400对中，90对由于每条边具有相同最短环长度和重数导致描述符恒定，模型全部无法区分。

Conclusion: 边围长描述符在回归任务上有效且无需预设子图大小，但在边对称场景下表达能力受限，证明其固有边界与1-WL一致，提示需结合其他非局部分量才能突破该瓶颈。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNN) based on message passing are provably no more powerful than the one-dimensional Weisfeiler--Leman colour-refinement test (1-WL): two graphs it cannot tell apart receive identical representations, however deep or wide the network. A common remedy augments node or edge features with precomputed structural descriptors, most often counts of a fixed small subgraph such as triangles or longer cycles, but such counts require committing in advance to the size of the substructure counted, a choice usually made blind to the data. We study a descriptor that avoids this choice. The edge-girth of an edge is the length of a shortest cycle through it, and its multiplicity is the number of such shortest cycles; together they form a per-edge invariant that reports cycles of arbitrary length, computable exactly by a single breadth-first search per edge.
