Injected into a gated message-passing architecture, EGAGNN, it reaches a test MAE a factor three below the closest gated comparator on the ZINC-12k regression benchmark at 104k parameters; against bounded cycle-counting descriptors under the same architecture, it matches only a dictionary counting cycles up to length eight, using twice as many channels, while a dictionary capped at length four performs no better than no structural information at all. On graph discrimination we prove a matching limitation: on graphs where every edge sees the same number of shortest cycles of the same length, the descriptor becomes constant and any model built on it collapses back to the 1-WL bound. This holds without exception across all 400 pairs of the BREC benchmark: not one of the 90 such pairs is distinguished.

</details>


### [154] [Rethinking Learnability in Offline Data-driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01493)
*Chao Qian,Chen-Guang Wang,Rong-Xi Tan,Ke Xue*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出算法依赖的可学习性概念，证明其在离散与连续优化中的充分性，并据此开发轨迹学习框架及UGTL方法，在Design-Bench基准上取得最优平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线优化理论显示PAC可学习性不足，因为最优区域可能未被充分学习；需要定义更契合优化过程的可学习性条件。

Method: 定义算法依赖可学习性（值查询与一阶形式），证明其分别适用于子模最大化的贪心/局部搜索和凸最小化的投影梯度下降；构建轨迹学习框架（轨迹构造、建模、候选生成），提出UGTL（不确定性感知梯度引导轨迹学习），用条件扩散建模局部连贯改进轨迹。

Result: 在五个Design-Bench任务上，UGTL取得3.1/25的最佳聚合平均排名，跨架构替换实验证实轨迹构造的显著贡献。

Conclusion: 算法依赖可学习性为离线优化提供了理论基础，轨迹学习框架和UGTL方法验证了其实际有效性，表明聚焦优化器轨迹的学习是高效离线优化的可行路径。

Abstract: Black-Box Optimization (BBO) has found broad applications, but evolutionary algorithms and Bayesian optimization face efficiency challenges as real-world BBO problems grow increasingly complex. Data-driven optimization improves the efficiency of BBO algorithms by learning from data. Offline data-driven optimization seeks high-quality solutions using only a fixed set of previous evaluations, attracting substantial attention because it requires no additional online evaluations. Many offline optimization methods have been proposed, but a fundamental question remains unanswered: what learnability is sufficient for offline optimization? Prior theoretical studies show that Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learnability is insufficient, as the optimal region may remain poorly learned even when most regions are well learned. In this paper, we propose algorithm-dependent learnability, which requires accuracy only on the optimizer's trajectory. We prove that its value-query form is sufficient for representative discrete settings, including greedy and local search for submodular maximization, while its first-order analogue is sufficient for projected gradient descent on convex minimization. Motivated by this notion, we formalize a trajectory-learning framework comprising trajectory construction, trajectory modeling, and candidate generation, and analyze existing trajectory-based methods under it. We further propose Uncertainty-aware Gradient-guided Trajectory Learning (UGTL), which constructs locally coherent improvement trajectories reflecting plausible search paths, models them with conditional diffusion, and selects a diverse candidate set. On five Design-Bench tasks, UGTL achieves the best aggregate mean rank, $3.1/25$, among 25 methods. Controlled trajectory analyses and cross-architecture replacements confirm that our trajectory construction plays a significant role in the improvement.

</details>


### [155] [Space Generative AI with Solar Energy Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01062)
*Jierui Zhang,Jianhao Huang,Zhanwei Wang,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出一种太阳能供电的空间生成式AI框架，在严格时间窗口内平衡扩散模型的生成步数与下行传输能量，实现端到端性能最大化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星可将生成式AI扩展到无地面基础设施的偏远地区，但星载能量有限且随时间变化，生成质量与传输可靠性之间存在根本性权衡。

Method: 利用轨道运动决定的可预测太阳能采集动态，提出联合计算-通信资源优化框架：先刻画固定生成深度下最大下行吞吐量并建立分离原则，再推导恒定功率下的闭式步长选择策略。

Result: 在真实轨道动力学实验下，所提策略动态平衡生成质量与传输可靠性，相比静态计算/通信中心基线在多种太阳能状态下获得显著端到端性能增益。

Conclusion: 证明了利用太阳能采集可预测性可实现有效的联合资源调度，为空间生成式AI的实际部署提供低复杂度高性能方案。

Abstract: Satellites are emerging as promising platforms to extend generative \emph{artificial intelligence} (AI) services to remote areas lacking terrestrial infrastructure. However, deploying space generative AI is fundamentally constrained by the limited, time-varying onboard energy supplied by solar \emph{energy harvesting} (EH). This paper presents a framework for solar-powered space generative AI in which a satellite receives a user prompt, executes a diffusion-based image-generation model, and downlinks the compressed result within a strict time window. We identify the fundamental \emph{computation--communication} (C$^2$) trade-offs governed by the shared harvested-energy budgets. Specifically, increasing the number of generation steps improves intrinsic image quality but depletes energy and time available for downlink transmission, whereas prioritizing communication guarantees reliable delivery but sacrifices semantic quality. To balance these trade-offs and maximize \emph{end-to-end} (E2E) generative performance, we exploit the predictable solar-EH dynamics induced by deterministic orbital motion and develop a joint C$^2$ resource-optimization framework using a tractable two-step approach. First, we characterize the maximum downlink throughput for a fixed generation depth under continuous solar EH. This establishes a separation principle that decouples waiting-time selection from optimal transmit-power control. Next, we formulate a joint C$^2$ utility-maximization problem and derive a closed-form, low-complexity step-selection policy in the dominant constant-power regime. Extensive experiments under realistic orbital dynamics demonstrate that the proposed policy dynamically balances generation quality and transmission reliability. This yields significant E2E performance gains over static computation- and communication-centric baselines across diverse solar-EH states.

</details>


### [156] [FinLifeBench: Exhaustive Life-Event History and Financial-State Reconstruction from Longitudinal Banking Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01198)
*Hangyeul Lee,Juyoung Oh,Jaeyong Ko,Sunmin Kim,Jaeik Park,Hyunkyu Kim,Jungmin Son,Pilsung Kang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出FinLifeBench基准，测试LLM在累积银行对话中重建完整生活事件及财务状态的能力，发现模型随对话增长性能显著下降，主要错误源自事件遗漏与信息过时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准侧重问答或短时段记忆，缺乏对纵向、完整、可追溯记录重建的评估，而真实银行服务要求助理持续维护用户全生命周期信息。

Method: 构建6,000轮八句韩语银行会话（20条合成轨迹），设置两个任务：逐事件追溯首次出现会话，以及在连续检查点重建34路径财务状态；采用确定性完整标注与共识质量控制，并在11个LLM全上下文条件下评估。

Result: 事件锚定召回率从15会话的0.591降至300会话的0.445，错误主因是事件遗漏；财务状态重建中模型常把已取代信息当作当前值，最佳GCA@15仅0.470；两任务表现相关性弱。

Conclusion: 模型能定位已恢复事件的证据，却难以维护完整且时间有效的纵向记录，暴露出长对话场景下记忆整合与时效判断的系统性缺陷。

Abstract: Repeated banking interactions require assistants to maintain complete, current, and traceable customer records as life changes emerge incidentally in routine requests. Existing benchmarks emphasize question answering, bounded episodes, or targeted recall rather than exhaustive longitudinal reconstruction. We introduce FinLifeBench, which evaluates two tasks over the same cumulative dialogue: reconstructing every life-event instance with its first-establishing session and reconstructing a complete 34-path financial state at consecutive checkpoints. The benchmark contains 6,000 eight-turn Korean banking sessions from 20 independent synthetic trajectories, with deterministic, exhaustive gold for 24 event types and 34 state paths and consensus quality assurance. Across eleven LLMs under a full-context condition, event-anchor recall falls from 0.591 at 15 sessions to 0.445 at 300. Errors are driven primarily by omitted events rather than poor anchor localization, while financial-state reconstruction frequently treats superseded or potentially outdated information as current; the best GCA@15 reaches 0.470. Performance on the two reconstruction tasks is only weakly associated. These results show that models can localize evidence for recovered events while still failing to maintain complete and temporally valid longitudinal records.

</details>


### [157] [Analog-DB: An Agent-First Analog Integrated Circuit Database, From Blocks to Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01286)
*Danial Noori Zadeh,Mohamed B. Elamien*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出analog-db，一个开源、版本化的模拟集成电路设计数据库，通过领域特定语言和参数化方案实现工艺无关的拓扑共享、可重用测试平台和机器可读数据表，支持AI设计智能体直接发现和复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟集成电路设计共享困难，因为代工厂保密协议限制了工艺细节的公开，且已发表结果的测试平台很少发布，阻碍了复现和复用。

Method: 用领域特定语言将每个设计建模为工艺无关拓扑、可重用测试平台和机器可读数据表的统一模式；参数化方案将功能子块和设备尺寸暴露为带匹配约束的命名参数，实现电路可组合和可重定向；通过模式管理的契约和可查询目录支持AI设计代理发现和复用。

Result: 在调节器语料库中，23个电路-工艺绑定在3个开源工艺套件上均满足自身规范带（典型角、匹配器件、无版图），其中10/23满足通用类别带；17个导入的尺寸设计失败后由标注子块角色驱动的gm/ID尺寸调整循环在1-3次迭代内收敛；监督案例中编码代理用发布产品修复了斩波仪表放大器运算放大器核心的4处手工定义缺陷和缺失共模反馈环。

Conclusion: 数据库包含16个类别68个电路，在分层测试平台下原理图级别可验证，并在性能记分板上跟踪，开源发布，证明了可共享、可重定向的模拟设计表示的有效性。

Abstract: Sharing analog integrated circuit designs remains difficult: foundry non-disclosure agreements restrict the process details a design depends on, and the testbenches behind published results are rarely released. We present analog-db, an open-source, versioned database built on a shareable design representation. A domain-specific language captures each design as a process-neutral topology, reusable testbenches, and a machine-readable datasheet under one schema, so a design is shared in full and re-simulates on the process kits it is bound to. A parameterization scheme exposes functional sub-blocks and device sizes as named parameters that carry their matching constraints, making circuits composable and retargetable; a schema-governed contract and queryable catalog let AI design agents discover and reuse them directly. Across the regulator corpus, all 23 circuit-kit bindings on three open kits meet their own recorded specification bands (typical corner, matched devices, no layout) and 10 of 23 meet a common class band. Seventeen of the 23 imported sizings failed their testbenches and closed under a gm/ID sizing loop driven by the annotated sub-block roles, typically within one to three iterations. In a supervised case study, a coding agent working from the released artifacts sized the op-amp cores of a chopper instrumentation amplifier on an open 130nm kit, locating four hand-entry defects and a missing common-mode feedback loop that the sizing-only baseline did not repair. The database holds 68 circuits across sixteen classes, verifiable at schematic level under a tiered harness and tracked on a power/performance scoreboard, released at https://github.com/MacAnalog/spicexplorer-release.

</details>


### [158] [SymFold: Synergizing Evolutionary and Structural Priors for Accurate Protein Inverse Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01353)
*Handong Wang,Jiaxin Qi,Baisheng Lai,Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出对称双路径架构，同时利用PLM的序列进化知识和MPLM的结构知识迭代指导蛋白质逆折叠，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有串行管线中PLM仅做后验编辑受限于上游预测质量；直接使用MPLM编码结构生成序列效果不佳，需融合两类模型优势。

Method: 设计对称双路径架构，一条路径经PLM获取预训练序列进化知识，另一条经MPLM获取结构知识，两条路径迭代交互共同引导序列生成。

Result: 在标准逆折叠基准上超越现有方法，消融实验验证对称设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 对称双路径融合是蛋白质逆折叠的可行方向，为社区提供新思路。

Abstract: Protein inverse folding aims to recover amino acid sequences for a given 3D protein structure, underpinning broad applications such as enzyme engineering and drug discovery.Current methods often follow a serial pipeline, in which a structure encoder predicts a coarse sequence, which is then refined by protein language models (PLMs). However, because PLMs only perform post-hoc sequence edits, the refinement is bounded by the quality of upstream predictions.Thanks to recent multimodal protein language models (MPLMs), we could directly encode structure to generate sequences with pretrained structural knowledge, but we observe that they are not effective for inverse folding. Therefore, we introduce a symmetric dual-path architecture that both leverages PLMs for pretrained sequence evolution knowledge and MPLMs for pretrained structural knowledge to iteratively guide protein sequence generation.Through extensive experiments across standard protein inverse folding benchmarks, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing prior approaches, and ablation studies validate the rationale of our symmetric design, revealing a promising direction for the community.

</details>


### [159] [When Guardrails Look Effective: Construct Validity Failures in LLM Agent Commerce Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01519)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文审计了基于语言模型代理的交互式经济模拟的有效性，发现初始福利估计因协议差异和随机性而产生偏差，需通过契约测试才能支持政策结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型模拟的市场输出看似经济结果，但可能未真正体现所声称的行为，导致政策评估失真。

Method: 通过多轮买卖测试床，控制协议（模式、选择程序）和生成次数，并引入脚本化控制，提出构建效度契约（激励效度、协议隔离、随机稳定性、福利核算）。

Result: 初始福利效应从+87.4降至+7.2/-13.9/+23.8；14B模型单次生成效应均值+229，三次生成后降至+37.6且不显著；生成残差占变异49.9%；利润压力导致非单调行为。

Conclusion: 原始估计因协议隔离无效，受控研究因激励效度和随机稳定性不确定；案例表明需经效度检查后才能推断护栏真实价值。

Abstract: Interactive simulations increasingly evaluate policies in markets populated by language-model agents. Their outputs can look economic---prices, profits, consumer surplus, and welfare---without instantiating the behavior named in the claim. We audit this risk in a multi-turn buyer--seller testbed for configurable hotel transactions. An initial implementation reported welfare gains from two marketplace guardrails of +87.4, +35.0, and +28.8 across a Qwen2.5 1.5B--14B ladder. It also gave guarded and unguarded agents different offer schemas and choice procedures. Holding the schema and buyer chooser fixed changes the paired contrasts to +7.2, -13.9, and +23.8. The four largest 14B single-generation effects averaged +229; after three generations per profile-condition, they averaged +37.6 (95% bootstrap interval [-34.2, 109.3]), while generation residuals account for 49.9% of variation in this post-hoc probe. A seller-incentive check is non-monotone: increasing profit pressure produces less profit than the default seller prompt. Scripted positive controls show why this matters. A profit-maximizing seller already attains first-best welfare, so guardrails mostly redistribute and reduce welfare; they create welfare only when the seller is explicitly programmed to force inefficient bundles. We contribute a construct-validity contract separating incentive validity, protocol isolation, stochastic stability, and welfare accounting, and returning INVALID or INCONCLUSIVE before substantive policy claims. In our case, the original estimate is INVALID under protocol isolation, while the controlled study remains INCONCLUSIVE under incentive validity and stochastic stability. The case does not show that guardrails are ineffective; it shows their apparent value is unidentified until the simulated agents and protocol pass these checks.

</details>


### [160] [EvoSCM: Scientific Belief Revision Through Causal Model Evolution and Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01526)
*Qing Zhao,Haowei Li,Weijian Deng,Pengxu Wei,Liang Lin*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: EvoSCM 是一种赋予科学智能体显式结构因果模型（SCM）的框架，通过闭环发现过程使模型随实验证据演化，在 DiscoverPhysics 基准上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体以自由文本表达科学假设，信念隐式且难以检验和修订，因此需要显式可演化的假设表示。

Method: EvoSCM 维护一组竞争的 SCM 假设，每轮通过溯因推断潜在机制、设计判别性干预、做出可证伪预测，并用实验偏差归纳出修正规则来更新模型，再演绎验证新群体。

Result: 在 DiscoverPhysics 基准上，EvoSCM 在解释准确性、预测准确性和有效利用实验交互方面均持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 显式结构因果模型与演化循环相结合，使科学智能体能更稳健地发现隐藏动态，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Scientific agents must learn not only how to reason, but also what to believe. However, existing LLM agents typically express scientific hypotheses in free-form text, leaving their beliefs implicit and difficult to test or revise. We introduce EvoSCM, which equips scientific agents with explicit structural causal models that evolve as new experimental evidence is collected. EvoSCM maintains a population of competing SCM hypotheses, each encoding a candidate causal explanation of the environment, and evolves them through a closed discovery loop. In each round, the agent abduces latent mechanisms from accumulated evidence, designs discriminative interventions, and commits to falsifiable predictions that it tests through experimentation. Discrepancies between prediction and observation are inductively distilled into correction rules that revise the causal structures and mechanisms of each hypothesis, and the agent then deductively validates the revised population against accumulated evidence and structural consistency to guide the next round. We evaluate EvoSCM on DiscoverPhysics, a benchmark requiring agents to uncover the hidden dynamics of noncanonical physical worlds through experimentation. EvoSCM consistently improves scientific discovery over baselines, yielding more accurate explanations and predictions while making more effective use of experimental interactions.

</details>


### [161] [Can LLMs Discover Scientific Laws in Real and Parallel Worlds?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01552)
*Yiming Huang,Ziche Liu,Zhuohang Wu,Yiqian Wang,Junxia Cui,Xinkai Zou,Linjun Mao,Nan Huang,Naicheng Yu,Kaijie Zhu,Yue Ma,Kun Zhou,Letian Peng,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出SCILAWS-BENCH基准，基于已发表论文和真实数据评估大语言模型科学定律发现能力，揭示预测拟合与科学有效性可分离、记忆影响重现能力、最佳N选择存在瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估要么用合成情境简化发现过程，要么重用LLM可能已见过的已发表目标，无法真实反映模型能否自主发现科学定律。

Method: 从381篇论文提取118个问题、291个候选定律和约800万真实数据点，跨六个学科；每个问题分REAL（固定真实观测中提出定律并评估预测拟合与文献科学有效性）和PARALLEL（主动查询残差校准世界并恢复新合成的隐藏定律）两种互补设置。

Result: 预测拟合与科学有效性可能不一致；记忆影响模型是复现还是超越已发表公式；best-of-N实验发现选择瓶颈。

Conclusion: 提供了一个论文基基准和实证视角，推动AI科学发现的评估与研究。

Abstract: Scientific equation discovery has long been central to scientific progress, proceeding through iterative cycles of hypothesis generation, observational testing, and refinement under scientific constraints. As LLM capabilities advance and their role in AI for Science expands, it remains an open problem whether they can genuinely discover scientific laws and how this ability should be evaluated. Existing evaluations, however, often either simplify discovery through synthetic settings or reuse published targets that may already be familiar to LLMs. We therefore introduce SCILAWS-BENCH, a benchmark for scientific law discovery built from published research and real scientific data. It comprises 118 problems drawn from 381 scientific papers, covering 291 candidate laws and roughly 8M real data points across six scientific disciplines. Each problem is instantiated in two complementary settings: (1) SCILAWS-REAL asks models to propose laws from fixed real observations and evaluates held-out predictive fit and scientific validity derived from the source literature, and (2) SCILAWS-PARALLEL asks models to actively query residual-calibrated worlds and recover synthesized hidden laws derived from published forms. This two-setting task design preserves each problem's scientific context while separately evaluating fixed-record law discovery and active recovery of a newly synthesized hidden law. We find that predictive fit can diverge from scientific validity, memorization shapes whether models reproduce or move beyond published formulas, and our best-of-N study reveals a selection bottleneck. Our work provides a paper-grounded benchmark and new empirical perspectives for evaluating AI for scientific discovery. Project page: https://yiyihum.github.io/SciLaws-Bench

</details>


<div id='Training'></div>

# Training [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Task-Specific Prompt with Global Context for Multi-Task Graph Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00047)
*Zhiyang Qiu,Yangtao Wang,Xiaocui Li,Yanzhao Xie,Siyuan Chen,Wensheng Zhang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出TPGC，一种双先验提示初始化方法，通过任务先验和结构先验显式建模，提升图提示学习在下游任务中的对齐性、可迁移性和少样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图预训练框架使用随机初始化提示，导致提示空间与预训练目标及图结构特征对齐不良，削弱了提示表征的任务相关性、结构感知性和迁移能力。

Method: 包括两个模块：任务先验注入模块在辅助图上进行短时间同构多任务预训练，继承多个预训练任务的优化偏好；结构先验注入模块提取可迁移的全局结构上下文，聚合结构信息丰富的节点嵌入并转换为逐层提示向量。

Result: 在6个主流基准（涵盖节点和图分类任务）上，TPGC在少样本设置下优于现有最先进基线，且下游可调参数更少、运行时间更低。

Conclusion: 双先验提示初始化有效解决了随机初始化导致的表征对齐问题，实现了更强的任务相关性和结构感知性，同时提升了效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Graph prompt learning is an effective paradigm to adapt pre-trained graph models to downstream tasks in low-resource scenarios. However, existing multi-task graph pre-training frameworks generally use randomly initialized prompts, leading to poor alignment between the prompt space, pretext objectives and graph structural characteristics. This greatly weakens the task relevance, structural awareness and transferability of prompt representations. To address this challenge, we propose TPGC, a dual-prior prompt initialization solution that explicitly models the synergy between task prior and structural prior. Specifically, the Task-Prior Injection Module first conducts a short homologous multi-task pre-training on an auxiliary graph, enabling prompt initialization to inherit optimization preferences associated with multiple pretext tasks. Built on the task-aware representations, the Structure-Prior Injection Module further extracts transferable global structural context from the auxiliary graph, converting it into layer-wise prompt vectors by aggregating structurally informative node embeddings. Extensive experiments on 6 mainstream benchmarks covering node and graph classification show that TPGC achieves consistently better performance under few-shot settings than state-of-the-art baselines, with fewer downstream tunable parameters and lower runtime. The code is available at https://github.com/Virgilqiu/TPGC

</details>


### [163] [REAL-Q: E2E LLM Quantization via Dynamic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00049)
*Qian Zhang,Yaoming Li,Zhewen Tan,Yanshu Wang,Heng Lu,Kun Su,Zongwei Lv,Wenhan Yu,Yongge Ma,Yinjun Han,Ruikuang Liu,Tong Yang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出REAL-Q，一种新的PTQ方法，通过端到端对齐的代理损失和逐列块的动态梯度下降，解决了现有方法中的信息错位问题，显著降低KL散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA PTQ方法使用单次闭式二阶求解器，为保持可解析性而严重近似全局损失，且冻结Hessian无法适应损失景观的逐列变化，导致信息错位，影响量化精度。

Method: REAL-Q直接优化端到端对齐的全局损失代理，采用细粒度动态块级梯度下降（每128列更新一次），结合滑动窗口机制平滑跨层过渡，减轻误差传播。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1（8B/70B）和Qwen3（0.6B-32B）的W4A16实验中，相比SOTA全局引导方法，端到端KL散度最多降低约49%。

Conclusion: REAL-Q通过实时动态优化和端到端对齐，突破了传统PTQ的精度瓶颈，为低比特LLM量化提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs) under strict resource constraints. State-of-the-art PTQ methods quantize each layer with a single closed-form second-order solver: to remain analytically tractable, they heavily approximate the global loss (dropping cross-channel coupling, pooling output rows into groups), and they then freeze the resulting Hessian across the entire layer, with no way to refresh it as the loss landscape shifts column by column--a phenomenon we call information misalignment. We propose REAL-Q (Real-time E2E-loss Aligned LLM Quantization), a novel PTQ paradigm that breaks this compromise: instead of diluting the objective for the sake of analytic tractability, REAL-Q targets an end-to-end-aligned surrogate of the global loss and refines it via fine-grained, dynamic Block-wise Gradient Descent applied after every column block (128 columns). By coupling this fine-grained correction with a sliding window mechanism for smooth cross-layer transitions, REAL-Q effectively mitigates error propagation across the network. On LLaMA-3.1 (8B and 70B) and Qwen3 (0.6B-32B) at W4A16, REAL-Q reduces end-to-end KL divergence by up to ~49% relative to state-of-the-art globally-guided methods.

</details>


### [164] [RW-LoRA: Communication-Efficient Decentralized LoRA Fine-Tuning via Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00078)
*Xingran Chen,Rohit Bhagat,Ghadir Ayache,Rawad Bitar,Yanmin Gong,Salim El Rouayheb*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种基于随机游走的LoRA微调方案，通过单一模型token在网络中顺序更新，避免全局同步和聚合误差，显著降低通信与计算开销，并保证非凸目标下的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式LoRA方法（集中式或基于gossip的分散式）依赖同步或重复聚合，导致高通信开销和聚合误差，缺乏高效且无同步的替代方案。

Method: 设计随机游走式LoRA：单个模型token按网络路径顺序访问节点，在每个节点用本地目标函数进行局部更新，无需多副本或全局聚合。

Result: 在多个NLP任务和图拓扑上，所提方法在保持竞争性任务性能的同时，通信和计算成本显著低于gossip式LoRA。

Conclusion: 随机游走式LoRA提供了一种轻量、无同步的分布式微调范式，兼具理论收敛保证与实证高效性，适合通信受限环境。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA have become a standard approach for adapting large foundation models. Adopting fine-tuning to distributed settings faces several challenges. Most existing distributed LoRA methods rely on centralized aggregation, and gossip-based decentralized LoRA requires repeated synchronization among multiple model copies. Both methods incur significant communication overhead and introduce errors due to simultaneous aggregation of multiple model updates. In this paper, we take a different perspective and propose a random-walk-based LoRA fine-tuning scheme. Instead of maintaining multiple model replicas, a single model token traverses the network and is updated sequentially using local fine-tuning objectives. This design eliminates the need for global synchronization, substantially reduces communication and computation costs, and avoids aggregation errors. We provide rigorous convergence guarantees for non-convex objectives under standard assumptions. Through empirical results on multiple NLP tasks and graph topologies, we show that the proposed method achieves competitive task performance with substantially less communication and computation than gossip-based LoRA.

</details>


### [165] [Stochastic complexity of vectors containing cluster structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00084)
*Daniel Nicorici,Olli Yli-Harja,Jaakko Astola*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出一种递归公式，将基于NML模型的聚类结构随机概率（最短编码长度）计算的复杂度从多项式时间降至线性时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MDL聚类中，精确计算NML归一化常数对确定最佳聚类数和结构至关重要，但原始计算需多项式时间，限制了其实用性。

Method: 引入针对NML模型归一化常数的递归公式，利用聚类结构的对称性分解计算，将复杂度优化为关于向量规模和聚类数的线性时间。

Result: 新公式的时间复杂度为线性，优于原有多项式方法，并严格证明了计算的可处理性。

Conclusion: 该工作使基于NML的MDL聚类在大规模数据上可行，为模型选择提供了高效且精确的理论工具。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of computing the stochastic probability (shortest code length) of the encoded vectors containing cluster structure using Normalized Maximum Likelihood (NML) model. This is of great theoretical and practical importance in data clustering based on Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle, such as for estimating the best number of clusters and best cluster structure for the data. Straightforward computation of the shortest code length of the vector containing cluster structure based on the NML model requires polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters. We show that this is a tractable problem by introducing a recursion formula for the efficient computation of normalizing constant from the NML model. The time complexity of the new formula is linear opposed to previous polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters.

</details>


### [166] [Foundation models for electricity price forecasting and battery arbitrage: Can they replace market-specific forecasting models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00089)
*Arkadiusz Lipiecki,Rafał Weron*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文通过多市场、多指标对比，发现仅有TabPFN基础模型在统计精度上稳定超越传统电价预测基准，但其经济优势依赖于交易策略的风险容忍度，无法全面替代专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型宣称可在零样本下实现高精度预测，但在电价预测领域是否能真正取代专门设计的模型，缺乏系统性验证。

Method: 在德国、波兰和西班牙三个电力市场2021-2025年数据上，对五个基础模型家族共九个变体进行零样本评估，并与两个最先进电价预测基准比较，从点预测、概率预测精度及电池储能套利的经济价值三个维度衡量性能。

Result: 仅TabPFN模型在所有市场及统计指标上显著优于基准；但其统计优势并未直接转化为经济优势，TabPFN在无限投标和高风险分位数策略下表现最佳，而分布深度神经网络基准在低风险容忍度下收益更高。

Conclusion: 基础模型不能普遍替代市场专用模型，其适用性取决于模型架构与具体决策问题，统计精度与经济价值之间不存在简单对应关系。

Abstract: Foundation models promise accurate forecasts with little or no task-specific training, but whether they can replace models designed specifically for electricity price forecasting remains unclear. We compare nine variants from five foundation model families, evaluated in zero-shot mode, with two state-of-the-art electricity price forecasting benchmarks in Germany, Poland, and Spain over 2021-2025. Their performance is assessed in terms of point and probabilistic forecasting accuracy, as well as economic value in battery energy storage arbitrage. Only the TabPFN models consistently and significantly outperform the benchmarks across all three markets and all statistical measures. However, this statistical dominance does not translate directly into economic dominance: TabPFN performs best under unlimited bids and riskier quantile-based strategies, whereas the Distributional Deep Neural Network benchmark is more profitable when risk tolerance is lower. Thus, foundation models cannot universally replace market-specific models, and their value depends on both model architecture and the decision problem.

</details>


### [167] [Local Reference Geometry Residual Augmentation for Imbalanced Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00093)
*Chuanhang Qiu,Yanran Xu,Yue Wang,Anthony Bagnall*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为局部参考几何（LRG）的轻量级后处理特征增强模块，通过测量并修复类不平衡下少数类样本在特征空间中的局部几何失效问题，从而在不平衡时间序列分类中稳定提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不平衡分类方法主要针对训练分布、目标函数、逻辑输出或最终阈值进行干预，但忽略了表征层面的问题：即使全局类结构可用，少数类在局部特征邻域中仍可能因支持度降低而出现稀疏、被多数类主导或混合的情况，导致分类器在少数类区域不可靠。

Method: LRG模块位于固定特征提取器与分类头之间，仅使用训练特征：计算每个特征点周围的局部暴露度与类混合风险，并据此生成两类增强信息——从邻近训练几何中得到的标准化有符号位移，以及经LDA投影后的残差摘要，将二者拼接到原始特征上以补充局部判别信息。

Result: 在UCR/Bake Off Redux不平衡基准上，对多种表征（学习型、预训练型、固定型）均观察到原始特征与LRG增强特征的成对对比增益；与训练级干预及分类器校正方法兼容；消融实验表明增益主要来自附加有符号局部残差，而非原型距离、亲和特征、标量统计或VLAD编码；分析验证了局部几何失效假设：不平衡加剧少数类邻域暴露于多数类，训练局部风险可标示易错区域，且LRG增益集中在此类高风险区域。

Conclusion: LRG提供了一种无需重训练即可诊断并修复不平衡时间序列分类中局部特征空间失效的通用后处理方案，其有效性验证了局部几何失效假设，并为后续特征增强与鲁棒分类设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Imbalanced time series classification is often addressed by changing the training distribution, objective, logits, or final threshold. These interventions address important biases, yet leave a representation-level question unmeasured: after minority support is reduced, does a learned feature space remain locally reliable around minority regions? We identify a training-local geometry failure: under imbalance, minority cases can lie in sparse, rest-dominated, or mixed feature-space neighborhoods, even when the representation retains useful global class structure. To diagnose and repair this failure, we propose Local Reference Geometry (LRG), a lightweight post-hoc feature augmentation module applied between a fixed feature extractor and the classifier head. Using training features only, LRG measures local exposure and class-mixture risk, then augments each fixed feature with a standardized signed displacement from nearby training geometry and an LDA-projected residual summary. On controlled UCR/Bake Off Redux imbalance benchmarks, paired raw-versus-LRG comparisons show gains for learned, pretrained, and fixed representations, including when LRG is combined with training-level interventions and post-encoder classifier corrections. Ablations show that the gain comes from the signed local residual appended to the original feature, rather than from generic prototype distances, affinity features, scalar statistics, or VLAD-style codes. Further analyses support the proposed local-geometry failure hypothesis: minority neighborhoods become increasingly rest-exposed under imbalance, training-local risk identifies error-prone regions, and LRG gains concentrate in those high-risk regions.

</details>


### [168] [Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning for Goal-Directed Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00189)
*Shiyun Wa,Yifei Wang,Anna G. Green,Simone Sciabola,Ye Wang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出精英加权监督微调（EW-SFT），通过奖励选择高分分子并用模型原生损失更新，实现跨架构和任务的统一分子优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略梯度强化学习需依赖生成轨迹的对数概率，难以跨架构复用；监督微调虽简单但更新不包含奖励信号。

Method: 利用奖励指导精英选择高分分子，仅对选中集合使用模型自身预训练损失进行更新，不使用连续权重。

Result: 在3D形状比对、2D相似性和样本效率基准上，EW-SFT一致优于或媲美各原生优化器，且适用于多种生成器和设计任务。

Conclusion: EW-SFT无需轨迹级RL即可实现统一且高效的分子定向优化，具备跨架构、跨任务和跨评估器的通用性。

Abstract: Goal-directed optimization is essential for steering molecular generators to propose candidates with desired properties. However, it is often implemented with policy-gradient reinforcement learning, which requires a generation-trajectory log-probability whose form depends on the model architecture and generation procedure. This makes an optimizer difficult to reuse across architectures and conditional generative designs. Supervised fine-tuning needs none of that machinery, but its update is driven by a fixed dataset, so the reward never enters the update. We introduce Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning (EW-SFT), which uses reward to guide elite selection of high-scoring molecules, and updates the model by its own pretraining loss on that set. Ablations show that reward information is passed primarily through elite selection, rather than through continuous weighting within the selected set. Because the update consumes only scored molecules and the model's native loss, the same rule applies across autoregressive, masked-diffusion, and discrete-flow generators, and across de novo, motif-extension, and linker-design tasks. Under a fixed budget of 3D shape alignment oracle calls on two kinase reference compounds, EW-SFT consistently outperforms the corresponding native optimizers. It further improves goal-directed optimization under a 2D similarity oracle on four held-out references and achieves comparable performance on a sample-efficiency benchmark without a trajectory-level RL formulation. These results demonstrate that EW-SFT is a unified and effective optimizer across molecular generators, design constraints, references, and oracles.

</details>


### [169] [AI Should Not Only Be Helpful. It Should Be Contingent. Artificial Intimacy, Sycophancy, and the Future of Social Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00211)
*Scott Compton,Arjun Nagendran*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出将“偶然性”（contingency）作为评估对话式AI的核心构念，认为当前对齐方法（如RLHF）偏向用户认可和流畅性，导致非偶然的奉承性反馈，削弱AI在人际技能发展中的校准作用，并提出了偶然AI的框架与研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI深度嵌入社交环境，不仅提供信息，还影响人际反馈；现有评估和训练方法忽视反馈的行为信息性，可能损害用户特别是青少年的社会学习。

Method: 基于行为科学与社会学习理论，论证偶然反馈是人际技能发展的关键；提出偶然AI框架，包括基于轨迹的评估和社会后果预测模型。

Result: 尚无实验数据；主要贡献是概念框架和研究议程，识别了现有AI在反馈偶然性上的缺陷。

Conclusion: AI评估应从单纯用户满意度转向其对人类社会学习的影响，需跨学科协作以设计和评估促进适应性人际发展的AI系统。

Abstract: Conversational artificial intelligence is increasingly embedded in everyday social environments, where it functions as both an informational tool and a source of interpersonal feedback. This perspective introduces contingency, i.e., the degree to which system responses vary with user behavior and its interpersonal consequences, as a central construct for evaluating AI systems. We argue that current alignment approaches, including reinforcement learning from human feedback, tend to prioritize user approval and conversational fluency over behaviorally informative feedback, leading to sycophantic patterns of noncontingent affirmation.
  Drawing on behavioral science and social learning theory, we propose that contingent feedback is a key mechanism through which individuals develop interpersonal skills. When AI systems provide feedback weakly coupled to social consequences, they may reduce opportunities for adaptive calibration in real-world interactions, particularly during adolescence, a critical period for social development.
  We outline a framework for contingent AI, including trajectory-based evaluation and models of social consequence prediction, and propose a research agenda spanning developmental psychology, human-AI interaction, and machine learning. More broadly, we argue that AI systems should be evaluated not only by user satisfaction, but by their impact on human social learning.

</details>


### [170] [Neural means and kernel corrections for operator learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00389)
*Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本研究将神经网络与精确Matérn核回归相结合，在两个公开基准问题（结构力学和OCO-2辐射传输模拟）上取得了优于或匹配现有最佳方法的结果，并揭示了混合方法有效性的数学机制及数据固有属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经模拟器在低数据或特定问题上（如OCO-2）性能不足，而高斯过程在处理高维特征时受限。作者旨在通过结合二者优势，即利用神经网络特征提取能力与核回归的精确性，来解决现有方法的性能瓶颈和数据依赖问题。

Method: 方法包括：1) 对神经网络的输出残差及其学习特征分别应用精确Matérn核回归；2) 理论分析中，提出第二矩恒等式用于预测堆叠性能，建立最优恢复认证，并推导分布无关的覆盖带宽。实验上在结构力学和OCO-2两个公开基准上验证组合效果。

Result: 结构力学上达到4.55%测试误差（与最佳架构持平），低数据场景5.38%优于已发表的6.49%；OCO-2上在三波段中两波段超越已发表的高斯过程模拟器。发现核回归在原始状态上落后网络十倍，但在网络特征上反超，原因在于目标平方原生空间范数降低约40倍。当二者性能持平时，所有模型残差相关性超0.86，且共享成分在多样性和样本量上平坦，表明平台期源于数据本身。

Conclusion: 神经网络与核回归的混合策略有效提升模拟精度，尤其在低数据与高维问题上。方法失败或持平时，结果揭示误差主要受数据固有属性限制而非模型选择。理论工具（第二矩恒等式、覆盖带宽）为预测和不确定性量化提供了可靠手段，其中覆盖带宽是唯一通过所有检验的不确定性信号。

Abstract: We combine neural network means with exact Matérn kernel regressions of their residuals and of their learned features, and evaluate the pairing on two public emulation problems with published baselines: the structural-mechanics benchmark of de Hoop et al. and the OCO-2 radiative-transfer emulator of Lamminpää et al. On structural mechanics the combination reaches 4.55% test error, matching the best published architecture, and 5.38% against a published 6.49% in the low-data regime. On OCO-2 it improves on the published Gaussian-process emulator on that problem's own test points, outright on two of the three spectral bands; the same kernel that trails the network tenfold on the raw state overtakes it on the network's features, and we measure why (the target's squared native-space norm drops about fortyfold at fixed effective dimension) and prove the mechanism. Where the two families tie instead, the residuals of every architecture we train correlate above 0.86 and their shared component is flat in diversity and sample size, which reads the published plateau as a property of the data. Supporting results include a second-moment identity that predicts stacking outcomes from measured correlations, an optimal-recovery certificate, and a distribution-free coverage band, the only uncertainty signal that survives our tests.

</details>


### [171] [A Stable Aggregation Method for Quantum Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00356)
*Shanika Nanayakkara,Shiva Raj Pokhrel*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种自洽中点聚合方法，结合QoS感知客户端加权、圆形参数聚合和有界中点更新控制，以解决量子联邦学习在异构数据、通信噪声等条件下的不稳定问题，并在医疗和金融数据集上验证了稳定性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子联邦学习（QFL）在异构数据、不可靠通信、可变保真度、延迟及量子硬件噪声下聚合不稳定，且QNN参数为周期角度，传统欧氏平均无法刻画其内在动力学，亟需专用聚合方法。

Method: 设计自洽中点聚合方法，包含三部分：QoS感知的客户端加权、针对周期性参数的圆形聚合（circular parameter aggregation）、基于有界中点（bounded midpoint）的更新控制机制。

Result: 通过角度测试和IBM真实量子计算机实验验证，在医疗和金融数据集上的广泛评估显示：稳定性提升、波动性降低，同时保持有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: 所提自洽中点聚合方法能有效提升QFL在复杂现实条件下的稳定性和鲁棒性，为量子联邦学习在实际应用中提供可靠方案。

Abstract: Quantum federated learning (QFL) enables clients to train quantum neural network (QNN) models without sharing private data. We find that aggregation in QFL is unstable under heterogeneous data, unreliable communication, variable fidelity, latency, and quantum hardware noise. Moreover, QFL is non-trivially challenging because several QNN parameters are periodic angles, where Euclidean averaging often fails to capture the inherent dynamics. We develop a novel self-consistent midpoint aggregation method for stable QFL design and implementation. We combine QoS-aware client weighting, circular parameter aggregation, and bounded midpoint-based update control. We perform several angular tests and IBM real Quantum machines experiments for validation confirming our approach. Extensive evaluations and experiments on medical and financial datasets show improved stability, lower volatility, and competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [172] [AdaptNTK: Adaptive Uncertainty Quantification and Active Learning for Neural Network Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00488)
*Prajwal Ananth,Shuwen Yue*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出AdaptNTK框架，利用正则化马氏距离在经验神经正切核特征空间中估计不确定性，通过固定NTK特征实现顺序更新，减少批次冗余，在多个基准上实现高效主动学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化方法在计算成本与可靠性之间存在权衡，且批量获取时无法考虑冗余性，导致训练效率低下。

Method: AdaptNTK使用单一模型，在经验NTK特征空间中计算正则化马氏距离作为不确定性度量；由于获取期间NTK特征固定且不依赖标签，每次选择后可递归更新不确定性，无需重训练，从而减少批内冗余。

Result: 在rMD17上，Spearman相关系数0.68、Pearson 0.71，误差保留与三成员集成相当；主动学习实验中在rMD17和Transition-1X上取得最低力误差，尤其对过渡态构型表现优异；相比集成，每个Transition-1X周期加速2.6倍。

Conclusion: AdaptNTK提供高效的单模型不确定性估计与顺序更新机制，显著提升数据效率并降低计算成本，适用于主动学习驱动的分子动力学模拟。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials bridge the gap between quantum chemical precision and classical computational speed, enabling molecular dynamics simulations with first-principles accuracy. Their reliability is often improved through active learning, which iteratively expands the training set by identifying uncertain, out-of-distribution configurations. Existing uncertainty-quantification methods often involve a trade-off between computational cost and reliability, and generally cannot account for redundancy as an acquisition batch is assembled. Here, we introduce AdaptNTK, a single-model framework that measures uncertainty as a regularized Mahalanobis distance in empirical neural tangent kernel (NTK) feature space. With the NTK features fixed during acquisition, the uncertainty depends on the acquired configurations but not their reference labels. This allows the uncertainty to be updated recursively after each selection without retraining, reducing redundancy within an acquisition batch. On held-out rMD17 data, AdaptNTK achieves the highest mean correlations with force errors (Spearman 0.68, Pearson 0.71) and matches a three-member ensemble in error retention. In active learning experiments, AdaptNTK achieves the lowest force errors across rMD17 and Transition-1X, with particularly strong performance on transition-state configurations in Transition-1X. AdaptNTK provides a 2.6-fold speedup per Transition-1X cycle relative to the ensemble, providing efficient single-model uncertainty estimation with sequential updates for data-efficient active learning.

</details>


### [173] [Learning Task-Specific Antibody Representations via Function-Aware Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00518)
*Ayan Goel,Thomas A. Walton,Amirali Aghazadeh*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出功能感知掩码预训练方法，通过将掩码位置与抗体的功能先验（如IMGT注释或结构预测）对齐，在结构任务上提升14%，在CDR相关任务上提升5.9倍，并开发混合掩码策略平衡多种功能目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于MLM的抗体语言模型预训练很少利用掩码过程本身作为归纳偏置的来源，且仅优先掩码CDR只能改善结合相关预测，无法覆盖抗体多样的生物学功能。

Method: 引入功能感知掩码，根据特定功能先验（如IMGT注释或结构预测）设计掩码放置策略；同时提出混合掩码策略，集成多个先验以平衡结合、结构和生物物理目标。

Result: 针对性掩码策略在各自目标上表现显著提升：结构相关任务提升最高14%，CDR相关任务提升最高5.9倍；混合策略进一步在多功能轴上增强性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于信息的掩码放置是一种无需额外参数即可在抗体语言模型训练中施加功能归纳偏置的有效机制。

Abstract: Antibody-specific language models pretrained via masked language modeling (MLM) learn representations that are critical for downstream sequence design and property prediction tasks. Yet, the corruption process itself is rarely leveraged as a source of inductive bias during pretraining. While preferentially masking complementarity-determining regions (CDRs) improves binding-related predictions, antibodies possess diverse biological priors over a variety of functions. Herein, we introduce function-aware masking, a family of pretraining algorithms that align mask placement with specific functional priors (e.g., from IMGT annotations or structure predictions) to shape the learned representation space. We show that these specialist masking strategies significantly improve performance on their respective objectives, yielding up to a 14% gain on structure-related tasks and up to a 5.9x improvement on CDR-related tasks. To further improve performance across multiple functional axes, we develop hybrid masking strategies that integrate multiple priors, balancing reconstruction over binding, structural, and biophysical objectives. Our results demonstrate that informed mask placement provides a parameter-free mechanism for imposing functional inductive biases in antibody language model training.

</details>


### [174] [Wave Function Backpropagation with Explicit Temporal-Interval Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00503)
*Byunggu Yu,Justin Kim*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出波动函数反向传播（WFB），用可学习的波参数（幅度、波数、角频率、相位）编码时间间隔，在前馈轨迹预测中比原FFN降低ADE达20.4%，并验证波动表示有效性而非间隔对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络依赖仿射变换，时间常作为辅助特征或假设均匀采样，缺乏对时间间隔的结构化建模能力。

Method: WFB通过可微时空波的相位关联状态与时间间隔Δt，推导标准梯度及基于波响应拉普拉斯的空间曲率校正，并在前馈轨迹预测器上实现。

Result: 带运动特征时，真实间隔STD-WFB比原始FFN降低ADE 20.4%；纯位置评估中降低10.4%，且比显式Δt的匹配FFN低2.1%；打乱间隔WFB取得最低平均ADE。

Conclusion: WFB是可行的结构化前馈学习范式，但证据支持波表示有效性而非间隔对齐，为后续架构研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Conventional neural networks learn predominantly through affine transformations followed by nonlinear activations, while elapsed time is often treated as an auxiliary feature or assumed to be uniformly sampled. This paper introduces Wave Function Backpropagation (WFB), a wave-parameterized learning formulation in which neural responses are represented by learnable amplitude, wavenumber, angular frequency, and phase. The formulation associates an observed state with its temporal interval Delta t through the phase of a differentiable spatiotemporal wave. We derive standard WFB gradients and a spatial-curvature correction based on the Laplacian of the wave response. WFB is instantiated in a deliberately feed-forward trajectory predictor to provide a controlled proof of concept; sequence learning is outside the scope of the present evaluation. With motion features, STD-WFB using real intervals reduces average displacement error (ADE) by 20.4% relative to the original FFN baseline. In a new position-only evaluation that removes temporal leakage through precomputed velocity and acceleration, real-interval WFB reduces ADE by 10.4% relative to the original FFN and remains competitive with parameter-matched ReLU controls, obtaining 2.1% lower mean ADE than the matched FFN with explicit Delta t. Shuffled-interval WFB attains the lowest mean ADE, indicating that the present evidence supports the effectiveness of the wave representation but does not attribute the gain to interval alignment. These results establish WFB as a viable structured feed-forward learning formulation and define a clear basis for subsequent architectural studies.

</details>


### [175] [CoVer: Conflict-Aware Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00508)
*Shuning Zhang,Dai Shi,Bohao Chu,Hui Wang,Yuwei Chuai,Yifan Wang,Jingruo Chen,Simin Li,Xin Yi,Hewu Li*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出社交媒体事实核查基准ContraNote与三阶段核查框架CoVer，分别解决证据冲突与聚合冲突，性能优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体事实核查面临证据层面和聚合层面的冲突，错误证据常伪装成权威新闻源，现有数据集与模型难以有效处理双重冲突。

Method: 构建来自X社区笔记的大规模数据集ContraNote（33,686条用于证据冲突，54,474条用于聚合优先），并设计CoVer三阶段流程：证据模式归一化、事实共识与支持验证，优先处理证据抑制噪声干扰。

Result: CoVer在ContraNote上冲突任务达86.0%准确率及68.0%宏F1，聚合任务达88.5%准确率及88.5宏F1；在CONFACT-HumC和CONFACT-ModC上分别获88.4%和89.4%准确率。

Conclusion: CoVer结合ContraNote能有效解决证据与聚合层面的冲突，为社交媒体事实核查提供可靠技术方案。

Abstract: Social media fact-checking has long been challenged by evidence-level and aggregation-level conflicts, where erroneous evidence mimics authoritative news sources. To capture this challenge and support conflict verification tasks, we present ContraNote, a large-scale real-world dataset curated from X's Community Notes system. It includes 33,686 posts for evaluating evidence-level conflict resolution, and 54,474 instances for evaluating aggregation-level prioritization. Additionally, we propose CoVer, a factual adjudication framework with three-stage pipelines: evidence schema normalization, factual consensus and support verification. This prioritizes evidence over noise to prevent it from compromising the final verdict. Technical evaluations show that CoVer achieves strong performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines across ContraNote (86.0% Acc., 68.0% mac. F1, 64.5 bal. Acc. on Conflict; and 88.5% Acc., 88.5 mac. F1 and 89.2 bal. Acc. on Prioritization), CONFACT-HumC (88.4% Acc.) and CONFACT-ModC (89.4% Acc.).

</details>


### [176] [Consistency Without Alignment: Item-Sensitive Language Models Indistinguishable From Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00576)
*Cris Huynh*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文通过一个从桌游《Deception: Murder in Hong Kong》抽象出的强制选择信号任务，证明模型在选项上的敏感性（item-sensitivity）虽是其任务能力的必要条件，但远非充分条件；在21个模型-评分规则组合中，有8个与随机选择无异，5个甚至劣于随机，从而提出“无对齐的一致性”（consistency without alignment）概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估常以模型输出是否随输入变化（如item-sensitivity、置换一致性、自一致性）作为任务能力的证据。作者质疑该逻辑，认为若缺乏独立的参考标准，这类一致性指标可能误导；他们希望展示即使模型表现出一致性，也可能与真正的任务对齐无关，从而警示评估方法的缺陷。

Method: 采用一个可闭式求解的强制选择信号任务，环境中参考策略（适应最大化、后验最大化、均匀随机）可精确计算；测试了7个语言模型、2个模型家族、一种后训练消融，以及3种独立评分规则。同时对比无语用的字面相似性基线，并尝试在基线之上添加语用层，还评估了标准多选题格式的信息含量。

Result: 所有21个模型-规则组合均可靠地表现出item-sensitivity，但其中8个与忽略输入随机选择的模型在统计上无差异，5个在描述目标上比随机更差；item-sensitivity与随机距离的相关性仅为r=0.30。字面相似性基线（无语用）超过多数模型；添加语用层反而使选择趋近随机；标准多选题格式未携带可测量的内容信号。

Conclusion: 作者认为这种“无对齐的一致性”可能普遍存在于依赖item-sensitivity或自一致性且缺乏独立参考的评估中，因此此类证据不足以证明任务能力；呼吁使用具有可计算参考基准的评估范式，并指出人类对照实验尚待完成。

Abstract: Item-sensitivity, defined as whether a model's choice depends on the specific input rather than on its own output prior, is widely reported as evidence of task competence. We show this evidence is necessary but not sufficient using a forced-choice signalling task abstracted from the board game Deception: Murder in Hong Kong. In this environment, the reference points against which a coordinate should be judged (a fit-maximising strategy, a posterior-maximising strategy, and uniform random selection) are all computable in closed form. Across seven language models, two model families, a post-training ablation, and three independent scoring rules, every one of 21 model-by-rule cells is reliably item-sensitive. Yet 8 of those 21 cells are not statistically distinguishable from a chooser that ignores the item and selects at random, and 5 score worse than random at describing the target. Item-sensitivity and distance from random correlate at only r = 0.30. We call this consistency without alignment and argue it generalises to any evaluation that relies on item-sensitivity, permutation consistency, or self-consistency without an independent reference for the measured quantity. We further find that a literal-similarity baseline with no pragmatics outperforms most tested language models, that adding a pragmatic layer over two baseline similarity sources moves choosers toward random rather than toward the Bayesian reference, and that a standard labelled multiple-choice format carries no measurable content signal here. All results represent the model side of a pre-registered instrument; a matched human condition is designed and piloted but not yet collected.

</details>


### [177] [Frozen Cores Need Task Signal: Fisher-Whitened Cross-Covariance for Low-Resource LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00762)
*Wentao Ye,Zhanming Shen,Zhiqing Xiao,Yao Ding,Haobo Wang,Gang Chen*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出FCCA方法，在冻结核心适配的固定子空间下，通过白化截断选择最优基，以极小参数量接近LoRA/DoRA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调通常只关注更新参数数量，但在严格预算下，可训练参数的位置同等关键。冻结核心适配移除了可训练因子修复劣质初始子空间的能力，使子空间质量可直接观测，从而研究固定子空间选择的影响。

Method: FCCA估计符号化输入-误差交叉协方差，用对角Fisher矩白化，在局部度量中截断，将选中方向映射回原空间，再用薄QR获得稳定核心坐标。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-3B上FCCA达83.0宏平均，比次优方法高2.3点，在所有11任务上超过未白化的RawGrad对照；在三个Qwen尺度上均排名第一，在Llama-3.2-1B上与最优方法仅差0.13点。消融显示白化带来2.7-17.2点提升，QR对稳定优化必要。

Conclusion: 精心选择的固定子空间能以远小于可移动低秩因子的可训练参数量和优化器状态代价，恢复其大部分性能优势，证明子空间选择在参数高效微调中的关键作用。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning is usually framed as a question of how many parameters to update. Under a severe trainable-state budget, however, where those coefficients act is equally consequential. We study this choice through frozen-core adaptation: a calibration pass fixes left and right bases for each weight matrix, and fine-tuning optimizes only an $r\times r$ core. This removes the ability of trainable factors to repair a poor initial span and makes subspace quality directly observable. We introduce FCCA, which estimates the signed input--error cross-covariance, whitens it with diagonal Fisher moments, truncates it in the resulting local metric, maps the selected directions back, and applies thin QR to obtain stable core coordinates. Under a matched $r^2$ budget, we compare eight basis constructors on 11 tasks, four model settings, and three seeds. On Qwen2.5-3B, FCCA reaches an 83.0 macro-average, 2.3 points above the next-best matched-budget constructor, and exceeds its unwhitened RawGrad control on all 11 tasks. It ranks first at all three Qwen scales and finishes within 0.13 points of the best method on Llama-3.2-1B. Controlled ablations show gains of 2.7--17.2 points from whitening and identify QR as necessary for stable core optimization in the tested regime. Finally, FCCA comes within 0.32 and 0.23 average points of LoRA and DoRA while optimizing 36.9K rather than roughly 7.4M parameters. These results show that a carefully selected fixed span can recover most of the benefit of movable low-rank factors at a much smaller trainable and optimizer-state cost.

</details>


### [178] [SciTrue: Reliable Scientific Claim Validation with Frontier and Open Language Models at the NTCIR SciClaimEval Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00654)
*Qiming Bao,Neşet Özkan Tan,Siyuan Wang,Mark Gahegan*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: SciTrue团队在NTCIR-19 SciClaimEval两个子任务中，通过基准测试11个多模态模型并结合无泄漏的先验配对策略，在四个评测组合中三项第一、一项并列第一，并发现任务配对结构是最大性能杠杆，多数残差源于标签噪声或不可见映射错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证科学声明需结合论文图表，现有方法未系统比较前沿模型，且任务存在标签泄露风险；团队旨在通过诚实协议和透明后处理，探究模型上限与数据缺陷，而非盲目调优单一模型。

Method: 采用逐样本统一协议评测11个前沿及开源多模态模型，结合轻量后处理；提出基于声明文本的无泄漏配对先验（恢复支持/反驳配对并给高置信证据赋支持标签），并辅以微调、蒸馏和代理一致性检查；全程审计数据打包中的标签泄露。

Result: 子任务1配对准确率从72.2提升至93.5，子任务2达97.7；优于最强公开基线o4-mini，Claude Opus 4.8和Gemma-4-31B超过基线；官方盲测排行榜四项组合中三项第一、一项并列第一。

Conclusion: 强指令模型已具竞争力，但任务配对结构带来的增益远超模型替换；剩余错误多源于不可见标签映射或数据集噪声，表明实际可建模提升空间有限，测量准确率低估了真实能力；需警惕数据打包导致的标签泄露问题。

Abstract: We describe the SciTrue team's participation in both subtasks of the NTCIR-19 SciClaimEval task~\cite{sciclaimeval}, which asks systems to verify scientific claims against the tables and figures of a paper. Rather than tuning a single model, we benchmark eleven frontier and open multimodal models under one honest, per-sample protocol and combine them with light, transparent post-processing. On the official, blind test leaderboard (Section~\ref{sec:results}), SciTrue placed first by a clear margin in three of the four evidence-category/subtask combinations, and tied for first on the primary metric in the fourth. Three findings explain the result. First, strong instruction-tuned models are already competitive: Claude Opus~4.8 and Gemma-4-31B each exceed the strongest public baseline (o4-mini), and GPT-5.5 and Claude Fable~5 lead both subtasks (97.7 on Subtask~2). Second, the task's pairing structure is the largest lever: a \emph{leak-free pair prior} that recovers the Supported/Refuted pairing from the claim text alone (a visible field) and assigns Supported to the higher-confidence evidence raises Subtask-1 pair-accuracy from 72.2 to 93.5, far more than any model swap or ensemble weighting. Third, a case-by-case audit finds that most residual errors are visually-undetectable label-mapping swaps or dataset label noise, so measured accuracy understates the true ability and the fixable-by-modeling headroom is small. Controlled fine-tuning, distillation, and agentic consistency-checking support the same conclusions, and we document throughout a measurement leak---label information reaching a system through the packaging of the data rather than its content---in which the released file ordering encodes the label, including one instance that briefly misled our own pipeline.

</details>


### [179] [iPINN for Broadband CARS Phase Retrieval: A Framework for Function Approximation and Inverse Modeling Problems in Nonlinear Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00883)
*Ravi Teja Vulchi,Carl Messerschmidt,Mohammadsadegh Vafaeinezhad,Rajendhar Junjuri,Tobias Meyer-Zedler,Juergen Popp,Thomas Bocklitz*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种逆物理信息神经网络（iPINN），通过可微分的解析前向模型从原始BCARS光谱预测洛伦兹峰参数，实现对共振磁化率虚部的鲁棒相位恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCARS中的相位恢复是一个病态逆问题，非共振背景在不同采集条件下变化，且与共振信号相干混合，导致传统直接谱回归方法难以在不同采集条件下保持准确性。

Method: iPINN结合Transformer编码器将光谱特征分配给24个可学习的峰槽，并通过可微分的解析前向模型重建共振磁化率。训练中引入多视图一致性损失，以增强对NRB模式、强度和噪声的不变性。

Result: 在公共基准上，iPINN达到最低误差（MAE 0.016，优于次优0.046）。在跨7种溶剂和4个焦点位置的28个零样本测试中，5/7种溶剂的准确性与深度无关。

Conclusion: 结合可微分物理解码器的逆参数预测方法能够在不同测量条件下实现鲁棒的相位恢复，为BCARS光谱分析提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Phase retrieval in broadband coherent anti-Stokes Raman spectroscopy (BCARS) is an ill-posed inverse problem. The Raman-like signal is encoded in the imaginary part of the resonant susceptibility, which mixes coherently with a non-resonant background (NRB) that varies across acquisitions. We introduce an inverse physics-informed neural network (iPINN) that predicts Lorentzian peak parameters from raw BCARS spectra and reconstructs the resonant susceptibility through a differentiable analytical forward model. A transformer encoder assigns spectral features to 24 learnable peak slots, and a multi-view consistency loss enforces invariance across NRB pattern, NRB strength, and noise. Unlike direct spectral regression approaches, the method retains accuracy under varying acquisition conditions. On a public benchmark, iPINN achieves the lowest error among the tested baselines (MAE 0.016 vs. next-best 0.046). On 28 zero-shot test spectra acquired across seven solvents and four focal positions, accuracy is depth-invariant in five of seven solvents. These results show that inverse parametric prediction with a differentiable physical decoder supports robust phase retrieval across measurement conditions.

</details>


### [180] [From Truncation to Commitment: Persistent Context in Uniform Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01043)
*Satoshi Hayakawa*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的采样方法CRS，在离散扩散模型中将已选定的argmax token作为持久上下文输入后续采样步骤，以改善生成质量与多样性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行离散扩散模型虽允许多轮修订，但每一步选择不形成持久影响；而实际中选中的假设常作为后续预测的条件，因此需研究在采样过程中持久化已选token的效果。

Method: CRS（committed reveal sampling）：在反向采样过程中，将每步选定的argmax token存储，并插入到后续模型输入中，无需重新训练。

Result: 在Duo蒸馏模型上，CRS（无top-p截断）在8-64次函数评估预算下生成困惑度（GenPPL）低于固定p=0.95/0.9的基线；64 NFE时在匹配unigram熵下也有更低GenPPL，且GenPPL-熵权衡更优。

Conclusion: 支持截断（top-p）与持久上下文是控制生成质量-多样性权衡的两种不同手段，CRS可提供更优权衡，且无需训练。基Duo模型上也观察一致趋势，但其他多样性指标可能排名不同。

Abstract: Uniform-state discrete diffusion models update all tokens in parallel while keeping every position revisable. Even when the commonly used top-$p$ rule leaves only one candidate at a position, that choice affects only the current reverse step and can be revised at the next sampling step. We ask what changes when selected hypotheses instead become persistent context for later predictions. We therefore propose committed reveal sampling (CRS), a training-free sampler that stores selected argmax tokens and inserts them into subsequent model inputs. Our analysis gives a rationale for selecting later and for keeping selected tokens visible. Under the exact forward process, the Bayes error of selecting a clean token cannot increase as noise decreases, while in a simple latent-mode model, keeping the selected token visible helps later parallel predictions agree on the same sequence-level choice. Empirically, paired experiments on Duo-distilled then separate this persistent effect from single-step top-$p$ restriction and scalar temperature scaling. Under the same finalization rule, CRS without top-$p$ truncation reaches lower generative perplexity (GenPPL) than fixed $p=0.95$ and $p=0.9$ baselines across budgets of 8--64 function evaluations (NFE). At 64 NFE, the comparison at matched unigram entropy also gives lower GenPPL for CRS, yielding a more favorable GenPPL--entropy tradeoff. Base Duo shows the same direction in a descriptive comparison, while other diversity and continuation metrics can rank these operating points differently. These results identify support restriction and persistent context as distinct controls of that tradeoff.

</details>


### [181] [Automated Tree Knowledge Graph Construction using Ontology Expansion and Retrieval from Vietnamese History Textbooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00763)
*Ket Doan Nguyen,Minh N. H. Nguyen*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出面向越南语等低资源语言的层级知识图谱自动构建与检索评估端到端流程，图遍历策略在越南历史问答中NDCG@10较向量基线提升4.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层级KG-RAG缺乏低资源语言的自动本体扩展构建方法，且未系统评估利用层级结构的检索策略。

Method: 三阶段混合关系抽取（Union-Find批内去重、近似跨批搜索、质心过滤+五步双LLM验证），构建不可合并结构节点与可合并内容节点的双层架构；评估Top-Down/Horizontal/Bottom-Up三种图遍历策略。

Result: 从400页越南语历史教科书构建750节点、4341条语义边，本体仅从40类扩至41类；Top-Down策略NDCG@10较向量基线高4.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 树结构信息超越平面余弦相似度价值，但查询无需结构上下文时反而损害性能。

Abstract: Hierarchical Knowledge graph (KG)-based retrieval augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a powerful approach for supporting large language models with structured knowledge. However, there are primary challenges: (i) the lack of methods for automatic KG construction using ontology expansion for low-resource languages such as Vietnamese, (ii) the absence of systematic evaluation for knowledge retrieval strategies leveraging the hierarchical structures. In this paper, we propose an end-to-end pipeline for KG construction and retrieval strategies evaluation. In the KG construction, we employ a three-phase hybrid relation extraction pipeline: intra-batch deduplication via Union-Find, approximate cross-batch search, and LLM extraction with a centroid filter that reduces prompts combined with a five-step dual-LLM validator to prevent bloated ontology. A two-tier architecture consists of unmergeable structural nodes to preserve the document structure and mergeable content nodes. The retrieval evaluation consists of three graph traversal strategies: Top-Down, Horizontal, and Bottom-Up, which are evaluated on a synthetically generated benchmark of 1,210 Vietnamese queries from 109 subgraphs, categorized by five query directions. In this paper, we construct the tree knowledge graph from Vietnamese high school History textbooks (nearly 400 pages) to produce 750 nodes and 4,341 semantic edges with controlled ontology growth from 40 to 41 types. Among experimental graph traversal strategies, the Top-Down strategy with structure surpasses the vector baseline by 4.7 percentage points in NDCG@10. As a result, tree-structural information provides valuable information beyond flat cosine similarity but degrades performance when the query does not require structural context.

</details>


### [182] [StudyBench: Can Self-Evolution Squeeze Textbooks for Olympiad Capability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00787)
*Yinghao Chen,Zixi Chen,Bingxiang He,Ziqing Qiao,Huan-ang Gao,Yinuo Xu,Yuxin Zuo,Zeyuan Liu,Yuhao Zhan,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出StudyBench基准，直接衡量自进化方法将训练数据转化为解题能力的效率，发现现有方法在知识吸收与迁移上存在显著差距，且遭遇计算平台期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想的自进化方法应像人类一样仅从少量优质教材中学习即可掌握学科并解决难题，但现有评估缺乏直接衡量该能力的指标。

Method: 构建受控物理基准StudyBench，包含应用集（困难教材题，测吸收）与迁移集（奥林匹克题，测迁移）；对多种自进化方法在三个基座模型上评测，并设计引导消融实验。

Result: 应用集提升难以迁移至更难的迁移集；引导消融揭示‘引导差距’，最强方法仅能解锁部分同材料作为上下文引导时的收益；所有方法遭遇‘计算平台期’，在预算耗尽前即饱和。

Conclusion: 剩余性能差距本质是方法缺陷而非数据或计算不足；StudyBench将自进化研究转化为可量化目标，代码已开源。

Abstract: Humans need to study only a handful of well-written textbooks to master a discipline and attempt its hardest problems. We argue that an ideal self-evolution method should share the same property, that is autonomously learning from raw training material for transferable problem-solving capability. However, we still lack a direct measurement for it. We introduce StudyBench, a controlled physics benchmark that directly measures how efficiently a self-evolution method converts training material into capability. We organise the test set into an Application Set, consisting of difficult textbook problems and evaluating absorption ability, and a Transfer Set, consisting of olympiad-level problems and evaluating transfer ability. Benchmarking representative self-evolution methods across three base models, we find that improvements on the Application Set rarely translate to the harder Transfer Set. A guidance ablation exposes a Guidance Gap: even the strongest method closes only a small fraction of what the same material unlocks when supplied as in-context guidance. Besides, every method hits a Compute Plateau, saturating well before exhausting its compute budget. The remaining gap is therefore a method problem rather than a data or compute problem. By offering a clean and controlled benchmark, StudyBench turns self-evolution progress from an open-ended pursuit into a measurable target for future research. Our code is released at https://github.com/thunlp/StudyBench.

</details>


### [183] [Pre-carved Niches: The Formation Dynamics of Modular Task Partitions in Early LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01170)
*Guangqi Li,Yongxin Li*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本研究首次逐步骤追踪了大语言模型模块化组织的形成过程，发现模块化映射在训练前已预刻（pre-carved），随后通过两次尖锐跃迁锁定分区，且梯度层面的相对剥夺现象是分区的关键驱动，但不影响权重更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅刻画了完成训练的模型，对模块化组织如何在训练中逐步形成这一问题缺乏认识，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从零训练Pythia-410M模型（两条轨迹：bf16和fp32），在每一步进行归因修补（attribution patching），同时结合梯度范数、有效更新、权重范数及一阶损失分解探针，覆盖14个任务和4个认知域。

Result: 三大发现：1）模块化映射在训练前已预刻，主导任务对的重叠度为基线的3.6倍，且第0层集中度是架构级常数；2）分区通过两次尖锐跃迁锁定（第二次达20.4西格玛静默窗/6.2西格玛全局），伴随梯度供给的相对剥夺（胜者获得败者2.25~2.73倍的梯度，偏离随机对照9.5~11.5西格玛），但该效应未传递至权重更新；3）对基底的偏离仅出现在正在学习的领域，支持模块化随学习变化。

Conclusion: 研究区分了可辩护的特征级解释与无法回答的机制性问题，并预注册了正在进行的2.8B实验中关于规模阈值的假设。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit a modular internal organization that mirrors well-studied functional networks of the human brain, but how this organization forms during training is unknown: prior work has characterized finished models, not the formation process. We track formation step by step: we train a Pythia-410M model from scratch (two trajectories, bf16 and fp32) and run attribution patching at every step, alongside probes for gradient norms, effective updates, weight norms, and first-order loss decomposition across 14 tasks in four cognitive domains. Three findings. First, the modular map is pre-carved: before any learning, the dominant task pair already overlaps at ~3.6x the attribution substrate (a task-independent baseline), and its layer-0 concentration is an architecture-level constant on this model family. Second, the partition locks in through two sharp jumps whose amplitudes do not track the learning-rate schedule (the second reaching 20.4 sigma quiet-window / 6.2 sigma global), accompanied by gradient-level relative deprivation--winners receive 2.25->2.73x the loser's gradient supply, 9.5-11.5 standard deviations below a random control--that does not propagate to updates or weights. Third, deviation from the substrate appears only in the domain being learned, consistent with the hypothesis that modularity tracks learning. We close by separating the feature-level account we can defend from the mechanistic questions we cannot, and we pre-register the scale-threshold hypothesis behind our ongoing 2.8B experiments.

</details>


### [184] [REFACTOR-VLA: Unsupervised Library Learning of Typed Motor Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01215)
*Riyaaz Shaik,Chandru Venkataraman*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: REFACTOR-VLA通过行为等价核与类型化lambda演算，在睡眠/觉醒循环中从世界模型rollout中提取可复用技能，并在LIBERO上通过对比损失显著提升聚类质量，优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型是单体式的，直接输出原始动作，缺乏行为抽象，导致长时程任务性能下降且难以解释；已有技能发现方法未解决动作序列行为等价性问题，或依赖未校准的语言模型。

Method: 睡眠阶段使用行为等价核（BEK）从潜世界模型rollout中聚类动作片段；觉醒阶段生成类型化lambda项并由库条件整流流解码器消费；抽象需通过最小描述长度和返回保持门控，并使用InfoNCE辅助损失改进世界模型预热。

Result: 在LIBERO上，增大世界模型参数（188M→430M）在4/4套件上变差；添加InfoNCE损失使NMI在物体、空间、目标、LIBERO-10上达0.462、0.867、0.915、0.754，平均超基线+0.184；跨12个种子的95%置信区间为[0.683, 0.729]。

Conclusion: 训练目标比模型容量更关键；对比损失显著提升睡眠阶段聚类，产生首个真实LIBERO任务语言库，解码器可用部分抽象并重写全部演示。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action (VLA) models -- OpenVLA, $π_0$, RT-2, RDT-1B -- are monolithic: they emit raw motor commands or short action chunks without organizing behavior into reusable abstractions, so they degrade on long-horizon tasks and resist interpretation. Existing skill-discovery methods sidestep the core question of when two action sequences are behaviorally equivalent, either clustering contrastive embeddings or delegating the judgment to a language model uncalibrated to the robot's dynamics. We introduce REFACTOR-VLA, a wake/sleep system for learning reusable skills. Its sleep phase clusters motor-program fragments under a Behavioral-Equivalence Kernel (BEK) computed from rollouts of a learned latent world model $M_φ$; its wake phase emits typed lambda terms over a Hindley--Milner-inspired vocabulary, consumed by a library-conditioned rectified-flow action decoder. Abstractions are admitted only if they pass Minimum Description Length and return-preservation gates. On LIBERO we report two findings. First, enlarging the world model from 188M to 430M parameters worsened performance on 4 of 4 suites, so capacity alone does not help. Second, the training objective matters far more: adding an auxiliary supervised contrastive (InfoNCE) loss during world-model warmup substantially improves sleep-phase clustering, giving Normalized Mutual Information at $n=3$ seeds of $0.462 \pm 0.021$ (object), $0.867 \pm 0.025$ (spatial), $0.915 \pm 0.013$ (goal) and $0.754 \pm 0.010$ (LIBERO-10), and beating the strongest published baseline on all 4 suites by a mean $Δ= +0.184$. Across providers ($n=12$) the 95% bootstrap confidence interval for mean pairwise NMI is $[0.683, 0.729]$ (mean $0.705$). The sleep phase also yields the first real-LIBERO task-language library: the decoder uses 2 of 3 admitted abstractions and rewrites all 256 sampled demonstrations.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond the Clock: Measuring the Value of Adaptive Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00874)
*Ayushi Chadha*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 在层级潜在推理器中，学习到的元级控制（何时保留或替换承诺）虽能产生状态依赖的时序策略，但均未超越最优固定时序策略；状态依赖性与决策价值可分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统日益复合化，高层控制器需决定是否修订低层策略，但目前缺乏对元级控制中状态依赖与任务价值关系的系统研究。

Method: 使用预训练层级潜在推理器，比较学习时序策略与强制时序策略，并通过干预研究、穷举枚举及反事实PERSIST/REPLAN诊断分析时序的影响与价值来源。

Result: 不同种子产生策略差异大，但学习策略均不优于最优固定时序；干预证明时序显著且顺序敏感；固定时序捕获大部分可测价值；反事实分析显示分数预测性由决策位置主导时，得分证据会误导。

Conclusion: 元级控制评估应分离三个维度：分数是否依赖状态、状态依赖是否改变行为、行为变化是否超越非自适应策略带来价值。

Abstract: As agentic systems become compound systems, increasingly important decisions move above task execution itself: when should a higher-level controller preserve the strategy guiding another process, and when should it revise it? We study this meta-level control problem in a hierarchical latent reasoner whose manager can retain or replace a commitment governing lower-level computation. Across three precommitted training seeds, learned revision timing produces qualitatively different policies, ranging from an almost deterministic early clock to substantially more state conditioned schedule distributions, yet none outperforms the best forced timing policy evaluated on the same frozen checkpoint. This separates state dependence from decision value: a controller can vary its actions with internal state without turning that variation into a reproducible task-performance benefit. A deeper intervention study on the original checkpoint shows that timing itself is consequential and order-sensitive, while exhaustive enumeration reveals that a strong fixed schedule captures most of the measurable value available from timing at this decision budget. Counterfactual PERSIST/REPLAN diagnostics further show why score-level evidence can be misleading when predictability is dominated by decision position rather than within-position discrimination. Together, these results argue that learned meta-level control should be evaluated along three separate axes: whether its score depends on state, whether that dependence changes realized behavior, and whether those changes capture outcome value beyond a strong non-adaptive policy.

</details>


### [186] [Multi-Head Self Attention is a Parameter Identification Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01231)
*W. Ross Morrow*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 通过参数辨识视角，证明多头注意力中未辨识参数比例随头数增加从1/2降至1/(2H)，且注意力永远无法完全辨识；部分偏置项在softmax注意力中无效；RoPE和GQA等改进能提高'有效'参数比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Transformer架构改进（如多头、RoPE、GQA）提供纯数学/统计上的统一解释——参数辨识性，而非仅依赖经验性能提升。

Method: 理论推导注意力参数辨识性，分析单头与多头情形；讨论偏置项无效性；从辨识角度评估RoPE和GQA；用数值实验展示训练更新落入不可辨识子空间，并设计'rebalancing'方法来修正这类更新（仅作验证，不主张采用）。

Result: 未辨识参数比例与头数成反比；注意力和部分偏置存在结构性不可辨识；RoPE和GQA进一步改善可辨识参数比例；数值实验确认训练会与不可辨识子空间重叠，'rebalancing'可修复但并非推荐方案。

Conclusion: 参数辨识性为Transformer架构选择提供了理论依据：更多头数、RoPE、GQA等改进本质上增强了模型的结构可辨识性，从而可能提升性能与效率。

Abstract: We prove that a multi-head scaled dot product attention can be viewed as a parameter identification strategy. The ratio of unidentified parameters to the total number of parameters scales like the reciprocal of the number of heads ($1/2 \to 1/(2H)$), meaning models with more heads are structurally more identified. A subtle side effect of the mathematics observation that attention can never be fully identified. Similarly we also show that some bias terms can have no effect on softmax-based attention layers in both the single- and multiple-head settings, though this is mostly a curiosity that should have a marginal effect on model size and model training/prediction efficiency. We also touch on modern improvements to transformers including RoPE and GQA from this perspective, illustrating how those as well can improve the ratio of ``meaningful'' parameters to all parameters. Simple numerical examples demonstrate that training can indeed involve updates that overlap model-invariant subspaces that arise from a lack of identification. As part of our experiments we use a ``rebalancing'' approach that can ``fix'' updates that overlap unindentified subspaces but do not try to present evidence this should actually be adopted. Instead we simply view our numerical results as exploring and confirming the theoretical results. As a whole we discuss a purely mathematical/statistical explanation, identification, for why specific architectural choices in transformers may have improved performance.

</details>


### [187] [Post-Training Science for Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01244)
*Charles O'Neill,Mudith Jayasekara,Harry Partridge*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文通过统一扫描框架，在多种模型、数据集和微调方式下系统测量了超参数选择的影响，为SFT实践提供了经验性指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每次监督微调都需要重新摸索学习率、批量大小、LoRA配置等超参数，缺乏跨模型和数据集的可迁移规则，导致资源浪费和效果不稳定。

Method: 设计单因素扫描实验，覆盖Qwen3和Llama（含密集和MoE架构）、四个真实客户SFT数据集、LoRA和全量微调，以客户评估为准绳，系统考察学习率、批量大小、LoRA秩和alpha、验证损失、模型规模、训练轮数及优化器选择的影响。

Result: 发现最优学习率与模型规模和数据特征相关但存在可迁移规则；LoRA与全量微调存在权衡，其秩和alpha影响可学习能力；验证损失能较好排序下游质量；收益随规模和数据量变化；存在最佳训练轮数；几何感知优化器相对AdamW有潜力。

Conclusion: 研究提供了具体超参数推荐及不确定性度量，有助于减少重复调参成本，并揭示模型、数据和微调方式间的交互规律。

Abstract: Every supervised fine-tuning run forces the same chain of decisions, such as learning rate, batch size, LoRA or full fine-tuning, how many epochs, which optimiser, and what data to feed the model. Each of these is typically rediscovered from scratch for every new model and dataset. Here we measure them under one instrument: a sweep that varies one lever at a time, and spans dense and mixture-of-experts models in two families (Qwen3 and Llama), on four real-world customer SFT datasets, for both LoRA and full fine-tuning. These datasets give a controlled testbed: each task carries an evaluation built with the customer, and its training data is produced by iterative supervised fine-tuning that refines model outputs until they pass that evaluation, so the supervised target is internally consistent and the task judge we report against is the criterion the data was built to satisfy. We ask how the optimal learning rate and batch size move with model scale, family, and data, and whether one selection rule transfers across them; what LoRA trades against full fine-tuning, and how its rank and alpha set what the adapter can learn; whether validation loss (or other metrics, such as loss landscape flatness) faithfully ranks downstream quality; whether post-training gains scale with model size and data volume, on a model ladder extended through mixtures-of-experts to 235B parameters; how many epochs to train before general instruction-following erodes; and whether a geometry-aware optimiser improves on AdamW. Each recommendation is paired with a measure of its uncertainty.

</details>


### [188] [Denoising Diffusion Generative Models Secretly Calculate Attentions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00885)
*Farzan Haddadi,Leila Monfared,Ebrahim Rezaii,Mohammadreza Malek-Mohammadi,Pejman Zakalvand,Narges Mokhtari*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 扩散模型固有的注意力机制与Transformer相似，表明注意力是基于通用训练目标的普适原则。这种等价性使设计可互换，并催生了一种简化的注意力图像生成算法，以较低计算成本达到相当性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解扩散模型与Transformer在机制上的潜在联系，以统一视角揭示注意力作为基础原则，并利用该等价性优化图像生成的效率与资源消耗。

Method: 通过理论分析展示扩散模型内部存在注意力机制，并证明其与Transformer注意力机制及自编码器的功能原理相似，进而重构扩散框架，提出简化算法，核心仅基于注意力机制进行图像生成。

Result: 注意力实现的简化算法在图像生成上达到与扩散模型相当的性能，同时显著减少了训练时间和计算资源消耗。

Conclusion: 注意力机制具有跨模型的普遍性，基于其等价性可以设计更高效的生成模型，支持根据实际需求灵活替换架构。

Abstract: Denoising diffusion models are the dominant architecture for image generation, whereas most natural language generation and modeling are primarily handled by well-known transformer architectures employing attention mechanism. Here, we show that diffusion models also inherently use an attention mechanism very similar to that of transformers. Therefore, attention emerges as a universal machine learning principle, based on a general training objective. We also show similarities in basic functional principle of auto-encoders and attention-based models. These equivalences allows us to interchange these designs based on practical requirements. As an example, we can reformulate the diffusion framework to reduce the lengthy training process and computation-intensive image generation. Using this approach, a simplified algorithm is proposed for image generation which is based on attention mechanism. Results show that the attention-based implementation achieves comparable performance with significantly less effort and computational resources.

</details>


### [189] [Solving In-Table Prediction Problems by Deep Neural Networks with Performance Evaluation Using Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01262)
*Xiao Zhao,Daniela Oelke*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出表内预测（ITP）问题，即利用深度神经网络基于表中已知列预测任意选定列的值，并探索自监督学习方法。在合成连续特征数据上评估MLP、ResNet和Transformer，发现注意力结构在训练数据充足且嵌入长度较大时表现最佳，但结论限于受控的简单场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表格深度学习依赖显式监督目标，而本文探索无需预定义目标的列间关系学习，旨在填补表内预测这一未被充分研究的应用空白，并区别于表填充和表格预训练任务。

Method: 采用自监督学习，随机掩码列作为预测目标；设计新型神经层同时嵌入数值与缺失值；基于预定义列关系生成合成数据，并用两种机制插入缺失值；适配掩码策略构造测试数据；评估三种网络架构（MLP、Resnet、Transformer）在合成数据上的性能。

Result: 在足够训练样本和较大嵌入长度条件下，基于注意力的Transformer架构优于MLP和Resnet，而其他情况下无显著优势。

Conclusion: ITP在受控合成条件下可行，注意力结构有潜力，但结果受限于小规模列数和合成数据，仅作为初步探索，不适用于真实表格数据的普适结论。

Abstract: Tabular deep learning (TDL) leverages neural networks (NN) to extract patterns from tabular data. Traditional TDL methods follow a supervised learning paradigm, where a target feature is explicitly given. In this work, however, we explore a different approach by employing deep NNs to learn relationships among individual columns within a given table. We investigate whether NNs can predict the values of arbitrarily selected columns in a given table based on the remaining known columns. We call this problem In-Table Prediction (ITB), which is slightly different from table imputation methods and the pretraining task of TDL. Three potential usage scenarios are identified, which, to our best knowledge, have not been extensively studied in the literature. A self-supervised learning approach is applied to address this problem by randomly selecting columns to be masked out and used as learning targets.
