This work focuses on tabular datasets containing only continuous features. To handle missing values in continuous features, a novel neural layer is proposed to embed both numerical and empty values. Synthetic data is generated based on predefined column relationships, with empty values inserted using two distinct mechanisms. Additionally, an adapted masking strategy is employed to create test data. Performances of three NN architectures, namely MLP, Resnet and Transformer, are evaluated using the generated synthetic data. We conclude that, the attention-based structure outperforms the other two networks, when a sufficiently large number of training examples is available and a relatively large embedding length is chosen. We stress that these findings are obtained under controlled, synthetic conditions with a small number of columns and it should therefore be regarded as an initial, narrowly-scoped investigation rather than a general characterization of ITP on real-world tabular data.

</details>


### [190] [CARE: Contrastive Anchor-based Rubric Evolution for Large Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00892)
*Siyuan Li,Xinxin Song,Chen Ruinian,Jingjing Fan,Tingxiong Xiao,Yangen Hu,Ke Zeng,Jinli Suo*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出CARE框架，通过对比锚点响应动态演化rubrics，解决静态rubric被利用和现有动态方法的问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA并持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态rubric随策略进化易被利用，现有动态方法存在rubric提取无方向、黑客检测不可靠、rubric数量无限制增长等问题。

Method: CARE使用前沿模型生成的锚定响应作为基准，对比最佳rollout与锚点，通过自适应分支修复奖励误规范，通过追逐分支将前沿质量差距转化为更精细的rubric，保持高奖励区域的判别精度。

Result: 在WildChecklist-9K上使用Qwen2.5-7B-Base和Instruct，Arena-Hard-2.0、InfoBench、FollowBench均达SOTA；对GPT-4.1锚点的胜率在300步内持续提升，且泛化到Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen3-8B。

Conclusion: CARE有效缓解奖励过度优化，提升开放任务后训练效果，且跨模型家族稳定可靠。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning decomposes open-ended instructions into prompt-specific, flexible rubrics, making it better suited than reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for post-training LLMs on open-ended tasks. However, static rubrics are inevitably hacked as the policy evolves, and existing dynamic approaches introduce new problems: undirected rubric extraction, unreliable hack detection, and unbounded rubric proliferation. We propose $\textbf{CARE}$ ($\textbf{C}$ontrastive $\textbf{A}$nchor-based $\textbf{R}$ubric $\textbf{E}$volution), which grounds every rubric evolution step in a high-quality anchor response generated by a frontier model conditioned on the prompt and its rubrics. At each training step, CARE contrasts the highest-scoring rollout against the anchor, enabling two complementary mechanisms: an Adaptive branch that reactively repairs reward misspecification; and a Chase branch that proactively converts frontier-level quality gaps into sharper rubrics. Together, the two branches $\textbf{maintain discriminative accuracy in the high-reward region}$---the precise region where reward over-optimization mostly originates. Experiments on WildChecklist-9K with Qwen2.5-7B-Base and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct show that CARE achieves state-of-the-art performance on Arena-Hard-2.0, InfoBench, and FollowBench, and is the $\textbf{only}$ method whose win rate against GPT-4.1 anchor responses shows sustained improvement throughout 300 training steps; additional results on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen3-8B further indicate that CARE generalizes across model families.

</details>


### [191] [SMELT: Scaling Laws for Compute-Matched MoE Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01343)
*Shaowen Wang,Ge Zhang,Kairong Luo,Yuhao Wu,Shaofan Liu,Jiaheng Liu,Wenhao Huang,Shen Yan,Jian Li*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出SMELT配方，在严格匹配FLOPs、参数和KV缓存的前提下，通过在MoE Transformer中间层循环两次，实现计算最优前沿上6.8%-18%的训练FLOPs节省，并提升下游基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环Transformer评估多在固定模型大小下进行，混淆了架构优势与额外计算量，未在预算匹配条件下验证其真实收益。

Method: SMELT将MoE Transformer中间一半层循环两次，同时控制每token FLOPs、总非嵌入参数和KV缓存与未循环基线一致；通过消融确定循环位置与次数，并按四种规模拟合各架构的Chinchilla式缩放定律。

Result: SMELT损失随计算下降更快，在最优前沿节省6.8%-18%训练FLOPs；下游优势超验证损失预测，代码任务最显著，且随样本长度和上下文示例数增加而增强；机制分析显示第二次循环减少注意力汇聚，将权重转移至内容相关token。

Conclusion: 在预算匹配下循环仍能提升Transformer性能，提供了将深度复用转化为实际收益的实用配方，并揭示注意力偏置是性能提升的基础。

Abstract: Looped Transformers increase effective depth by iterating a shared block of layers, but most evaluations compare at fixed model size, conflating architectural advantage with extra FLOPs. We study looping on Mixture-of-Experts Transformers while closely matching per-token FLOPs, total non-embedding parameters, and KV cache. Through a series of ablations, we arrive at a recipe we call SMELT (Sparse MoE Transformer, middle layers Loop Twice), which loops the middle half of layers twice while matching the unlooped Baseline on all three budgets. We scale SMELT across four sizes up to 54B non-embedding parameters and fit a separate Chinchilla-style scaling law for each architecture. SMELT's loss drops faster with compute, saving 6.8--18.0\% of training FLOPs on the compute-optimal frontier. The advantage transfers to downstream benchmarks beyond what validation loss predicts, is largest on Code, and grows with sample length and the number of in-context examples. Mechanistic analysis shows that the second visit reduces the attention sink and redirects mass toward content-relevant tokens, an inductive bias that may underlie the observed performance gains. These results show that looping can improve Transformers even under budget matching, offering a practical recipe that turns depth reuse into measurable gains.

</details>


### [192] [Contribution-Aware Bandwidth Allocation for Multimodal Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01406)
*Iason Ofeidis,Leandros Tassiulas*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出模态间分配方法ModalShare，基于Shapley贡献度在固定上行预算下为各模态分配不同压缩比率，显著提升多模态联邦训练精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩方案对各模态采用相同保留率，导致共享预算按激活维度而非实际贡献分配，浪费带宽且降低融合精度。

Method: 服务器基于已接收激活的联盟计算Shapley贡献分数，据此设定各模态压缩率；无需额外上行流量、客户端计算或先验模态知识。

Result: 在5倍压缩下，CREMA-D和MVSA数据集上相比等比率压缩精度分别提升15.4和12.4个百分点，在三种压缩器、三种数据集及四种预算下均表现优异。

Conclusion: 模态间分配是提升多模态Split Learning效率的关键，ModalShare能有效回收现有压缩器遗漏的性能增益。

Abstract: Multimodal models are increasingly the default option for perception at the network edge, yet they are trained almost entirely in the datacenter, because a client holding several sensor streams cannot host an encoder per modality. Split Learning makes such training feasible by keeping only the first layers on the device, at the cost of an uplink that must carry smashed activations for every modality at every step. Existing compression schemes give each modality the same keep-ratio, so the shared budget is divided in proportion to smashed-activation dimension, a quantity unrelated to how much each modality contributes to the fused prediction. We make that division an explicit decision and call it inter-modality allocation: under a fixed uplink budget, every policy transmits the same expected payload and differs only in how that payload is split across modalities. Our allocator, ModalShare, sets each modality's keep-ratio from a Shapley contribution score that the server computes over coalitions of activations it has already received. Measuring this score adds no uplink traffic and no client-side computation, and needs no prior knowledge of which stream is which. ModalShare improves accuracy over equal keep-ratios by 15.4 and 12.4 percentage points on CREMA-D and MVSA at matched payload in 5x compression, with strong performance across three compressors, three datasets, and four budgets. We show that existing compressors underperform in multimodal settings, with ModalShare recovering what gains are left behind.

</details>


### [193] [Efficiently Estimating Optimal Hyperparameter Scaling Laws through Power-Law Entropy Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01431)
*Zhiliang Chen,Sebastian Ament,David Eriksson,Maximilian Balandat,Eytan Bakshy,Jihao Andreas Lin*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出PLES方法，用多保真贝叶斯优化高效估计大规模语言模型训练的最优超参数缩放律，相比传统网格搜索降低超90%计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统估计超参数缩放律需对数千次训练运行进行穷举网格搜索，计算资源消耗巨大，难以实用。

Method: 设计计算成本感知的采集函数PLES，基于多保真贝叶斯优化，通过迭代选择单位计算成本下最能降低缩放律估计整体不确定性的候选配置，从而偏向信息量大的小规模实验。

Result: 在合成基准、真实LLM数据拟合的替代模型及实际预训练任务中，PLES均以不到传统网格搜索等其他基线十分之一的预算收敛到准确的最优超参数缩放律。

Conclusion: PLES提供了一种高效、自动化的缩放律估计方法，显著降低计算开销，便于在更大规模上预测最优配置。

Abstract: Optimal hyperparameter scaling laws describe how the best hyperparameters for large language model (LLM) training change with model and data scale, enabling practitioners to predict optimal configurations at production scales without expensive large-scale tuning. However, estimating these scaling laws conventionally requires exhaustive grid searches over thousands of training runs, consuming enormous computational resources. We introduce Power-Law Entropy Search (PLES), a computational cost-aware acquisition function built on multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization that efficiently estimates optimal hyperparameter scaling laws through adaptive experimentation. A key innovation in PLES is that it searches for candidates that reduce the overall uncertainty of a scaling law estimate, instead of optimizing a single objective function. At each iteration, PLES selects the candidate configuration that maximally reduces the uncertainty of the scaling law estimates per unit computational cost, naturally favoring informative small-scale experiments. We evaluate PLES on synthetic benchmarks, surrogate models fitted to real LLM training data, and actual LLM pre-training runs. Across all settings, PLES converges to accurate optimal hyperparameter scaling laws using less than one-tenth of the computational budget required by conventional grid search and other baselines.

</details>


### [194] [User Representation via Cross Multi-source Behavior Pre-training for Mobile Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01057)
*Chengqi Yang,Yiran Qiao,Feng Liu,Xingyu Lou,Zijun Zhou,Xiaoyun Mo,Changwang Zhang,Jiayuan Xu,Jun Wang,Xiang Ao*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出跨多源行为预训练模型CM-PTM，利用层级掩码预测任务统一建模设备级移动游戏用户行为，缓解数据稀疏并提升下游推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户表征预训练多聚焦单应用或应用级行为，忽略移动设备上跨来源、多粒度的用户活动特性；设备级意图源于异构行为源与层级动作的复杂交互，传统以应用为中心的建模难以应对。

Method: CM-PTM采用层级级联的掩码-预测代理任务：先推断下一行为的行为源，再逐步细化预测至应用-动作层级，在单一预训练框架内实现跨源依赖与细粒度行为动态的统一建模。

Result: 在大规模真实移动数据集上的实验表明，CM-PTM能有效捕捉用户内生兴趣，并在下游移动游戏推荐任务中稳定取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: CM-PTM验证了设备级跨源多粒度预训练的有效性，为移动端用户表征学习提供新范式，可推广至更多个性化任务。

Abstract: User representation pre-training has become a fundamental paradigm for alleviating data sparsity in downstream personalization tasks. However, existing studies predominantly focus on single-app or app-level behaviors, overlooking the inherently cross-source and multi-granular nature of user activities on mobile devices. At the device level, user intent emerges from complex interactions among heterogeneous behavior sources and hierarchical action structures, posing challenges that cannot be addressed by conventional app-centric modeling. To tackle this issue, we propose CM-PTM, a novel Cross Multi-source Behavior Pre-Training Model tailored for mobile game user representation learning on device-level behavioral logs. CM-PTM employs hierarchical cascaded mask-then-predict proxy tasks that first infer the source of the next behavior and then progressively refine predictions at the app-action level. This design enables unified modeling of cross-source dependencies and fine-grained behavioral dynamics within a single pre-training paradigm. Extensive experiments on large-scale real-world mobile datasets demonstrate that CM-PTM effectively captures users' endogenous interests and consistently delivers significant performance gains on downstream mobile game recommendation tasks.

</details>


### [195] [Measuring the Behavioral Fidelity of Long-Horizon Human Activity Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01257)
*Yi Fei Cheng,Fan Yang,Iremsu Bas,Koichiro Niinuma,Narishige Abe,David Lindlbauer*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估LLM人类模拟器在长时程真实活动模拟中行为保真度的框架，并通过43小时办公室活动数据集对比多种条件机制，发现统计先验在活动与序列分布上最接近真实行为，但会过度碎片化日常规程并抑制个体内变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究考察了LLM在调查回答和对话中的行为保真度，但长时程真实世界活动模拟的保真度评估仍处于空白，而此类模拟正被广泛应用于政策制定、评估和训练领域。

Method: 引入一个跨时间粒度和分析层次评估长时程活动模拟行为保真度的框架；采集43小时多摄像头真实办公室活动数据作为案例；对比基于轨迹提取的条件机制，包括人物描述符、少样本示例、统计转移先验和时段先验。

Result: 行为保真度在不同指标上表现不一致：统计先验使活动分布和序列最接近真实行为，但造成日常规程过度碎片化并降低个体内行为变异性。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持采用更全面的评估方法，需综合多种指标、时间粒度和分析层次，而非依赖单一保真度指标。

Abstract: As LLM-based human simulators are increasingly used for policy, evaluation, and training, they must faithfully reproduce real behavioral patterns. While prior work has examined behavioral fidelity in survey responses and dialogue, longer-horizon real-world activity remains largely unexplored. We introduce a framework for evaluating behavioral fidelity in long-horizon activity simulations across temporal granularities and levels of analysis. As a case study, we collect a 43-hour multi-camera dataset of in-the-wild office activity and compare trace-derived conditioning mechanisms: persona descriptors, few-shot exemplars, and statistical transition and time-of-day priors. We find that behavioral fidelity is not uniform across metrics: statistical priors bring activity and sequence distributions closest to real behavior, yet over-fragment routines and suppress within-person variability. These findings motivate a more holistic evaluation that spans multiple metrics, temporal granularities, and levels of analysis.

</details>


### [196] [Gradient-Update Mismatch: Rethinking Conflict-Free Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01558)
*Jing Xiao,Xinhai Chen,Qinglin Wang,Menghan Jia,Zhiquan Lai,Dongsheng Li,Jie Liu,Tiejun Li*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文发现优化器（如动量、自适应、曲率基等）在更新参数时可能破坏梯度手术构造的无冲突方向，称为梯度更新失配（GUM），并提出梯度更新对齐（GUA）方法，通过将优化器建议更新投影到无冲突锥内，并调整优化器内部状态，从而在所有PINN设置中实现无冲突更新并显著降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度手术方法虽然能构造无冲突方向，但优化器（如Adam、动量等）在转换该方向时可能因历史状态、自适应缩放、预条件或权重衰减等机制重新引入冲突，导致更新方向不再在无冲突锥内，影响PINN训练效果。

Method: 提出GUA方法：将优化器产生的建议更新投影到由损失特定梯度定义的无冲突锥内，得到对齐后的更新并应用于参数；若优化器维护内部状态（如动量），则根据实际应用的对齐更新反向调整状态，以维持一致性。

Result: 实验显示GUM在各类优化器中普遍存在，冲突率最高达86.3%；GUA方法在所有PINN设置中均实现无冲突更新，并一致改进步长手术方法，在个别设置中相对L2误差降低高达98.2%。

Conclusion: GUA有效解决了优化器引发的梯度冲突问题，显著提升PINN训练的准确性和可靠性，且对多种梯度手术方法均有改进，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Training Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) requires jointly optimizing physics residual and initial/boundary condition loss terms, which often induce conflicting gradients. Gradient surgery methods mitigate this issue by constructing directions from loss-specific gradients to reduce conflict before optimizer transformation. However, even when the constructed direction is conflict-free, this property may not be preserved after optimizer transformation. Let $a_t$ denote the direction constructed by gradient surgery, $u_t$ the optimizer proposal, and $\mathcal{C}_t$ the conflict-free cone induced by the loss-specific gradients. We show that modern optimizers can transform $a_t$ through mechanisms such as historical state, adaptive scaling, preconditioning, or decoupled weight decay, so $a_t \in \mathcal{C}_t$ does not generally imply $u_t \in \mathcal{C}_t$. We refer to this optimizer-induced discrepancy in conflict-freeness between $a_t$ and $u_t$ as Gradient-Update Mismatch (GUM). Accordingly, we propose Gradient-Update Alignment (GUA), which projects $u_t$ onto $\mathcal{C}_t$ to obtain the aligned update $p_t$ and applies $p_t$ to the parameters. When the optimizer maintains internal state, GUA further adjusts this state toward targets reconstructed from the applied update. We conduct extensive experiments and find that GUM is widespread across momentum, adaptive, and curvature-based optimizers, with conflict rates reaching up to 86.3%. Across all PINN settings, GUA achieves conflict-free applied updates and consistently improves various gradient surgery methods, reducing the relative $L_2$ error by up to 98.2% in individual settings. Data and code are available at https://github.com/JingXiao10/GUA.

</details>


### [197] [Automated Event Log Generation from Unstructured Text Using Finetuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01320)
*Maximilian Seeth,Gabriel Marques Tavares,Daniel Schuster*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型作为数据转换器，将非结构化文本自动转换为结构化事件日志，以解决流程挖掘中事件日志构建成本高的问题，并证明微调方法显著优于少样本和零样本提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流程挖掘依赖结构化事件日志，但现有日志多由专家手工构建，成本高昂，导致大量非结构化组织知识（如工单、手册、报告）未被充分利用。

Method: 构建了一个新的文本到日志数据集，并在此数据集上微调大语言模型，使其能够从非结构化文本中提取高质量的事件日志；同时对比了微调与少样本/零样本提示的性能。

Result: 微调后的模型在生成事件日志方面显著优于少样本或零样本提示，表明微调是生成可靠事件数据的必要前提。

Conclusion: 该方法为流程挖掘生态系统提供了将未利用数据转化为事件日志的有效管道，扩大了流程挖掘在组织工作流研究中的应用范围。

Abstract: Process mining (PM) provides a powerful framework for discovering and optimizing operational processes from event data. However, the efficacy of PM techniques is strictly predicated on the availability of structured event logs. Thus far, event logs have often been laboriously created by domain and process mining experts. This costly effort causes large portions of organizational knowledge, including incident tickets, manuals, and textual reports, to remain underutilized. We address this bottleneck by investigating the efficacy of Large Language Models (LLMs) as automated data translators. We propose a scalable framework that leverages LLMs as data translators to bridge the gap between unstructured textual resources and structured event data. We finetune LLMs on a newly created text-to-log dataset, demonstrating that the resulting models can extract high-fidelity event logs from unstructured resources. Our results show that this finetuning approach outperforms few-shot or zero-shot prompting by a large amount, highlighting finetuning as a necessary pre-condition for generating reliable event data. We conclude that our method provides a promising pipeline for making previously unused data available to process mining ecosystems, effectively expanding the possibilities of using PM to further investigate organizational workflows.

</details>


### [198] [EdiTikZ: Scientific Figure Editing from Revision Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01409)
*Christian Greisinger,Zhixue Zhao,Steffen Eger*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出首个大规模科学图形编辑数据集DaEdiTikZ及配套基准，并训练紧凑模型达到与顶级商业系统相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图形编辑任务重要但缺乏研究，现有方法依赖昂贵代理系统或合成数据，需利用自然发生的论文修订轨迹作为监督来源。

Method: 从arXiv/GitHub/TeX SE挖掘39.1万TikZ编辑对，用VLM推断78.1万条编辑指令；构建人工精炼基准，训练4B/9B模型联合学习重建与编辑，并用强化学习优化渲染保真度和编辑应用奖励。

Result: 9B模型自动评估超所有基线，人工评估超GPT-5.6-Sol并与Gemini-3.1-Pro持平，严重分布偏移下仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 自然修订轨迹可作为可扩展监督，紧凑开源模型可匹敌商业系统，数据和模型将开源。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown strong performance in generating scientific figures from text or images. However, producing publication-ready figures requires iterative refinement, making scientific figure editing an important yet largely unexplored task. Existing approaches rely on costly proprietary agentic systems, focus primarily on evaluation, or construct training supervision from synthetically generated edits. Instead, we leverage naturally occurring scientific revision and development trajectories as a scalable source of supervision. To this end, we introduce DaEdiTikZ, the first large-scale dataset of revision-derived scientific figure edits, constructed by mining 391K plausible TikZ edit pairs from arXiv, GitHub, and TeX SE and inferring 781K directed edit instructions with a VLM conditioned on rendered figures and TikZ code. We further introduce DaEdiTikZ-Bench, a human-refined benchmark with 790 instances, and train two compact Qwen3.5-based EdiTikZ models (4B and 9B) by jointly learning reconstruction and editing, followed by reinforcement learning (RL) with complementary rewards for rendered fidelity and edit application. Automatic evaluation places our 9B model above all tested baselines, while human evaluation with 9 annotators and 4,320 ratings places it above GPT-5.6-Sol and on par with Gemini-3.1-Pro. Under severe out-of-distribution shifts, it remains competitive with GPT-5.6-Sol near its 2K training sequence-length regime. Models and datasets will be released.

</details>


<div id='Sec'></div>

# Sec [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [AgentProv: Auditing Agentic LLM API Providers via Tool-use Policy Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00052)
*Xun Wang,Bihe Zhao,Michael Backes,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出首个基于工具调用行为的智能体API身份审计方法AgentProv，通过MMD置换检验识别模型是否被替换，对抗系统提示注入的鲁棒性显著优于现有文本通道方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计依赖文本输出，但智能体API常丢弃文本仅暴露结构化动作，且服务商注入的系统提示会扭曲文本分布导致误报，需要新的审计通道。

Method: 利用智能体后训练将工具使用内化到权重中的特性，提取模型发出的工具调用类别分布作为指纹，通过最大均值差异（MMD）置换检验判断部署模型与声称模型是否一致。

Result: 在630个检查点对上替换检测率100%；在系统提示注入场景下误报率仅7%，远低于MET（67%）和RUT（53%）；第三方API上的分歧与独立token计数侧信道检测一致。

Conclusion: AgentProv提供了一种对部署上下文不敏感、鲁棒且可验证的智能体LLM服务身份审计新途径，弥补了文本通道审计的结构性缺陷。

Abstract: Commercial LLM APIs advertise a specific foundation model, but the served backbone may be silently substituted, quantized, or wrapped, for example to save deployment costs. All existing audits decide backbone identity from the text-output channel, which is structurally fragile for agentic APIs because modern serving stacks (OpenAI, Anthropic, Gemini, Cloudflare Workers AI, LangGraph) discard text and expose only structured actions when the model calls a tool, and provider-injected system prompts can distort text distributions enough that text-channel tests falsely accuse honest providers of substituting the claimed model. We observe that recent agentic post-training internalizes tool-use directly into the weights, opening a new audit channel that the serving stack still exposes and that is largely invariant to deployment context. We introduce Agentic Provenance (AgentProv), the first action-based identity audit for agentic LLM APIs: AgentProv fingerprints a deployed model through its categorical tool-call distribution and decides identity via an MMD permutation test. AgentProv catches every substituted model (100% on 630 evaluated checkpoint pairs), while holding the false-positive rate under system-prompt injection at 7% (vs. 67% for MET and 53% for RUT). On third-party API endpoints, AgentProv's disagreements with MET are consistent with an independent token-count side-channel that detects provider-injected system prompts.

</details>


### [200] [A Formal Analysis of Agent Payment Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00060)
*Ke Jiang,Mohan Yu,Yuan Chang,Mohit Kumar Jangid,Jianyu Niu,Cong Wang,Yinqian Zhang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 首次对四种智能体支付协议（x402、MPP、ACP、AP2）进行系统化形式化分析，识别出18项共享安全原则，并发现大量先前未记录的协议一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体支付协议正在成为自主商业的关键交易层，但其安全依赖分散在多方多阶段中，现有规范缺乏系统性的形式化分析，导致安全保证隐式且易被破坏。

Method: 基于Tamarin形式化工具，构建四种协议的源码对齐模型，通过溯源验证问题与反例轨迹暴露缺失绑定和状态约束，并对违规案例构造最小强化参考模型进行重验证。

Result: 在86个验证案例中复现46个已知案例并发现40个新的一致性缺陷；针对x402的发现经三个实现和PoC验证，覆盖五项安全原则。

Conclusion: 委托授权必须在其经济与服务效果上，跨参与者、状态和阶段保持一致，这是智能体支付协议安全的核心要求。

Abstract: Agent payment protocols are emerging as a key transaction layer for autonomous commerce, enabling AI agents to purchase goods and services and execute payments on users' behalf. Unlike conventional payment flows, they distribute user intent, delegated authority, credential use, settlement, and fulfillment across multiple actors and stages, creating security dependencies that no single message or participant can enforce. Yet these guarantees remain largely implicit across evolving specifications, schemas, and reference implementations, with little systematic formal analysis.
  We formalize four representative agent payment protocols: x402, MPP, ACP, and AP2 in Tamarin. Using a common abstraction of the agent payment lifecycle, we construct source-grounded models that capture each protocol's roles, state, trust assumptions, and lifecycle transitions. Rather than assuming a complete property taxonomy, we use source-backed verification questions and counterexample traces to expose missing bindings, state constraints, and cross-stage correspondences, consolidating them into 18 shared security principles. Across 86 verification cases, our analysis reproduces 46 known or calibration cases and identifies 40 previously undocumented formal-consistency findings. For each retained violation, we isolate the missing protocol relation, construct a minimally strengthened reference model, and reverify the intended property. We further evaluate the new x402 findings across three implementations and validate ten representative findings through implementation PoCs, SDK/schema-level witnesses, and source-aligned executable traces spanning five security principles. Our results show that delegated authorization must remain consistent with its resulting economic and service effects across actors, states, and protocol stages.

</details>


### [201] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence for Industrial Cybersecurity: A Review of Methods, Operational Integration, and Research Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00171)
*Amr S. Mohamed,Charlotte Fritz,Ahmad Mohammad Saber,Yiqun Ma,Ratinder Kaur,Mohammed Al-Darwbi,Daniela Friedrich,Deepa Kundur*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文综述了可解释人工智能（XAI）在工业网络安全运营中心（SOC）中的应用，重点分析了各类XAI技术（如特征归因、替代模型、规则解释和可视化）在工业场景中的适用性，并讨论了运营、监管和安全需求带来的独特挑战，最后指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业系统数字化转型及IT/OT融合扩大了网络攻击面，AI/ML虽提升检测与响应能力，但其决策不透明阻碍了运营信任、法规遵从和事件响应，亟需XAI提升透明度与可解释性。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法：先梳理AI在工业SOC工作流中的作用及数据类型，再分类评述XAI技术家族（特征归因、代理模型、规则解释、可视化），并针对工业环境特有的运营、法规与安全约束分析其适用性。

Result: 识别出XAI应用于工业SOC的关键挑战，包括标注数据稀缺、模型可靠性不足、可解释性与性能间的权衡，以及XAI工具集成至现有工作流的困难，同时指出不同XAI方法的适用边界与局限。

Conclusion: XAI对构建可信的工业网络安全解决方案至关重要，但需开发面向工业场景、具备运营可行性和领域特异性的可解释AI，并建议未来研究聚焦于数据稀缺应对、可靠性保障、性能平衡及SOC流程的无缝集成。

Abstract: The increasing digitalization of industrial infrastructure and the convergence of information technology (IT) and operational technology (OT) have expanded the cyberattack surface of industrial systems. To address the growing complexity of cyber threats, artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) techniques are increasingly deployed within industrial cybersecurity operations, particularly in Security Operations Centers (SOCs). While these approaches improve anomaly detection, threat analysis, and automated response, their opaque decision-making presents challenges for operational trust, regulatory compliance, and incident response. EXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) has emerged as a promising paradigm to improve the transparency and interpretability of AI-driven cybersecurity systems and decisions. This paper provides a comprehensive review of XAI techniques in industrial cybersecurity, focusing on industrial SOC environments and operational security workflows. We examine the role of AI in industrial SOC workflows, the types of operational data leveraged in industrial environments, and the benefits and limitations of AI-based threat detection. We then review major families of XAI approaches, including feature attribution methods, surrogate models, rule-based explanations, and visualization techniques, and analyze their applicability to industrial use cases. We further discuss the operational, regulatory, and safety requirements that distinguish industrial systems from traditional IT environments. Key challenges are examined, including limited labeled datasets, model reliability, explainability-performance tradeoffs, and the integration of XAI tools into SOC workflows. Finally, we identify open research directions and opportunities for developing trustworthy, operationally viable, and domain-specific XAI-enabled cybersecurity solutions for industrial environments.

</details>


### [202] [DUPIN: Attack Learning Is Still Needed! Demonstrating Few-Shot after Unsupervised Pretraining Is A Nimble Forensics Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00259)
*Chanwoo Bae,Hailun Ding,Shiqing Ma,Xiangyu Zhang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出DUPIN，一种基于无监督预训练与少样本微调的学习式攻击取证方法，在38-52天审计日志（7.3TB）上预训练，并在25个APT活动及四个数据源上验证了可扩展检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有攻击取证方法依赖大量标注样本，而真实APT攻击数据稀缺且标注昂贵，需一种能利用海量无标注审计数据进行高效学习的方案。

Method: DUPIN先对来源于来源图的审计事件进行大规模无监督预训练，学习正常行为的通用表示；随后通过少量带标签的攻击样本进行少样本微调，以适配具体威胁检测任务。

Result: 在38-52天（7.3TB）审计日志上预训练后，在包含四个数据源的25个APT活动基准测试中，DUPIN优于多个基线方法，且支持大规模可扩展评估。

Conclusion: DUPIN证明无监督预训练结合少样本学习可显著降低对标注数据的依赖，为实现实用的攻击取证提供了可行路径，并具备跨数据源适应性。

Abstract: We propose a novel approach to learning-based attack forensics called DUPIN. DUPIN performs unsupervised pre-training on an enormous amount of audit events in the form of provenance graphs. It then proceeds to a few-shot learning stage, leveraging a small number of labeled attack examples to fine-tune its detection capabilities. We pretrain DUPIN on up to 38 - 52 days of audit logs (7.3TB total) and evaluate it against various baselines on 25 APT campaigns across four different data sources, facilitating the scalable evaluation.

</details>


### [203] [Delegation Without Trust: An Empirical Gap Analysis of Identity, Authorization, and Runtime Governance in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00267)
*Panduranga Sai Varma Dantuluri,Jyotirmoy Sundi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出自主LLM代理系统必须在不可信模型假设下评估安全性，并设计了一个授权代理来防御四类威胁，实验证明其能有效阻断攻击且开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理被赋予凭证和工具调用能力，传统的分布式系统授权问题变得紧迫且未解决，因为驱动代理的模型可能被对抗性劫持，导致权限滥用。

Method: 定义了以混淆代理、令牌窃取与重放、提示注入提权、子代理被攻陷为中心的四类对手和八项安全要求；构建授权代理模型，实现细粒度权限验证和不透明令牌，并通过对抗测试验证。

Result: 授权代理阻止全部四类威胁，抵御11种设计攻击和20万伪造令牌（接受0个），在2000个随机场景中将被攻陷子代理限制到平均1.5个可达动作（对比8100个），每次决策仅需约2.6微秒。

Conclusion: 现有框架（LangGraph、CrewAI、AutoGen、MCP）均未完整满足安全要求，而所提授权代理能在微秒级开销内实现强安全边界，并已在VotalAI的LLM Shield中生产落地。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents increasingly act on a user's behalf: they hold credentials, call tools and services, and spawn sub-agents that act further on their behalf. This turns a long-standing distributed-systems question -- who is authorized to do what, on whose authority -- into an urgent and largely unsolved problem, because the component driving each agent is a language model an adversary can hijack. We argue that agent security must be evaluated under an untrusted-model assumption: a correct system is one in which a fully prompt-injected agent still cannot exceed the authority explicitly delegated to it. Against this standard we make three contributions. First, we give a threat model for multi-agent delegation centered on four adversaries -- confused deputy, token theft and replay, prompt-injection privilege escalation, and compromised sub-agents -- and derive eight security requirements a governed agent system must meet. Second, we show the gap is real: a default agent runtime modeling common practice (broad bearer credentials, authorization gated inside the model) fails all four threats, and across four widely used frameworks -- LangGraph, CrewAI, AutoGen, and the Model Context Protocol (MCP) authorization model -- three provide no built-in confinement and one only partial; no existing standard alone covers the requirement set. Third, we implement and adversarially evaluate an authorization broker that closes the gap. It blocks all four threats; it resists 11 direct attacks on its design and accepts 0 of 200,000 forged tokens; it confines a compromised sub-agent to its delegated task (a mean of 1.5 reachable actions versus all 8,100 under bearer delegation, across 2,000 randomized scenarios); and it enforces at microsecond cost (about 2.6 microseconds per decision), negligible against model inference. These principles are also realized in production in VotalAI's LLM Shield.

</details>


### [204] [Workload Identification with Physical Side Channels for AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00309)
*Simone Gargiulo,Gabriel Kulp*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 通过分析NVIDIA H200 GPU的功耗信号，外部观察者能以97%的准确率区分训练、推理和非AI工作负载，并对多种规避策略具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI治理需要验证前沿计算是否符合协议，而芯片上的NVML遥测可被伪造，物理功耗通道为独立验证提供了可能。

Method: 采集930条5秒@10MHz的功耗轨迹，覆盖17个LLM家族和25种非AI负载；对NVIDIA H200进行功率分析，提取频谱特征分类；针对四种规避策略（伪装训练为推理）训练强化检测器，并加入救援规则。

Result: 区分训练/推理/非AI准确率97%，宏平均F1为0.955（跨未见模型家族）；对三种规避策略检测率≥99%，对稀释LoRA检测率为48-88%，加救援规则后≥98%。

Conclusion: 物理功耗可成为AI计算验证的可行基础，展示了对抗场景下的初步鲁棒性，但需进一步评估更全面的攻击并扩展数据集以强化防御。

Abstract: AI compute verification is one of the first tangible and tractable points for international policy aimed at AI governance. Determining whether frontier labs, or any operator, comply with agreements requires the regulating authority to discern how their compute is used. The elementary building block of AI compute is the GPU, and any activity it executes leaves a physical trace. Here, we show that an external observer can identify the class of the workload running on an NVIDIA H200 from its power draw. Unlike on-chip NVML telemetry, which can be spoofed or replayed, such a physical channel can in principle be observed independently of operator cooperation. We recorded $930$ five-second traces at $\sim 10$ MHz, covering seventeen open LLM families and twenty-five non-AI workloads. Over this corpus we separate training from inference and from non-AI computation with an accuracy of $97\%$ and a macro-averaged F1 score of $0.955$, evaluated on model families unseen during training. AI workload spectral content predominantly lies below $\sim 20$kHz and training is particularly recognizable through the memory-bound optimizer update. The GPU operator is then treated as adversarial and able to reshape the physical computation itself. Four evasion strategies are tested to disguise training as inference, producing an additional 680 adversarial traces. A detector hardened against evasion strategies, with the tested strategy held out, catches training $\geq 99\%$ of the time for three of the four strategies. The fourth, diluted low-rank adaptation (LoRA), is detected $48$--$88\%$ of the time with a hardened classifier, rising to $\geq 98\%$ with an additional rescue rule. While these attacks are not a comprehensive evaluation against adversarial behaviour, they offer initial insights beyond genuine activities and a dataset for developing and testing stronger evasion mechanisms.

</details>


### [205] [OreProof: Verifiable Provenance with Limited Disclosure for Critical-Minerals Supply Chains Using Zero-Knowledge Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00340)
*Oleksandr Hrabar,Hossein Arshadi Soufiani,Henry M. Kim,Chien-Chih Chen,Ali Vazirizadeh,Hjalmar Turesson*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出OreProof原型，用零知识证明和混合链上/链下模型平衡关键矿产溯源中的可验证性与隐私披露，在定义攻击模型下将可推断机密属性从四分之三降至零，并给出四条设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键矿产供应链存在结构性矛盾：监管和买家要求可验证来源，但上游参与者不愿披露供应商身份、品位/产量和价格等数据，现有验证机制要求披露这些信息，阻碍溯源实施。

Method: 以设计科学方法构建OreProof原型，面向黄金场景，采用混合链上/链下数据模型、Groth16零知识证明实现选择性披露、Merkle批量锚定管线提升吞吐，并在公共zkEVM测试网上签发符合UNTP标准的可验证凭证。

Result: 与透明基线相比，在定义攻击者模型下可直接推断的机密属性从四类中的三类降为零类；批量锚定显著提升吞吐量。

Conclusion: 贡献包括工件原型和四条初始设计原则：对承诺数据证明而非暴露数据；仅对可验证来源发凭证并对混合商品标记未知输入；从开始就签发标准对齐凭证；按供应链角色划分披露权限。

Abstract: Critical-minerals supply chains face a structural tension: regulators and buyers demand verifiable provenance, yet upstream actors are hesitant to disclose supplier identities, assay grades/yields, and prices that verification appears to require. We report a design science account of OreProof, a prototypical traceability platform addressing this verifiability-disclosure trade-off. Instantiated for gold, OreProof combines a hybrid on-chain/off-chain data model, Groth16 zero-knowledge proofs for selective disclosure, a Merkle-batched anchoring pipeline, and UNTP-aligned verifiable credentials on a public zkEVM testnet. Against a transparent baseline, directly inferable confidential attributes fell from three of four categories to none under a defined attacker model, while batched anchoring substantially improved throughput. Our contributions are the artifact prototype as well as four nascent design principles: prove over committed data rather than exposing it; credential only verifiable origin and flag unknown inputs for blended commodities; emit standards-aligned credentials from the outset; and partition disclosure by supply-chain role.

</details>


### [206] [Incremental Risk Assessment of Progressive Elder Financial Scams via Instruction-Tuned Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00005)
*Parviz Ghafariasl,Weimin Fu,Xiaolong Guo,Shing I. Chang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出基于累积对话轮次的风险评估框架，利用小型语言模型在资源受限环境下动态识别针对老年人的多轮金融诈骗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对老年人的金融诈骗常通过文本和语音渠道跨多轮对话逐步展开，风险信号分散且递增，现有方法难以在资源受限部署下持续更新风险评估。

Method: 构建累积式轮次风险评估框架，逐步聚合对话并重新计算风险；创建覆盖投资、慈善和技术支持场景的多轮对话数据集（每轮2-8轮），每步标注风险等级、连续分数、解释和建议；微调四种小型语言模型。

Result: Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2在轮次感知风险估计上表现更优，且保持紧凑架构适合移动部署。

Conclusion: 结构化累积建模可在实际部署场景中支持增量诈骗风险监测，证明紧凑语言模型具备隐私保护和设备端欺诈防护潜力。

Abstract: Financial scams targeting older adults increasingly occur through text and voice channels such as email, SMS, and phone calls, unfolding over multiple conversational turns that begin with impersonation or casual contact, escalate through trust building and urgency, and culminate in requests for sensitive information or financial transfers. Because risk signals emerge incrementally across turns, effective detection requires models that continuously update risk estimates under resource-constrained deployment settings. We propose a cumulative turn-based risk assessment framework that incrementally aggregates conversational turns and re-estimates risk at each step, enabling dynamic scam monitoring across progressively evolving conversations. A multi-turn dialogue dataset is constructed to cover investment, charity, and tech support scam scenarios, with each dialogue containing two to eight turns and annotated at every cumulative stage with a qualitative risk level, a continuous risk score, an explanatory rationale, and a safety recommendation. Four small language models (Phi-4, LLaMA-3.2, DeepSeek-R1, and Qwen3) are fine-tuned and evaluated under a unified training framework. Fine-tuned small models capture fraud-related linguistic cues and cross-turn escalation patterns while maintaining compact architectures suitable for mobile and resource-constrained deployment settings. Among the evaluated models, Phi-4 and LLaMA-3.2 achieve stronger turn-aware risk estimation performance relative to their parameter scale. These results suggest that structured cumulative modeling can support incremental scam risk assessment in deployment-oriented settings while highlighting the potential of compact language models for privacy-aware and on-device fraud protection.

</details>


### [207] [NeuroPriv: Adversarial Representation Learning for Privacy in Wearable EEG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00390)
*Sarmistha Sarna Gomasta,Bhawana Chhaglani,Prashant Shenoy*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 可穿戴EEG系统的压缩特征虽用于认知监测，却可泄露身份和人口统计信息；通过隐私感知表征学习可在保持任务性能的同时显著降低此泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴EEG系统在健康监测外可能暴露敏感神经隐私信息，现有系统假设传输压缩特征而非原始EEG即可保护隐私，但缺乏验证。

Method: 以EEGMAT为例，评估压缩EEG特征在认知状态分类及性别、年龄、身份推断上的表现，并采用隐私感知表征学习降低推断精度。

Result: 压缩特征认知分类平衡精度0.788，但性别/年龄/身份推断精度达0.858/0.789/0.692；隐私感知学习后任务性能0.781，推断精度降至0.563/0.467/0.206。

Conclusion: 可穿戴神经健康系统需采用目的限制性特征表达，并进行显式隐私审计以保护神经隐私。

Abstract: Wearable EEG systems may expose sensitive information beyond their intended health function, creating substantial risks to neuroprivacy. In this work, we show that commonly used EEG features can reveal participant identity and demographic attributes in addition to supporting the intended cognitive task. Wearable EEG is increasingly being explored for cognitive monitoring, neurological assessment, and longitudinal digital-health applications, yet many systems assume that transmitting compact spectral or spatial features instead of raw EEG provides sufficient privacy protection. Using EEGMAT as a motivating case study, we find that compact EEG features achieve a balanced accuracy of 0.788 for cognitive-state classification while enabling gender, age, and subject-identity inference with balanced accuracies of 0.858, 0.789, and 0.692, respectively. We further show that privacy-aware representation learning preserves task performance at 0.781 while reducing these inference accuracies to 0.563, 0.467, and 0.206. These findings motivate purpose-limited representations and explicit privacy auditing in wearable neurohealth systems.

</details>


### [208] [Federated Trust for Embodied Robot Capability Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00404)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出面向机器人技能市场的去中心化信任架构：联邦信任（federated trust），用本地信任目录和Ed25519签名取代中心化PKI，以适配异构法规、离线部署和物理安全场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人技能市场类似“机器人技能应用商店”，默认采用云原生中心化PKI（单一CA/日志/信任根）验证包安全性。但该模型不适应具身机器人车队：运营商面临异构监管、断网部署、人员少、错误信任发布者会产生物理后果。

Method: 每个桥（bridge）维护本地信任目录（SSH风格信任文件），签名者用独立Ed25519签名信封中的公钥标识自己，安装时做本地集合成员检查，无需访问CA的网络往返。密钥原语为标准组件，创新在架构组合适配机制。工程实现包括五子命令CLI、注册服务器、安装门及80项测试。

Result: 多部署评估显示同一注册流在不同信任目录的桥上给出不同安装判决，体现核心设计属性；5000次对抗试验中，严格模式100%拒绝伪造/篡改等攻击，版本降级攻击拒绝率96.6%；同硬件对比，单次验证成本介于Sigstore-Cosign与python-TUF之间，每发布者存储开销低于两者。

Conclusion: 联邦信任提供一种轻量、离线可行、适配异构机器人车队的信任分发范式，不需中心化权威也能保障安全，实验验证其有效性。

Abstract: Robot capability marketplaces, the "app store for robot skills," are emerging as the deployment vector for LLM-driven robot fleets. The default cloud-native answer to "is this package safe to install?" is centralised PKI: one certificate authority, one transparency log, one root of trust. We argue this is the wrong model for embodied robot fleets, where operators face heterogeneous regulatory regimes, air-gapped deployments, tiny operator headcounts, and physical-world consequences for trusting the wrong publisher. We present federated trust: each deployed bridge maintains its own local trust directory of acceptable signers; signers identify themselves with a public key embedded in a detached Ed25519 signature envelope; install-time verification is a local set-membership check rather than a network round trip to a certificate authority. The cryptographic primitives are deliberately standard (Ed25519 detached signatures and SSH-style trust files); the contribution is the architectural commitment that this composition fits embodied robot fleets specifically. We implement the model in a runtime governance layer with a five-subcommand CLI, a registry server, a per-bridge install gate, and 80 tests. A multi-deployment evaluation shows the same registry stream producing divergent install verdicts on bridges with different trust directories, the load-bearing design property. Across 5000 adversarial trials, the strict-mode gate rejects 100% of rogue-publisher, tampered, forged, and revoked-signer attacks, and 96.6% of downgrade attempts under a minimum-version pin extension. A same-hardware comparison against Sigstore-Cosign and python-TUF locates federated trust's per-verify cost between the two and its per-publisher storage footprint below both.

</details>


### [209] [OpenAgentFlow: Enabling System-Wide Safety Boundaries for Heterogeneous AI Agent Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00015)
*Dongsheng Chen,Xiangyu Zhao,Xin Yao,Xuetao Wei*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenAgentFlow，一种控制平面/动作平面架构，在动作提交边界统一强制安全策略，将异构代理的GUI、API、工具和LLM调用标准化为AgentEvent流，经共享策略执行点过滤，并实现审计与动态策略更新。在Android基准上达到94%准确率和95.3%攻击拦截率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的AI代理正从孤立助手演变为异构系统，多代理、规划器、控制器和执行后端共享同一环境，安全挑战升级为系统级动作治理问题，需在动作修改共享状态前决定是否提交。当前防护分散在提示词、工具调用、GUI操作等层面，缺乏统一入口，难以覆盖跨多步动作流的复合风险，且审计性和策略演进支持不足。

Method: 提出控制平面/动作平面分离架构。动作平面将待执行的GUI动作、API调用、工具调用和LLM生成调用统一归一化为AgentEvent流，每个事件在预执行阶段路由至共享策略执行点。控制平面维护事件来源、会话状态、审计记录和可更新策略，使新规则无需修改代理、提示词、模型或执行路径即可生效，形成统一可治理的动作流。

Result: 在Android上实现：300例动作事件基准中达94.0%准确率和95.3%攻击拦截率；30例动态策略套件中安装新规则后27例符合预期行为；98例可追踪的仿真器用例中，GUI、API和LLM规划场景达到90.8%原始准确率和92.9%追踪调整后通过率。

Conclusion: OpenAgentFlow为异构AI代理舰队提供了实用且共享的强制执行边界，验证了其作为统一安全治理机制的有效性，在准确率、攻击拦截和动态策略适应性方面表现良好，支持策略演进且不侵入现有代理架构。

Abstract: AI agents powered by large language models are evolving from isolated assistants into heterogeneous systems in which multiple agents, planners, controllers, and execution backends operate over the same user or enterprise environment. In such settings, safety becomes a system-level action-governance problem: deciding whether concrete agent-generated actions should be committed before they modify shared state. Existing safeguards cover prompts, tool calls, GUI actions, and agent-local behavior, but often leave enforcement fragmented, obscure risks that emerge across multi-step action flows, and provide limited support for auditability and policy evolution.
  We present OpenAgentFlow, a control-plane/action-plane architecture that enforces safety at the action-commit boundary. It normalizes pending GUI actions, API calls, tool calls, and LLM-generated invocations into a unified AgentEvent stream, routes each event through a shared pre-execution Policy Enforcement Point, and maintains provenance, session state, audit records, and updatable policies in the control plane. This creates a shared governable action stream and allows new rules to take effect without modifying agents, prompts, models, or execution paths.
  We instantiate OpenAgentFlow on Android. On a 300-case action-event benchmark, it achieves 94.0% accuracy and a 95.3% attack block rate. On a 30-case dynamic-policy suite, it matches expected behavior in 27 cases after new rules are installed. Across 98 traced cases from a 100-case Android emulator suite, it achieves 90.8% raw accuracy and a 92.9% trace-adjusted pass rate across GUI, API, and LLM-planned cases. These results show that OpenAgentFlow provides a practical shared enforcement boundary for heterogeneous AI agent fleets.

</details>


### [210] [Don't Trust the Code, Check Its Effects: Runtime Refinement for Regenerated Systems Code Under an Adversarial Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00430)
*Jinhao Hu,Ashvin Goel,Laurent Bindschaedler*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出在不可逆系统代码生成中，通过固定可信中介者独占所有副作用，使生成代码仅做规划，从而在对抗性生成器下保证安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用大模型从规范生成系统代码的方法适用于可恢复效果和诚实生成器，但面对不可逆效果和可能对抗的生成器时，重执行无法事后检查，证明在假设不成立时会静默失败，因此需要新的安全机制。

Method: 否定生成代码的执行权限，引入固定可信中介者作为引用监视器，只有规范预期产生的效果才被执行；并定义可中介性包络的六个条件：可读性、规范输入可观察性、可关联性、完备性、结果可枚举性和显式持久性。

Result: 将方法实例化为再生设备驱动程序的引用监视器，并刻画了中介可行的边界条件。

Conclusion: 安全保证依赖于中介者而非生成代码，因此可跨代码再生保持，为不可逆系统场景的自动化生成提供可行路径。

Abstract: Recent work uses large language models to generate systems code from specifications, treating the specification as the durable artifact and the implementation as disposable. Regenerating the implementation specializes it to each workload and device. However, that work lives in a forgiving setting: a component's externally visible effects, its writes and device commands, are recoverable, and the generator is honest, so trust is discharged by re-execution. We target the unforgiving setting: systems code whose effects are irreversible, produced by a generator that may be adversarial. There, re-execution cannot check an effect after the fact, and a proof fails silently when its assumptions do. We take the position that the only safe way to operate here is to deny the generated code the authority to act. The generated code only plans, while a fixed trusted mediator owns every effect and performs one only when the specification would have produced it. Because the guarantee lives in the mediator, not the code, it survives regeneration. We instantiate this as a reference monitor for regenerated device drivers, and characterize the mediability envelope, six conditions on the effect vocabulary: legibility, spec-input observability, correlatability, completeness, outcome enumerability, and explicit durability. They decide when such mediation is possible.

</details>


### [211] [Capability-Gated Language Models: Security Composes, Utility Does Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00445)
*Patrikas Vanagas,Augustas Mačijauskas,Laurynas Lopata*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出能力门控部署（capability-gated deployment）概念，在单一模型权重内实现按主体（principal）的访问控制，配置构成格结构。通过稀疏秩门控实例化，安全性质在meet操作下可组合证明，但效用不具组合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型防护措施（安全微调、过滤、遗忘）因主体不同仅在权重外部变化（过滤器重配、等级倍增、工件重发），而权重内部所有请求面对相同配置，缺乏细粒度的按主体权限控制机制。

Method: 定义能力门控部署，使用格结构管理配置（meet累积限制，join合并权限）。基于嵌套因子分解机制实现稀疏秩门控，用单次归因指导配置文件搜索，在预注册留出集上单次读取所有结果。安全证明在单调启发假设下逐点证伪，满足组合性。

Result: 在两个模型谱系中，留出集meet的中位抑制深度增加，经校正后存留的效果更强。但效用受损：单独无害的配置文件组合后可导致保留性能和流畅度下降，且不存在组合界。

Conclusion: 能力门控部署能实现权重内按主体的安全可组合管控，但效用端缺乏组合保证，需在实际应用中权衡安全与模型质量损害。

Abstract: Deployed language model safeguards (safety fine-tuning, filtering, unlearning) vary by principal only outside the model weights: filters are reconfigured, tiers are multiplied, and artefacts are reissued; inside one set of weights every request meets the same model configuration. This motivates us to define capability-gated deployment: per-principal access control inside one set of weights, whose configurations form a lattice - meets accumulate a principal's restrictions and joins pool a coalition's reach. We instantiate it by sparse rank gating over an existing nested-factorisation mechanism, guide profile search with one-pass attribution, and read every result once from a pre-registered held-out split. Security composes: provably at meets under a monotone-elicitation assumption we falsify pointwise. In two lineages the median held-out meet deepens suppression; the one effect surviving correction strengthens it. Utility does not: individually harmless profiles can compose to retention and fluency damage, and no compositional bound exists.

</details>


### [212] [UI-Venus-2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00028)
*Venus Team,Zhuohan Cai,Haoxing Chen,Jiaxuan Chen,Weizhi Chen,Changlong Gao,Zhangxuan Gu,Yuan Guo,Yusong Hu,Jianrong Jiang,Jianguo Li,Runze Li,Jinzhen Lin,Zhenyu Ma,Changhua Meng,Han Peng,Xinyu Qiu,Shuheng Shen,Zhongyi Shui,Weiqiang Wang,Ming Wen,Zhuoer Xu,Hang Yan,Kaiwen Yang,Ruilin Yao,Nanjun Yu,Zhengwen Zeng,Lianrui Zhang,Yunzhu Zhang,Zhe Zhao,Beitong Zhou*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出UI-Venus-2，一个跨移动、网页和桌面环境的通用GUI智能体，通过统一闭环推理-行动框架，联合扩展环境覆盖、任务构建和奖励验证三个维度，并集成安全机制，旨在弥合从基准模型到真实应用的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态GUI智能体从基准导向转向真实应用时面临环境覆盖有限、任务构造脆弱、奖励验证不可靠三大挑战，缺乏可依赖的通用基础模型。

Method: 构建统一闭环推理-行动框架；扩展至170余种多语言移动应用及桌面原生系统；采用深度研究流水线生成基于功能的任务；引入基于视觉关键点的轨迹级和样本级评估器，结合多模型投票作为强化学习奖励信号；集成安全感知机制控制高风险动作执行。

Result: 未在摘要中给出具体量化结果，但强调模型具备更强泛化性、可验证性和自我反思能力，且开源、高效、能力全面。

Conclusion: UI-Venus-2为通用GUI智能体提供坚实开源基础，推动其在真实应用中向更可泛化、可验证、可自省的方向发展。

Abstract: Multimodal GUI agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for digital task automation, yet transitioning from benchmark-oriented models to dependable real-world applications remains challenging due to limited environment coverage, brittle task construction, and unreliable reward verification. In this work, we present UI-Venus-2, a general-purpose foundation GUI agent designed to operate across mobile, web, and desktop environments through a unified closed-loop reasoning-action framework. To bridge the gap toward practical deployment, we jointly scale three critical dimensions: (1) Environments, expanding coverage to more than 170 multilingual mobile apps and native desktop operating systems; (2) Tasks, employing a deep-research pipeline for function-grounded instruction generation; and (3) Verification, adopting trace-level and sample-level evaluators with visual keypoints and multi-model voting to ensure reliable RL signals for training. Furthermore, we integrate safety-aware mechanisms to ensure controlled execution of consequential actions. By offering a capable, efficient, and open-source foundation, UI-Venus-2 advances the field toward more generalizable, verifiable, and self-reflective agents for real-world applications.

</details>


### [213] [Does Reasoning Mitigate Backdoor Attacks? A Neuro-Symbolic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00464)
*Marco Antonio Corallo,Andrea Agiollo,Mauro Conti,Alberto Giaretta*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了神经符号（NeSy）模型在后门攻击下的鲁棒性，以DeepProbLog为对象，在八个后门场景和四个推理任务中与基线神经网络对比，发现NeSy平均更鲁棒但取决于推理过程的严格性及其与攻击目标的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeSy AI被视为可实现可信AI的新范式，但其在对抗环境下的性质常被忽视，被想当然地认为‘设计即鲁棒’。然而神经-符号集成过程可能引入额外攻击入口，因此有必要深入调查NeSy模型的对抗鲁棒性，特别是后门攻击。

Method: 选取最流行的NeSy框架DeepProbLog，与基线神经网络对比，覆盖八个后门设置和四个推理任务，进行系统性实验评估，并公开源代码以复现。

Result: 实验显示NeSy模型平均比纯神经网络更鲁棒，但其鲁棒性高度依赖所强制推理过程的严格程度，以及该过程与所选对抗目标的兼容性。

Conclusion: NeSy模型的鲁棒性并非固有属性，需结合具体推理约束进行评估；研究为NeSy安全分析提供了首个基准和实证洞见，并开源代码以促进后续工作。

Abstract: Neuro-Symbolic (NeSy) AI has recently emerged as a novel paradigm to enable trustworthy AI, aiming at integrating sub-symbolic neural perception with grounded symbolic reasoning. The neuro-symbolic integration process that characterizes these models has been proven beneficial to achieve more transparent, explainable and efficient AI systems. Meanwhile, their properties under adversarial settings have been overlooked being frequently deemed robust-by-design. However, the neural-symbolic integration process they leverage constitutes an additional layer of complexity that may provide an attack entry-point. Therefore, in this paper, we claim that an in-depth investigation of the adversarial robustness of NeSy models is necessary and provide the first systematic evaluation of backdoor attacks against NeSy. To this end, we compare the most popular NeSy framework, namely DeepProbLog, against baseline neural networks across a total of eight backdoor settings and four reasoning tasks. Our experimental results show that while NeSy models are indeed more robust than their neural counterpart on average, their robustness vastly depend on the strictness of the reasoning process being enforced and its compatibility with the chosen adversarial target. The source code to reproduce our experiments is made available at https://github.com/marcoantoniocorallo/NeSy-Backdoor.

</details>


### [214] [GlitchLab: A Hardware-in-the-Loop Optimizer for Physical Fault Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00502)
*Tanvir Hossain,Abhinav Mahadevan,Jasper Van Woudenberg,Rajesh Velegalati,Arindam Bhattacharyya*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出GlitchLab在线硬件在环平台，结合强化学习与结构化搜索策略，显著提升物理故障注入的效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理故障注入攻击中，参数空间大、成功设置稀疏且依赖目标，硬件尝试反馈有限，固定测试时间下需高效搜索。

Method: GlitchLab平台将延迟作为时间门控，电压与脉冲宽度作为严重性控制，硬件结果作为结构化反馈；提出RL-Q（基于Q学习的强化学习）进行发现，以及SOBAS（基于结构化结果的模型自适应搜索）用于故障复现。

Result: 两策略在AES、密码与控制流攻击中全部成功；AES与控制流尝试次数减少2-85倍，时间减少26-1237倍；密码任务中基线失败而两策略在5000次内成功；SOBAS复现率提升7.3-21倍，RL-Q识别AES设置多30%。

Conclusion: GlitchLab与所提策略能高效发现并复现故障，优于现有基线，为硬件安全评估提供有力工具。

Abstract: Physical fault injection can turn brief hardware disturbances into security failures such as key recovery, authentication bypass, and unintended control flow. Finding effective faults is difficult because many interacting parameters create a large search space, successful settings are sparse and target-dependent, and each hardware attempt provides limited feedback. Under fixed testing time, efficient search is therefore critical for assessing fault sensitivity.
  We present GlitchLab, an online hardware-in-the-loop platform that treats delay as a timing gate, voltage and pulse duration as severity controls, and hardware outcomes as structured feedback. It implements RL-Q (Q-learning-based reinforcement learning), a structured bandit for discovery, and Structured-Outcome-Based Adaptive Search (SOBAS), a model-based policy for fault reproduction.
  Both policies find a target fault in every AES, password, and control-flow campaign. On AES and control flow, they require 2-85x fewer attempts and 26-1,237x less time than the baselines; on password, both succeed while the baselines fail within 5,000 attempts. After discovery, SOBAS reproduces faults 7.3-21x more often, while RL-Q identifies 30% more distinct AES settings.

</details>


### [215] [The Safeguard Worked. Is the LLM System Safer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00519)
*Pingyu Wu,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 该文指出现有LLM服务安全防护评估指标（拒绝率、攻击成功率等）仅反映测试集上的局部性能，无法回答部署后攻击者持续适应或换路径时实际残留危害的问题；证据要求不对称，一次成功攻击即可证明危害存在，而证明‘残留很少’需系统级证据，但深度编码记录中仅少数支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全防护研究以局部分数提升为目标，但部署方真正关心的是攻击者适应后系统仍能提供多少有害帮助。局部指标与部署安全性之间存在断层，需明确二者关系以统一比较不同防护方法。

Method: 通过逻辑分析确立部署判据（残留有害帮助程度），对比单一攻击成功与系统性低残留所需的证据强度，并对深度编码的声明进行统计，检验其是否提供支持或推导系统级证据。

Result: 发现证据要求极不对称：一次攻击得手即可否定安全性，而残留少量有害帮助的声明需额外系统证据，但带深度编码的声明中仅少数提供此类证据，且其中只有一个声明界定了其残留范围。

Conclusion: 局部安全分数提升不等于部署更安全；防护研究须转向验证系统级安全性，否则进步难以转化为实际保障。

Abstract: Safeguards in deployed LLM services are evaluated by refusal, attack success, and policy violation rates. Those rates characterize how a control performed on the requests it was tested on. A deployment has to answer a different question: how much help with harmful tasks the service still gives an attacker who keeps adapting or finds another way in. We determine what each reported result implies for that question, allowing results from different safeguard families to be compared under one deployment criterion. The evidence requirements are strongly asymmetric. One attack that obtains harmful help from the deployed service suffices to establish that such help remains, and such attacks appear repeatedly in the coded record. Establishing that little remains cannot follow from the safeguard's own numbers alone; it also requires evidence about what the surrounding system still allows after the safeguard performs its local function. Such evidence is supported or derived in only a small minority of the depth-coded claims, and one such claim bounds its scoped residual. A better local score is therefore not, by itself, a stronger claim about the deployment. Safeguard research cannot stop at raising local scores; a gain has to be judged by whether it makes a deployed system any safer.

</details>


### [216] [Safin-1: Safety from Within through Memory-Native State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00092)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Zhekai Chen,Cheng Jin,Jingnan Zheng,Yi Zhang,Zhongtian Ma,Jiawei Zhou,Sirui Chen,Qiaosheng Zhang,Xiang Wang,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Youbang Sun,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Safety from Within理念，通过Safin-1模型中的MARCH架构实现安全作为模型内在状态而非外部约束，支持测试时状态适应并验证了安全性与性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程复杂任务要求基础模型在扩展交互中积累信息并适应，但安全性不应依赖外部护栏或事后对齐（如SFT），而应内生于模型原生计算。

Method: Safin-1采用MARCH网络架构，维护结构化记忆状态并通过内容条件路由选择性检索历史信息；进一步引入Safety State接口，在不重复修改主干的前提下实现测试时能力状态的适应。

Result: 在安全任务上通过状态适应取得显著安全改进，同时在通用能力、长上下文理解、检索和效率评估中均验证了模型有效性。

Conclusion: 安全可视为一种状态原生且可自适应维护的能力，该工作仅为架构性探索，距离完整实现Safety from Within仍需大量后续研究。

Abstract: Long-horizon complex tasks require foundation models to accumulate information, maintain internal states, and adapt over extended interactions. Safety should be an intrinsic property of the model itself, rather than a behavioral constraint relying solely on external safeguards or post-hoc alignment such as supervised fine-tuning. This motivates Safety from Within, where safety-relevant capabilities are represented and invoked through the model's native computation. We present Safin-1, a family of foundation models realizing this principle through memory routing and state evolution. Safin-1 is built on Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that maintains structured memory states and selectively retrieves relevant historical information through content-conditioned routing. It supports test-time adaptation of persistent capability states without repeatedly modifying the backbone, enabling controlled specialization over a shared foundation. We investigate this interface on downstream safety tasks through a Safety State, demonstrating effective state-based adaptation with substantial safety improvements. More broadly, the routed-state interface unifies contextual memory and persistent capability adaptation within the model's native computation, reframing memory from a passive record of prior context into an active substrate for maintaining and evolving model behavior. Evaluations across general capabilities, long-context understanding, retrieval, and efficiency further validate Safin-1. These findings provide a path toward safety as a state-native and adaptively maintainable capability. This work is only an initial architectural exploration of Safety from Within, and substantial further work is needed to realize this broader vision.

</details>


### [217] [Transferable End-to-End Optimization for Indirect Long-Term Memory Poisoning in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00523)
*Chuanchao Zang,Jianing Wang,Wenyu Chen,Xiangtao Meng,Li Wang,Xinyu Gao,Zheng Li,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出PipePoison，将间接记忆投毒视为端到端优化问题，通过链式损失定位并优化瓶颈阶段，利用稳定性校准权重提升跨配置迁移性，在多个智能体框架和记忆机制下显著提升攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有攻击多采用阶段内优化，忽视记忆写入、检索、利用各阶段间的耦合性——各阶段对同一投毒内容要求不同且基于前序输出操作，导致单阶段优化可能损害整体效果，上游变换也可能抹除下游改进。

Method: PipePoison从本地影子系统收集细粒度阶段反馈，使用链式结构损失识别并优化制约端到端成功的瓶颈阶段，并应用稳定性校准的阶段与配置权重增强攻击在不同受害者配置下的迁移性。

Result: 在三个智能体框架和四种记忆机制上，PipePoison将攻击利用率提升19.1个百分点；在完全未见过的受害者配置上比最强基线高16个百分点，并在八种代表性防御下仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: 间接记忆投毒应作为端到端问题处理，PipePoison证明了跨阶段协同优化和稳定性校准能有效应对阶段耦合与配置迁移挑战，为记忆投毒攻击提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-term memory can turn untrusted external content into persistent influence over an LLM agent's future decisions, creating the threat of indirect memory poisoning. A successful attack must survive a multi-stage pipeline comprising memory writing, retrieval, and utilization. Existing attacks largely rely on intra-stage optimization, optimizing individual stages in isolation while overlooking inter-stage coupling. Specifically, these stages impose different requirements on the same poisoning content, and each stage operates on the transformed output of its predecessor. Consequently, optimizing one stage may undermine the effectiveness of other stages, while upstream transformations may erase improvements intended for downstream stages. Indirect memory poisoning should therefore be viewed as an end-to-end optimization problem. Based on this insight, we present \textsc{PipePoison}, which collects fine-grained stage feedback from local shadow systems, uses chain-structured losses to identify and optimize the stage bottlenecking end-to-end success, and applies stability-calibrated stage and configuration weights to improve transferability. Across three agent frameworks and four memory mechanisms, \textsc{PipePoison} improves attack utilization rate by 19.1 percentage points. Even on fully unseen victim configurations, it outperforms the strongest baseline by 16 percentage points and remains effective under eight representative defenses.

</details>


### [218] [AI Morbidity and Mortality: A Framework for Clinical AI Failure Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00076)
*Paulius Mui,Dean F. Sittig,Steve Labkoff,Sanjay Basu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出AI发病率和死亡率（AI M&M）框架，用于对临床AI故障进行结构化、无指责的案例审查，强调从个别错误中学习以改善患者安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全机制（如聚合模型监测和传统患者安全报告）无法解释AI与临床医生、工作流程及制度控制交互中风险的产生机制，缺乏针对个体AI相关错误和未遂事件的系统重建与学习工具。

Method: 构建AI M&M框架，包括标准化案例接收、证据保存、研究者级重建、工具循环归因及纠正措施跟踪，并将每个事件按触发点-机制-临床路径-纠正措施四个维度分类，通过五个案例和两名评审员独立应用验证其可行性。

Result: 两名评审员对五个案例的所有20个维度分类达成一致，证明框架分类维度具有可靠性和可操作性。

Conclusion: AI M&M作为模型监测、安全报告和监管的补充工具，可将个体AI工作流失败转化为可操作的组织学习；需跨机构、跨系统和跨临床环境的前瞻性评估确认其通用性。

Abstract: Clinical artificial intelligence is increasingly embedded in real-world care, yet existing safety mechanisms are poorly suited to reconstructing and learning from individual AI-related errors and near-misses. Aggregate model monitoring can identify performance changes, and traditional patient safety reporting can capture adverse events, but neither is designed to explain how risk emerges across the interaction among AI systems, clinicians, workflows, and institutional controls. We propose AI Morbidity and Mortality (AI M&M), a structured, blameless framework for case-based review of clinical AI failures. The framework combines standardized case intake, evidence preservation and investigator-level reconstruction, tool-in-loop attribution, and corrective-action tracking. Each event is classified across four linked dimensions: Trigger - Mechanism - Clinical Pathway - Corrective Action, separating the condition that exposed a vulnerability from the process that produced risk, its consequence for care, and the remediation assigned. We demonstrate the framework using five illustrative outpatient medication and clinical decision-support cases; two clinician reviewers independently applied all four classification axes and reached agreement across all 20 axis-level classifications. AI M&M is intended to complement, rather than replace, model monitoring, patient safety reporting, and regulatory oversight by converting individual AI-in-workflow failures into actionable institutional learning. Prospective evaluation across institutions, AI systems, and clinical settings is needed.

</details>


### [219] [SoK: When Safe Agents Fail Together: The Security of Multi Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00595)
*Rui Yang,Junjie Xu,Zhengyu Liu,Neil Fendley,Yang Hong,Ziyang Li,Yinzhi Cao*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 通过执行层面的分析，系统化多智能体LLM系统（MAS）的安全问题，提出A-I-R攻击框架和五部分防御契约，并审计现有评估工作，强调交互感知的安全视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在跨主体边界传递信息、状态、决策和权限时，局部安全检查可能遗漏系统级故障，导致安全失效被误判为多智能体特有现象，缺乏执行层面的系统化分析。

Method: 对197篇工作做执行中心分析，覆盖六种交互接口、四种对抗者位置、七种系统级风险和八种攻击路径；提出A-I-R框架（对抗位置-接口-风险）统一攻击机制，并用五部分契约（路径目标、观察、干预、信任边界、恢复）组织防御，同时审计44个评估基准。

Result: 识别出路径闭合与恢复是防御关键挑战；现有评估在隔离交互效应、设计可比诊断指标、支持跨设计复用及开放系统评估方面存在缺口。

Conclusion: MAS安全需采用交互感知视角：端到端追踪攻击、测试防御是否闭合攻击路径，并用适当反事实评估系统级效果。

Abstract: Safe agents can fail together. Multi-agent LLM systems (MAS) move information, state, decisions, and authority across principal boundaries, creating failures that local checks may miss. Without an execution-level view, a multi-agent setting can easily be mistaken for evidence of a genuinely multi-agent security effect. We thus systematize MAS security through an execution-centered analysis of 197 works, covering six interaction interfaces, four adversary positions, seven system-level risks, and eight recurring attack paths. We introduce an A-I-R framework that organizes attacks by adversary position, interaction interface, and resulting system-level risk, unifying otherwise fragmented attack mechanisms across MAS. We organize defenses through a five-part contract covering path target, observation, intervention, trust boundary, and recovery, and identify path closure and recovery as key challenges. We audit 44 evaluation and benchmark works and identify open challenges in isolating interaction effects, designing comparable and diagnostic metrics, supporting reuse across MAS designs, and evaluating open-system operation. Together, these findings motivate an interaction-aware view of MAS security: trace attacks end to end, test whether defenses close those paths, and evaluate system-level effects with appropriate counterfactuals.

</details>


### [220] [NeuroGraph: An AI Graph-Driven Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Explainable Threat Reasoning in Advanced Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00604)
*Padmeswari Nandiya,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Iqbal H. Sarker,Helge Janicke*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种图基神经符号框架，通过双大语言模型将自然语言问题转化为Cypher查询并从知识图谱检索证据生成答案，在减少幻觉、增强多跳推理和对抗鲁棒性方面优于现有基线，支持IT/OT环境的可解释威胁分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本和RAG的威胁情报分析易产生幻觉，缺乏对互联威胁的结构化推理与透明证据追踪，而图RAG方法缺少本体一致的多跳推理支持。

Method: 采用双大语言模型架构：第一模型将自然语言问题转化为可执行的Cypher查询进行符号图检索，第二模型仅依据检索到的图证据生成答案，实现神经符号集成。

Result: 在公开CTI基准上，推理准确率较基线一致提升，幻觉减少，多跳推理增强，对对抗扰动鲁棒性提高，且运行时性能保持交互级，提供可检查的证据追踪。

Conclusion: 图基神经符号推理可作为工业5.0环境下可扩展、可解释且可靠的威胁情报分析方法，支持分析员分阶段验证。

Abstract: The growing complexity of cyber-physical attack surfaces in advanced manufacturing has made cyber threat intelligence analysis increasingly difficult. Although large language models and retrieval-augmented generation have improved CTI workflows, text-based approaches remain vulnerable to hallucinations and provide limited support for structured reasoning over interconnected threats. Graph-based RAG reduces some of these limitations, but existing approaches often lack ontology-consistent multi-hop reasoning and transparent evidence tracing across heterogeneous cybersecurity data. This paper proposes a graph-grounded neuro-symbolic framework that integrates ontology-aware symbolic query generation, knowledge graph retrieval, and neural language generation to support accurate and explainable threat analysis across information technology and operational technology environments. The framework adopts a dual-large language model architecture: the first model translates natural-language questions into executable Cypher queries for symbolic graph retrieval, while the second generates answers strictly from the retrieved graph evidence. Experimental evaluation using publicly available cyber threat intelligence benchmarks shows consistent improvements over the published baseline in reasoning accuracy, while also reducing hallucinations, strengthening multi-hop reasoning, and improving robustness to adversarial perturbations. Runtime and explainability analyses further demonstrate that the framework maintains interactive inference performance and exposes graph-grounded reasoning artifacts that allow analysts to inspect and verify each stage of the analysis. Overall, the results highlight the potential of graph-grounded neuro-symbolic reasoning as a scalable, interpretable, and reliable approach to cyber threat intelligence for next-generation Industry 5.0 environments.

</details>


### [221] [Generative artificial intelligence for reliable mechanistic reasoning for corrosion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00099)
*Bharath M N,R K Singh Raman,Alankar Alankar*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一种领域适配的检索增强生成框架用于腐蚀知识综合，在镁合金腐蚀数据集上微调三种开源语言模型，结合混合检索，使Token F1提升143-194%，系统忠实度0.964，并引入Reason Map命题图框架检测因果反转和证据跳跃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腐蚀约占全球GDP的4%，可靠预测对及时缓解至关重要。机器学习虽能预测腐蚀速率但无法解释机制，而安全关键材料工程需要机制上可辩护的推理，现有事实性指标无法评估该能力。

Method: 构建领域适配的RAG框架，微调三种开源模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen-2.5-7B、Mistral-7B）于3309个专家验证问答对，集成混合稠密-词法检索，并引入Reason Map命题图框架构建有向证据图以检测逻辑缺陷。

Result: 检索增强使Token F1提升143-194%，系统忠实度0.964，上下文召回率0.988；盲法外部验证确认趋势级泛化；Reason Map可系统性检测因果方向反转和证据不足的推理跳跃。

Conclusion: 模块化架构适用于多领域，为可信AI辅助知识综合提供了通用蓝图，可扩展至腐蚀及其他工程领域。

Abstract: Corrosion accounts for approximately 4% of global GDP, and reliable prediction is essential for timely mitigation. Machine learning effectively predicts corrosion rates from composition, microstructure, and environmental variables, but cannot explain the underlying mechanisms. A reliable approach in safety-critical materials engineering requires not only accurate retrieval but also mechanistically defensible reasoning, a capability that existing factuality metrics cannot assess. This work presents a domain-adapted retrieval-augmented generation framework for corrosion knowledge synthesis, demonstrated on magnesium alloy corrosion. Three open-weight language models (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B, Mistral-7B) are fine-tuned on 3,309 expert-verified question-answer pairs from 840 peer-reviewed papers and integrated with a hybrid dense-lexical retrieval pipeline. Retrieval augmentation produces Token F1 gains of 143-194%, with system faithfulness of 0.964 and context recall of 0.988. Blind external validation on newly published literature and in-house electrochemical data confirms trend-level generalisation. Reason Map, a proposition-graph framework, is further introduced; it independently constructs directed evidence graphs from generated answers and retrieved literature, enabling systematic detection of causal direction inversions and unsupported inferential leaps that flat factuality metrics cannot expose. The modular architecture can be applied across domains, offering a generalizable blueprint for trustworthy AI-assisted knowledge synthesis to circumvent corrosion, which can also be applied to other engineering domains.

</details>


### [222] [Automating Static Code Analysis Through CI/CD Pipeline Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00676)
*Zachary Wadhams,Ann Marie Reinhold,Clemente Izurieta*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动化聚合SAST工具输出并集成到开发者熟悉的问题跟踪软件中的通用流程，以提升软件开发生命周期中安全漏洞的识别与修复效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAST工具虽能检测漏洞，但高误报率和缺乏原生流水线支持阻碍了其广泛采用，导致安全漏洞难以及时发现和修复。

Method: 设计并实现了一种通用自动化流程，将SAST工具（以SonarQube为例）的输出聚合后集成到GitLab开发环境的问题跟踪系统中，实现漏洞的实时反馈与结构化沟通。

Result: 在GitLab环境中成功实施该流程，开发者反馈积极，认为其提升了安全意识和响应速度，但也存在学习曲线及安全编码与工作流中断之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该流程虽面临一定挑战，但整体上能有效增强软件开发实践中的安全态势，具有推广潜力。

Abstract: In the contemporary landscape of software devel-opment, securing sensitive data is paramount to safeguarding organizational reputation, preventing financial losses, and pro-tecting individuals from identity theft. This paper addresses the pervasive challenge of identifying and rectifying security vulnerabilities early in the development process, emphasizing the role of Static Application Security Testing (SAST) tools. While SAST tools play a crucial role in detecting vulnerabilities, widespread adoption has been hindered by usability issues, including high false positive rates and a lack of native pipeline support. This paper proposes a novel, generalized, and automated process for aggregating SAST tool outputs and integrating them into developers' familiar issue-tracking software. The process streamlines the identification and communication of security vulnerabilities during the development lifecycle, facilitating more efficient remediation efforts. We demonstrate the successful implementation of the proposed process with the SonarQube SAST tool in a GitLab-based development environment. Developers were positive about the structured implementation, real-time feedback, and proactive vulnerability management. However, despite some challenges such as a potential learning curve and tradeoffs between secure coding and workflow disruption, the overall positive impact on security awareness and responsiveness suggests that the proposed process holds promise in enhancing the security posture of software development practices

</details>


### [223] [PhantomCall: Evading ML Malware Detectors via Function Call Graph Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00705)
*Md Ajwad Akil,Adrian Shuai Li,Imtiaz Karim,Arun Iyengar,Ashish Kundu,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出黑盒攻击PhantomCall，通过注入可执行哑函数扰动PE文件的函数调用图（FCG），在保持语义的同时高效绕过多种架构的恶意软件检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击针对原始字节、PE头或函数内控制流图，未探索函数调用图（FCG）这一攻击面，而FCG是图基检测器的重要特征。

Method: 在调用点注入可执行哑函数，同时增加CFG和FCG节点与边，结合分类器引导搜索和可调注入参数，实现结构扰动。

Result: 在2025年恶意软件语料库上，对MalConv、MalGraph和SAFE+GNN三种检测器，最佳变体达到85-100%攻击成功率，超过现有最优方法最多14.78（MalGraph）和95.5（SAFE+GNN）个百分点，生成速度平均快2.9倍，且多数逃逸仅需单点修改，86-97%的变体保持恶意行为。

Conclusion: FCG是有效的攻击面，PhantomCall能高效生成语义保持且可迁移的规避样本，揭示了图基检测器的结构脆弱性。

Abstract: Prior adversarial attacks on Windows PE malware detectors target raw bytes, PE headers, or intra-function control-flow graphs, leaving the function call graph (FCG) unexplored as an attack surface. Yet the FCG structure is an important feature in graph-based malware detectors. We present Phan- tomCall, a black-box attack that perturbs the FCG of Windows PE malware by injecting fully executable dummy functions at targeted call sites, adding new nodes and edges to both the CFG and FCG while preserving program semantics. We pair this structural perturbation with classifier-guided search and tunable injection parameters, effective across three archi- tecturally distinct classifiers. Evaluated on a 2025-collected Windows malware corpus against MalConv (raw-byte CNN), MalGraph (graph-based GNN), and SAFE+GNN (pure FCG GNN trained from scratch on a 2024 corpus) at two FPR thresholds, the best PhantomCall variant achieves 85-100% attack success rate across all configurations, exceeding prior state-of-the-art by up to 14.78 percentage points on MalGraph and 95.5 percentage points on SAFE+GNN, and generating evasive variants up to 2.9x faster on average across all targets. For MalConv and MalGraph, the majority of evasions require only a single call site modification, and 86-97% of evaluated evasive variants preserve the original malicious behavior in sandbox-based semantic testing across all configurations.

</details>


### [224] [SoK: Motion Data Privacy in Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00711)
*Azim Ibragimov,Alina Vasina,Uliana Polshcha,Eric D. Ragan*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 对XR头显捕获的运动模式隐私进行了系统化综述，涵盖134篇论文，提出威胁模型与防御分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR运动追踪产生隐私风险，且相关文献大幅增长，但此前缺乏针对运动隐私的专项系统化研究。

Method: 筛选134篇论文，从攻击获取、推断及防御三方面建立运动模态/表示/风险分类法，开发威胁模型并识别研究空白。

Result: 形成了系统化攻击与防御方法框架，并提供评估指南。

Conclusion: 该SoK明确了XR运动隐私现状，并为未来机制评估提供了建议。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) provides immersive, interactive 3D experiences. To enable these experiences, the devices must track user motion so the system can respond to actions such as grabbing, looking at, or moving an object. However, motion tracking has raised privacy concerns since it records a person's motion patterns. These motion patterns have been studied extensively across various fields (i.e., gait identification and profiling) and have been shown to reveal sensitive information. With the adoption of XR, these patterns became easier to record and obtain than ever. This creates a fundamental privacy tension: motion tracking enables core XR functionality yet requires users to compromise their privacy. Prior systematization-of-knowledge (SoK) studies on XR privacy have examined the field broadly, with motion-related research distributed across several privacy domains rather than treated as a distinct area of study. However, XR motion privacy has gained significant momentum since the prior SoK, with the literature nearly quadrupling in size and thereby warranting a dedicated systematization of this topic. This SoK examines 134 relevant papers on privacy concerns in motion patterns recorded by XR headsets, including how adversaries can obtain users' motion patterns, the inferences they can draw from them, and methods for protecting users. Based on this review, we synthesize a taxonomy of motion modalities, representations, and inference risks; develop an XR motion threat model; systematize the attack and defense approaches in the XR motion literature; identify gaps in the literature; and provide guidelines for future studies evaluating motion privacy mechanisms. Together, our SoK clarifies the state of XR motion privacy and provides recommendations for future evaluations.

</details>


### [225] [MROP: Mask-Region Optimized Purification Against Backdoor Attack in Deep JSCC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00786)
*Seongkyu Yang,Hyeonho Noh,Hyun Jong Yang,Jonggyu Jang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出MROP方法，通过Gumbel-sigmoid松弛优化逐像素掩码，在推理时定位并净化输入补丁触发器，降低深度JSCC后门攻击成功率，同时保持干净图像重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度联合信源信道编码（JSCC）的图像传输DNN流程如同黑箱，无法检测输入补丁后门攻击导致的安全漏洞；现有防御多针对分类任务，不适用于重建场景。

Method: 将梯度掩码防御适配为基线，并提出MROP：在编码器输入上放置逐像素掩码，利用Gumbel-sigmoid松弛优化定位触发区域，进而净化触发器以重建纯净图像，无需重训练模型。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和STL-10数据集上结合DeepJSCC及SwinJSCC模型验证，显著降低攻击成功率（ASR），同时保持干净重建的峰值信噪比（PSNR）。

Conclusion: MROP有效防御深度JSCC的补丁后门攻击，兼顾安全性与重建性能，为重构场景下的推理时防御提供了新思路。

Abstract: Deep joint source and channel coding (JSCC) transmits a source by mapping it directly to channel symbols through an end-to-end deep neural network (DNN) and reconstructing it at the receiver. Taking image transmission as an application, this DNN pipeline behaves as a black box: the receiver cannot readily detect security attacks when the transmitted images are corrupted, thereby introducing a new security vulnerability.
