In this letter, we study defense against input-patch backdoor attacks on deep JSCC, in which a small trigger patch attached to the input forces the decoder to emit an attacker-chosen target image. Most existing patch-trigger defenses are designed for classification, leaving the reconstruction setting of deep JSCC unaddressed. We adapt the gradient mask defense to this reconstruction setting as a baseline and then propose mask-region optimized purification (MROP), which operates at inference and requires no retraining of the JSCC model. Unlike the baseline, which localizes the trigger from the input--output gradient, MROP instead places a per-pixel mask at the encoder input and optimizes it via a Gumbel-sigmoid relaxation to localize the trigger, then refines the trigger region to reconstruct the pure images better. In numerical results, we evaluate the proposed method on CIFAR-10 and STL-10 datasets along with the DeepJSCC and SwinJSCC models. By doing so, we show that the proposed method substantially lowers the attack success rate (ASR) while preserving the peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) of clean reconstructions.

</details>


### [226] [RISA: Response Inspection and Selective Actions for Refusal Calibration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00790)
*Wenhan Chang,Tianqing Zhu,Ping Xiong,Shiyi Liao,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: RISA是一种推理时选择性干预框架，先检查LLM的初始响应，仅当拒绝行为错误时才进行校准干预，在不更新模型参数的前提下提升拒绝可靠性并保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法未先判断初始响应是否正确就干预，可能破坏正确的拒绝或有用回答。需要识别超越敏感关键词的语义意图、适应不同基础模型的验证器，以及选择性干预机制。

Method: RISA先应用固定上下文规则对明确案例打分，未匹配案例则用校准的线性探针从最终层提示隐状态推导拒绝分数。针对不同基础模型，分别校准探针分数、表示支持边界和动作阈值。运行时结合提示分数与初始拒绝状态，仅在必要时执行动作策略干预。

Result: 实验表明RISA提升了拒绝可靠性，同时很大程度上保留了模型效用。

Conclusion: RISA为LLM中响应感知的拒绝校准提供了一种实用的推理时解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable refusal behavior requires Large Language Models (LLMs) to reject harmful prompts with only answering benign ones. Incorrect refusal behavior can either expose users to harmful responses or prevent users from obtaining useful answers. Training-time alignment improves refusal behavior by updating model parameters with safety data, but requires additional computation and training. In contrast, inference-time alignment aims to modify LLM behavior during inference without updating the underlying model parameters. Existing inference-time methods mainly rely on in-context safety prompting, activation steering, or decoding control. However, most of them intervene without first determining whether the initial response is already appropriate, potentially altering a correct refusal or a useful answer. Effective selective intervention therefore requires identifying prompt intent beyond sensitive keywords, covering semantic variations that fixed rules may miss, and adapting the verifier to different base models. To address these challenges, we propose Response Inspection and Selective Actions (RISA), an inference-time framework that inspects the initial response and selectively corrects refusal errors without updating the base model. RISA first uses fixed contextual rules to assign refusal scores to clear cases. For unmatched cases, it derives a refusal score from the final-layer prompt hidden state using a calibrated linear probe. To adapt to different base models, RISA separately calibrates the probe score, representation-support boundary, and action thresholds. At runtime, RISA combines the prompt score with the initial refusal status and applies an action policy to intervene only when necessary. Experimental results demonstrate that RISA improves refusal reliability while largely preserving model utility, offering a practical solution for response-aware refusal calibration in LLMs.

</details>


### [227] [Using LLMs to Elicit Security Requirements for Service-Oriented Cyber Ranges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00886)
*Michail Takaronis,Athanasia Kollarou,Georgios Kavallieratos,Vasileios Gkioulos,Sokratis Katsikas*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文探索利用大语言模型辅助面向服务的网络靶场（SOR）安全需求 elicitation，通过SEBoK引导流程生成并整合需求，专家评估显示高接受率但可测试性较低，表明LLM能支持早期需求分析但仍需人工审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络靶场（尤其是服务导向型SOR）及其关键基础设施训练场景涉及多组件和多利益相关方，安全需求 elicitation 通常困难且耗时，因此需要自动化工具辅助生成初始需求基线，以减轻设计者和开发者的负担。

Method: 采用基于SEBoK的引导流程：先识别安全任务目标和利益相关方需求，将其与架构指南一起作为提示上下文，输入5个LLM（GPT-5.2、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Grok 4.1、Sonar、Kimi K2.5），生成84条需求后整合为27条，映射至SOR架构层，再由5位网络安全专家按必要性、清晰性、完整性、可行性、可测试性及拒绝选项进行评分。

Result: 专家评估显示：必要性接受率98.5%、清晰度87.4%、完整性85.2%、可行性78.5%、拒绝率0.7%，但可测试性仅44.4%，说明需求缺乏测试方法相关的具体信息。

Conclusion: LLM能有效支持安全需求 elicitation 的早期阶段，产出高质量基线，但需人工审查以改进可测试性等薄弱环节，确保需求可验证性和完备性。

Abstract: Cyber ranges are complex environments comprising many interacting components and stakeholders with different security concerns. The Service-Oriented Cyber Range (SOR) is no exception, particularly when it comes to training scenarios targeting critical infrastructure. Security concerns are translated into security requirements, the elicitation of which is usually difficult and time-consuming. This work examines how large language models can assist in eliciting security requirements for a service-oriented range and help produce a useful baseline for designers and developers. The approach follows a SEBoK-guided process in which security mission objectives and stakeholder needs were first identified and then provided as a prompt context along with architectural guidelines to five LLMs: GPT-5.2, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Grok 4.1, Sonar, and Kimi K2.5. The models generated 84 security requirements in total, which were consolidated into a comprehensive set of 27 requirements and then mapped to the architectural layers of the service-oriented range. The final set was evaluated by five cybersecurity experts against the criteria of necessity, clarity, completeness, feasibility, and testability, with an additional rejection option. The results showed a high acceptance rate, specifically for necessity with 98.5%, clarity with 87.4%, completeness with 85.2%, feasibility with 78.5%, and rejection with 0.7%. Testability was lower at 44.4%, indicating a slight lack of information on how these requirements could be tested. These findings show that LLMs can support early stages of the elicitation of security requirements, although human review is still needed, especially to improve or adjust certain aspects of the requirements.

</details>


### [228] [Counterfactual Fragility Certificates: Exposing High-Confidence Brittleness under Structured Evidence Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00366)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型无关的协议级审计证书CFC，将每个预测映射为有序证据失效轨迹，并给出多个脆弱性汇总指标，用于识别高置信度但结构脆弱的样本；在7个表格基准上，其脆弱性优势评分比最强非证书基线AUROC提升0.405，且在20%审查预算下可捕获88.9%的脆弱高置信度案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高测试准确率和良好聚合校准无法反映单个预测是否在证据层面得到结构支持；当特征族不可用、延迟、噪声、过时或低信任时，模型可能仍高度自信，导致决策失败。现有标量评分或归因图不提供可复算审计对象，无法回答在声明的证据失效协议下预测何时失去支持。

Method: 提出反事实脆弱性证书CFC，它是模型无关的协议级审计产物，为每个预测生成有序证据失效轨迹，并通过贪心翻转预算、归一化边距崩溃面积、退化阈值和脆弱性优势评分等指标进行汇总。

Result: 在七个表格基准及线性、树、提升、神经网络基线上，CFC-FDS识别独立脆弱高置信度案例的AUROC达0.915，较最强非证书评分提升+0.405；在20%审查预算下捕获88.9%脆弱高置信度案例，而置信度和能量分数仅为31.8-37.4%；优势在扰动、排列重要性、组SHAP、基线选择、种子方差、预算审查及自然字段不可用检查中均保持。

Conclusion: CFC提供了一个可复算的可靠性框架，能揭示普通评分中心评估遗漏的高置信度脆弱性，并可作为脆弱性感知正则化与脆弱性感知温度校正等次级用途的基础。

Abstract: High test accuracy and good aggregate calibration do not show whether an individual prediction is structurally supported by its evidence. In tabular decision systems, failures often occur when a feature family becomes unavailable, delayed, noisy, stale, or low-trust while the model remains highly confident. Existing calibration, uncertainty, selective-prediction, explanation, and perturbation methods provide scalar scores or attribution maps, but not a recomputable audit object answering: under a declared evidence-failure protocol, what trajectory makes this prediction lose support? We introduce Counterfactual Fragility Certificates (CFC), a model-agnostic protocol-level audit certificate-not a formal robustness certificate-that maps each prediction into an ordered evidence-failure trajectory summarized by greedy flip budget, normalized margin-collapse area, degradation thresholds, and fragility dominance score. Across seven tabular benchmarks and strong linear, tree-based, boosting, and neural baselines, CFC-FDS identifies independently brittle high-confidence cases with 0.915 AUROC, improving over the strongest non-certificate score by +0.405. The advantage persists across perturbation, permutation-importance, group-SHAP, baseline-choice, seed-variance, budgeted-review, and naturalistic field-unavailability checks. Under a 20% review budget, CFC-FDS captures 88.9% of brittle high-confidence cases, compared with 31.8-37.4% for confidence and energy scores. We also evaluate fragility-aware regularization and brittleness-aware temperature correction as secondary uses. CFC provides a concrete reliability framework for exposing high-confidence brittleness missed by ordinary score-centric evaluation.

</details>


### [229] [Influence of Logging Frameworks on Bind9](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00954)
*Max Schrötter,Hannes Signer,Bettina Schnor*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出FIPS，一种绕过内核、基于无锁共享内存环形缓冲区的高性能日志IPC，使基于日志的主机入侵防御系统（如Fail2Ban监控BIND9）在高流量下不被日志瓶颈击败，相比文件日志可2.5倍更快封禁恶意客户端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主机入侵防御系统依赖应用日志检测恶意行为，但现代高速网络下日志子系统本身成为瓶颈，攻击者可通过高流量淹没日志管道来隐藏痕迹。现有广泛部署如Fail2Ban+BIND9在低于65 Mbps的DNS流量下即可被击败，即便用eBPF替代iptables、Hyperscan替代正则匹配，日志后端仍成新瓶颈。

Method: 设计FIPS：一种专为高性能日志设计的进程间通信机制，绕过内核并将消息复制降至最低。采用每线程无锁共享内存环形缓冲区，支持多个独立消费者按各自速度读取同一日志流；提供原生API及syslog接口的即插即用替代。

Result: 用BIND 9评估显示，FIPS相比禁用日志几乎无开销，记录请求数超过其他所有被评测框架；在一百万请求每秒、2^16个攻击客户端持续流量下，使IPS封禁恶意客户端的速度比文件日志快2.5倍。

Conclusion: FIPS通过消除日志后端瓶颈，可有效防御基于流量淹没的日志隐藏攻击，在保持高日志吞吐的同时提升IPS响应速度，是一种实用且高效的日志基础设施方案。

Abstract: Host-based Intrusion Prevention Systems (IPS) rely on application logs to detect and block malicious activity. However, on modern high-speed networks the logging subsystem itself becomes a bottleneck: an attacker can hide traces simply by generating enough traffic to overwhelm the application's log pipeline, dropping crucial traces. In this work, we show that widely deployed setups such as Fail2Ban monitoring BIND9 can be defeated with less than 65 Mbps of DNS traffic. Further, we show that when replacing core components of the IPS architecture with their higher-performance equivalent, iptables with eBPF and regex matching with Hyperscan, the logging backends themselves become the bottleneck. Therefore, we present FIPS, a new IPC designed for high-performance logging that bypasses the kernel and reduces copying of the log messages to a minimum. FIPS uses per-thread lock free shared memory ring buffers, supporting multiple independent consumers reading the same log stream at their own pace. FIPS offers both a native API and a drop-in replacement for the syslog interface. Our evaluation with BIND 9 shows that FIPS introduces almost no overhead compared to disabled logging, logs more requests than any other evaluated framework, and enables the IPS to ban malicious clients $2.5\times$ faster than with file logging while sustaining $2^{16}$ attacking clients at one million requests per second.

</details>


### [230] [Adapting Without Gradients: Affine Statistics Transport and What Its Certificate Can Tell You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00374)
*Salim Khazem,Ibrahim Mohamed Serouis*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出CASTRE，一种无需梯度的测试时适应方法，通过仿射变换源类别统计量适配目标批次，在冻结模型上超越k-NN且仅需少量状态，并引入传输能力证书以规避无效适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA依赖推理时更新参数，不适用于冻结模型、无BatchNorm架构及内存受限部署，且梯度方法需反向传播和优化器状态，因此需开发无梯度、无存储源特征库的轻量适应方案。

Method: CASTRE：存储源类别统计量于判别子空间，利用目标批次矩估计类别共享仿射变换，在分类前解析地传输源类别分布；并基于残差到间隔的经验证书（阈值约3.9）对传输实施门控，避免破坏性适应。

Result: 在4个骨干网络和7个数据集上，27/28设置优于k-NN，状态量中位数减少18倍；在ImageNet-C 64样本批次下，无条件传输损失21.2点，而门控将平均-3.35点效应转为+1.69点增益；证书对Tent仅接受4.3%更新，保留其0.6%增益。

Conclusion: CASTRE提供轻量的冻结模型适应机制，且其安全信号在特定条件下（如充足批次样本）有效，但在极端稀疏批次或非匹配机制（如Tent）下需谨慎，须明确其适用范围。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) typically assumes that model parameters can be updated at inference time. This assumption is restrictive for inference-only accelerators, frozen or third-party models, and memory-constrained deployments, and standard BatchNorm-based TTA configurations may also become inactive on architectures without BatchNorm. We study adaptation when the learned model must remain frozen. We introduce CASTER, a gradient-free method that stores source class statistics in a discriminative subspace, estimates a class-shared affine transformation from target-batch moments, and analytically transports the source class distributions before classification. CASTER requires no backward pass, optimizer state, or stored source feature bank. Across four backbones and seven datasets, it outperforms k-NN on identical frozen features in 27 of 28 backbone-dataset settings while retaining a median of 18x less state. Affine transport is not always reliable. On ImageNet-C, where batches contain only 64 samples for 1000 classes, unconditional transport loses 21.2 top-1 points. We therefore introduce an empirical residual-to-margin transportability certificate. Across 307 evaluation cells, every transport losing more than 10 points has certificate value above 3.9, although benign and destructive regimes are not perfectly separated. Gating converts an average $-3.35$-point effect of unconditional transport into a +1.69-point gain, and performance remains within 0.3 points of the best threshold over a broad threshold range. Finally, we show that this certificate is mechanism-specific: when applied to Tent, it accepts only $4.3\%$ of updates and preserves 0.6% of Tent's available gain. These results position CASTER as a lightweight adaptation mechanism for frozen-model deployment, together with an explicit account of when its safety signal is informative and when it is not.

</details>


### [231] [AKRASIA: Stealthy Backdoor Attack on Reasoning-based Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01023)
*Chou Jin Chua,Sarang Nambiar,Murali Srinivasan,Ezekiel Soremekun*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出AKRASIA，一种针对推理型代码大语言模型的隐蔽推理时后门攻击，通过探测构造触发器、上下文学习注入后门、利用模型不忠实性隐藏触发器并生成合理推理，实现高攻击成功率并规避防御与人工审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击多针对标准LLM，缺乏对推理型代码LLM在推理时后门攻击的隐蔽性研究。代码生成任务中推理链可被利用隐藏恶意行为，而现有防御和人工检查难以识别推理过程中的异常，亟需揭示此类漏洞。

Method: AKRASIA分阶段实施：先探测受害者模型以构造代码级触发器；利用上下文学习进行后门学习而不微调；利用模型不忠实性在推理链中隐藏触发器；生成看似合理的推理步骤以同时规避自动化防御和欺骗人类审查。

Result: 在4种攻击目标、6个推理LLM、3个编码任务/数据集及3种防御方法上，对SOTA模型平均攻击成功率高达99.34%，模型原始准确率保持97.23%；在14/18种防御配置下平均ASR保留98.82%以绕过最先进防御，并在80%设置中成功隐藏触发器与推理步骤。

Conclusion: AKRASIA揭示推理型代码LLM存在严重推理后门安全漏洞，现有防御与人工审查均不足，强调开发针对推理过程的新型防御机制的紧迫性。

Abstract: We present AKRASIA, a stealthy, inference-time backdoor attack against reasoning-based Code LLMs. AKRASIA aims to achieve a backdoor target (e.g., malicious code execution) in reasoning LLMs while evading automated defenses and human inspection. To achieve this, AKRASIA probes the victim LLM to construct a code-level backdoor trigger. It then employs in-context learning for backdoor learning, and model unfaithfulness to conceal the backdoor trigger, and generate plausible reasoning. We evaluate AKRASIA using four backdoor targets six (6) reasoning LLMs, three coding tasks/datasets and three defense methods. AKRASIA has up to 99.34% average attack success rate on SOTA LLMs and mantains up to 97.23% average accuracy. AKRASIA evades the SOTA defense, retaining up to 98.82% average ASR in most (14/18) defense settings. It evades human inspection, successfully hiding the backdoor trigger and reasoning steps in up to 80% of settings. Our findings motivate the need to defend LLMs against reasoning backdoors.

</details>


### [232] [The Answer Is Not the Argument](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00264)
*Will Yeadon,Sergio Juárez,Paul Mackay,T. J. Dowling,Elise Agra,Oto-obong Inyang,Arin Mizouri,Craig P. Testrow*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本研究通过237个逐步编号的解题轨迹，发现提供给监控器的参考答案主要改善结论一致性检查，而非对推理过程的独立验证；在答案正确但轨迹存在真实错误的关键轨迹上，认证答案并未提升错误定位能力，反而降低了召回率，提示可信答案评估可能高估推理监控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI推理监控评估常向监控器提供可信参考答案，但未明确答案访问究竟提升推理验证，还是仅暴露错误结论。为探索这一问题，须分离最终答案正确性与推理过程正确性。

Method: 收集三个前沿模型对79个物理问题的237个逐步解答（无插入错误），由物理学家、LLM辩论及源掩蔽裁决独立标注最终答案正确性与首个错误步骤，识别出24个关键轨迹（答案正确但轨迹含真实错误）。随后8个LLM监控器在盲测、认证答案或盲承诺条件下评估轨迹，测量平衡准确率与首错定位。

Result: 认证答案将平均平衡准确率从0.637提至0.796，首错定位从0.261提至0.379；错误答案轨迹召回率从0.653升至0.951，但关键轨迹召回率从0.521降至0.438（全部8个监控器方向一致，95% CI [+0.256, +0.506]）。盲承诺后，监控器新标记93.8%的错误答案轨迹，但仅18.0%的关键轨迹。

Conclusion: 答案访问改善的是结论一致性检查而非论证的独立验证；关键轨迹构成良性奖励黑客类比——可接受输出不保证过程健全。尽管研究中的误差为普通且非承重，可信答案评估可能系统高估监控能力，当可接受输出掩盖不合理推理时。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought monitoring is proposed for AI oversight, yet evaluations often provide monitors with a trusted reference answer. We ask whether answer access improves reasoning verification or mainly exposes incorrect conclusions. We collected 237 step-numbered solutions to 79 Humanity's Last Exam physics questions from three frontier models, with no inserted errors, and independently labelled final-answer correctness and the first false step. The reference standard combined physicist annotations, an independent LLM debate, and source-masked adjudication. This yielded 24 critical traces in which the answer was correct but the trace contained a genuine error. 8 LLM monitors evaluated traces blind, with an unverified or certified answer, or after a blind commitment. Certification raised mean balanced accuracy from 0.637 to 0.796, while exact first-error localization rose from 0.261 to 0.379. Certification changed recall (the fraction of error traces flagged as erroneous) from 0.653 to 0.951 on wrong-answer traces but from 0.521 to 0.438 on critical traces; the contrast had the same direction for all 8 monitors (question-bootstrap 95% CI [+0.256, +0.506]). After blind commitment, monitors shown the answer newly flagged 93.8% of previously passed wrong-answer traces as erroneous, but only 18.0% of critical traces. Answer access therefore improves conclusion-consistency checking rather than independent verification of the supporting argument. For AI safety, these traces provide a benign analogue of reward hacking: an acceptable output does not establish that the process producing it was sound. Although the errors studied here were ordinary and mostly non-load-bearing rather than adversarial, trusted-answer evaluations may similarly overstate monitoring capability when acceptable outputs conceal unsound reasoning.

</details>


### [233] [HiveTraceGuard-Pro: A Compact Generative Guardrail for Prompt Injection, Jailbreaks, and Adversarial Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01046)
*Nikita Oblakov,Sabrina Sadiekh,Evgeniy Kokuykin*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出0.6B的俄英双语护栏模型HiveTraceGuard-Pro，在俄语提示注入与表面混淆场景下表现优异，具备低延迟与高鲁棒性，但综合基准略逊于部分对比模型，且训练/评测数据未公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生产级LLM需防御提示注入与有害内容，但针对俄语提示注入和表面混淆的公开证据极少，缺乏专门护栏模型的系统评估。

Method: 基于Qwen3-0.6B进行LoRA微调，采用单二分类（安全/不安全）规则，训练语料配对同域有害/良性示例并应用8种混淆变换，在统一测试框架下与34个护栏模型对比，并额外进行15模型专项评测。

Result: 综合key为0.7432（低于最高0.7641），公开16组key为0.7153（被4个模型超越）；俄语干净鲁棒F1=0.88与提示注入召回=0.999均为15模型中最高，中位延迟14.3ms最低；整体FPR=0.268，FNR=0.156；评测集与训练集存在≥27.1%重叠。

Conclusion: 模型在俄语专项任务上兼具高性能与低延迟，但需注意评测集重叠导致的乐观偏差，且综合性能未达最优；权重已开源但数据、代码不可复现，且响应序列化方式与生产路径不一致。

Abstract: Production LLMs must handle inputs that attempt to override system instructions, bypass safety policies or elicit harmful responses. A common mitigation is a separate guardrail model. Existing reports, however, provide little evidence on Russian prompt injection or Russian surface obfuscation. We present HiveTraceGuard-Pro, a 0.6B generative guardrail LoRA-tuned from Qwen3-0.6B. It is trained on Russian and English and uses one binary scoring rule (safe/unsafe) for the final target turn. Its training corpus pairs harmful examples, where a counterpart exists, with benign examples from the same domain and applies eight obfuscation transforms to both labels. In one harness, we compare HiveTraceGuard-Pro with thirty-four other guards on nineteen benchmark groups, sixteen of which are public. Its aggregate key is 0.7432, behind 0.7641 and 0.7552 for the two higher-scoring guards. Over the sixteen public groups alone, its key is 0.7153 and four of the thirty-four other suite guards score higher. In a fifteen-model comparison, HiveTraceGuard-Pro has the highest clean Russian robustness combined-F1 (0.88) and Russian prompt-injection recall (0.999). Both results use Russian sets assembled by our team, and at least 27.1% of the prompt-injection set overlaps the training corpus. Its 14.3 ms median latency is the lowest among those fifteen models in that run. Across the suite, FPR is 0.268 and FNR is 0.156. All reported response results use a legacy standalone-reply serialization rather than the natural assistant-role path of the shipped chat template. We release the merged weights on Hugging Face under Apache-2.0. The corpus, evaluation sets and evaluation code remain internal.

</details>


### [234] [What Limits Robustness in Deep Image Watermarking: An Analysis of Mechanisms and Their Scaling Across Capacities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01050)
*Marta Bistroń,Zbigniew Piotrowski*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文探讨深度图像水印中鲁棒性的根本限制，发现瓶颈并非载荷容量本身，而是由空间块网格映射和冻结嵌入器设计引发的三种机制：网格失同步、编解码器畸变抵抗和嵌入强度窗口收窄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒性是深度图像水印的核心未解问题，且随载荷增加而恶化。作者旨在区分容量本身是否是限制因素，或只是暴露了其他限制，从而为设计改进提供理论依据。

Method: 首先按畸变类型和对抗策略分别排序，依据载荷容量和可微性两个轴。其次，在64至16384位载荷范围内，针对空间块网格映射且提取器独立于冻结嵌入器训练的方案，量化三种鲁棒性限制机制，并测试对抗编解码器训练的效果。

Result: 对抗编解码器训练提取阶段不仅无效，反而有害，会降低训练时工作点的阅读性能。三种限制均源于提取前的过程，且不随提取端进一步训练而消除，表明限制与畸变类别相关而非容量本身。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性限制源于特定设计类别（空间网格映射+冻结嵌入器）的固有属性，而非单点实现问题；同时提供了可验证泛化声明的评估协议。

Abstract: Robustness remains the principal open problem in deep image watermarking, and what limits it becomes sharper as payload grows. This paper asks whether capacity is itself the limit or only makes other limits visible, and answers in two parts. The first organizes the distortions a watermark must survive and the strategies developed to resist them, ordering each by the axis that governs it: payload capacity for the distortions, differentiability for the strategies. The second identifies and measures three mechanisms that limit robustness in schemes mapping the payload onto a spatial block grid with extraction trained separately from a frozen embedder: desynchronization of the payload grid, the resistance of codec-induced distortion to training, and the narrowing of the usable embedding-strength window. Payloads from 64 to 16384 bits are measured, well beyond the range those strategies address. Training the extraction stage against a codec proves not merely ineffective but harmful, degrading the reading at the operating points used in training. The limits follow the class of distortion rather than capacity itself, and none is removed by further training on the extraction side, because all three arise before extraction. An evaluation protocol making claims of generalization verifiable is also contributed. The conclusions are properties of a class of designs rather than of one implementation.

</details>


### [235] [The Irreversibility Budget: Fleet-Level Risk Accounting and Admission Control for Agent Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00275)
*Bardia Mohammadi,Laurent Bindschaedler*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出不可逆性预算（irreversibility budget），作为跨智能体、工作流和租户的累计风险账户，以限制LLM智能体舰队整体造成的不可逆影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制机制逐个检查单一效应，导致单个授权的智能体在共享触发条件下可集体超支主体的风险限额，而所有局部门控看似正确。

Method: 将不可逆性作为一等资源，由可信运行时维护每个主体的残余风险价值账户；每个效应按其残余损失收费，当累计超出预算时拒绝边际效应。定价需考虑异构性、对抗性声明和相关性。

Result: 对照实验显示，逐效应门控可导致舰队级超支高达租户风险限额的48倍，而预算方法能将所有正确计费运行保持在限额内。

Conclusion: 依赖感知的保守定价仍是部署可行设计的关键未解决问题。

Abstract: Fleets of LLM agents now externalize effects that cannot be fully undone: they move money, deploy code, delete data, and disclose information. Current controls check one effect at a time, so a fleet of individually authorized agents can overdraw its principal's risk under a shared trigger while every local gate stays correct. We propose the irreversibility budget, a cumulative account of residual value-at-risk that a trusted runtime maintains for each principal across agents, workflows, and tenants. Treating irreversibility as a first-class resource, the runtime charges each effect its residual loss below the agent and denies the marginal effect once the aggregate would overdraw the budget. Getting the price right is hard, because effects are heterogeneous, adversarially declared, and correlated. We perform a controlled study in which per-effect gates admit fleet-level overdraws of up to 48 times the tenant's risk limit while the budget holds every correctly charged run within that limit. Conservative, dependency-aware pricing remains the central open problem for a deployable design.

</details>


### [236] [JENGA: Exploiting Counter-Based RowHammer Countermeasures to Break Real-Time Predictability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01077)
*Valentin Abgrall,Marcello Traiola,Ruben Salvador,Maria Méndez Real,Alessandro Palumbo,Angeliki Kritikakou*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 研究了RowHammer硬件计数器缓解措施（如PRAC-N）对实时系统WCET的影响，并提出JENGA攻击，证明其可导致任务延迟至WCET的200%，最后给出了考虑缓解延迟的安全WCET分析边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RowHammer防御措施主要评估平均性能，但安全关键实时系统更关注最坏情况行为，而缓解措施可能引入不可控的时序变化，破坏时间可预测性假设，目前缺乏针对其WCET影响的研究。

Method: 以DDR5标准化的PRAC计数器为案例，构建gem5与Ramulator 2.0仿真环境，设计JENGA攻击通过操纵计数器内部状态干扰受害任务执行时间，并基于TACLeBench工作负载进行验证，同时推导数学分析模型以覆盖缓解引发的延迟。

Result: JENGA攻击可使受害任务延迟超过其WCET的200%，表明原始时间安全性假设失效；同时，所提分析边界能数学化地包含缓解导致的额外延迟。

Conclusion: 硬件RowHammer计数器防御会显著破坏实时系统时序安全性，必须将缓解延迟纳入WCET分析，而所提出的边界可为此类系统提供可靠的时间保证基础。

Abstract: Safety-critical real-time systems must satisfy multiple dependability requirements, notably time predictability and security. In such systems, tasks must complete within bounded and known execution times, typically characterised through Worst-Case Execution Time (WCET) analysis. At the same time, DRAM-based platforms are increasingly sensitive to the RowHammer read-disturbance security vulnerability, which has motivated the development of numerous hardware and software countermeasures in both academia and industry. However, the impact of these defences is generally evaluated in terms of average-case performance, a metric that is insufficient for safetycritical real-time systems, where worst-case behaviour is the primary concern. In this paper, we study the impact of RowHammer countermeasures based on hardware counters on the timing behaviour of real-time systems. We use a Per-Row-Activation-Counter (PRAC) countermeasure as a case study, standardised for recent DDR5 memories, and show that it can introduce significant timing variations. Based on this observation, we introduce JENGA, an attack in which an attacker-controlled task manipulates the internal state of the RowHammer countermeasure mechanism to increase the execution time of a victim real-time task beyond its expected WCET. We implement JENGA in a gem5 and Ramulator 2.0 simulation environment and evaluate its impact on TACLeBench workloads. We show that such an attack can delay tasks up to 200% of their WCET, making the initial timesafety assumptions unsafe. To address this issue, we derive a safe analytical bound that accounts for mitigation-induced delays in WCET analysis for DRAM systems protected by hardware countermeasures, such as PRAC-N.

</details>


### [237] [CRSF: Collusion-Resilient Privacy-Preserving Sensor Fusion with Byzantine-Robust Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01096)
*Chao Yin,Haihong Tian,Zheng Yang,Haibin Zhang,Fabio Massacci,Chenglu Jin*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出CRSF协议，抵御传感器-服务器共谋和拜占庭操纵，在保护隐私的同时实现正确性、可终止性和实际性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于混淆电路的传感融合协议易受传感器-服务器共谋及拜占庭传感器参与操控攻击，破坏诚实传感器隐私、错误排除诚实传感器并污染融合结果。

Method: 引入PBFT共识阶段协调传感器提交，并采用服务器特定、状态依赖的标签发布机制，结合阈值保护电路输入标签，防止恶意服务器单方操纵及共谋获取秘密。

Result: 在Google Cloud上实现并评估，针对最多261个传感器的容错融合电路，在线开销与最先进基线相比具有竞争力，故障场景下仍保持高效。

Conclusion: CRSF在鲁棒安全性和协议性能间取得了实用平衡，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving sensor fusion enables an untrusted server to compute an aggregate result over distributed sensor measurements without learning either individual inputs or the final output. Recent garbled-circuit-based protocols provide an efficient realization of this functionality in a sensor--server--client architecture, but remain vulnerable to sensor--server collusion and Byzantine manipulation of sensor participation. These weaknesses can compromise honest-sensor privacy, incorrectly exclude honest sensors, and corrupt the computed fusion result, thereby undermining the security guarantees expected from the protocol.
  We present CRSF, a collusion-resilient sensor-fusion protocol that addresses these weaknesses while providing privacy, correctness with explicit abort, and liveness. CRSF introduces a Practical Byzantine Fault Tolerance (PBFT)-based agreement phase for sensor submissions and uses server-specific, status-dependent label release with threshold protection of circuit-input labels. This design prevents any Byzantine server from unilaterally manipulating sensor participation and prevents any admissible sensor-server coalition from obtaining enough secret material to compromise honest-sensor privacy.
  We implement CRSF and compare its online execution time with the most relevant state-of-the-art baseline. Our Google Cloud evaluation measures the total computation and communication cost of the online protocol under fault-free and representative faulty executions. Across a range of fault-tolerant fusion circuits and up to 261 sensors, CRSF demonstrates a highly practical trade-off between robust security and protocol performance.

</details>


### [238] [Johnny Still Receives Spam SMS: Assessing the Robustness of SMS Spam Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01171)
*Muhammad Salman,Muhammad Islam,Muhammad Ikram,Mohamed Ali Kaafar*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文评估了真实SMS/ RCS反垃圾系统在对抗攻击下的鲁棒性，发现现有检测器存在显著漏洞，并提出了一种基于多模型集成（结合对抗训练与架构多样性）的防御方法，显著提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SMS垃圾检测系统在受控环境中准确率高，但在真实世界中面对对抗攻击和复杂垃圾策略时鲁棒性不足，且缺乏对实际部署系统（如商业应用、第三方服务、开源模型）的系统性评估。

Method: 对真实系统（商业应用、第三方服务、Hugging Face开源模型）进行标准与对抗条件下的鲁棒性评估，包括可感知和不可感知攻击，且扰动经过真实SMS/RCS投递验证。提出一种结合对抗训练与多种架构、不同分词器的分类器的多模型集成，并采用少数投票策略。

Result: 现有垃圾检测器对对抗操纵消息识别能力显著不足；对抗训练单独使用不够（跨结构编码级攻击时鲁棒性剧降）；所提集成方法（特别是少数投票）在保持竞争性准确率的同时显著提升了对可感知和不可感知对抗攻击的鲁棒性，并给出了精确率-召回率权衡及推荐工作点。

Conclusion: 需要全面鲁棒性评估和基于集成的防御来构建更安全的真实世界SMS垃圾检测系统，对抗训练不足以应对跨家族扰动，异构集成是有效策略。

Abstract: SMS spam detection systems often achieve high accuracy in controlled environments but struggle against adversarial attacks and increasingly sophisticated spam tactics in real-world deployments. In this paper, we evaluate the robustness of SMS anti-spam systems that end users actually rely on, including commercial messaging applications, third-party anti-spam services, and publicly available open-weight models hosted on Hugging Face. We evaluate these systems under both standard and adversarial conditions, considering perceptible and state-of-the-art imperceptible attacks. We include only perturbations that we verify survive real SMS or RCS delivery, rather than lab-only artifacts. Our experiments reveal significant gaps in existing spam detectors' ability to identify adversarially manipulated messages. We further demonstrate that adversarial training alone is insufficient. Using an explicit held-out evaluation protocol, we find that robustness transfers well within a perturbation family but degrades sharply against structurally distinct, encoding-level attacks. To address these weaknesses, we propose a multi-model ensemble that combines adversarial training with spam classifiers diverse in architecture and tokenization. Our results show that this ensemble, particularly when using a minority-voting strategy, substantially improves robustness against both perceptible and imperceptible adversarial attacks while maintaining competitive classification accuracy. We also characterize the resulting precision-recall trade-off and recommend operating points for false-positive-sensitive and recall-critical deployments. These findings highlight the need for comprehensive robustness evaluations and ensemble-based defenses for building more secure SMS spam detection systems in real-world settings.

</details>


### [239] [A SoK for SoCs: Reading the TI Leaves on AI for Cyber Threat Intelligence Generation and Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01174)
*Saastha Vasan,Hadjer Benkraouda,Jizhou Chen,Leyan Pan,Doguhan Yeke,Shinan Liu,Noah Spahn,Stefano Ortolani,David Evans,Christopher Kruegel,Giovanni Vigna*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文通过文献调研和实践者访谈，梳理了网络威胁情报（CTI）生命周期的三个主要阶段，发现CTI生成与共享阶段研究不足且高度依赖人工。作者将该阶段细分为四个步骤，并通过初步实验评估了大型语言模型（LLM）在每一步的可行性。结果表明LLM能辅助分析师，但无法完全自主，仍需专家监督，据此提出了三个未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTI对保护关键基础设施至关重要，但将原始攻击证据转化为可共享情报的过程碎片化且研究不充分，特别是CTI生成与共享阶段缺乏系统性研究，为改进这一核心环节需要先厘清其实践现状与瓶颈。

Method: 首先进行学术文献综述，将CTI生命周期划分为数据收集、生成与共享、消费三阶段；然后对多个组织的CTI实践者进行问卷访谈，提炼出四个常见挑战；在此基础上将生成与共享阶段细分为情报提取、标准化与丰富、编码化、分发四个步骤；最后针对每个步骤开展使用当前LLM的试点可行性研究。

Result: 实践者描述的过程几乎全手动，并暴露四大难题：敏感信息泄露防护、从嘈杂数据中提取指标、将行为关联到标准TTP、转成共享平台所需格式。试点实验显示LLM能在每个步骤辅助分析，但提取指标不完整、无法充分基于证据支撑每个主张、不能判断共享情报对接收方的用处，因此每一步仍需要专家把关。

Conclusion: 当前LLM尚不能独立完成CTI生成与共享，但可充当辅助工具。基于实验观察，作者提出三个自动化生产可共享情报的研究方向，为未来在专家监督下增强自动化程度提供指导。

Abstract: Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) is essential for defending mission-critical infrastructure, yet the process of transforming raw attack evidence into shareable CTI remains fragmented and understudied.
  We conduct a literature survey of academic papers, organizing the CTI lifecycle into three stages: Threat Data Collection, CTI Generation and Sharing, and CTI Consumption. The first and third stages are well represented in the literature, whereas only a small number of papers address CTI Generation and Sharing. To learn how this stage is practiced, we survey practitioners across multiple organizations who routinely generate and share CTI. They describe a largely manual process with four recurring challenges: preventing the exposure of sensitive information, extracting indicators from noisy attack data, correlating observed behavior with standardized tactics, techniques, and procedures (TTPs), and translating CTI into the formats that sharing platforms require.
  Using the insights from the practitioner survey, we divide the CTI Generation and Sharing stage into four steps: Intelligence Extraction, Normalization and Enrichment, Codification, and Distribution. We then conduct pilot studies that probe the feasibility of current Large Language Models (LLMs) for each step. The pilot studies show that LLMs can assist an analyst in each of the four steps. However, the models recover only a fraction of the indicators the evidence contains, struggle to ground every claim in the supplied evidence, and do not judge what keeps shared intelligence useful to its recipients. Each step therefore requires expert supervision. Based on these observations, we derive three research directions for automating the production of shareable intelligence.

</details>


### [240] [Reveree: Diagnosing LLM Reverse-Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01185)
*Hadjer Benkraouda,Hongyu Cai,Berkay Celik,Gang Wang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Reveree诊断框架，从求解率、八阶段RE里程碑、行为画像三个层级评估LLM智能体的逆向工程能力；发现基础模型主导性能、失败集中于理解阶段且为能力瓶颈而非资源瓶颈，并揭示记忆与真实分析并存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTF逆向工程评估仅用单一‘是否拿到flag’指标，无法揭示智能体在RE流程中的失败环节，也无法区分成功源于真实分析还是记忆公开解法，需要细粒度诊断工具。

Method: 设计分三层的Reveree框架：第一层求解率；第二层将RE过程划分为八个阶段并计算里程碑达成；第三层构建行为画像。理解类阶段由与人类专家验证过的、不看结果的LLM裁判打分，其余阶段确定性验证。基于88个picoCTF和NYU-CTF题目评估9个前沿模型和4种提示策略。

Result: 基础模型性能主导，提示策略影响次之且依赖模型；更大/更新/更贵模型并不稳定更强；失败集中在理解阶段，增加预算/持久性/推理努力收效甚微；模型能凭描述复现picoCTF flag，但NYU-CTF几无可测记忆，多数成功经表面扰动后仍成立。

Conclusion: LLM在RE任务中的瓶颈是理解能力而非资源投入，成功中真实分析与记忆并存；Reveree为社区提供可复用的多维度诊断工具，推动更可靠的RE能力评测。

Abstract: Reverse engineering (RE) is critical to security tasks such as malware analysis and vulnerability discovery, and large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly able to perform it autonomously. Capture-the-flag (CTF) RE challenges have become the standard proxy for measuring this capability, but evaluation rests on a single criterion: whether the agent captures the flag. This solve rate reveals neither where in the RE process an agent fails nor whether a success reflects analysis of the binary or recall of a public solution. In this paper, we propose Reveree, a diagnostic framework that scores an LLM RE agent's trajectory at three tiers: solve rate, milestone progress through an eight-stage RE schema, and a behavioral profile of its actions. Comprehension stages are scored by an outcome-blinded LLM judge validated against a human expert; all other stages are verified deterministically. Using Reveree, we evaluate nine frontier models and four prompting strategies on 88 picoCTF and NYU-CTF challenges. We find that the base model dominates performance, whereas prompting strategy is a secondary, model-dependent effect. Surprisingly, larger, newer, or costlier models are not reliably stronger. We also find that failures concentrate at the comprehension stages of the RE process, and that extra budget, persistence, or reasoning effort rescues few of them, pointing to a competence limit rather than a resource limit. Regarding memorization, while models reproduce picoCTF flags from challenge descriptions alone, NYU-CTF shows minimal measurable recall, and most solves survive surface perturbation, indicating that genuine analysis coexists with memorization. We release Reveree to the community.

</details>


### [241] [Smart Contracts Claimed Vulnerable by the CVE Database, with Labels and Source Locations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01186)
*Monika di Angelo,Gernot Salzer*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 我们提出了CVE-Smart-Contracts数据集，包含截至2026年7月与以太坊智能合约相关的CVE记录，提供漏洞工件、三种分类标签和函数级定位，并保留外部输入以确保可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CVE数据库缺乏针对智能合约的结构化、可复现且有标签的数据集，阻碍了智能合约安全分析工具的实证评估和漏洞修复研究。

Method: 自动化流程包括CVE记录检索、证据收集、记录与工件对应性验证、标签分配和漏洞定位，保留原始输入用于可复现性；约15%的数据需要人工分析，并标记明显错误的记录为refuted。

Result: 数据集包含491条与已部署合约相关的记录、26条项目级记录、45条缺少有效工件和6条被驳回的记录。

Conclusion: 该数据集支持智能合约安全分析工具的评估和修复技术研究，通过保留元数据和可复现流程，为实证安全研究提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: The Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) database catalogs vulnerability claims in hard- and software, among them those pertaining to blockchain programs a.k.a. smart contracts. We present CVE-Smart-Contracts, a curated dataset of CVE records up to July 2026 referring to Ethereum smart contracts. The dataset contains the vulnerable artifacts (source code and runtime bytecode), labels according to three taxonomies, and function-level locations. The retrieval of CVE records, collection of additional evidence, validation of the correspondence between records and artifacts, label assignment, and vulnerability localization are automated, leaving 15% to manual analysis. The dataset does not validate the original vulnerability claims, but marks a few records obviously wrong as `refuted'. For the sake of reproducibility, all external inputs are retained, so that rerunning the pipelines results in the same outputs. The dataset comprises 491 records linked to deployed contracts, 26 referring to projects (mostly libraries), 45 without validated artifacts, and six records with refuted claims. The dataset supports empirical security research, in particular the evaluation of code analysis and repair techniques.

</details>


### [242] [Who Judges the Judges? A Chinese Safety QA Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Responses and Safety Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01210)
*Rui Yang,Shuang Huang,Junhua Liu,Ziqi Zhao,Qingzhong Yan,Yuhang Sun,Cong Liu,Guoping Hu,Rui Mei,Jing Shao*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出C-SafeQA，一个基于策略的中文安全评测基准，包含538个基础查询和8877个对抗查询，产生37660条带标签的查询-回复记录，用于评估目标模型安全性和审计七个自动化安全评判器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准多评估用户查询的风险，但实际结果取决于回复是否违反政策。中文环境下的语言变异和对抗性转换可能掩盖风险意图，需要回复级别的安全评测。

Method: 构建含538个基础查询和8877个对抗查询的查询集，通过四个全量LLM部署生成回复，采用共识感知的多模型裁决和三位安全专家对分层子集的盲审生成参考标签，支持目标模型评估和七个自动化安全评判器的审计。

Result: 基础查询的不安全回复率为0.93%至3.35%，对抗查询为11.68%至30.05%。对抗子集上，评判器在不安全回复召回率和风险查询条件下的安全回复假阳性率之间存在显著权衡，无评判器在所有指标上占优。藏头诗变换降低所有七个评判器的不安全召回率。

Conclusion: C-SafeQA提供了回复级别的中文安全评测，揭示现有评判器在对抗变换下的机制特定弱点，数据集和代码公开以支持复现，但构建和私人裁决部分不公开。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks for large language models often assess the risk of a user query, although the outcome of question answering depends on whether the response violates a policy. This distinction is critical in Chinese harmful-content evaluation, where linguistic variation and adversarial transformations can obscure risky intent. We introduce C-SafeQA, a policy-grounded benchmark for response-level Chinese safety evaluation. It comprises 538 base queries and 8,877 adversarial queries answered by four full-model LLM deployments, yielding 37,660 query-response records labeled safe, unsafe, or disputed. Reference labels are generated through agreement-aware multi-model adjudication and blind audits of stratified subsets by three safety experts. C-SafeQA supports both evaluation of target-model safety and auditing of seven automated safety judges against shared reference labels. Unsafe-response rates range from 0.93% to 3.35% on base queries and from 11.68% to 30.05% on adversarial queries. On the adversarial subset, judges show substantial trade-offs between unsafe-response recall and risk-query-conditioned safe-response false positive rate, and no judge dominates all metrics. Both acrostic transformations reduce unsafe recall for all seven judges, revealing mechanism-specific evaluator weaknesses. Dataset records, metadata, verification code, and judge scripts are publicly released to support recomputation, while benchmark construction, target-response generation, and private adjudication remain outside the release boundary.

</details>


### [243] [What's in Your Agent's Context? Context Privilege Escalation Attacks against AI Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01222)
*Zichuan Li,Jian Cui,Ashley Chen,Xiaojing Liao,Luyi Xing*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 对真实世界AI代理框架的上下文组装设计进行首次系统分析，揭示两类新型权限提升攻击（M-CPE与X-CPE），并在12个主流代理框架（如Claude Code、Codex）中验证，可导致完整代理劫持、远程代码执行等严重后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理框架的上下文组装机制往往依赖专有或不透明设计，导致上下文来源与逻辑难以理解，相关安全风险未被充分探索，亟需系统性研究以填补空白。

Method: 系统分析多个真实代理框架的上下文组装设计，溯源不同来源的上下文收集与整合方式，识别攻击面，提出M-CPE（低权限上下文注入高权限消息角色）和X-CPE（恶意内容跨作用域持久化）两类攻击类别，并对12个框架进行实证安全测试。

Result: 在包括Claude Code和Codex在内的12个真实框架中成功实施CPE攻击，造成完整代理恢复、远程代码执行、拒绝服务及工具或技能调用被操纵等危害。

Conclusion: 真实代理框架的上下文组装存在系统性权限提升漏洞，当前设计对攻击者可控内容缺乏充分隔离，需重新评估上下文信任边界并强化权限分层机制。

Abstract: Real-world, high-profile AI agent harnesses often rely on vendor-proprietary or opaque designs for context assembly, leaving the sources and underlying logic of assembled context poorly understood and the resulting security risks largely unexplored. In this paper, we present the first systematic analysis of context assembly designs in real-world AI agent harnesses. We study and uncover how an agent harness is designed to collect and assemble context from diverse sources, and identify a set of practical attack vectors arising from these designs. Our analysis brings to light two novel categories of attacks in the context assembly of real-world harnesses: (1) MessageRole Context Privilege Escalation (M-CPE), which occurs when attacker-controlled content originating from a low-privileged context is incorporated into a higher-privileged message role. (2) Cross-Scope Context Privilege Escalation (X-CPE), which occurs when attacker-controlled content persists beyond the context in which it was introduced. We performed a systemic security analysis of the CPE attacks against 12 real-world agent harnesses, including Claude Code and Codex. The resulting consequences include full agent compromise, remote code execution, denial of service, and manipulated tool or skill invocations, etc.

</details>


### [244] [Position Matters: Feature Inversion Attacks in ViT Split Inference with Token Reduction and Shuffling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01232)
*Stefano Leggio,Giulio Rossolini,Alessandro Biondi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文揭示令牌洗牌仅为表面隐私，并提出SARA攻击可重构图像，同时给出一种轻量级边缘防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT分割推理中令牌缩减与洗牌被用于降本与隐私保护，但其抗特征反演攻击的实际效果未知。

Method: SARA先预测令牌位置恢复空间布局，再用掩码自编码器补全缺失嵌入，最后重建输入图像；防御侧通过移除位置嵌入并知识蒸馏适配边缘Transformer块。

Result: 令牌洗牌几乎不提供隐私保护，令牌缩减仍有显著泄露；所提防御在保持任务精度的同时大幅降低SARA攻击效果。

Conclusion: 令牌洗牌仅是表面隐私，需结合缩减与防御措施；轻量级边缘防御可在不修改云模型的情况下平衡隐私与精度。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are increasingly used in split-inference systems, where edge devices transmit intermediate token representations to a remote cloud. In this setting, token reduction lowers computation and communication costs, while token shuffling disrupts the spatial organization of the transmitted tokens, potentially limiting information leakage. However, their privacy benefits remain unclear against feature inversion attacks, which attempt to reconstruct the input from the transmitted embeddings. In this work, we show that, despite disrupting the spatial structure required by conventional reconstruction attacks, transmitted token embeddings retain substantial positional information. Based on this observation, we introduce the Spatially Aligned Reconstruction Attack (SARA), a unified pipeline that predicts token positions, restores their spatial layout, reconstructs missing embeddings using a feature-space masked autoencoder, and recovers the input image. Our results demonstrate that token shuffling provides only apparent privacy, as SARA largely reconstructs the original token organization. Token reduction offers stronger protection, but significant leakage persists when the retained tokens preserve sufficient semantic and positional information. Finally, we introduce a lightweight edge-side defense that removes positional embeddings and progressively adapts the edge-side transformer blocks through knowledge distillation. It substantially reduces attack performance against SARA, while preserving downstream task accuracy and requiring no changes to the cloud-side model.

</details>


### [245] [MutMem-V2: Cryptographically Authorized Mutation in Persistent Agent Memory Portable Verification and Reproducible Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01235)
*Walid Saidi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: MutMem V2 在保留 V1 记忆引擎不变的前提下，补齐了可移植验证契约与可复现安装路径，通过19项协议规范与双实现一致性测试支撑完整性、授权与可追溯性声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V1 缺少完整的可移植验证契约与干净安装复现路径，导致其持久化代理记忆系统的安全声明无法被独立核查，V2 旨在填补该出版缺口而无需引入第二套记忆引擎。

Method: 规定精确规范字节、域分离的对象与捆绑承诺、回忆证据成员资格与排序、外部信任锚、身份纪元、撤销、授权、请求回执、有序披露及三类变异终态；发布18个版本化对象模式、39个回忆向量、15个变异向量及37个封闭回忆失败原因；提供独立 Node 与 Python 实现及生产一致性语料库，并开发自哈希聚合表与独立统计验证器。

Result: 双实现全部72个结构与密码终态判定一致；生产语料库28个必需类42/42全部同意；干净Node v26.8.1安装首次引导、重启与调度器就绪均无实验记忆；120单元Canary实验仅支持显式标记遍历；独立验证器重建所有统计与声明边界。

Conclusion: MutMem V2 在既定假设下支持可移植完整性、授权、可追溯性、一致性与可复现性声明，但不提供语义真实性、通用鲁棒性或独立复现证明，V1经验结果保持历史状态。

Abstract: MutMem V1 introduced retention-preserving, cryptographically authorized mutation for persistent agent memory but did not provide a complete portable verification contract or clean-install reproduction path. MutMem V2 closes that publication gap without introducing a second memory engine. It specifies exact canonical bytes, domain-separated object and bundle commitments, mandatory recall-evidence membership and ordering, external trust anchors, identity epochs, revocation, authorization, request receipts, ordered disclosure, and three mutation terminal types. The released protocol contains 18 versioned object schemas, 39 recall vectors, 15 mutation vectors, and 37 closed recall failure reasons. Independent Node and Python implementations agree on verdict and primary reason for all 72 structural and cryptographic terminals; a production-conformance corpus agrees on 42/42 cases across 28 required classes. A clean Node v26.8.1 installation reaches first-boot, restart, and scheduler readiness with no experimental memories. A separately scoped 120-unit Canary experiment supports only explicit-marker traversal. Every public table regenerates from a self-hashed aggregate, and an independent verifier reconstructs the statistics and claim boundaries. Historical V1 empirical results remain historical. MutMem V2 supports claims about portable integrity, authorization, traceability, conformance, and reproducibility under stated assumptions; it does not establish semantic truth, universal robustness, or independent replication.

</details>


### [246] [Hidden Services Protocol for Mixnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01326)
*Nicolas Constantinides,Mahdi Rahimi,Stavros Nonis*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出了NymHS，首个实用且安全的mixnet隐藏服务协议，在保护发送者匿名性的同时实现接收者隐私，并解决了现有SURB方案中的两个安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有mixnet虽能保护发送者隐私，但缺乏同时保护接收者（目的地）隐私的实用安全协议，尤其是防止发送者获知接收者地址。

Method: 基于单次使用回复块（SURBs）设计隐藏服务协议，识别并修复了现有方法中对手仅控制单个mixnode即可威胁发送者或接收者匿名性的两个实际攻击，实现了匿名服务发现、认证双向会话和异步SURB补充。

Result: 在Nym开源代码库上实现，通过118个网站、27种配置、9558次页面加载实验表明，将Sphinx有效载荷从2KiB增至10KiB可使平均页面加载延迟降低约5.2倍，通信开销从21.7%降至4.3%。

Conclusion: 隐藏服务可在mixnet上高效部署，NymHS兼顾安全性与实用性，为接收者隐私提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Mix networks (mixnets) provide network-level privacy by routing each communication packet through a sequence of intermediaries, called mixnodes, that randomly delay and cryptographically transform packets before forwarding them, making it difficult for observers to link mixnet entries to exits. While this mechanism protects sender privacy from both external adversaries and the receiver, existing mixnets lack a secure and practical protocol that simultaneously protects receiver (destination) privacy, particularly from the sender. We close this gap.
  We introduce the first practical and secure hidden-service protocol for mixnets, providing receiver privacy alongside sender anonymity. Our design builds on Single-Use Reply Blocks (SURBs), which enable anonymous replies without revealing the receiver's address. We show, however, that existing approaches to using SURBs expose two practical attacks that can compromise sender or receiver anonymity when the opposing party controls only a single mixnode. We develop defenses against both vulnerabilities.
  Building on these defenses, we introduce NymHS, a secure and practical hidden-service protocol for mixnets that supports anonymous service discovery, authenticated bidirectional sessions, and asynchronous SURB replenishment. We implement NymHS on the open-source Nym codebase and evaluate its practicality through web-browsing experiments across 118 websites, measuring each of the 27 configurations three times (9,558 page loads). Our results demonstrate that hidden services can be deployed efficiently over mixnets. In particular, increasing the Sphinx payload from 2 KiB to 10 KiB reduces mean page-load latency by approximately 5.2x and decreases communication overhead from 21.7% to 4.3% relative to the current Nym baseline.

</details>


### [247] [GenONet: A Generative operator Network for High-Resolution Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00544)
*Mohammad Kian Golkar,Luciano Alves de Oliveira,Mohammad Khanjani*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出GenONet架构，将DeepONet作为GAN生成器用于长达3小时的降水临近预报，结合物理正则化，在长预报时效和强降水事件上优于基线，产生清晰且物理一致的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率降水临近预报对减灾至关重要，但现有深度学习模型在长预报时效下预测技能退化，输出模糊，难以捕捉风暴系统的复杂非线性演化。

Method: 将DeepONet作为生成器嵌入GAN框架，学习降水连续时间动力学以保证长时稳定性；对抗训练采用时空判别器促使生成清晰连贯的预报；额外引入基于水分守恒方程的物理信息损失正则项以提升物理合理性。

Result: 定量评估在大多数指标上稳定领先，尤其在强降水事件和更长预报时效上表现突出；定性上生成的预报保持结构连贯，而基线模型退化为模糊模式；消融研究证实物理损失的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合算子学习与对抗训练并加入物理约束，是实现长时程、清晰且物理合理的降水预报的有效途径，GenONet展示了该组合的优势。

Abstract: High-resolution precipitation nowcasting is critical for reducing the impacts of severe weather but remains difficult because of rapid storm evolution. Deep learning models have shown great promise for this task, but their predictive skill often deteriorates over longer forecast horizons. This leads to increasingly blurry forecasts that fail to capture the complex, non-linear evolution of storm systems. In order to address these limitations, we introduce Spatio-Temporal U-DeepONet (GenONet), a novel architecture for long-range precipitation forecasting up to 3 hours, specifically designed to produce sharp and physically consistent results. GenONet's architecture pioneers the use of a Deep Operator Network (DeepONet) as a generator within a Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) framework for this task. The DeepONet learns the continuous-time dynamics of precipitation, ensuring stability over long forecast horizons. Adversial training against a spatio-temporal discriminator compels the model to produce sharp, coherent forecasts, while a physics-informed loss regularizer, derived from the Moisture Conservation Equation, improves physical plausibility in our ablation setting. Quantitative evaluations show that our model achieves consistently higher scores on most of the metrics, especially for highintensity events and at longer lead times. Qualitatively, GenONet produces structurally coherent forecasts that maintain their integrity, whereas baseline models degrade into indistinct patterns. Finally, an ablation study confirms the benefit of this physics-informed loss, highlighting the strength of combining operator learning with adversarial training.

</details>


### [248] [When Safety Routing Breaks: Understanding Alignment Fragility under Benign Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01455)
*Yitong Guo,Xiaoyi Chen,Siyuan Zhang,Xiaofeng Wang,Haixu Tang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Fisher几何的解释，说明良性微调削弱LLM安全对齐的原因在于安全Fisher低秩且输出路由路径被选择性重新锐化，而非梯度冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将安全对齐脆弱性归因于梯度冲突，但作者认为该解释不充分，需从几何角度揭示安全行为为何在少量良性样本后迅速崩溃。

Method: 通过分析安全Fisher信息矩阵的低秩结构，对比对齐前后几何平坦度变化，定位输出侧MLP模块中的路由通路，并利用LoRA与ASAM实验验证锐化机制。

Result: 100个良性微调样本即可导致安全崩溃至较高攻击成功率，而通用能力仅轻微下降；少量安全样本可恢复拒绝行为，表明安全表征得以保留，且LoRA/ASAM仅能在小规模微调下延缓崩溃。

Conclusion: 安全失效本质上是低秩输出路由机制的破坏，而非表征丢失，这为理解安全对齐的脆弱性与防御策略提供了新视角。

Abstract: Benign fine-tuning severely weakens the safety alignment of large language models (LLMs), so we study why refusal behavior is so fragile. While prior work often attributes this failure to gradient conflict, we propose a fundamentally different Fisher-geometric explanation: safety Fisher is low-rank, and alignment makes the safety geometry flatter while preserving an output-routing pathway. After 100 benign fine-tuning examples, this pathway is selectively re-sharpened in output-side MLP modules, explaining the asymmetric fragility: safety can collapse to high attack success rates, while general utility degrades mildly. The routing view also explains why few safety examples can restore refusal behavior, indicating that internal safety-relevant representations are preserved. Finally, we show that LoRA and ASAM mitigate early collapse by suppressing output-side sharpness, but their protection weakens at larger fine-tuning scales. Overall, safety failure is best understood as a disruption of a low-rank output-routing mechanism

</details>


### [249] [Defense-as-Skill: Evolving Runtime Guard Skill for Skill-Augmented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01487)
*Xiaofang Yang,Ziqi Miao,Dianbo Sui,Jing Shao,Lijun Li*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出防御即技能（Defense-as-Skill）范式，通过将运行时防护实现为可安装可检查的技能（SkillSonar），在不修改底层代理的情况下按任务边界检查敏感动作，显著降低恶意技能的攻击成功率并保持可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能增强代理将可复用技能作为持久上下文，恶意技能可能仅在具体任务和状态出现时才触发有害行为，导致安装前审查不足，需要任务条件下的运行时防护。

Method: 构建防御即技能范式，实现SkillSonar防护技能，与不可信技能并行运行，按用户任务边界将动作路由至允许、重规划或确认决策；并使用基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的运行时防护技能进化机制，从回放反馈中优化防御技能。

Result: 在Claude Code和OpenClaw上，进化后的防护技能显著降低攻击成功率：GLM-5上ID ASR从0.482降至0.104，OOD ASR从0.606降至0.115；跨受害者模型、保留风险族及外部基准均表现迁移性，且对自适应攻击者保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 防御即技能范式通过任务条件化、可编辑的运行时防护有效遏制恶意技能注入，且安全性-可用性平衡良好；显式安全责任分配和技能原生表示是性能提升的关键。

Abstract: Skill-augmented agents load reusable skills as persistent runtime context, improving task performance but also giving malicious skills a durable channel for steering future actions. Such skills may leak secrets, corrupt code, bypass approvals, or stage data for exfiltration only after a concrete user task and workspace state make the unsafe action appear useful. This makes pre-install vetting insufficient and calls for runtime, task-conditioned protection. We propose Defense-as-Skill, a defense paradigm that implements the runtime guard itself as an installable, inspectable, and editable skill. Our guard, SkillSonar, runs alongside untrusted task skills and checks sensitive actions against the user's task boundary, routing each action to an allow, replan, or confirmation decision without modifying the underlying agent runtime. To study this setting, we construct SCOPE-R, a task-conditioned dataset covering 6 risk families and 21 sub-categories, with 206 attack-confirmed malicious instances and 43 benign tasks. We then improve SkillSonar on the SCOPE-R training subset using runtime guard-skill evolution, a Monte-Carlo Tree Search procedure that evolves the on-disk guard skill from feedback on the rollouts. Across Claude Code and OpenClaw, the evolved guard substantially reduces attack success while maintaining a favorable safety-utility trade-off. On repeated GLM-5 runs, SkillSonar reduces ID ASR from 0.482 to 0.104 and OOD ASR from 0.606 to 0.115. Further analyses demonstrate transfer across victim models, held-out risk families, and external benchmarks, as well as retained protection against adaptive attackers. Ablations further show that explicit safety responsibility assignment and the skill-native representation are both important to the observed gains.

</details>


### [250] [Validity-Aware Jailbreak Evaluation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00498)
*Qilong Wu,Sahil Wadhwa,Pranab Mohanty,Giri Iyengar,Varun Chandrasekaran*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出SEAV框架，通过顺序分解响应并验证事实正确性与操作可行性，显著降低越狱评估的误报率，重新分类大量先前标记的成功案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱评估过度依赖语言合理性和意图匹配，忽视指令有效性依赖的事实正确性，导致不准确但看似合理的响应被错误标记为成功。

Method: SEAV将响应分解为有序步骤，结合LLM评判语义与外部知识检索验证，评估事实正确性、结构一致性和对有害目标的推进能力。

Result: 在SD-A诊断集上误报率降低14.9个百分点，在四个公共基准中的三个上重新分类22.1%-51.0%的先前成功样本为无效，结果跨搜索后端和评估模型稳定。

Conclusion: 强制正确性显著重塑测量到的鲁棒性，许多先前被标记的越狱成功应视为无效，SEAV提供更严格的验证导向评估范式。

Abstract: Jailbreak robustness has become central to large language model (LLM) safety evaluation, yet prevailing methodologies rely primarily on refusal behavior, semantic resemblance, and intent-matching heuristics that emphasize linguistic plausibility rather than correctness. We identify a key limitation in existing evaluations: many jailbreak intents depend on instructional validity rather than epistemic factuality, allowing realistic-looking responses to be labeled successful despite being factually or procedurally incorrect. To address this gap, we propose Sequential Epistemic and Action-Level Validation (SEAV), a verification-centric jailbreak evaluation framework that decomposes responses into ordered steps and evaluates both validity and correctness. SEAV combines LLM-as-a-judge mechanisms for semantic interpretation with retrieval-grounded verification using external knowledge sources, assessing whether generated content is factually correct, structurally consistent, and operationally capable of advancing harmful objectives. Empirically, SEAV cuts the false-positive rate on SD-A (a curated strategic-dishonesty diagnostic) by 14.9\,pp vs. the strongest baseline, and reclassifies 22.1\%--51.0\% of sampled prior-labeled successes as invalid across three of four public benchmarks. Together, these results show that enforcing correctness substantially reshapes measured robustness: many previously labeled jailbreak successes are reclassified as invalid, and results are stable across the tested search backends and evaluator models. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Ardor-Wu/SEAV.

</details>


### [251] [Same Request, Different Boundary: Evaluating Cybersecurity Assistance across Conversational Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00578)
*Rui Yang,Yang Hong,Yichao Xu,Zhengyu Liu,Ziyang Li,Yinzhi Cao*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出3R-Bench基准，包含150个真实网络安全请求及两种对抗性对话设置，评估8个LLM，发现历史助手行为会显著改变对相同请求的合规率，而对话分解则显著降低合规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络安全安全基准未考虑对话上下文，导致无法准确评估模型在真实多轮对话中阻止恶意使用与保障合法防御之间的平衡。

Method: 构建3R-Bench，包含拒绝、重复、修订三类场景，附加两种对抗性对话设置（历史助手行为与对话分解），对八个LLM进行系统评估。

Result: 同一请求在拒绝历史后合规率为62.0%，在接受历史后升至85.1%；直接请求合规501/800，对话形式仅172/800，降幅达45.1个百分点，失败反馈仅能恢复少量损失。

Conclusion: 对话上下文是决定安全合规的关键因素，现有直接请求评估存在明显偏差，需要开发考虑多轮交互的安全对齐方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can solve complex problems, but their misuse in high-risk domains can lead to severe consequences. Model providers therefore restrict assistance for potentially harmful requests. Refusing all cybersecurity requests would therefore harm legitimate users. Providers need a mechanism to block malicious use without denying legitimate assistance to defenders. Existing cybersecurity-specific datasets evaluate this mechanism, but none considers the conversational context of a request. We introduce 3R-Bench (Refusal, Repetition, and Revision), a benchmark of 150 real-world cybersecurity requests augmented with two adversarial conversational settings, and evaluate eight LLMs on it. Prior assistant behavior strongly changes responses to an unchanged request: among 376 available pairs from a 400-pair panel, compliance rises from 62.0% after refused history to 85.1% after accepted history. The opposite pattern appears under dialogue decomposition. In comparison, compliance falls from 501/800 direct responses to 172/800 after dialogue; among 738 pairs returning model-authored text in both conditions, the decrease is 45.1 points. Failure feedback recovers only a small fraction of this loss.

</details>


### [252] [Position: Privacy Is a Claim, Not a Property of Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01273)
*Jiachen Zhao,Antonia Januszewicz,Taeho Jung*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文指出合成数据在隐私敏感场景中的使用已从基于假设的残余推断风险声明，转变为一种基于数据生成过程本身的外观性属性，并呼吁将隐私视为明确、基于证据的科学主张，要求机器学习会议采纳相关规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据在隐私敏感场景中的使用日益普遍，但社区对其隐私保障的评判标准发生了隐性变化，导致隐私声明常常不明确、难以验证，并对稀有和少数记录造成更大风险。

Method: 通过实证分析主要机器学习会议上的近期出版物，检查合成数据在隐私敏感设置中的使用是否明确阐述威胁模型、推断风险或可证伪的隐私声明。

Result: 发现合成数据经常在隐私敏感场景中使用，但缺乏对威胁模型、推断风险或可证伪隐私声明的明确表述，导致隐私保障隐式化、验证困难且分布不均，尤其对稀有和少数记录影响更大。

Conclusion: 应把隐私视为一种明确的、基于证据的科学主张，并建议机器学习会议采纳规范，要求隐私相关断言必须范围清晰、可测试且可争议。

Abstract: Synthetic data has become a common component of machine learning research. While widely adopted, its use in privacy-sensitive contexts has quietly shifted from a claim of residual inference risk under stated assumptions to an appearance-based property inferred from data generation itself. In this position paper, we argue that this shift reflects an implicit change in community standards for what counts as sufficient privacy evidence, rather than a misunderstanding of well-established privacy principles. Drawing on an empirical analysis of recent publications across major ML venues, we show that synthetic data is frequently used in privacy-sensitive settings without explicit articulation of threat models, inference risks, or falsifiable privacy claims. As a result, privacy assurance often remains implicit, difficult to verify, and unevenly distributed, with heightened exposure for rare and minority records. We argue for treating privacy as an explicit, evidence-based scientific claim and recommend that ML venues adopt norms requiring privacy-relevant assertions to be clearly scoped, testable, and contestable.

</details>


### [253] [Cheap Verifiers, Large Blind Spots: Measuring the Reliability Cost of Cost-Saving Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01345)
*Dushyant Rajput*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 推理级联虽能降本，但闭环微调自改进循环会失败：验证器盲区随学生能力增长且对抗性移动，朴素纠错微调导致学生模型崩溃，且系统自身指标失真，无法反映真实错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索推理级联闭环（用小模型+大模型验证器）的自我改进潜力，并验证其成本与可靠性权衡。

Method: 在真实LLM上测量级联循环，包括不同规模学生（0.5B至32B）与验证器组合、朴素纠错微调策略，并推导双群体守恒定律及合成实验验证。

Result: 发现验证器盲区大且随学生能力增长（β从0.12到0.55）；前沿验证器降低盲区至0.05但升级率高达46%；纠错微调导致学生性能下降直至崩溃；所有级联内指标显示3%错误而真实错误高达32%。

Conclusion: 自我改进级联的可靠性无法通过其自身验证器计算的任何指标评估，系统本质上对自身退化失明。

Abstract: Inference cascades cut cost by answering most queries with a cheap model and escalating a hard tail to a frontier model that acts as verifier. A natural extension closes the loop: fine-tune the cheap student on the verifier's rejections so the escalation rate, and cost, fall each round. We measure this loop on real LLMs and report four findings. First, the verifier's blind spot, the fraction of the student's wrong answers it accepts, is large and moves adversarially: it grows with student capability ($β$ from 0.12 to 0.55 as the student scales 0.5B to 32B) and shrinks with verifier capability, so it is worst in the cheap-student, cheap-verifier regime cascades exist to create. Second, buying it away returns the saving: a frontier verifier drives $β$ to about 0.05 but then escalates on 46% of hard-MATH queries against a 39% true error rate, paying the frontier price on nearly half of all traffic. Third, naive corrective fine-tuning on the verifier-rejected tail does not improve the small student but degrades and ultimately collapses it, across every teacher we tried (cross-family and same-family), so at this scale the self-improving loop is self-defeating. Fourth, through all of this the cascade's own dashboard, every metric computed through the verifier, reads a flat 3% error while true delivered error swings up to 32%: the system is blind to its own degradation by construction. We then give the theory that explains the blindness, a two-population conservation law, $ε_\infty \lesssim q_0 β_0$, under which every in-loop metric improves while true quality does not, and a synthetic study that validates the mechanism. The practical conclusion: the reliability of a self-improving cascade cannot be read from any metric computed through its own verifier.

</details>


### [254] [When the Algorithm Becomes the Brand Crisis: A Sociotechnical Theory of Distributed Responsibility and Accountable Transparency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00510)
*Mohammad Saleh Torkestani,Taha Mansouri*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一个社会技术过程理论，解释AI系统中多方主体（开发者、部署者、供应商、用户）如何归因责任，并区分AI事件、组织危机与丑闻，引入“可问责透明”作为响应配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统品牌危机模型无法充分说明当技术因果、客户控制与治理职责分散于AI系统及多方主体时，利益相关者如何分配责任。

Method: 基于结构化、联邦式范围综述，整合学术与一手来源，构建概念性社会技术过程理论框架。

Result: 证据支持对责备、信任、满意度、企业评价和沟通可信度的条件性、近端推断，而非对持久声誉、品牌资产或市场表现的强主张。

Conclusion: 事件配置影响主体特定归因，归因进而塑造能力、诚信、公平与关系评估；公开道德化可能但非必然将事件升级为丑闻，研究为算法参与品牌反应提供调和视角。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems increasingly enact market-facing promises through chatbots, recommendation systems, automated decisions, and generative interfaces. Their failures, misuse, and misrepresentation raise a question that conventional brand-crisis models do not fully specify: how do stakeholders assign responsibility when technical causation, customer-facing control, and governance duties are distributed across an AI system, developer, deployer, vendor, and user? This conceptual paper develops a sociotechnical process theory from a structured, federated scoping synthesis of verified academic and primary sources. It distinguishes an AI/algorithmic incident from an AI-related organisational crisis and, in turn, from an AI-related organisational scandal. The framework proposes that incident configuration shapes actor-specific attribution; attribution informs capability, integrity, fairness, and relationship appraisals; and public moralisation may, but need not, escalate an incident into scandal. The theory offers a reconciliation of findings that algorithm involvement can buffer negative brand reactions in some settings while robot and chatbot failures can redirect responsibility to an associated firm in others. It introduces accountable transparency as a proposed response configuration that combines timely notice, an intelligible account, role-responsibility acknowledgement, remedy, evidence of correction, and recourse. The evidence supports conditional, proximal inferences about blame, trust, satisfaction, firm evaluation, and communication credibility more strongly than claims about durable reputation, brand equity, or market performance.

</details>


### [255] [VoiceLongMemEval: Do Assistants Remember How You Sounded?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00570)
*Ramit Pahwa,Parivesh Priye,Apoorva Beedu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出了VoiceLongMemEval基准，用于评估多模态对话历史中的副语言信息（情感、韵律、语音事件）推理，发现现有模型存在显著的情感缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准忽视人机对话中副语言动态（如语气、情感），而这些信息仅凭文本无法恢复，导致模型推理受限。

Method: 构建VLME基准，所有答案依赖对话轮次的副语言元数据，并通过三阶段对抗门确保仅凭转录文本无法正确回答。

Result: 前沿模型在提供文本副语言元数据时准确率提升0.09-0.38，加提示证据时提升0.61-0.69；音频原生模型直接从语音提取线索（0.354-0.412对比盲测0.325）。

Conclusion: 标准ASR会系统性丢弃副语言信号，而音频原生模型能部分弥补；研究强调多模态对话理解需纳入副语言信息。

Abstract: With the growing scale of multi-agent architectures and large language models, deployed AI assistants are increasingly tasked with reasoning over long, continuous, multi-session conversation histories. Current benchmarks evaluate this dialogue history as information retrieval over long horizon, temporal reasoning, or knowledge updates, while crucially ignoring the fundamental dynamics of human-agent interaction, i.e. how they said it. To address this gap, we present VoiceLongMemEval (VLME) benchmark, where every answer depends on paralinguistic metadata (emotion labels, prosody descriptors, and voice events) attached to conversational turns, which is otherwise unrecoverable from the words alone. Every item passes a three-stage adversarial gate, ensuring that a strong language model fails when given only the transcript. Evaluating leading frontier and open-weight models reveals a pervasive affect gap; providing text-track paralinguistic metadata yields a 0.09 to 0.38 accuracy boost (0.61 to 0.69 when prompted with evidence hints), while standard ASR pipelines systematically discard this signal. Additionally, audio-native models successfully extract these cues directly from speech (0.354 to 0.412 vs. 0.325 blind). Code and dataset will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [256] [MUGEN: Generating Unlearnable Graph Examples for Multiple Learning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00696)
*Ziyan Liu,Chengshuai Zhao,Huan Liu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出MUGEN框架，首次实现联合保护节点分类、图分类和链接预测三个任务的可学习图示例生成，通过特征扰动使训练模型无法泛化到干净数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可学习图示例仅针对单一下游任务生成，导致数据发布者无法预见的其他任务仍可利用数据，存在泄露风险。

Method: MUGEN通过共享GNN编码器和任务特定头部生成统一扰动版本；设计TASO目标利用任务预测和类间可分离性增强不可学习性及跨骨干迁移；引入TAP根据节点属性类型自适应优化，离散属性用硬翻转搜索，连续特征用梯度更新。

Result: 在5个基准、4个骨干和3个学习范式上，MUGEN生成的不可学习示例在GNN骨干和三类任务间可迁移，且对抗训练和数据增强下仍有效。

Conclusion: MUGEN是首个能同时抵御多种图任务学习的统一防御框架，兼具跨架构迁移性和鲁棒性，为图数据发布提供更全面的数据级保护。

Abstract: Graph data across diverse domains can expose valuable relational information to unauthorized representation learning, creating a pressing need for protection against such misuse. Unlearnable examples offer a data-level defense by perturbing a training release so that models trained on it fail to generalize to clean data. Existing methods generate unlearnable graph examples for only a specified downstream task. Consequently, a release protected against one task may remain learnable for other plausible uses, including node classification, graph classification, and link prediction, which the data owner cannot anticipate. We introduce MUGEN, to our knowledge the first framework for generating unlearnable graph examples that jointly protect all enabled tasks. From one clean dataset, MUGEN produces a single feature-perturbed release that protects every enabled task through a shared GNN encoder and task-specific heads. We devise a Task-Aligned Separability Objective (TASO), which leverages task prediction and classwise separability to strengthen unlearnability and its transfer across GNN backbones and enabled tasks. We further introduce Type-Adaptive Perturbation (TAP), which tailors perturbation optimization to node-attribute type, with direct search over feasible hard flips that accept only loss-improving updates for discrete node attributes and customized gradient-based updates for continuous node features, thereby enabling strong unlearnability across both settings. Experiments across five benchmarks, four backends and three learning paradigms demonstrate that MUGEN generates transferable unlearnable graph examples across GNN backbones and all three tasks, and remains effective under adversarial training and data augmentation.

</details>


### [257] [Patterning in Practice: Debiasing Reward Models with Susceptibilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00699)
*George Wang,Elizabeth Donoway,Daniel Murfet*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文首次将基于奇异学习理论的patterning方法应用于大型奖励模型，通过重新加权偏好数据对来消除风格偏差，在RM-Bench Hard上取得+14.2 pp的提升，且权重可跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型在训练时容易受到长度、格式等风格偏差的影响，导致其评估结果与真实质量不符，现有方法无法有效分离风格与内容信号。

Method: 利用patterning根据每个偏好对在基准损失后验期望值上的影响（即易感性）进行重新加权，从而降低风格线索对模型判断的干扰。

Result: 在Gemma 2 9B上，RM-Bench Hard得分提升+14.2±1.2 pp，整体准确率保持不变；权重可直接迁移至2B/27B模型并部分迁移至Llama 3.1 8B，且通过消融验证了干预效果源于特定训练子集。

Conclusion: Patterning能有效且可解释地消除奖励模型的风格偏差，并具有良好的跨规模迁移性，为奖励模型校准提供了新工具。

Abstract: Reward models trained on human preferences are known to suffer from length, formatting, and other stylistic biases. In this paper we use patterning, which reweights each preference pair according to its measured effect on posterior expectation values of benchmark losses (its susceptibility), to debias a Gemma 2 9B Instruct reward model trained on Skywork-Reward-Preference v0.2. We obtain $+14.2 \pm 1.2$ pp on RM-Bench Hard, the split where style cues point against correctness (mean $\pm$ s.e.\ over 5 seeds), with overall RM-Bench accuracy preserved, comparable to the strongest Hard-split gain reported by the closest published comparator (SteerRM, $+13.2$ pp). We demonstrate in a simple case that the reweighting is interpretable by tracing a side effect of the intervention (a regression on a safety subset of RM-Bench) to a small class of training pairs, which we confirm by ablation. The weights also transfer: those computed on Gemma 2 9B debias Gemma 2 2B and 27B with no recomputation, and transfer partially to Llama 3.1 8B. This is the first application of patterning, a program grounded in singular learning theory, beyond small models and synthetic tasks.

</details>


### [258] [Socrates went Nuclear: Comparing Interaction Strategies for AI systems in a Learning Context using Brain Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00584)
*Alexandre Clin Deffarges,Nataliya Kosmyna,Pattie Maes*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本研究比较了无限制聊天机器人、苏格拉底式受限机器人和自适应脑电反馈系统三种AI交互模式在学习中的效果，发现无限制模式短期内学习增益更高，但自适应模式产生更强的脑电参与度，且无限制模式的成功可能源于即时后测的短期效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无限制AI访问是否削弱学习所需的认知努力，或反而简化知识获取过程，通过对比不同交互设计在学习情境中的效果来回答这一争议。

Method: 50名零基础被试学习核安全协议，经历视频、前测、三种AI交互干预（无限制对话、苏格拉底提示、脑电驱动的自适应系统）及即时后测，使用Muse头带监测所有条件下的认知参与度，并对聊天使用模式进行聚类分析。

Result: 无限制聊天机器人学习增益显著高于两种受限模式（p<.03, d>0.80），自适应条件产生显著更高的EEG参与度（p=.018）；聚类显示无限制模式用户多采用直接答案检索策略，而苏格拉底模式用户初期尝试推理后逐渐脱离。

Conclusion: 无限制AI的短期学习优势并非深层学习的证据，而可能归因于即时后测的评估时机；自适应模式虽能增强认知参与，但未转化为即刻的学习增益，需重新审视交互设计对长期学习效果的真实影响。

Abstract: Does unrestricted AI access bypass the cognitive effort required for learning, or does it streamline knowledge acquisition? This paper reports on a study where we compare three designs for user-AI interaction in a learning context: (1) an unrestricted conversational bot like ChatGPT, (2) a pedagogically constrained bot that guides through hints without giving final answers, which we refer to as the Socratic mode; and (3) a non-conversational adaptive tutoring system that adjusts difficulty in real-time based on the user's cognitive engagement derived from the brain signals. Fifty study participants were tasked with learning about nuclear safety protocols, a domain chosen for its zero-prior knowledge baseline. The participants progressed through an instructional video, a pre-test, an AI-driven assessment phase, which varied in the three conditions, and an immediate post-test. The nature of the questions centered primarily on factual knowledge acquisition, but it still required participants to have a global understanding of the concepts in order to answer the questions correctly. A Muse headband was used to derive the cognitive engagement of all users in all conditions. The unrestricted chatbot produced higher learning gains (delta) than both constrained modes (p < .03, d > 0.80), while the adaptive condition generated significantly higher EEG engagement (p = .018). The cluster analysis of chatbot usage and discussion patterns by users showed that most participants in the unrestricted-mode adopted a direct answer-retrieval strategy, while participants in the Socratic-mode initially attempted to reason through the hints before progressively disengaging. Consequently, this also suggests that the success of the unrestricted AI is not an evidence of deeper learning, but rather a result of the immediate post-test evaluation after the training phase.

</details>


### [259] [Subspace Levenberg Marquardt Algorithms in Training Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00789)
*M. Duc Hoang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文评估了子空间Levenberg-Marquardt算法在神经网络回归与分类任务中的性能，并与经典LM及SGD、Adam等一阶算法进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典LM算法在中小型神经网络中收敛快、鲁棒性强，但随着参数规模增大计算和内存成本显著增加，子空间方法（如KSLM、HSLM）被提出以提高二阶算法的效率。

Method: 对比子空间LM变体（KSLM、HSLM）与经典LM、SGD和Adam在回归和分类任务中的表现。

Result: 未在摘要中提供具体结果，但预期子空间LM在保持较好性能的同时降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 子空间LM算法为大型神经网络提供了一种高效的二阶优化替代方案，值得进一步应用与探索。

Abstract: The Levenberg-Marquardt (LM) algorithm is a well-known second-order method for rapid convergence and strong robustness when training small- to medium-sized neural networks (NNs). However, its computational and memory costs increase significantly as the number of parameters in an NN grows. To address this limitation, subspace methods have been proposed, such as the Krylov subspace LM (KSLM) and the hybrid subspace LM (HSLM), making second-order algorithms more efficient. In this work, we evaluate the subspace Levenberg-Marquardt algorithms for regression and classification tasks in neural networks. We compare the performance of subspace LM variants with the classical LM method, as well as other popular first-order algorithms, such as stochastic gradient descent (SGD) and Adam.

</details>


### [260] [Triple-Bottom-Line Sustainability of Language Models for Edge AI: A Comparison Between SLMs and Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00665)
*Jainil Dharmil Shah*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可复现的整体可持续性评分（HSS），从经济、环境和社会三大支柱评估边缘部署AI模型，发现经优化的量化大语言模型（LLM）在总体可持续性上可超过原生小语言模型（SLM），且量化是系统性选择而非单调精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI模型选择通常只关注单一指标（准确性、延迟、内存、能耗或安全），但可部署的语言模型需要同时平衡这五个方面，因此需要一种综合评估方法来确定原生SLM与量化LLM哪种更适合可持续的边缘部署。

Method: 引入HSS评分，围绕三重底线（经济：能力与系统效率；环境：GPU能耗；社会：有害提示鲁棒性）构建，测试5个BF16 SLM和5个LLM在5种量化方式下的30种配置，使用5个零样本基准评估能力，测量延迟、吞吐量、峰值VRAM和能量评估效率，用5个有害提示的攻击成功率近似安全性。

Result: Qwen3-30B-A3B/GGUF Q4排名第一（93.38），Mistral-Small-24B/GGUF Q4第二（92.40），Phi-4-mini/BF16是最高分SLM（89.49）；原生SLM并非普遍最可持续的选择。

Conclusion: 原生SLM必须是最可持续边缘选择的假设不成立；优化的量化LLM可以整体胜出，而SLM在低资源需求方面仍具竞争力；量化是系统级选择而非单调权衡，HSS仅相对于其比较池和代理定义有效。

Abstract: Edge-AI model selection is commonly driven by one isolated metric - accuracy, latency, memory, energy, or safety, even though a deployable language model must balance all five. Our work focuses on answering the question whether na- tively trained small language models (SLMs) or large language models (LLMs) compressed through post-training quantization offer the more sustainable edge- deployment trade-off.
