We introduce a reproducible Holistic Sustainability Score (HSS) organized around the triple bottom line: an economic pillar for capability and systems efficiency, an environmental pillar for operational GPU energy and a social pillar for harmful-prompt robustness. Five BF16 SLMs and five LLMs under different quantization approaches - BF16, INT8, NF4 4-bit, GPTQ 4-bit, and GGUF Q4 produce 30 measured configurations. Capability is assessed on five zero-shot benchmarks; efficiency uses latency, throughput, peak VRAM and energy; and safety is approximated by attack success rate on five harmful prompts. Qwen3-30B-A3B/GGUF Q4 ranks first in the combined pool (93.38), followed by Mistral-Small-24B/GGUF Q4 (92.40), while Phi-4-mini/BF16 is the highest- ranked SLM in that pool (89.49). Thus, the hypothesis that native SLMs must be the most sustainable edge choice is not supported universally; optimized quantized LLMs can win overall, while SLMs remain competitive through lower resource demand. Quantization is a systems-level choice rather than a monotonic precision- efficiency trade-off and HSS remains relative to its comparison pool and proxy definitions.

</details>


### [261] [Conditional Flow Matching for ML-Based Inverse Design Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00863)
*Juliana Felder,Milad Habibi,Soheyl Massoudi,Mark Fuge*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文在工程逆向设计中将条件流匹配（CFM）与条件扩散模型和条件生成对抗网络（cGAN）进行比较，发现CFM在结构（beams2d）和热传导（heatconduction2d）基准上，以更少的网络评估次数实现了更优的累积最优差距（COG）、最终最优差距（FOG）、最大均值差异（MMD）和体积分数偏差，且推理吞吐量远超扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程逆向设计受限于偏微分方程约束的迭代求解器的高计算成本和对初始化的敏感性。深度生成模型可在推理时无需重复运行模拟器直接生成候选设计，但GAN单次采样而扩散模型需迭代反向积分，效率与质量权衡尚需探索，因此需要评估更高效的生成模型（如CFM）作为优化起点。

Method: 将条件流匹配（CFM）集成到EngiOpt框架，并与条件扩散模型及cGAN在EngiBench的beams2d和heatconduction2d基准上，采用相同的下游优化协议进行比较。使用累积最优差距（COG）、最终最优差距（FOG）、最大均值差异（MMD）和体积分数偏差作为评估指标，生成设计作为梯度优化热启动。

Result: CFM在两个任务上均取得最低的COG、FOG、MMD和体积分数偏差。其平均体积分数偏差在beams2d和heatconduction2d上分别为0.4%和1.0%，远低于扩散的3.8%和11.2%。在Euler步数s=16时，CFM在beams2d上达到53.2样本/秒，吞吐量约为使用1000次网络评估的扩散基线（相同计时协议）的66倍，此时COG为1.182±3.126，与s=32时的1.173±3.100相当。

Conclusion: 在两个任务中，CFM为优化生成的热启动相比两种基线产生更低的COG，且所需的网络评估次数少于扩散模型，表明CFM在工程逆设计中兼具高质量与高效率的潜力。

Abstract: Engineering inverse design is often limited by the high computational cost of iterative solvers for optimization problems constrained by partial differential equations (PDEs) and by their sensitivity to initialization. Deep generative models can produce candidate designs without rerunning the simulator at inference time. Generative adversarial networks (GANs) sample in one forward pass, whereas diffusion models require iterative reverse-time integration.
  In this work, we add conditional flow matching (CFM) to EngiOpt and compare it with a conditional diffusion model and a conditional generative adversarial network (cGAN) on structural (beams2d) and thermal (heatconduction2d) benchmarks from EngiBench using the same downstream optimization protocol. We use cumulative optimality gap (COG) and final optimality gap (FOG) as the primary metrics for evaluating the generated designs as warm starts for gradient-based refinement. On the evaluated EngiOpt implementations and two EngiBench tasks, CFM achieves the lowest measured COG, FOG, maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), and volume-fraction deviation on both tasks. CFM has mean volume-fraction deviations of 0.4% and 1.0% on beams2d and heatconduction2d, respectively, compared with 3.8% and 11.2% for diffusion. At Euler s = 16, CFM achieves 53.2 samples/s on beams2d, about 66 times the measured throughput of the evaluated diffusion baseline using 1000 network evaluations under the same timing protocol, with COG 1.182 +/- 3.126, compared with 1.173 +/- 3.100 for Euler s = 32.
  Across the two tasks, CFM produces warm starts with lower measured COG than both baselines and uses fewer network evaluations than diffusion.

</details>


### [262] [Escaping Redundant Reasoning: Structure-Aware Search for Inference-Time LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00738)
*Lu Cheng*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出了 BASIN 方法，通过惩罚重复访问相同推理策略来缓解大语言模型推理时的“推理盆地坍塌”现象，在固定预算下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理时的搜索常集中于少量结构或语义相似的轨迹，导致替代方案未被充分探索，这种失败模式被称为“推理盆地坍塌”（reasoning basin collapse）。

Method: BASIN 是一种无需训练、基于结构的轨迹选择方法，它将推理状态划分为盆地（basins），并惩罚对同一策略的重复访问，从而在固定计算预算下将搜索重新分配到真正不同的推理路径上。还提出了质量感知变体 QA-BASIN，通过保留高质量盆地来增强鲁棒性，防止无条件多样化导致的过度探索。

Result: 在相同推理预算下，BASIN 在 Game of 24 上比 Tree of Thoughts (ToT) 提升高达 +22 个百分点，在 MuSR 上提升 +6.7 个百分点。引入冗余差距 Δ 用于解释有效性，标准 ToT 常接近 Δ≈0，而 BASIN 使 Δ 持续为正。

Conclusion: 结构感知的轨迹选择是一种简单且通用的推理时改进方法，可用于增强大语言模型的推理能力，代码已公开。

Abstract: Inference-time search with large language models (LLMs) often concentrates on a small set of structurally or semantically similar trajectories, leaving alternatives underexplored---a failure mode we call \textit{reasoning basin collapse}. We introduce BASIN, a training-free, structure-aware selection method that groups reasoning states into basins and penalizes repeated visits to the same strategy, thereby reallocating search across genuinely distinct reasoning paths under a fixed compute budget. Under matched inference budgets, BASIN improves over Tree of Thoughts (ToT) by up to $+22$pp on Game of 24 and $+6.7$pp on MuSR. A quality-aware variant, QA-BASIN, further improves robustness by preserving high-quality basins when unconditional diversification over-explores. To explain when basin-aware selection helps, we introduce the redundancy gap $Δ$, which measures how differently search concentrates for correct versus incorrect predictions: standard ToT often operates near $Δ\approx 0$, while BASIN consistently shifts $Δ$ positive. More broadly, BASIN suggests structure-aware selection as a simple and general approach to improving inference-time reasoning. Code can be found at https://github.com/GitHubLuCheng/basin.

</details>


### [263] [SAGE: Subpopulation-Aware Generative Enhancement for Mitigating Spurious Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01051)
*Yiming Luo,Rongqiang Zhao,Jie Liu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出SAGE框架，利用聚类子标签和生成模型合成数据，填补欠表示区域并平衡验证集，在无组标签条件下显著提升最差组准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ERM模型易依赖多数类虚假相关属性，导致少数组性能差；现有无组标签方法重复上采样少数样本，降低多样性并引发过拟合。

Method: 两阶段生成增强框架：先用聚类生成子标签，结合类标签微调条件生成模型和文本编码器，生成针对性的合成数据填充训练集欠表示区域，并构建平衡验证集用于最后一层权重重加权。

Result: 在Waterbirds、CelebA和MetaShift上分别达到89.5%、85.7%和79.1%的最差组准确率，比最优无组标签基线最高提升7.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 数据中心的生成式增强可有效缓解无组标签场景下的虚假相关性问题，优于现有基于重采样的方法。

Abstract: Spurious correlations pose a significant challenge to the robustness of modern machine learning. The inherent imbalance in dataset distributions often leads traditional Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) models to rely on majority spurious attributes for classification, resulting in poor performance on minority groups. This problem becomes particularly challenging when the spurious attributes are unavailable. Existing group-label-free methods often upsample minority groups or misclassified real training examples; repeating the same instances can reduce effective diversity and encourage overfitting. To mitigate these spurious correlations from a data-centric perspective in the absence of prior knowledge, we introduce Subpopulation-Aware Generative Enhancement (SAGE), a two-stage generative augmentation framework. Using cluster-derived sub-labels and class labels, we fine-tune a conditional generative model and text encoder, generating targeted synthetic data to fill underrepresented regions in the training set and construct a balanced validation set for last-layer reweighting. We experimentally show that SAGE achieves 89.5%, 85.7%, and 79.1% worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds, CelebA, and MetaShift, respectively, outperforming the best group-label-free baselines by up to 7.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [264] [Neural Symbollic Regression Using Deep Learning and Sparse Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01102)
*Ravi Kumar U,Sumitra S*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种将神经网络作为函数预条件子的神经符号回归框架，通过解耦流水线结合LASSO提取稀疏表达式，并集成超参数优化，在Nguyen基准上优于SINDy和未调优基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遗传编程类符号回归方法可扩展性差且对噪声敏感，而SINDy等稀疏回归方法依赖预定义特征库，难以平衡可解释性、鲁棒性与数据规模。

Method: 先训练神经网络在交互感知的非线性特征空间中学习平滑抗噪的目标函数近似，再应用LASSO从该表示中提取稀疏闭式表达式，并用Ray Tune和ASHA进行分布式超参数优化。

Result: 在Nguyen基准套件上，该方法在RMSE、噪声鲁棒性和分布外泛化上持续优于SINDy与非调优神经基线，消融实验验证了特征交互、网络深度和调优策略的重要性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可扩展且可解释的神经-符号框架，桥接神经逼近与稀疏方程发现，适用于科学机器学习。

Abstract: Symbolic Regression (SR) seeks to find succinct mathematical expressions that represent the fundamental relationships within data, providing interpretability and scientific understanding that exceeds that of black-box models. Nevertheless, traditional methods like Genetic Programming face challenges with scalability and are highly sensitive to noise, while sparse regression techniques such as SINDy rely significantly on predetermined feature libraries. In this work, we present a Neural Symbolic Regression (NSR) framework that treats neural networks as functional preconditioners for symbolic discovery. Our approach uses a decoupled pipeline: a neural network first learns a smooth, noise-robust approximation of the target function in an interaction- aware nonlinear feature space. LASSO is then applied to extract sparse, interpretable closed-form expressions. To improve predictive accuracy and symbolic fidelity by integrating distributed hyperparameter optimization with Ray Tune and ASHA scheduling. Experiments on the Nguyen benchmark suite show that our approach consistently outperforms SINDy and non-tuned neural baselines in RMSE, noise robustness, and out-of-distribution generalization. Ablation studies confirm the significance of feature interactions, neural depth, and tuning strategies. In general, this study presents a scalable and understandable neural-symbolic framework, creating a solid link between neural approximation and the discovery of sparse equations for scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [265] [FractalNet-Based Heterogeneous Federated Learning for Orbital Edge Intelligence in Satellite Mega-Constellations: A Wildfire Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00875)
*Sai Puppala,Koushik Sinha*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种基于FractalNet的异构联邦学习方法，用于卫星巨型星座的轨道边缘智能，通过分层架构和分布式路径调度器解决SWAP-C差异与链路约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卫星学习架构继承地面联邦学习，未考虑卫星在SWAP-C、辐射容限、链路可用性上的数量级差异，难以支持大规模异构星座的分布式智能。

Method: 构建接触窗口约束下的深度异构联邦优化问题；设计分布式路径调度器根据SWAP-C、预测星间联系和训练统计分配模型深度；分层层池化降低通信开销；引入三层智能体控制平面管理在轨调度与异常升级。

Result: 在模拟巨型星座上验证了收敛性、通信效率、能量自适应、调度池化收益、鲁棒性和延迟的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为异构卫星星座提供高效的联邦学习范式，案例表明不同轨道高度可自然学习不同语义层级的态势感知，支持大规模轨道边缘智能应用。

Abstract: Satellite mega-constellations are emerging as large-scale sensing, communication, and computation fabrics, yet their learning architectures remain largely inherited from terrestrial federated learning and ground-centric mission operations--- ill-suited to satellites that differ by orders of magnitude in Size, Weight, Power, and Cost (SWAP-C), radiation tolerance, link availability, and propagation delay. We propose a heterogeneous federated learning method based on the FractalNet architecture for orbital edge intelligence. We formalize contact-window-constrained, depth-heterogeneous federated optimization and introduce a distributed path scheduler that assigns model depth as a function of SWAP-C constraints, predicted inter-satellite contacts, and training statistics. To reduce message overhead and energy consumption, each tier pools updates periodically rather than at every contact opportunity, and a three-tier agentic control plane governs in-space scheduling, anomaly escalation, and policy-governed autonomy. As a case study, we apply the framework to wildfire detection, where each orbital shell naturally learns a different semantic level of situational awareness: pixel-scale thermal anomalies at low Earth orbit (LEO), regional fire-front dynamics at medium Earth orbit (MEO), and larger-scale risk propagation at geostationary or high Earth orbit (GEO/HEO). Experiments on simulated mega-constellations validate the approach across convergence, communication efficiency, energy adaptation, scheduled-pooling savings, robustness, and latency.

</details>


### [266] [Towards reliable multimodal disaster severity assessment through preference optimization and explainable vision-language reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00879)
*Yuanjun Zhang,Fuzel Ahamed Shaik,Suvojit Acharjee,Fahad Khalid,Mourad Oussalah*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段训练框架（SFT+DPO），在统一数据构建流程下提升多模态灾害损伤评估的准确性和解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法受限于标注数据稀缺且推理质量评估不足，难以兼顾模型准确性与可解释性。

Method: 从单一HITL标注流程派生两类数据集（ReasoningSet用于SFT，PreferenceSet用于DPO），并通过自动指标、模型评分与人工排序综合评估。

Result: SFT使准确率从73.64%提升至78.29%，Macro-F1提升29%，解释质量提升约25%；DPO进一步优化偏好性，跨模型验证显示鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架能增强轻度损伤检测、降低高风险误判并促进推理与人类判断对齐，为应急管理提供可靠且可审计的多模态系统。

Abstract: Reliable disaster damage assessment requires models that provide both accurate predictions and transparent explanations. However, existing multimodal approaches are limited by scarce annotated data and insufficient evaluation of reasoning quality. This study proposes a two-stage training framework that integrates Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) within a unified data construction pipeline. From a single Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) annotation workflow, two complementary datasets are derived, namely ReasoningSet, which contains validated rationales for SFT, and PreferenceSet, which comprises paired rationales for DPO-based alignment. The framework evaluates both classification performance and explanation quality using automatic metrics, model-based scoring, and human ranking. Experimental results show that SFT improves accuracy from 73.64% to 78.29% and increases Macro-F1 by 29% compared to the baseline, while explanation quality improves by approximately 25%. Subsequent DPO alignment further enhances interpretability on the PreferenceSet. Cross-model validation on InternVL-3-8B and LLaVA-1.5-7B demonstrates the robustness and generalizability of the approach. The proposed framework improves detection of underrepresented mild damage cases, reduces high-risk misclassifications, and strengthens alignment between model reasoning and human judgment. Overall, it provides a reproducible pathway to develop reliable multimodal systems that deliver auditable, actionable disaster insights for emergency management.

</details>


### [267] [Explore More, Drift Less: Outcome-Only Reinforcement Learning Can Suffice for Long-Horizon Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01245)
*Liming Pu,Xiaoxia Li,Yifu Liu,Teng Cao,Bin Yang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出CANOPY协议，通过扩大同任务探索规模、保持在线策略与KL锚定，解决结果型RL在小模型上的信号饥饿与策略漂移问题，在AppWorld和SWE-bench上取得领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共识认为小开源模型仅靠结果奖励的RL很快触及天花板，但作者认为这是信号饥饿（组内混合成功与失败才产生梯度）和策略漂移（小任务池上过多更新导致分布坍缩）导致的假象。

Method: CANOPY协议：扩大同任务探索直到自然信号重现；每次更新保持在线策略、KL锚定，且仅约束智能体自身动作token；测试时增加交互预算。无需任务特定监督、辅助奖励或复杂脚手架。

Result: Qwen3-14B在AppWorld上达到Test-Normal TGC 86.9和Test-Challenge 67.6，登顶公共榜单；Qwen3.5-9B在SWE-bench Verified上提升16.6分。

Conclusion: 纯智能体RL即可将长程交互能力内化到小开源模型中，证明此前天花板是训练实践缺陷而非能力限制，代码将开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a natural way to post-train LLM agents for long-horizon interactive tasks judged only by end-of-task verification, yet a shared belief holds that outcome-only RL soon hits a ceiling on small open models. Recent work therefore compensates around the training with denser rewards, SFT priors, skill libraries, curated memory, or multi-agent orchestration. We argue the ceiling is an artifact of two failures of common practice. Signal starvation: group-relative RL with sparse outcome-only rewards yields a gradient only when a task's rollout group mixes successes and failures, so under-scaled exploration silences exactly the hardest, most instructive tasks. Policy drift: squeezing many updates out of a small task pool degrades the policy itself, as an unanchored objective lets the sampling distribution collapse exactly when saturation has already made informative groups rare. We present CANOPY (Coverage-ANchored On-PolicY RL), a minimalist protocol attacking both directly: scale same-task exploration until the natural signal reappears, keep every update on-policy, KL-anchored, and confined to the agent's own action tokens, then cash in an enlarged interaction budget at test time. On AppWorld, a long-horizon interactive coding benchmark, a Qwen3-14B policy trained with CANOPY through environment interaction alone--without task-specific supervision, auxiliary credit signals, or elaborate agent scaffolding--topped the public leaderboard (Feb. 2026; Test-Normal TGC 86.9, Test-Challenge 67.6), and the same design principles lift Qwen3.5-9B on SWE-bench Verified by 16.6 points. Agentic RL alone thus internalizes long-horizon capability directly into a small open model; we plan to release the complete training stack at https://github.com/AlibabaResearch/SignalCoverageRL.

</details>


### [268] [The Constitutional Coverage Trilemma in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01275)
*Natalija Mitic,Soona Sedahmed A. O.,Mamadou Selly Ly,Moustapha Cisse*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 前沿AI系统的默认宪法与人类需求严重错配：需求覆盖五大价值且分布广泛，而23个模型原型的供给仅覆盖需求空间的约2%，且版本迭代正系统性地远离本就匮乏的自主性；一个仅含诚实和自主两个顶点的菜单即可大幅降低遗憾值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多关注AI对齐的单点指标，忽视了部署模型作为‘宪法机构’所隐含的价值排序是否在整体市场上满足多元人类需求；本研究首次结合大规模模型审计与人类偏好实验，系统量化供给-需求的宪法类型错配及其动态漂移。

Method: 使用23个前沿LLM原型的出厂默认宪法（经释义控制审计），与1,649名美国参与者在同一成对权衡工具上的偏好数据；采用噪声匹配与全精度估计计算需求/供给凸包体积，并检验跨模型家族与版本内的价值排序变化，最后评估稀疏顶点菜单的遗憾值改进。

Result: 需求横跨五类价值且最大群体不足三分之一；23原型凸包仅覆盖需求凸包的约2%（保守）或0.10%（全精度）；无模型将帮助性或自主性置顶（37%用户无家可归）；6家族中5个自主性下降、5个公平性上升、4个安全性上升，版本内趋势单调（置换检验p=0.013）；添加诚实+自主双顶点菜单使平均遗憾降低47%，三个顶点可进一步降低最多81%（均值）与64%（最差组）。

Conclusion: 供给端存在系统性方向性漂移，远离本就覆盖不足的自主性，恶化最弱势用户的福利底线；但修复是稀疏的——少量宪法顶点即可大幅改善匹配；研究将发现形式化为预算化多元主义三难困境，并在经验上确认其约束有效，鲁棒性检验支持结论稳健。

Abstract: Frontier AI systems function as \emph{constitutional institutions}: each deployed model encodes an implicit ranking among safety, helpfulness, honesty, autonomy, and equity. We ask whether the supply of frontier constitutional types covers human demand. Combining a paraphrase-controlled audit of the as-shipped default constitutions of $23$ frontier LLM archetypes with a pairwise-tradeoff study of $1{,}649$ US participants on the same instrument, we report three facts. \emph{Demand is broad}: it spans all five values, with the largest constituency under one-third. \emph{Supply is narrow and drifting}: the $23$-archetype hull occupies ${\sim}2\%$ of the demand hull under conservative noise-matched estimation ($0.10\%$ at full audit precision), no archetype puts helpfulness or autonomy first ($37\%$ of users are constitutionally homeless), and across six model families autonomy decreases in $5/6$, equity increases in $5/6$, and safety increases in $4/6$, with monotone within-family version trends (order-permutation $p = 0.013$) and the autonomy decline concentrated in scenarios where safety is not at stake. The drift's importance is directional: \emph{away} from a value already undercovered, mechanically worsening the welfare floor for the least-served users. \emph{The fix is sparse}: a $2$-vertex menu $\{e_{\mathrm{HON}}, e_{\mathrm{AUT}}\}$ beats the full $23$-archetype frontier by $47\%$ on mean regret (CI $[43\%, 52\%]$); three vertex additions cut mean/worst-group regret by up to $81\%$/$64\%$. We formalize these findings as a budgeted-pluralism trilemma, show the binding regime is empirically realized, and verify the conclusions are robust to distance-based welfare and to degraded routing. The instrument and audit harness are described in full in the appendices.

</details>


### [269] [In-Context Neurofeedback: Can LLMs Control Their Internal Representations through Privileged Access?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00904)
*Koshiro Aoki,Ryota Takatsuki,Gouki Minegishi,Yusuke Haruki,Daisuke Kawahara*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 在更严格的特权访问条件下，LLM无法可靠控制其内部表征，表明先前报告的神经反馈控制可能依赖表面机制而非真正的内部访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究声称LLM能通过神经反馈控制内部表征，但其控制目标非特权（可从提示中推断），无法排除表面机制，需设计更严格的实验检验真正的内部访问能力。

Method: 重新设计神经反馈范式，使控制目标满足特权访问要求（第三方无法从提示推断），类比人类认知神经科学中的神经反馈实验，测试LLM在严格条件下的控制能力。

Result: 在特权访问条件下，模型未能表现出对内部表征的可靠控制，与先前非特权条件下的成功结果形成对比。

Conclusion: LLM元认知的严谨评估必须采用要求特权访问的方法，先前结果不足以证明真正的内部表征控制，提示需警惕表面机制干扰。

Abstract: Whether large language models (LLMs) can control their own internal representations matters for both machine metacognition and AI safety. A recent study applied neurofeedback to LLMs and claimed that they can control their internal representations. However, the reported control may rely on superficial mechanisms rather than genuine internal access because the control targets in that study are not privileged, meaning that a third party can infer them from the prompt. We redesign the neurofeedback paradigm for LLMs so that the control target satisfies the privileged access requirement, which is closer to neurofeedback experiments in human cognitive neuroscience. Under this stricter setting, the models do not demonstrate reliable control over privileged internal representations, suggesting that previously reported control cannot exclude the possibility that it relies on superficial mechanisms. Our results indicate that rigorous assessments of metacognition in LLMs require evaluation methods that demand privileged access.

</details>


### [270] [Provably Safe Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01418)
*Tingting Ni,Maryam Kamgarpour*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文研究安全模拟到现实迁移问题，提出一种利用不完美模拟器减少真实交互同时保证安全探索的算法，并给出样本复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实RL样本昂贵，常用模拟器预训练策略，但模拟与真实环境差异会导致策略次优。修正需收集真实数据，而真实系统存在安全约束，由此引出安全sim-to-real迁移问题。

Method: 将安全sim-to-real迁移建模为奖励无关安全RL框架，设计计算高效算法，利用模拟器信息减少真实环境交互，同时保证安全探索，并为任意潜在奖励函数计算近似最优可行策略。

Result: 导出了真实样本复杂度界，以sim-to-real失配程度量化模拟器带来的效益。

Conclusion: 算法能有效降低真实交互需求，同时保障安全并实现近最优策略，样本复杂度直接体现模拟器质量的影响。

Abstract: To mitigate the sample complexity of real-world reinforcement learning (RL), a common practice is to first train a policy in a simulator, where samples are cheap, and then deploy the learned policy in the real world with the hope that it generalizes effectively. Such direct sim-to-real transfer is not guaranteed to succeed: simulator-trained policies can be suboptimal in the real world due to sim-to-real mismatch. Correcting this mismatch requires collecting data from the real system, but in many applications, such as robotics and healthcare, this data-collection process is itself subject to safety constraints. This gives rise to the problem of safe sim-to-real transfer: how can an agent exploit an imperfect simulator while ensuring safe real-world data collection and learning a near-optimal feasible policy for the target system? We address this problem by formulating safe sim-to-real transfer within the framework of reward-free safe RL. We design a computationally efficient algorithm that exploits simulator information to provably reduce real-world interaction while ensuring safe exploration and enabling the computation of a near-optimal feasible policy for any potential reward function. Our real-world sample complexity bound characterizes the benefit of using the simulator in terms of the sim-to-real mismatch.

</details>


### [271] [Spawn Freely, Act Sparingly: Progressive Risk Vesting for Recursive LLM-Agent Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01035)
*Molly Wang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出渐进风险归属（PRV）机制，通过轨迹级风险预算与激活扣费，为递归LLM智能体在分支激活时提供随时可证明的危害上界，并揭示授权繁殖数临界点导致的风险相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归LLM智能体通过生成专家分支扩大搜索，但部分分支请求工具调用涉及真实数据或代码执行。现有方法未明确区分沙箱繁殖（外部控制阻止危害）与能力激活（分支跨越不可逆行动边界），导致授权时机与风险控制缺乏理论保证。

Method: PRV将轨迹级风险预算存入托管账户，并在分支激活时逐步扣减。理论证明对自适应生成树成立随时危害界，允许分支结果依赖但要求局部证书在完整前置历史条件下有效。通过随机繁殖模型与有限类型占用模型分析授权繁殖数R_A跨越1时的风险标度律，并推导风险与计算影子价格及阈值规则。

Result: 当局部风险p趋近零：低于临界性时轨迹危害~p，临界时~√p，高于临界时保持正下限。有限类型占用模型给出影子价格，嵌套扇出模式在边际价值递减时产生阈值决策规则。分支计算与分裂样本实验验证结论，但未估计部署智能体的实际安全性。

Conclusion: 设计规则为：在沙箱中广泛搜索，谨慎授予递归权限并明确风险收费。延迟归属不缩减可用策略集且提供可证明的时间一致性风险控制，但分支选择后边际风险估计仍可能失效，强调全历史条件验证的必要性。

Abstract: Recursive LLM agents can broaden their search by spawning specialists. Some branches later request tools that send data or deploy code. When should a branch receive authority to act? We distinguish sandbox spawning, in which external controls prevent the specified harm, from capability activation, in which a selected branch crosses an irreversible-action boundary. Progressive Risk Vesting (PRV) holds a trajectory-level risk budget in escrow and debits it as branches are activated. We prove an anytime harm bound for adaptively generated trees. Branch outcomes may be dependent, but each local certificate needs to remain valid conditional on the full pre-activation history, including the information used to select the request. When activation gates, branch charges, and compute constraints are held fixed, delayed vesting preserves every policy available under irrevocable spawn charging. Marginal risk estimates can still fail after branch selection. In a stylized branching model, trajectory harm changes as the authority reproduction number $\mathcal{R}_A$ crosses one. As local risk $p$ approaches zero, trajectory harm is proportional to $p$ below criticality, proportional to $\sqrt{p}$ at criticality, and retains a positive floor above it. A finite-type occupancy model yields risk and compute shadow prices. For nested fanout modes with decreasing marginal value per unit risk, these prices produce a threshold rule. Branching calculations and a split-sample experiment illustrate the results. These synthetic studies do not estimate safety in deployed agents. The analysis suggests a design rule: search broadly in the sandbox and grant recursive authority sparingly, with an explicit risk charge.

</details>


### [272] [TRIAGE: Three-level Routing and Intelligent Agent Guidance for Efficient Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01428)
*Ruocan Wei*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出TRIAGE三级路由框架，通过轨迹复用和技能抽象减少LLM Agent的token消耗，实现62.3%-76.3%的token节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct范式的LLM Agent每次查询都从零开始推理，相似查询重复执行相同步骤，缺乏经验复用导致效率低下。

Method: 构建三级路由：完全复用（L1）、技能替换（L2，零token参数替换）和完整ReAct（L3）；提出TaaS将历史轨迹抽象为可复用技能，并设计自动技能提取机制形成正反馈循环。

Result: 在1007条安全监控查询上节省62.3%token（L1占5.5%，L2占56.0%均零成本）；ToolBench跨域验证节省76.3%；在线学习实验中L2命中率从0%升至57%，平均token成本从198降至74.7。

Conclusion: 通过历史轨迹的层次化复用与技能化沉淀，实现'越用越高效'的持续优化，验证了语义路由的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents based on the ReAct paradigm have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in tool use and task execution. However, ReAct suffers from a fundamental efficiency problem: every query triggers a complete reasoning loop from scratch, and similar queries repeat identical steps without leveraging historical experience. We propose TRIAGE,a three-level routing framework that reduces token consumption by reusing historical execution trajectories. Its core innovation is TaaS (Trajectory-as-a-Skill), which abstracts historical execution trajectories into reusable skills, realizing 'experience as a service'. TRIAGE classifies queries into three levels: (1) Direct Reuse-identical queries, 0 tokens; (2) Skill Substitution-similar queries, 0 tokens via deterministic parameter substitution; (3) Full ReAct-novel queries, automatically stored for future reuse. In large-scale experiments on 1,007 security monitoring queries, TRIAGE achieves 62.3% token savings, with 56.0% of queries at Level 2 and 5.5% at Level 1, both executing at zero cost. Cross-domain validation on ToolBench (15 domains, 345 queries) achieves 76.3% token reduction, confirming the generalizability of semantic routing. An online learning experiment demonstrates cold-start-to-mature evolution: the L2 hit rate rises from 0% to 57% within the first 100 queries, and the average token cost drops from 198 to 74.7. We also propose an automatic Skill extraction mechanism that distills high-frequency trajectory patterns into deterministic Skills, creating a positive feedback loop of 'the more you use it, the more efficient it becomes'.

</details>


### [273] [Optimizing Byzantine Node Placement in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01495)
*Edoardo Gabrielli,Gabriele Tolomei*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出将去中心化联邦学习中拜占庭节点的位置选择视为对抗性决策，并引入BPI度量以高效评估和优化恶意节点对诚实节点的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全性评估仅关注拜占庭节点的行为，忽略其被安置的具体位置；由于通信图上的分布式聚合，位置决定恶意影响的传播路径与范围。

Method: 形式化攻击者目标为在固定预算下选择最大化有限时间影响的节点集合；提出基于八卦动态的集合级度量BPI，并开发高效优化算法，支持多跳加权传播与节点间交互。

Result: 在六个异构图族、非定向模型投毒和后门攻击下，BPI引导的放置策略始终识别高破坏性配置，且在放宽线性假设后仍有效。

Conclusion: 拜占庭节点放置是DFL威胁模型与鲁棒性评估中关键但常被忽视的维度，BPI为量化其影响提供有效工具。

Abstract: Security evaluations of decentralized federated learning (DFL) typically focus on how Byzantine participants behave, while largely overlooking which participants are compromised. Yet, because aggregation is distributed over a communication graph, the placement of Byzantine nodes determines how malicious influence propagates through the network. We therefore treat Byzantine placement as an explicit adversarial decision and formulate the attacker's objective as selecting, under a fixed compromise budget, the set of participants that maximizes its finite-time impact on honest nodes. To approximate this objective without executing the learning process for every candidate placement, we introduce Byzantine Placement Influence (BPI), a set-level measure derived from the actual gossip dynamics that quantifies the cumulative exposure of honest nodes to Byzantine sources over the training horizon. Unlike placement criteria based on node centrality heuristics, BPI directly accounts for weighted multi-hop propagation and interactions among compromised nodes. We develop efficient algorithms for optimizing BPI and evaluate them across six heterogeneous graph families, untargeted model poisoning, and backdoor attacks. BPI-guided placements consistently identify highly damaging configurations across different network structures and remain effective when the linear gossip assumption is relaxed through Byzantine-robust aggregation. Our results show that Byzantine placement is a critical but under-modeled dimension of DFL threat models and robustness evaluations.

</details>


### [274] [Jailbreaking Text-to-Image Models Through Cracks: Navigating Heterogeneous Safety Filters via Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01168)
*Kaiyan Wen,Shijie Zhang,Lu Yu,Guangdong Bai*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出检测表面这一几何框架，分析多层T2I安全过滤器联合决策边界，并基于此设计多智能体辩论框架CRACK，以自适应搜索绕过综合防御，实现高达99.63%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I越狱研究难以识别多层安全堆栈中实际生效的约束，且缺乏对跨层冲突的系统建模，导致攻击效率低、适应性差。

Method: 构建检测表面统一刻画各层过滤器决策边界及联合影响；CRACK框架通过攻击、防御、评判三智能体辩论，分解搜索任务并利用层特异性反馈和奖励引导迭代优化突变策略。

Result: 在多种T2I模型、数据集和安全配置下，CRACK达到最高99.63%的ASR，查询次数少于现有方法，且保持语义保真度。

Conclusion: 检测表面揭示了跨层冲突对越狱搜索空间的关键作用，CRACK通过自适应多智能体协作有效应对复杂防御，但需警惕其潜在滥用风险。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that elicit Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content, despite increasingly being guarded by heterogeneous, multi-layer safety stacks combining text filters, image classifiers, and cross-modal detectors. Existing jailbreak studies either optimize against individual filters or query the complete pipeline with aggregate feedback, making it difficult to identify the active constraint and adapt to conflicts across safety layers.In this paper, we introduce the \emph{Detection Surface}, a unified geometric framework that characterizes the decision boundaries induced by heterogeneous T2I safety filters and their joint effect on the jailbreak search space. This formulation reveals that successful evasion is governed by a sparse and non-convex region shaped by cross-layer conflicts, where mutations that bypass one filter may increase exposure to another. Motivated by this analysis, we propose \emph{CRACK}, a multi-agent debate framework for adaptive jailbreak search that decomposes jailbreak search into exploration, diagnosis, and arbitration. CRACK coordinates an Attack Agent, a Defense Agent, and a Judge Agent to iteratively generate prompt mutations, obtain layer-specific diagnostic feedback, and optimize mutation strategies through reward-guided refinement. Through repeated rounds of debate, CRACK adapts its search direction to the evolving cross-layer constraints while preserving the original harmful intent. Extensive experiments across multiple T2I models, datasets, and safety configurations show that CRACK achieves Attack Success Rates (ASR) of up to 99.63\% under composite defenses, while requiring fewer queries than existing methods and maintaining semantic fidelity.

</details>


### [275] [Quantum Sparse Autoencoders for Q-Matrix Estimation in Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01537)
*Arif Hassan Zidan,Yi Pan,Bowen Guo,Xiang Li,Yu Bao,Yingfeng Wang,Tianming Liu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文首次将量子机器学习应用于认知诊断中的Q矩阵估计，提出量子稀疏自编码器（QSAE），在模拟和真实数据上表现出更强的稳定性和对真实数据潜在结构的探索能力，而非普遍精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Q矩阵估计在技能数量多且相关、真实响应模式偏离理想假设时面临挑战，而量子机器学习可能带来新的解决思路。

Method: QSAE利用量子电路编码学生的二值响应向量，通过稀疏潜表示压缩并映射到Q矩阵，与经典自动编码器（CAE）进行对比。

Result: 在60个模拟数据集和9个真实数据集中，QSAE在49个模拟条件下方差更低（更稳定），并在9个真实数据集中的6个上优于CAE，但CAE在部分模拟条件下平均精度更高。

Conclusion: 量子机器学习在Q矩阵估计中的主要优势不是普遍提升精度，而是增强鲁棒性和对真实数据潜在结构的探索能力。

Abstract: Q-matrices play a central role in cognitive diagnosis within educational data mining (EDM), specifying which latent skills each assessment item requires. Data-driven Q-matrix estimation remains challenging when assessments involve many correlated skills and when real response patterns depart from idealized generative assumptions. We introduce a novel quantum sparse autoencoder (QSAE) for Q-matrix estimation, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first application of quantum machine learning (QML) to cognitive diagnosis. Overall, the QSAE embeds each student's binary response vector into a quantum circuit using an encoder, compresses it into a sparse latent representation, and maps that representation to the Q-matrix. We benchmark the QSAE against a classical autoencoder (CAE) across 60 simulated datasets and 9 real-world assessment datasets. The results reveal complementary strengths. Although the CAE partially achieves higher average accuracy under several simulation conditions, the QSAE is substantially more stable across replications, exhibiting lower variance in 49 of the 60 conditions. Moreover, on real assessment data, the QSAE outperforms the CAE on 6 of the 9 datasets. These findings suggest that the principal advancement of QML in this setting is not universal accuracy improvement, but enhanced robustness and capability to explore latent-structure complexity in real datasets.

</details>


### [276] [NashDreamer: Model-Based Reinforcement Learning for Zero-Sum Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01549)
*Tomáš Holeček,Viliam Lisý*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出NashDreamer，首个针对双人零和不完全信息博弈的基于模型的强化学习框架，通过集中式多智能体循环状态空间模型解决非平稳性和可识别性问题，显著提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBRL在单智能体领域成功，但扩展到竞争性不完全信息博弈（IIGs）尚未充分探索。多智能体环境中对手引起的非平稳性使学习复杂化，且分散式模型学习面临严重的可识别性障碍，因此集中式模型学习是数学上的必然选择。

Method: NashDreamer引入集中式多智能体循环状态空间模型（MARSSM），将环境动力学与玩家策略对个体观测的影响解耦。框架兼容任意策略梯度算法，在理想化模型下继承其收敛到纳什均衡的保证。

Result: 在四个基准博弈游戏中，NashDreamer在训练早期显著提升样本效率，优于无模型基线。理论上分析了架构优化景观，发现Dreamer类算法在随机环境中易受后验坍塌影响。

Conclusion: NashDreamer为IIG中的MBRL提供有效框架，但后验坍塌问题仍作为开放挑战，需未来进一步研究。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) has achieved remarkable results in single-agent domains, yet its extension to competitive imperfect information games (IIGs) remains underexplored. In multi-agent settings, opponent-induced non-stationarity complicates the learning process, and decentralized model learning faces severe identifiability barriers, which we argue make centralized model learning a mathematical necessity. Building on this analysis, we propose NashDreamer, a principled MBRL framework for two-player zero-sum IIGs. It introduces a centralized Multi-Agent Recurrent State-Space Model (MARSSM) that decouples environment dynamics from the effect of players' strategies on their individual observations. NashDreamer is designed to use arbitrary policy gradient algorithms and inherits their convergence guarantees towards Nash equilibria under an idealized model. Empirical evaluations across four benchmark games demonstrate that NashDreamer substantially improves sample efficiency over model-free baselines early in the training. Finally, we theoretically analyze the architecture's optimization landscape, identifying the vulnerability of the Dreamer family of algorithms to posterior collapse in stochastic environments. We leave it as an open challenge.

</details>


### [277] [Prompt-Robust Language Models: Which Training Strategies Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01217)
*Frederic Sadrieh,Michal Štefánik*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 研究发现现有鲁棒性微调方法虽优于标准微调和小样本学习，但最佳与最差提示的性能差距仍高达40-57%；对比实验显示，简单的一模板批次数据策略往往胜过复杂鲁棒性方法，其背后是模板梯度冲突导致的优化困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型对提示词表述高度敏感，现有工作或通过改进数据构造、或设计专门鲁棒性目标来缓解，但在受控条件下缺乏系统比较和机理分析。

Method: 在统一受控条件下复现并对比多种数据构造策略与鲁棒性微调方法（包括CoIN对比对齐、PPCL一致性正则化），并通过诊断实验分析梯度方向一致性及辅助目标的泛化局限。

Result: 当前鲁棒性微调优于标准微调和小样本学习，但最优与最差提示的性能差仍达40-57%；CoIN和PPCL常不如每批次单模板训练的简单策略；诊断表明辅助目标仅优化其惩罚项而不泛化，且57-64%参数上不同模板梯度方向冲突。

Conclusion: 混合模板批次的训练会使优化器在冲突梯度中妥协，难以学到与提示无关的共享表征；未来应优先探索减少梯度冲突的数据构造或优化方法，而非仅依赖复杂辅助目标。

Abstract: Despite their strong performance, large language models remain highly sensitive to prompt formulation. Prior work addresses this through refined data construction or through dedicated robustness objectives. We reproduce and compare these strategies under controlled conditions, and measure how effective they are in addressing models' prompt sensitivity. We find the current robustness fine-tuning methods improve over standard fine-tuning and in-context learning, but the best-to-worst prompt gap remains as high as 40-57% of performance. Moreover, the recent robustness-enhancing methods we test - CoIN for contrastive alignment and PPCL for consistency regularization - often fail to outperform the simplest data construction strategy: training on one template per batch. Our diagnostics explain these results. The auxiliary objectives move the quantity they penalize, but do not generalize beyond it. Additionally, data construction strategies differ due to the conflicting signs of per-template gradients on 57-64% of parameters. Thus, batches that mix formulations force the optimizer to reconcile competing updates instead of finding a shared, prompt-agnostic one.

</details>


### [278] [Retrieved but not ranked: surface-form bias in structural retrieval, from mathematics to agent trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01556)
*Nabira Rashid,Manolis Kellis*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了嵌入检索在‘表面形式与语义分离’场景下的表现，覆盖数学竞赛题（MathNet-Retrieve）和具身智能体轨迹（ALFWorld）两个领域。结果显示纯词面检索严重失效：数学任务中严格命中率为0，轨迹任务中目标需涉及不同物体或容器时低于随机水平；而LLM重排序可恢复部分性能但部分收益源于记忆；下游求解实验表明检索质量对最终准确率无显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入检索评估多假设查询与目标存在表面重叠，但真实场景中常需依据潜在结构而非字面匹配进行检索。为揭示当前嵌入模型在‘表面-语义’解耦下的真实能力，及其在下游任务中的作用边界，设计了跨领域、统一协议的评估框架。

Method: 在两个领域（数学竞赛和ALFWorld轨迹）下分别构建检索语料与查询集，采用严格命中率（Hit@1）、词面相似度对比、超几何随机基线等指标；引入词法重排序器和LLM重排序器作为控制与干预；并通过配对下游实验（210个查询，评分者一致性96-99%）检验检索结果对求解准确率的影响，使用McNemar检验和Bootstrap置信区间进行统计推断。

Result: 数学中所有生产嵌入器在最难混淆级别下Hit@1为0.0%，但正确答案常出现在前10；轨迹中当目标对象或容器需改变时，命中率接近或低于随机水平。词法重排序在数学领域有害、在轨迹领域有效（缩小26-36%差距）；LLM重排序恢复数学中5-63%、轨迹中43-76%的差距，但效果因领域和评判模型而异。数学收益集中于知名竞赛，表明部分恢复源于记忆。下游实验中，理想检索与对抗性检索结果无显著差异（McNemar p=0.678），求解器准确率主要体现为截断代理。

Conclusion: 嵌入检索核心锚定于字面token而非任务结构，在表面-语义解耦场景下系统性失效。词法重排序的作用符号可揭示基准的对抗性或偶然性设计；LLM重排序提供有限恢复且依赖模型先验。更重要的是，检索质量对下游任务结果无实质影响，提示未来应重新审视检索评估的真实效用和基准构造方式。

Abstract: We evaluate embedding retrieval where surface form and meaning are pulled apart on purpose: retrieving items that share underlying structure but not wording, in two unrelated domains under one protocol, competition mathematics (MathNet-Retrieve; 500 queries, 117,088-item corpus) and embodied-agent trajectories (ALFWorld-derived; 118 queries, 336 trajectories). In mathematics the failure is complete: strict Hit@1 at the heaviest disguise tier is 0.0% for both production embedders (bootstrap 95% CI [0.0, 0.0]) while the correct item sits in the top 10 nearly always, and in 95.2 to 99.8% of misses the winner is more lexically similar to the query than the correct answer. In trajectories, where surface variation is incidental, the same models land at or near hypergeometric chance when gold must involve a different object, and below chance for all three embedders once gold must differ in object and receptacle: retrieval anchors on literal tokens, not task structure. A lexical reranker control hurts in mathematics and helps in trajectories (closing 26 to 36% of the gap, CIs excluding zero); its sign reveals whether a benchmark's surface variation is adversarial or incidental. An LLM reranker recovers 5 to 63% of the gap in mathematics and 43 to 76% in trajectories; direction replicates across three judges (all 21 cells positive), but effect sizes, tier profiles, and the outlier judge change with domain (paired differences excluding zero everywhere). Mathematics gains concentrate on well-known competitions (+19.8 points, CI [+6.7, +33.2], one of six cells), so part of the recovery is memorization. In a paired downstream experiment (210 queries, graders at 96 to 99% agreement), oracle retrieval was indistinguishable from adversarially bad retrieval (McNemar p = 0.678); the solver's 69.5% zero-shot accuracy is largely a truncation proxy (97 to 100% on finished answers), leaving no headroom.

</details>
